          localChanges += pushUniqueLocal(body.injuries, label);
        }
      }

      return localChanges;
    };

    const localChanges =
      Guard && typeof Guard.applyOnce === "function"
        ? Guard.applyOnce({
            kind: "player_" + String(candidate.type || "change"),
            target: target,
            payload: payload,
            sourceId: candidate.id || ""
          }, commit, root)
        : commit();

    changes += localChanges;
    candidate._playerApplied = true;
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    this.recomputeDerived(player);

    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("player", true, root);
    }
    if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
      U.invalidatePackets(root);
    }
    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Player consequences applied.");
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

extractIdentityHints(text) {
  const raw = String(text || "");
  const hints = {
    name: "",
    age: 0,
    species: "",
    titles: [],
    visibleTraits: [],
    bodyTags: []
  };

  let match = raw.match(/\bYou,\s*([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,30}),\s*(?:an?\s+)?\d{1,3}\s+year old\s+([A-Za-z][A-Za-z -]{1,40}?)(?=[,.]| with\b| at\b| from\b| who\b)/i);
  if (match) {
    hints.name = String(match[1] || "").trim();
    const ageMatch = raw.match(/\b(\d{1,3})\s+year(?:s)?\s+old\b/i);
    hints.age = ageMatch && ageMatch[1] ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(ageMatch[1]))) : 0;
    hints.species = String(match[2] || "").trim();
  }

  if (!hints.name) {
    match = raw.match(/\bThe player begins as\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,30})\b/i);
    if (match) {
      hints.name = String(match[1] || "").trim();
    }
  }

  if (!hints.name) {
    match = raw.match(/\bYou are\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,30})\s+now\b/);
    if (match) {
      hints.name = String(match[1] || "").trim();
    }
  }

  if (!hints.name) {
    match = raw.match(/^\s*Name\s*:\s*([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,30})\s*$/mi);
    if (match) {
      hints.name = String(match[1] || "").trim();
    }
  }

  if (!hints.species) {
    match = raw.match(/^\s*(?:Race|Species)\s*:\s*([A-Za-z][A-Za-z -]{1,40})\s*$/mi);
    if (match) {
      hints.species = String(match[1] || "").trim();
    }
  }

  if (
    /\bseven feet tall\b/i.test(raw) ||
    /\bhead brushing\b/i.test(raw) ||
    /\byou have to duck\b/i.test(raw) ||
    /\byour head brushing\b/i.test(raw)
  ) {
    hints.visibleTraits.push("very tall");
    hints.bodyTags.push("large_frame");
  }

  return hints;
},

normalizeSpeciesLabel(value) {
  const cleaned = String(value || "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[-_]+/g, " ")
    .replace(/^(?:an|a|the)\s+/i, "")
    .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  if (!cleaned) return "";
  if (/\d/.test(cleaned)) return "";
  if (cleaned.split(" ").length > 4) return "";

  const banned = new Set([
    "confused traveler",
    "traveler",
    "stranger",
    "person",
    "someone",
    "someone else",
    "man",
    "woman",
    "boy",
    "girl"
  ]);

  if (banned.has(cleaned)) return "";

  return cleaned;
},

applyIdentityHints(hints, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  if (!hints || typeof hints !== "object") return 0;

  const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
  const player = root.player || {};
  let changes = 0;

  const pushUniqueLocal = (list, value) => {
    const text = String(value || "").trim();
    if (!Array.isArray(list) || !text) return 0;
    if (list.indexOf(text) === -1) {
      list.push(text);
      return 1;
    }
    return 0;
  };

  const currentName = String(player.name || "").trim();
  const currentSpecies = this.normalizeSpeciesLabel(player.species || "");
  const nextName = String(hints.name || "").trim();
  const nextSpecies = this.normalizeSpeciesLabel(hints.species || "");

  if (nextName && (!currentName || currentName.toLowerCase() === nextName.toLowerCase())) {
    if (player.name !== nextName) {
      player.name = nextName;
      changes += 1;
    }
  }

  if (nextSpecies && (!currentSpecies || currentSpecies === "human" || currentSpecies === nextSpecies)) {
    if (player.species !== nextSpecies) {
      player.species = nextSpecies;
      changes += 1;
    }
  }

  const nextAge = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(hints.age || 0)));
  if (nextAge > 0 && (typeof player.age !== "number" || player.age <= 0 || player.age === nextAge)) {
    if (player.age !== nextAge) {
      player.age = nextAge;
      changes += 1;
    }
  }

  if (Array.isArray(hints.titles)) {
    for (let i = 0; i < hints.titles.length; i += 1) {
      changes += pushUniqueLocal(player.titles, hints.titles[i]);
    }
  }

  if (Array.isArray(hints.visibleTraits)) {
    for (let i = 0; i < hints.visibleTraits.length; i += 1) {
      changes += pushUniqueLocal(player.appearance.visibleTraits, hints.visibleTraits[i]);
    }
  }

  if (Array.isArray(hints.bodyTags)) {
    for (let i = 0; i < hints.bodyTags.length; i += 1) {
      changes += pushUniqueLocal(player.body.bodyTags, hints.bodyTags[i]);
    }
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    this.recomputeDerived(player);

    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("player", true, root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
      U.invalidatePackets(root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Player identity synced.");
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

recomputeDerived(player = (state.aidrpg && state.aidrpg.player) || null) {
  if (!player || typeof player !== "object") return null;

  const stats = player.stats || {};
  const levelState = player.levelState || {};
  const derived = player.derived || (player.derived = {});

  const level = Number(levelState.level || 1);
  const atk = Number(stats.atk || 0);
  const def = Number(stats.def || 0);
  const spd = Number(stats.spd || 0);
  const intl = Number(stats.intl || 0);
  const maxHp = Number(stats.maxHp || 0);
  const maxEp = Number(stats.maxEp || 0);

  derived.combatPressure = Math.max(1, Math.round(((atk + def + spd + intl) / 4) + level));
  derived.mobility = Math.max(0, Math.round(spd + (maxEp / 25)));
  derived.threatRating = Math.max(1, Math.round((level * 5) + derived.combatPressure + (maxHp / 10)));

  return derived;
},

  getCompactSummary(player = (state.aidrpg && state.aidrpg.player) || null) {
    if (!player || typeof player !== "object") {
      return "Player summary unavailable.";
    }

    const stats = player.stats || {};
    const levelState = player.levelState || {};
    const conditions = player.conditions || {};
    const body = player.body || {};
    const appearance = player.appearance || {};

    const active = Array.isArray(conditions.active) && conditions.active.length
      ? conditions.active.join(", ")
      : "none";

    const injuries = Array.isArray(body.injuries) && body.injuries.length
      ? body.injuries.slice(Math.max(0, body.injuries.length - 3)).join(", ")
      : "none";

    const outfit = appearance.outfitSummary && appearance.outfitSummary.trim()
      ? appearance.outfitSummary.trim()
      : "unspecified";

    const species = player.species && String(player.species).trim()
      ? String(player.species).trim()
      : "unknown";

    const agePart = typeof player.age === "number" && player.age > 0 ? `Age ${player.age}` : "Age unknown";
    const titles = Array.isArray(player.titles) && player.titles.length ? `Titles: ${player.titles.slice(-2).join(", ")}` : "Titles: none";

    return [
      `Lvl ${levelState.level ?? 1}`,
      agePart,
      species,
      titles,
      `HP ${stats.hp ?? 0}/${stats.maxHp ?? 0}`,
      `MP ${stats.mp ?? 0}/${stats.maxMp ?? 0}`,
      `EP ${stats.ep ?? 0}/${stats.maxEp ?? 0}`,
      `Cond: ${active}`,
      `Injuries: ${injuries}`,
      `Outfit: ${outfit}`
    ].join(" | ");
  }
},

    // === BUILD IDENTITY SYSTEM ===
BuildIdentitySystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const player = root.player || {};

    if (!root.build || typeof root.build !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.build)) {
      root.build = {};
    }

    const build = root.build;

    if (!Array.isArray(build.originTags)) build.originTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.combatTags)) build.combatTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.methodTags)) build.methodTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.resourceTags)) build.resourceTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.defenseTags)) build.defenseTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.specializationTags)) build.specializationTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.temperamentTags)) build.temperamentTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.styleTags)) build.styleTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.archetypeSummary)) build.archetypeSummary = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.masteryNotes)) build.masteryNotes = [];

    if (!build.matchupProfile || typeof build.matchupProfile !== "object" || Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile)) {
      build.matchupProfile = {};
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.weakAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.weakAgainst = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst = [];

    this.inferBaselineTags(build, player);
    this.rebuildArchetypeSummary(build, player);

    return build;
  },

  clearTagBuckets(build) {
    build.originTags = [];
    build.combatTags = [];
    build.methodTags = [];
    build.resourceTags = [];
    build.defenseTags = [];
    build.specializationTags = [];
    build.temperamentTags = [];
    build.styleTags = [];
  },

  addTag(bucket, value) {
    if (!Array.isArray(bucket)) return;
    const tag = String(value || "").trim();
    if (!tag) return;
    if (bucket.indexOf(tag) === -1) {
      bucket.push(tag);
    }
  },

  inferBaselineTags(build, player) {
    this.clearTagBuckets(build);

    const stats = player.stats || {};
    const levelState = player.levelState || {};
    const derived = player.derived || {};
    const body = player.body || {};
    const conditions = player.conditions || {};
    const appearance = player.appearance || {};

    const species = typeof player.species === "string" && player.species.trim()
      ? player.species.trim().toLowerCase()
      : "unknown";

    this.addTag(build.originTags, "species:" + species);

    if (species === "human") {
      this.addTag(build.originTags, "baseline_mortal");
      this.addTag(build.originTags, "adaptable");
    } else if (species !== "unknown") {
      this.addTag(build.originTags, "nonhuman");
    }

    const level = Number(levelState.level || 1);
    if (level <= 3) {
      this.addTag(build.originTags, "novice");
    } else if (level <= 10) {
      this.addTag(build.originTags, "developing");
    } else {
      this.addTag(build.originTags, "established");
    }

    const atk = Number(stats.atk || 0);
    const def = Number(stats.def || 0);
    const spd = Number(stats.spd || 0);
    const intl = Number(stats.intl || 0);
    const lck = Number(stats.lck || 0);
    const maxHp = Number(stats.maxHp || 0);
    const maxMp = Number(stats.maxMp || 0);
    const maxEp = Number(stats.maxEp || 0);
    const mobility = Number(derived.mobility || 0);
    const pressure = Number(derived.combatPressure || 0);

    const primaryStat = this.getPrimaryStatName({ atk, def, spd, intl, lck });

    if (primaryStat === "atk") {
      this.addTag(build.combatTags, "direct_force");
      this.addTag(build.styleTags, "offensive");
    } else if (primaryStat === "def") {
      this.addTag(build.combatTags, "anchor");
      this.addTag(build.styleTags, "steady");
    } else if (primaryStat === "spd") {
      this.addTag(build.combatTags, "mobile");
      this.addTag(build.styleTags, "agile");
    } else if (primaryStat === "intl") {
      this.addTag(build.combatTags, "technical");
      this.addTag(build.styleTags, "measured");
    } else if (primaryStat === "lck") {
      this.addTag(build.combatTags, "opportunistic");
      this.addTag(build.styleTags, "adaptive");
    }

    if (pressure >= 8) {
      this.addTag(build.combatTags, "combat_capable");
    } else {
      this.addTag(build.combatTags, "unproven");
    }

    if (atk >= def + 2) {
      this.addTag(build.methodTags, "pressure_first");
    } else if (def >= atk + 2) {
      this.addTag(build.methodTags, "durability_first");
    } else if (spd >= atk && spd >= def) {
      this.addTag(build.methodTags, "positioning_first");
    } else {
      this.addTag(build.methodTags, "balanced_method");
    }

    if (maxMp > maxEp) {
      this.addTag(build.resourceTags, "mana_leaning");
    } else if (maxEp > maxMp) {
      this.addTag(build.resourceTags, "stamina_leaning");
    } else {
      this.addTag(build.resourceTags, "mixed_resource");
    }

    if (intl >= 7) {
      this.addTag(build.resourceTags, "control_sensitive");
    }

    if (def >= 7 || maxHp >= 120) {
      this.addTag(build.defenseTags, "durable");
    }

    if (mobility >= 7 || spd >= 7) {
      this.addTag(build.defenseTags, "evasive");
    }

    if (maxHp < 90 && def < 5) {
      this.addTag(build.defenseTags, "fragile");
    }

    if (atk >= 7) {
      this.addTag(build.specializationTags, "power_bias");
    }
    if (def >= 7) {
      this.addTag(build.specializationTags, "resilience_bias");
    }
    if (spd >= 7) {
      this.addTag(build.specializationTags, "speed_bias");
    }
    if (intl >= 7) {
      this.addTag(build.specializationTags, "technical_bias");
    }
    if (lck >= 7) {
      this.addTag(build.specializationTags, "fortune_bias");
    }

    if (!build.specializationTags.length) {
      this.addTag(build.specializationTags, "generalist_bias");
    }

    if (Array.isArray(conditions.active) && conditions.active.length) {
      this.addTag(build.temperamentTags, "under_strain");
    } else {
      this.addTag(build.temperamentTags, "stable");
    }

    if (Array.isArray(body.injuries) && body.injuries.length) {
      this.addTag(build.temperamentTags, "pain_tested");
    }

    if (Array.isArray(body.mutations) && body.mutations.length) {
      this.addTag(build.originTags, "altered_body");
    }

    if (Array.isArray(appearance.visibleTraits) && appearance.visibleTraits.length) {
      this.addTag(build.styleTags, "visibly_distinct");
    }

    if (!build.styleTags.length) {
      this.addTag(build.styleTags, "unformed");
    }
  },

  getPrimaryStatName(statMap) {
    let bestName = "atk";
    let bestValue = -Infinity;

    const names = ["atk", "def", "spd", "intl", "lck"];
    for (let i = 0; i < names.length; i += 1) {
      const name = names[i];
      const value = Number(statMap[name] || 0);
      if (value > bestValue) {
        bestValue = value;
        bestName = name;
      }
    }

    return bestName;
  },

  rebuildArchetypeSummary(build, player) {
    const summary = [];

    const pushUnique = (value) => {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!text) return;
      if (summary.indexOf(text) === -1) {
        summary.push(text);
      }
    };

    if (build.combatTags.indexOf("direct_force") !== -1) pushUnique("force-leaning");
    if (build.combatTags.indexOf("anchor") !== -1) pushUnique("durability-leaning");
    if (build.combatTags.indexOf("mobile") !== -1) pushUnique("mobility-leaning");
    if (build.combatTags.indexOf("technical") !== -1) pushUnique("technical-leaning");
    if (build.combatTags.indexOf("opportunistic") !== -1) pushUnique("opportunistic");

    if (build.resourceTags.indexOf("mana_leaning") !== -1) pushUnique("mana-leaning");
    if (build.resourceTags.indexOf("stamina_leaning") !== -1) pushUnique("stamina-leaning");
    if (build.resourceTags.indexOf("mixed_resource") !== -1) pushUnique("mixed-resource");

    if (build.specializationTags.indexOf("generalist_bias") !== -1) pushUnique("generalist");
    if (build.specializationTags.indexOf("power_bias") !== -1) pushUnique("power-biased");
    if (build.specializationTags.indexOf("resilience_bias") !== -1) pushUnique("resilience-biased");
    if (build.specializationTags.indexOf("speed_bias") !== -1) pushUnique("speed-biased");
    if (build.specializationTags.indexOf("technical_bias") !== -1) pushUnique("technical-biased");

    if (!summary.length) {
      pushUnique("unformed");
    }

    build.archetypeSummary = summary;

    if (!build.matchupProfile || typeof build.matchupProfile !== "object") {
      build.matchupProfile = {};
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.weakAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.weakAgainst = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst = [];

    if (!build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst.length) {
      build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst.push("unknown_styles");
    }

    return build.archetypeSummary;
  },

  getCompactSummary(build = (state.aidrpg && state.aidrpg.build) || null) {
    if (!build || typeof build !== "object") {
      return "Build summary unavailable.";
    }

    const summary = Array.isArray(build.archetypeSummary) ? build.archetypeSummary : [];
    const styles = Array.isArray(build.styleTags) ? build.styleTags : [];
    const methods = Array.isArray(build.methodTags) ? build.methodTags : [];

    const parts = [];

    if (summary.length) {
      parts.push("Archetype: " + summary.join(", "));
    }

    if (styles.length) {
      parts.push("Style: " + styles.join(", "));
    }

    if (methods.length) {
      parts.push("Method: " + methods.join(", "));
    }

    if (!parts.length) {
      return "Build summary unavailable.";
    }

    return parts.join(" | ");
  }
},

    // === INTENT PARSER ===
IntentParser: {
  parseInput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const trimmed = U.normalizeSpaces(String(rawText || ""));

    if (!root.pending || typeof root.pending !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.pending)) {
      root.pending = {};
    }

    if (!trimmed) {
      root.pending.inputIntent = null;
      return null;
    }

    const lower = trimmed.toLowerCase();
    const scores = this.scoreActionFamilies(lower, trimmed);
    const primaryType = this.pickPrimaryType(scores);
    const alternates = this.buildAlternates(scores, primaryType);

    const intent = {
      primaryType: primaryType,
      confidence: this.scoreToConfidence(scores[primaryType] || 0),
      alternates: alternates,
      targetHint: this.extractTarget(trimmed, primaryType),
      weaponHint: this.extractWeapon(trimmed, primaryType),
      styleHint: this.extractStyle(trimmed, primaryType),
      abilityHint: this.extractAbility(trimmed, primaryType),
      itemHint: this.extractItem(trimmed, primaryType),
      destinationHint: this.extractDestination(trimmed, primaryType),
      trainingHint: this.extractTrainingFocus(trimmed, primaryType),
      environmentHint: "",
      tags: this.buildTags(primaryType, scores, trimmed),
      rawText: trimmed
    };

    root.pending.inputIntent = intent;

    if (root.meta && root.meta.debug === true && U && typeof U.logParse === "function") {
      U.logParse("Input intent: " + primaryType + " (" + intent.confidence + ")");
    }

    return intent;
  },

  scoreActionFamilies(lower, rawText) {
  const scores = {
    attack: 0,
    defend: 0,
    move: 0,
    travel: 0,
    inspect: 0,
    talk: 0,
    barter: 0,
    loot: 0,
    equip: 0,
    unequip: 0,
    rest: 0,
    train: 0,
    use_ability: 0,
    consume_item: 0,
    interact: 0
  };

  const addIf = (type, regex, amount) => {
    if (regex.test(lower)) {
      scores[type] += amount;
    }
  };

  addIf("attack", /\b(attack|strike|slash|stab|shoot|fire at|swing at|kick|punch|smash|hit|lunge|charge at|kill)\b/, 5);
  addIf("defend", /\b(defend|block|parry|guard|brace|protect|shield|take cover|hold position)\b/, 5);
  addIf("move", /\b(go|walk|run|step|move|enter|leave|approach|advance|retreat|back away|climb|crawl)\b/, 3);
  addIf("move", /\b(north|south|east|west|upstairs|downstairs|inside|outside)\b/, 2);
  addIf("travel", /\b(travel|journey|set out|head to|make for|ride to|sail to|return to)\b/, 5);
  addIf("inspect", /\b(look|look at|inspect|examine|search|study|check|observe|scan|peek|read)\b/, 5);
  addIf("talk", /\b(say|ask|tell|reply|speak|talk|whisper|shout|yell|greet|persuade|convince)\b/, 5);
  addIf("barter", /\b(buy|sell|trade|barter|haggle|purchase|pay|negotiate)\b/, 5);
  addIf("loot", /\b(loot|take|grab|pick up|collect|gather|steal)\b/, 5);
  addIf("equip", /\b(equip|wear|put on|draw|wield|ready|don)\b/, 5);
  addIf("unequip", /\b(unequip|remove|take off|sheath|stow|holster)\b/, 5);
  addIf("rest", /\b(rest|sleep|nap|wait|pause|camp|recover|breathe)\b/, 5);
  addIf("train", /\b(train|practice|drill|exercise|meditate|spar|work on)\b/, 5);
  addIf("use_ability", /\b(cast|channel|invoke|activate|unleash|focus)\b/, 5);
  addIf("consume_item", /\b(drink|eat|consume|swallow|sip)\b/, 5);
  addIf("interact", /\b(open|close|push|pull|touch|press|turn|unlock|lock|use|lift|drag)\b/, 4);

  if (/["“][^"”]+["”]/.test(rawText)) {
    scores.talk += 3;
  }

  if (/\bgo to\b/.test(lower) || /\bhead to\b/.test(lower) || /\breturn to\b/.test(lower) || /\breturn from\b/.test(lower) || /\bfrom\s+[^.!?]{2,50}\s+to\s+[^.!?]{2,50}/.test(lower)) {
    scores.travel += 2;
    scores.move += 1;
  }

  if (/^\s*(?:i\s+)?return\b/i.test(String(rawText || ""))) {
    scores.travel += 4;
  }

  if (/\blook around\b/.test(lower)) {
    scores.inspect += 3;
  }

  if (/\buse\b/.test(lower) && !/\bconsume\b/.test(lower)) {
    scores.interact += 1;
    scores.use_ability += 1;
  }

  // Phase 5 recovery:
  // Explicit training language at the front of the input must stay training,
  // even if the purpose phrase contains defensive words like protect/shield/against.
  if (/^\s*(?:i\s+)?(?:train|practice|drill|exercise|meditate on|spar with|work on)\s+/i.test(String(rawText || ""))) {
    scores.train += 7;
  }

  return scores;
},

  pickPrimaryType(scores) {
    let bestType = "interact";
    let bestScore = -1;

    const order = [
      "attack",
      "defend",
      "travel",
      "inspect",
      "talk",
      "barter",
      "loot",
      "equip",
      "unequip",
      "rest",
      "train",
      "use_ability",
      "consume_item",
      "move",
      "interact"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < order.length; i += 1) {
      const type = order[i];
      const value = Number(scores[type] || 0);
      if (value > bestScore) {
        bestScore = value;
        bestType = type;
      }
    }

    if (bestScore <= 0) {
      return "interact";
    }

    return bestType;
  },

  buildAlternates(scores, primaryType) {
    const entries = [];
    for (const key in scores) {
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(scores, key)) continue;
      if (key === primaryType) continue;

      const value = Number(scores[key] || 0);
      if (value > 0) {
        entries.push({ type: key, score: value });
      }
    }

    entries.sort(function (a, b) {
      return b.score - a.score;
    });

    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < entries.length && i < 3; i += 1) {
      out.push({
        type: entries[i].type,
        confidence: this.scoreToConfidence(entries[i].score)
      });
    }

    return out;
  },

  scoreToConfidence(score) {
    const raw = 0.2 + (Number(score || 0) * 0.11);
    if (raw < 0.2) return 0.2;
    if (raw > 0.95) return 0.95;
    return Math.round(raw * 100) / 100;
  },

  buildTags(primaryType, scores, rawText) {
    const tags = [];
    const addTag = (value) => {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!text) return;
      if (tags.indexOf(text) === -1) {
        tags.push(text);
      }
    };

    addTag("intent:" + primaryType);

    if ((scores.attack || 0) > 0) addTag("aggressive");
    if ((scores.defend || 0) > 0) addTag("protective");
    if ((scores.inspect || 0) > 0) addTag("observational");
    if ((scores.talk || 0) > 0) addTag("social");
    if ((scores.travel || 0) > 0 || (scores.move || 0) > 0) addTag("movement");
    if ((scores.train || 0) > 0) addTag("improvement");
    if ((scores.loot || 0) > 0 || (scores.barter || 0) > 0) addTag("acquisition");
    if ((scores.use_ability || 0) > 0) addTag("specialized");
    if ((scores.consume_item || 0) > 0) addTag("consumable_use");

    if (/["“][^"”]+["”]/.test(rawText)) {
      addTag("quoted_speech");
    }

    return tags;
  },

  extractTarget(rawText, primaryType) {
    const patterns = [
      /\b(?:attack|strike|slash|stab|shoot|hit|kick|punch|smash|lunge at|charge at)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i,
      /\b(?:talk|speak|say|ask|tell|reply|greet)\s+(?:to\s+)?(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i,
      /\b(?:inspect|examine|check|search|study|open|close|push|pull|touch|unlock|lock)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i,
      /\b(?:at|toward|towards|against|into)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const match = rawText.match(patterns[i]);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        return this.cleanHint(match[1]);
      }
    }

    if (primaryType === "inspect" && /\blook around\b/i.test(rawText)) {
      return "surroundings";
    }

    return "";
  },

  extractWeapon(rawText, primaryType) {
    if (primaryType !== "attack" && primaryType !== "defend" && primaryType !== "interact") {
      return "";
    }

    const match = rawText.match(/\bwith\s+(?:my\s+|the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return this.cleanHint(match[1]);
    }

    return "";
  },

  extractStyle(rawText, primaryType) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();

    if (primaryType === "attack") {
      if (/\bcareful\b|\bprecise\b|\bmeasured\b/.test(lower)) return "measured";
      if (/\bfierce\b|\bbrutal\b|\bhard\b|\bfull force\b/.test(lower)) return "forceful";
      if (/\bquick\b|\bfast\b|\bsudden\b/.test(lower)) return "fast";
    }

    if (primaryType === "talk") {
      if (/\bcalmly\b|\bpolitely\b/.test(lower)) return "calm";
      if (/\bangrily\b|\bthreaten\b/.test(lower)) return "aggressive";
    }

    if (primaryType === "train") {
      return "practice";
    }

    return "";
  },

  extractAbility(rawText, primaryType) {
    if (primaryType !== "use_ability" && primaryType !== "train") {
      return "";
    }

    const patterns = [
      /\b(?:cast|channel|invoke|activate|unleash|focus on|practice|train)\s+(?:my\s+|the\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i,
      /\buse\s+(?:my\s+|the\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)\s+(?:on|at|against)\b/i
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const match = rawText.match(patterns[i]);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        return this.cleanHint(match[1]);
      }
    }

    return "";
  },

  extractItem(rawText, primaryType) {
    if (
      primaryType !== "loot" &&
      primaryType !== "equip" &&
      primaryType !== "unequip" &&
      primaryType !== "consume_item" &&
      primaryType !== "barter" &&
      primaryType !== "interact"
    ) {
      return "";
    }

    const patterns = [
      /\b(?:pick up|grab|take|loot|collect|steal|equip|wear|put on|draw|wield|remove|take off|drink|eat|consume|buy|sell|trade|use)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+|my\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const match = rawText.match(patterns[i]);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        return this.cleanHint(match[1]);
      }
    }

    return "";
  },

  extractDestination(rawText, primaryType) {
    if (primaryType !== "travel" && primaryType !== "move") {
      return "";
    }

    const patterns = [
      /\b(?:go|walk|run|move|travel|journey|head|return|enter|leave|climb|crawl)\s+(?:to|toward|towards|into|inside|outside|back to)?\s*(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const match = rawText.match(patterns[i]);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        return this.cleanHint(match[1]);
      }
    }

    return "";
  },

  extractTrainingFocus(rawText, primaryType) {
    if (primaryType !== "train") {
      return "";
    }

    const patterns = [
      /\b(?:train|practice|drill|exercise|meditate|spar|work on)\s+(?:my\s+|the\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const match = rawText.match(patterns[i]);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        return this.cleanHint(match[1]);
      }
    }

    return "";
  },

  cleanHint(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .replace(/\b(?:using|with|at|against|toward|towards|into|to)\b\s*$/i, "")
    .trim();
}
},

    // === PROGRESSION SYSTEM ===
ProgressionSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.player || typeof root.player !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.player)) {
      root.player = {};
    }

    if (!root.player.levelState || typeof root.player.levelState !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.player.levelState)) {
      root.player.levelState = {};
    }

    const levelState = root.player.levelState;

    if (typeof levelState.level !== "number" || levelState.level < 1) levelState.level = 1;
    if (typeof levelState.xp !== "number" || levelState.xp < 0) levelState.xp = 0;
    if (typeof levelState.totalXp !== "number" || levelState.totalXp < 0) levelState.totalXp = 0;
    if (typeof levelState.growthClass !== "string" || !levelState.growthClass) {
      levelState.growthClass = "standard";
    }

    if (!levelState.history || typeof levelState.history !== "object" || Array.isArray(levelState.history)) {
      levelState.history = {};
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(levelState.history.recentGains)) {
      levelState.history.recentGains = [];
    }

    if (!levelState.antiFarm || typeof levelState.antiFarm !== "object" || Array.isArray(levelState.antiFarm)) {
      levelState.antiFarm = {};
    }

    if (typeof levelState.antiFarm.lastSource !== "string") levelState.antiFarm.lastSource = "";
    if (typeof levelState.antiFarm.repeatCount !== "number" || levelState.antiFarm.repeatCount < 0) {
      levelState.antiFarm.repeatCount = 0;
    }

    if (typeof levelState.lastGainTurn !== "number" || levelState.lastGainTurn < 0) {
      levelState.lastGainTurn = 0;
    }

    const expectedXpToNext = this.xpNeededForLevel(levelState.level, levelState.growthClass);
    if (typeof levelState.xpToNext !== "number" || levelState.xpToNext <= 0) {
      levelState.xpToNext = expectedXpToNext;
    }

    return levelState;
  },

  xpNeededForLevel(level, growthClass) {
    const lvl = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(level || 1)));
    const growth = String(growthClass || "standard");

    let base = 100 + ((lvl - 1) * 25) + Math.floor(((lvl - 1) * (lvl - 1)) * 0.5);

    if (growth === "accelerated") {
      base = Math.max(80, Math.floor(base * 0.85));
    } else if (growth === "distorted") {
      base = Math.floor(base * 1.15);
    } else if (growth === "cursed") {
      base = Math.floor(base * 1.25);
    }

    return base;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;
    const player = root.player || {};
    const levelState = player.levelState || {};
    const intent = root.pending && root.pending.inputIntent ? root.pending.inputIntent : null;

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    const cleanText = U && typeof U.normalizeSpaces === "function"
      ? U.normalizeSpaces(String(rawText || ""))
      : String(rawText || "").trim();

    if (!cleanText) {
      return 0;
    }

    let eventChanges = this.processNarrativeOutcomeEvents(cleanText, root);

    if (!intent || typeof intent !== "object") {
      return eventChanges;
    }

    const source = this.classifyIntent(intent);
    const baseXp = this.baseXpForSource(source, intent, cleanText);
    if (baseXp <= 0) {
      return eventChanges;
    }

    const multiplier = this.antiFarmMultiplier(levelState, source);
    const gained = Math.max(0, Math.floor(baseXp * multiplier));

    if (gained <= 0) {
      return eventChanges;
    }

    return eventChanges + this.awardXp(gained, source, intent.primaryType, root);
  },

  processNarrativeOutcomeEvents(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
    const U = A.Utils || null;
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!lower || !U || typeof U.logEvent !== "function") return 0;

    let changes = 0;
    const logOnce = (message, key) => {
      if (!root.pending || typeof root.pending !== "object") root.pending = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.pending.recentNarrativeEventKeys)) root.pending.recentNarrativeEventKeys = [];
      const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
      const fullKey = String(turn) + ":" + String(key || message);
      if (root.pending.recentNarrativeEventKeys.indexOf(fullKey) !== -1) return 0;
      root.pending.recentNarrativeEventKeys.push(fullKey);
      while (root.pending.recentNarrativeEventKeys.length > 20) root.pending.recentNarrativeEventKeys.shift();
      U.logEvent(message);
      if (typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") U.invalidatePackets(root);
      return 1;
    };

    const failedPurchaseItem = (() => {
      const patterns = [
        /\b(?:cannot|can't)\s+afford\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+|this\s+|that\s+)?([^.!?;,]+)/i,
        /\b(?:do\s+not|don't|does\s+not|doesn't|did\s+not|didn't)\s+have\s+enough\s+(?:money|silver|gold|copper|coins?|funds)\s+for\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+|this\s+|that\s+)?([^.!?;,]+)/i,
        /\bnot\s+enough\s+(?:money|silver|gold|copper|coins?|funds)\s+for\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+|this\s+|that\s+)?([^.!?;,]+)/i,
        /\binsufficient\s+(?:funds|money|silver|gold|copper|coins?)\s+for\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+|this\s+|that\s+)?([^.!?;,]+)/i,
        /\b(?:lack|lacks|lacked|lacking)\s+enough\s+(?:money|silver|gold|copper|coins?|funds)\s+for\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+|this\s+|that\s+)?([^.!?;,]+)/i
      ];

      for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
        const match = String(rawText || "").match(patterns[i]);
        if (match && match[1]) {
          return String(match[1] || "")
            .replace(/\s+(?:right\s+now|today|here|from\s+the\s+shop).*$/i, "")
            .replace(/^(?:the|a|an|this|that)\s+/i, "")
            .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
            .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
            .trim()
            .slice(0, 60);
        }
      }

      return "";
    })();

    const hasFailedPurchaseMoney =
      /\b(?:cannot|can't)\s+afford\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\b(?:do\s+not|don't|does\s+not|doesn't|did\s+not|didn't)\s+have\s+enough\s+(?:money|silver|gold|copper|coins?|funds)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\bnot\s+enough\s+(?:money|silver|gold|copper|coins?|funds)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\binsufficient\s+(?:funds|money|silver|gold|copper|coins?)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\b(?:lack|lacks|lacked|lacking)\s+enough\s+(?:money|silver|gold|copper|coins?|funds)\b/.test(lower);

    const hasPurchaseContext =
      /\b(?:merchant|shopkeeper|vendor|seller|trader|shop|sale|sell|sells|buy|purchase|payment|price|costs?)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\b(?:healing\s+kit|shield|armor|weapon|potion|item|goods?)\b/.test(lower);

    if (hasFailedPurchaseMoney && hasPurchaseContext) {
      const reason = /\b(?:cannot|can't)\s+afford\b/.test(lower)
        ? "cannot afford"
        : /\binsufficient\s+(?:funds|money|silver|gold|copper|coins?)\b/.test(lower)
        ? "insufficient funds"
        : "not enough money";
      const itemText = failedPurchaseItem ? " for " + failedPurchaseItem : "";
      const keyText = failedPurchaseItem ? failedPurchaseItem.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_") : "money";
      changes += logOnce("Purchase failed: " + reason + itemText + ".", "failed_purchase_" + keyText);
    }

    const bossMatch = String(rawText || "").match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{1,40}?(?:warden|boss|champion|chief|lord|beast|monster|golem|knight|guardian))\s+(?:collapses?|falls?|dies|is\s+defeated|is\s+slain)\b/i);
    if (bossMatch || /\bdungeon\s+cleared\b/i.test(rawText) || /\b(?:meaningful|major|combat)\s+(?:but\s+not\s+massive\s+)?experience\b/i.test(rawText)) {
      const target = bossMatch && bossMatch[1] ? bossMatch[1].replace(/^the\s+/i, "").trim() : "major foe";
      const dungeon = /\bdungeon\s+cleared\b/i.test(rawText) ? "; dungeon cleared" : "";
      const xp = /\bexperience\b/i.test(rawText) ? "; experience gained" : "";
      changes += logOnce("Combat result: " + target + " defeated" + dungeon + xp + ".", "combat_result_" + target.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_"));
    }

    const rawString = String(rawText || "");
    const bareActorMatch = rawString.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})\b[^.!?]{0,90}\b(?:is\s+wounded|is\s+injured|is\s+hurt|bleeds?|is\s+bleeding|is\s+poisoned|stays\s+with\s+the\s+party|stays\s+with\s+you|joins\s+the\s+party|follows\s+you|guards\s+you|protects\s+you)\b[^.!?]{0,90}/i);
    if (bareActorMatch && bareActorMatch[1]) {
      const actorName = String(bareActorMatch[1] || "").trim();
      if (!/^(?:You|The|A|An|Your|His|Her|Their|This|That)$/i.test(actorName)) {
        const note = String(bareActorMatch[0] || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").replace(/[.!?]+$/g, "").trim().slice(0, 120);
        changes += logOnce("Actor note: " + note + ".", "actor_note_" + actorName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_"));
      }
    }

    const bareAbilityMatch = rawString.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}){0,3})\s+(?:becomes|became|forms?|formed|turns?\s+into|turned\s+into|expands?\s+into|expanded\s+into|shifts?\s+into|shifted\s+into|surges?\s+into|surged\s+into)\s+([^.!?;]{3,120})/i);
    if (bareAbilityMatch && bareAbilityMatch[1] && bareAbilityMatch[2]) {
      const abilityName = String(bareAbilityMatch[1] || "").trim();
      const adaptation = String(bareAbilityMatch[2] || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "").trim().slice(0, 90);
      const singleToken = abilityName.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length === 1;
      const safeSingleAbility = /(?:ward|guard|blade|step|flame|frost|stone|shield|strike|sight|song|chain|veil|brand|aegis|barrier|bolt|spark)$/i.test(abilityName);
      if (!/\b(?:Captain|Commander|General|Sergeant|Queen|King|Lord|Lady|High\s+Priestess|Archmage|Archdruid|Master)\b/.test(abilityName)) {
        if (!singleToken || safeSingleAbility) {
          changes += logOnce("Ability note: " + abilityName + " becomes " + adaptation + ".", "ability_note_" + abilityName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_"));
        }
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  classifyIntent(intent) {
    const type = String(intent.primaryType || "");

    if (type === "attack" || type === "defend" || type === "use_ability") {
      return "combat";
    }

    if (type === "train") {
      return "training";
    }

    if (type === "travel" || type === "move" || type === "inspect") {
      return "exploration";
    }

    if (type === "talk" || type === "barter") {
      return "social";
    }

    if (
      type === "loot" ||
      type === "equip" ||
      type === "unequip" ||
      type === "consume_item" ||
      type === "interact"
    ) {
      return "logistics";
    }

    if (type === "rest") {
      return "recovery";
    }

    return "general";
  },

  baseXpForSource(source, intent, rawText) {
    const type = String(intent.primaryType || "");
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();

    if (source === "combat") {
      let xp = 3;
      if (/\b(hit|strike|wound|block|parry|fight|battle|bleed|stagger)\b/.test(lower)) {
        xp += 1;
      }
      return xp;
    }

    if (source === "training") {
      let xp = 3;
      if (/\b(practice|repeat|drill|exercise|meditate|focus|spar)\b/.test(lower)) {
        xp += 1;
      }
      return xp;
    }

    if (source === "exploration") {
      if (type === "inspect") return 2;
      if (type === "travel") return 2;
      return 1;
    }

    if (source === "social") {
      return 1;
    }

    if (source === "logistics") {
      if (type === "loot" || type === "consume_item") return 2;
      return 1;
    }

    if (source === "recovery") {
      return 1;
    }

    return 1;
  },

  antiFarmMultiplier(levelState, source) {
    const antiFarm = levelState.antiFarm || {};

    if (antiFarm.lastSource !== source) {
      antiFarm.lastSource = source;
      antiFarm.repeatCount = 0;
      levelState.antiFarm = antiFarm;
      return 1;
    }

    antiFarm.repeatCount += 1;
    levelState.antiFarm = antiFarm;

    if (antiFarm.repeatCount === 1) return 0.67;
    if (antiFarm.repeatCount === 2) return 0.5;
    return 0.34;
  },

  awardXp(amount, source, note, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;
    const player = root.player || {};
    const levelState = player.levelState || {};
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

    levelState.xp += amount;
    levelState.totalXp += amount;
    levelState.lastGainTurn = turn;

    if (!Array.isArray(levelState.history.recentGains)) {
      levelState.history.recentGains = [];
    }

    levelState.history.recentGains.push({
      turn: turn,
      amount: amount,
      source: String(source || "general"),
      note: String(note || "")
    });

    while (levelState.history.recentGains.length > 12) {
      levelState.history.recentGains.shift();
    }

    let leveled = 0;

    while (levelState.xp >= levelState.xpToNext) {
      levelState.xp -= levelState.xpToNext;
      levelState.level += 1;
      leveled += 1;

      this.applyLevelUpBenefits(root);

      levelState.xpToNext = this.xpNeededForLevel(levelState.level, levelState.growthClass);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("player", true, root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("XP +" + amount + " from " + source + (leveled ? " | level up +" + leveled : ""));
    }

    return amount;
  },

  applyLevelUpBenefits(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return;

    const player = root.player || {};
    const stats = player.stats || {};
    const build = root.build || {};

    if (typeof stats.maxHp === "number") {
      stats.maxHp += 10;
      stats.hp = stats.maxHp;
    }

    if (typeof stats.maxEp === "number") {
      stats.maxEp += 4;
      stats.ep = stats.maxEp;
    }

    if (typeof stats.maxMp === "number") {
      stats.maxMp += 2;
      stats.mp = stats.maxMp;
    }

    const specs = Array.isArray(build.specializationTags) ? build.specializationTags : [];

    if (specs.indexOf("power_bias") !== -1 && typeof stats.atk === "number") {
      stats.atk += 1;
    } else if (specs.indexOf("resilience_bias") !== -1 && typeof stats.def === "number") {
      stats.def += 1;
    } else if (specs.indexOf("speed_bias") !== -1 && typeof stats.spd === "number") {
      stats.spd += 1;
    } else if (specs.indexOf("technical_bias") !== -1 && typeof stats.intl === "number") {
      stats.intl += 1;
    } else if (specs.indexOf("fortune_bias") !== -1 && typeof stats.lck === "number") {
      stats.lck += 1;
    } else if (typeof stats.atk === "number") {
      stats.atk += 1;
    }

    if (globalThis.AIDRPG.PlayerSystem && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.PlayerSystem.recomputeDerived === "function") {
      globalThis.AIDRPG.PlayerSystem.recomputeDerived(player);
    }
  },

  getCompactSummary(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || !root.player || !root.player.levelState) {
      return "Progression summary unavailable.";
    }

    const levelState = root.player.levelState;
    return "Level " + levelState.level + " | XP " + levelState.xp + "/" + levelState.xpToNext + " | Total XP " + levelState.totalXp;
  }
},

    // === INVENTORY SYSTEM ===
InventorySystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.items || typeof root.items !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.items)) {
      root.items = {};
    }

    const items = root.items;

    if (!items.byId || typeof items.byId !== "object" || Array.isArray(items.byId)) {
      items.byId = {};
    }

    if (!items.ownership || typeof items.ownership !== "object" || Array.isArray(items.ownership)) {
      items.ownership = {};
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(items.ownership.player)) {
      items.ownership.player = [];
    }

    if (!items.ownership.storage || typeof items.ownership.storage !== "object" || Array.isArray(items.ownership.storage)) {
      items.ownership.storage = {};
    }

    if (!items.ownership.actors || typeof items.ownership.actors !== "object" || Array.isArray(items.ownership.actors)) {
      items.ownership.actors = {};
    }

    if (!items.equipment || typeof items.equipment !== "object" || Array.isArray(items.equipment)) {
      items.equipment = {};
    }

    const equipment = items.equipment;

    if (!("mainHand" in equipment)) equipment.mainHand = null;
    if (!("offHand" in equipment)) equipment.offHand = null;
    if (!("head" in equipment)) equipment.head = null;
    if (!("body" in equipment)) equipment.body = null;
    if (!("hands" in equipment)) equipment.hands = null;
    if (!("legs" in equipment)) equipment.legs = null;
    if (!("feet" in equipment)) equipment.feet = null;
    if (!("accessory1" in equipment)) equipment.accessory1 = null;
    if (!("accessory2" in equipment)) equipment.accessory2 = null;

    if (!items.currency || typeof items.currency !== "object" || Array.isArray(items.currency)) {
      items.currency = {};
    }

    if (!items.currency.byType || typeof items.currency.byType !== "object" || Array.isArray(items.currency.byType)) {
      items.currency.byType = {};
    }

    if (typeof items.currency.byType.copper !== "number") items.currency.byType.copper = 0;
    if (typeof items.currency.byType.silver !== "number") items.currency.byType.silver = 0;
    if (typeof items.currency.byType.gold !== "number") items.currency.byType.gold = 0;

    if (!Array.isArray(items.pendingNormalization)) {
      items.pendingNormalization = [];
    }

    this.ensureAllOwnedRecordsHaveShape(items);
    this.ensureEquipmentIntegrity(items);

    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
      root.cache = {};
    }
    if (typeof root.cache.visibleCarrySummary !== "string") {
      root.cache.visibleCarrySummary = "";
    }

    return items;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const U = A.Utils;
  const text = U && typeof U.normalizeSpaces === "function"
    ? U.normalizeSpaces(String(rawText || ""))
    : String(rawText || "").trim();

  if (!text) return 0;

  if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
    if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  const intent = root.pending && root.pending.inputIntent ? root.pending.inputIntent : null;
  let changes = 0;

  if (intent && typeof intent === "object") {
    changes += this.processIntentDrivenChanges(intent, text, root);
  }

  changes += this.processDirectStoryAcquisition(text, root);
  changes += this.processNamedItemPromotion(text, root);
  changes += this.processItemStateChanges(text, root);
  changes += this.normalizePending(root);
  changes += this.applyValidatedConsequences(root);

  if (changes > 0) {
    this.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);

    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Inventory changes: " + changes);
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

applyValidatedConsequences(root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const U = A.Utils;
  const Guard = A.OperationGuard;
  const pending = root.pending || {};
  const validated = Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences) ? pending.validatedConsequences : [];
  const items = root.items || {};
  const byId = items.byId || {};
  const equipment = items.equipment || {};
  const currency = items.currency && items.currency.byType ? items.currency.byType : {};
  const intent = pending.inputIntent && typeof pending.inputIntent === "object" ? pending.inputIntent : null;

  let changes = 0;
  let currencyChanged = false;

  const sameConsumeIntent = (label) => {
    if (!intent || String(intent.primaryType || "") !== "consume_item") return false;

    const hint = String(intent.itemHint || "").toLowerCase().trim();
    const cleanLabel = String(label || "").toLowerCase().trim();

    if (!hint || !cleanLabel) return false;

    return hint === cleanLabel
      || hint.indexOf(cleanLabel) !== -1
      || cleanLabel.indexOf(hint) !== -1;
  };

  const preferredWornItemId = () => {
    const slots = ["body", "head", "hands", "legs", "feet", "mainHand", "offHand", "accessory1", "accessory2"];

    for (let i = 0; i < slots.length; i += 1) {
      const itemId = equipment[slots[i]];
      if (typeof itemId === "string" && itemId && byId[itemId]) {
        return itemId;
      }
    }

    return "";
  };

  const damageLossForTag = (tag) => {
    const lower = String(tag || "").toLowerCase();

    if (lower.indexOf("broken") !== -1) return 35;
    if (lower.indexOf("burn") !== -1) return 20;
    if (lower.indexOf("torn") !== -1) return 18;
    if (lower.indexOf("rust") !== -1) return 10;
    return 15;
  };

  for (let i = 0; i < validated.length; i += 1) {
    const candidate = validated[i];
    if (!candidate || candidate.category !== "item" || candidate._itemApplied === true) {
      continue;
    }

    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const target =
      candidate.type === "currency_change"
        ? String(payload.currency || "currency")
        : candidate.type === "item_consumed" || candidate.type === "item_damage"
        ? String(payload.itemLabel || "item")
        : String(candidate.type || "item");

    const commit = () => {
      let localChanges = 0;

      if (candidate.type === "currency_change") {
        const currencyType = String(payload.currency || "").trim().toLowerCase();
        const amount = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(payload.amount || 0)));
        const direction = String(payload.direction || "").trim().toLowerCase();

        if ((currencyType === "copper" || currencyType === "silver" || currencyType === "gold") && amount > 0) {
          const currentValue = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(currency[currencyType] || 0)));
          const nextValue = direction === "loss"
            ? Math.max(0, currentValue - amount)
            : currentValue + amount;

          if (currency[currencyType] !== nextValue) {
            currency[currencyType] = nextValue;
            localChanges += 1;
            currencyChanged = true;
          }
        }
      } else if (candidate.type === "item_consumed") {
        const label = this.cleanPhrase(payload.itemLabel || "");

        if (label && !sameConsumeIntent(label)) {
          const item = this.findMatchingPlayerItem(label, root);
          if (item && item.id) {
            if (this.consumeItem(item.id, root)) {
              localChanges += 1;
            }
          }
        }
      } else if (candidate.type === "item_damage") {
        const label = this.cleanPhrase(payload.itemLabel || "");
        const damageTag = this.cleanPhrase(payload.damageTag || "damaged") || "damaged";

        let itemId = "";

        if (label === "worn gear") {
          itemId = preferredWornItemId();
        } else if (label) {
          const matched = this.findMatchingPlayerItem(label, root);
          itemId = matched && matched.id ? matched.id : "";
        }

        if (itemId && byId[itemId]) {
          const item = byId[itemId];
          this.ensureItemShape(item);

          const alreadyTagged = item.condition
            && Array.isArray(item.condition.stateTags)
            && item.condition.stateTags.indexOf(damageTag) !== -1;

          if (!alreadyTagged) {
            if (this.applyStateTag(itemId, damageTag, damageLossForTag(damageTag), root)) {
              localChanges += 1;
            }
          }
        }
      }

      return localChanges;
    };

    const localChanges =
      Guard && typeof Guard.applyOnce === "function"
        ? Guard.applyOnce({
            kind: "item_" + String(candidate.type || "change"),
            target: target,
            payload: payload,
            sourceId: candidate.id || ""
          }, commit, root)
        : commit();

    changes += localChanges;
    candidate._itemApplied = true;
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    this.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);

    if (currencyChanged && U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("items", "currency", root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
      U.invalidatePackets(root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Validated item consequences applied.");
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

  processIntentDrivenChanges(intent, text, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!intent || typeof intent !== "object") return 0;
  if (this.isClearlyFailedAction(text)) return 0;
  if (this.hasAcquisitionNegation(text, root)) return 0;

  let changes = 0;
  const type = String(intent.primaryType || "");
  const itemHint = this.cleanItemPhrase(String(intent.itemHint || "").trim());

  if (type === "loot" || type === "barter") {
    const phrases = this.extractAcquisitionPhrases(text, root);

    if (phrases.length) {
      for (let i = 0; i < phrases.length; i += 1) {
        if (this.queueNormalizationCandidate(phrases[i], "player", "packed", "story_gain", root)) {
          changes += 1;
        }
      }
    } else if (itemHint && this.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(itemHint)) {
      if (this.queueNormalizationCandidate(itemHint, "player", "packed", "intent_gain", root)) {
        changes += 1;
      }
    }
  }

  if (type === "equip" && itemHint) {
    const item = this.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase(itemHint, "carried", "equip_intent", root);
    if (item) {
      const slot = this.inferSlotFromItem(item);
      if (slot) {
        if (this.equipItem(slot, item.id, root)) {
          changes += 1;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if (type === "unequip") {
    if (itemHint) {
      const item = this.findMatchingPlayerItem(itemHint, root);
      if (item && item.slot) {
        if (this.unequipSlot(item.slot, root)) {
          changes += 1;
        }
      } else {
        const guessedSlot = this.inferSlotFromPhrase(itemHint);
        if (guessedSlot) {
          if (this.unequipSlot(guessedSlot, root)) {
            changes += 1;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if (type === "consume_item" && itemHint) {
    const item = this.findMatchingPlayerItem(itemHint, root);
    if (item) {
      if (this.consumeItem(item.id, root)) {
        changes += 1;
      }
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

  processDirectStoryAcquisition(text, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (this.hasAcquisitionNegation(text, root)) return 0;

  const phrases = this.extractAcquisitionPhrases(text, root);
  let changes = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < phrases.length; i += 1) {
    if (this.queueNormalizationCandidate(phrases[i], "player", "packed", "story_gain", root)) {
      changes += 1;
    }
  }

  return changes;
},


  processNamedItemPromotion(text, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const raw = String(text || "");
    if (!raw.trim()) return 0;

    const owned = root.items && root.items.ownership && Array.isArray(root.items.ownership.player)
      ? root.items.ownership.player
      : [];
    if (!owned.length) return 0;

    const cleanPersonalName = (value) => {
      const name = String(value || "")
        .replace(/["“”'`]+/g, "")
        .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
        .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
        .trim();
      if (!/^[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}){0,2}$/.test(name)) return "";
      if (/^(?:The|A|An|Your|My|It|This|That|You)$/i.test(name)) return "";
      return name;
    };

    const nameMatches = [];
    const patterns = [
      /\b(?:you\s+)?(?:name|named|call|called)\s+(?:your\s+|the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,70})\s+(?:as\s+|to\s+)?["“]?([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}){0,2})["”]?(?=[.!?;,]|$)/i,
      /\b(?:you\s+)?(?:name|named|call|called)\s+it\s+(?:as\s+|to\s+)?["“]?([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}){0,2})["”]?(?=[.!?;,]|$)/i
    ];

    let match = raw.match(patterns[0]);
    if (match && match[2]) nameMatches.push({ itemPhrase: this.cleanItemPhrase(match[1] || ""), personalName: cleanPersonalName(match[2]) });
    match = raw.match(patterns[1]);
    if (match && match[1]) nameMatches.push({ itemPhrase: "", personalName: cleanPersonalName(match[1]) });
    if (!nameMatches.length) return 0;

    let changes = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < nameMatches.length; i += 1) {
      const row = nameMatches[i];
      if (!row.personalName) continue;

      let item = row.itemPhrase ? this.findMatchingPlayerItem(row.itemPhrase, root) : null;
      if (!item) {
        const lastId = owned[owned.length - 1];
        item = lastId && root.items.byId ? root.items.byId[lastId] : null;
      }
      if (!item || typeof item !== "object") continue;
      this.ensureItemShape(item);

      const oldName = this.getItemDisplayName(item);
      if (!item.notes || typeof item.notes !== "object") item.notes = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(item.notes.observedChanges)) item.notes.observedChanges = [];
      if (!item.notes.baseDisplayName) item.notes.baseDisplayName = oldName;
      item.notes.personalName = row.personalName;
      item.notes.namedPersonalItem = true;

      const baseName = String(item.notes.baseDisplayName || oldName || item.baseType || "item").replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g, "");
      const nextDisplay = row.personalName + (baseName && baseName.toLowerCase().indexOf(row.personalName.toLowerCase()) === -1 ? " (" + baseName + ")" : "");
      if (item.displayName !== nextDisplay) {
        item.displayName = nextDisplay;
        changes += 1;
      }

      const detailMatches = [
        /\borange\s+line\s+in\s+(?:the\s+)?blade\b/i,
        /\b(?:rune|mark|scar|crack|glow|line)\s+(?:on|in|along)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:blade|hilt|surface|edge)\b/i
      ];
      for (let d = 0; d < detailMatches.length; d += 1) {
        const dm = raw.match(detailMatches[d]);
        if (dm && dm[0] && item.notes.observedChanges.indexOf(dm[0]) === -1) {
          item.notes.observedChanges.push(dm[0]);
          changes += 1;
        }
      }
      while (item.notes.observedChanges.length > 12) item.notes.observedChanges.shift();

      const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
      if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") U.markDirty("items", item.id, root);
    }

    if (changes > 0) {
      const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
      this.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);
      if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") U.invalidatePackets(root);
      if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") U.logEvent("Named item identity updated.");
    }

    return changes;
  },

  processItemStateChanges(text, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const patterns = [
      { regex: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]+?)\s+(?:breaks|break|shatters|shatter|snaps|snap|is destroyed|are destroyed|is ruined|are ruined)\b/gi, tag: "broken", delta: 45 },
      { regex: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]+?)\s+(?:is torn|are torn|tears|tear|rips|rip|is cut|are cut|gets cut|get cut)\b/gi, tag: "torn", delta: 18 },
      { regex: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]+?)\s+(?:is damaged|are damaged|dents|dent|is dented|are dented|cracks|crack|is cracked|are cracked)\b/gi, tag: "damaged", delta: 15 },
      { regex: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]+?)\s+(?:burns|burn|is burned|are burned|scorches|scorch|is scorched|are scorched)\b/gi, tag: "burned", delta: 20 },
      { regex: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]+?)\s+(?:rusts|rust|is rusted|are rusted)\b/gi, tag: "rusted", delta: 10 }
    ];

    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const pattern = patterns[i];
      let match;

      while ((match = pattern.regex.exec(text))) {
        const phrase = match[1] ? this.cleanPhrase(match[1]) : "";
        if (!phrase) continue;

        const item = this.findMatchingPlayerItem(phrase, root);
        if (!item) continue;

        if (this.applyStateTag(item.id, pattern.tag, pattern.delta, root)) {
          changes += 1;
        }
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  isClearlyFailedAction(text) {
    return /\b(fails?|failed|cannot|can't|unable to|could not|couldn't|refuses?|refused|without managing to)\b/i.test(String(text || ""));
  },

  extractAcquisitionPhrases(text, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const raw = String(text || "");
  const out = [];

  if (this.hasAcquisitionNegation(raw, root)) {
    return out;
  }

  const addCandidate = (value) => {
    if (this.shouldRejectAcquisitionCapture(value)) return;

    const cleaned = this.cleanItemPhrase(value);
    if (!cleaned) return;
    if (!this.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(cleaned)) return;

    if (out.indexOf(cleaned) === -1) {
      out.push(cleaned);
    }
  };

  // Phase 6 inventory hardening:
  // Only player-received/player-acquired wording may create inventory.
  // This blocks lines like "the guard takes your sword" from granting items.
  const patterns = [
    /\byou\s+(?:pick(?:s|ed)?\s+up|grab(?:s|bed)?|take|takes|took|collect(?:s|ed)?|gather(?:s|ed)?|loot(?:s|ed)?|snatch(?:es|ed)?|snag(?:s|ged)?|nab(?:s|bed)?|yoink(?:s|ed)?|scoop(?:s|ed)?\s+up|pocket(?:s|ed)?|stash(?:es|ed)?|retrieve(?:s|d)?|recover(?:s|ed)?|find(?:s|found)?|buy|buys|bought|purchase(?:s|d)?|receive(?:s|d)?|accept(?:s|ed)?)\s+([^.!?;\n]+)/gi,
    /\byou\s+are\s+(?:given|handed|offered|awarded|presented\s+with)\s+([^.!?;\n]+)/gi,
    /\byou\s+(?:tuck|tucks|tucked|slip|slips|slipped)\s+([^.!?;\n]{1,80}?)\s+(?:away|into\s+your\s+(?:pouch|pack|bag|sack|inventory|pocket))\b/gi,
    /\b(?:someone|somebody|a\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{1,32}|an\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{1,32}|the\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{1,32}|[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,40})\s+(?:gives|gave|hands|handed|offers|offered|passes|passed)\s+you\s+([^.!?;\n]+)/g,
    /\b([^.!?;\n]{1,80}?)\s+(?:is|are)\s+(?:placed|pressed|dropped|slipped|tucked)\s+(?:into|in|onto)\s+your\s+(?:hand|hands|palm|pack|bag|sack|inventory|pocket|pouch)\b/gi
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
    let match;
    while ((match = patterns[i].exec(raw))) {
      if (!match[1]) continue;

      const pieces = this.splitAcquisitionCapture(match[1]);
      for (let p = 0; p < pieces.length; p += 1) {
        addCandidate(pieces[p]);
      }
    }
  }

  out.sort((a, b) => {
    const wordDiff = a.split(/\s+/).length - b.split(/\s+/).length;
    if (wordDiff !== 0) return wordDiff;
    return a.length - b.length;
  });

  return out;
},

splitAcquisitionCapture(value) {
  let text = String(value || "")
    .replace(/[\r\n]+/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  if (!text) return [];

  text = text.split(/[.;!?]/, 1)[0];
  text = text.replace(/\s*,[\s\S]*$/, "");
  text = text.replace(/\s+(?:and then|then|but then|so then)\b[\s\S]*$/i, "");

  const rough = text.split(/\s+(?:and|plus)\s+/i);
  const out = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < rough.length && i < 4; i += 1) {
    const part = String(rough[i] || "").trim();
    if (part) out.push(part);
  }

  return out.length ? out : [text];
},


hasAcquisitionNegation(text, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const output = String(text || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  const input = root && root.pending && root.pending.inputIntent && root.pending.inputIntent.rawText
    ? String(root.pending.inputIntent.rawText || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim()
    : "";
  const combined = (input + " " + output).trim();
  if (!combined) return false;

  const blocked = [
    /\b(?:almost|nearly)\s+(?:grab|take|collect|pick\s+up|pocket|stash|snag|nab|yoink|scoop\s+up|accept|receive|retrieve|gather)\b/,
    /\b(?:decide|decides|decided)\s+not\s+to\s+(?:take|accept|grab|collect|pick\s+up|pocket|stash|snag|nab|yoink|scoop\s+up|receive|keep)\b/,
    /\b(?:do\s+not|don't|does\s+not|doesn't|did\s+not|didn't)\s+(?:take|accept|grab|collect|pick\s+up|pocket|stash|snag|nab|yoink|scoop\s+up|receive|keep)\b/,
    /\b(?:refuse|refuses|refused|reject|rejects|rejected|decline|declines|declined)\b/,
    /\bstop(?:s|ped)?\s+(?:before|yourself|short)\b[^.!?]{0,40}\b(?:taking|grabbing|collecting|picking\s+up|pocketing|stashing|snagging|nabbing|yoinking|scooping\s+up|accepting)\b/,
    /\bleave(?:s|d)?\s+(?:it|them|the\s+[^.!?]{1,40})\s+(?:where\s+it\s+is|where\s+they\s+are|behind|scattered)\b/,
    /\boffer(?:s|ed)?\s+you\b[^.!?]{0,80}\b(?:but|yet)\b[^.!?]{0,40}\b(?:refuse|reject|decline|do\s+not\s+accept|don't\s+accept)\b/,
    /\byou\s+are\s+offered\b[^.!?]{0,80}\b(?:but|yet)\b[^.!?]{0,40}\b(?:refuse|reject|decline|do\s+not\s+accept|don't\s+accept)\b/,
    /\b(?:pressed|slipped|pushed|held)\s+(?:toward|towards|out\s+to)\s+your\s+hand\b[^.!?]{0,60}\b(?:decline|refuse|reject|push\s+it\s+away|do\s+not\s+accept)\b/
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < blocked.length; i += 1) {
    if (blocked[i].test(combined) || blocked[i].test(output)) return true;
  }

  return false;
},

shouldRejectAcquisitionCapture(value) {
  const lower = String(value || "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[“”"'`]+/g, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  if (!lower) return true;

  // These are usually contact, movement, or idiom phrases, not inventory gain.
  if (/^(?:at|to|toward|towards|against|into|onto|in|on|from|with|using|by|behind|beside|near|around|through|across|along|under|over|beneath)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return true;
  }

  if (/^(?:hold of|ahold of|cover|care|notice|a look|the look|look|a glance|the glance|glance|a breath|the breath|breath|a moment|the moment|moment|a chance|the chance|chance|a rest|the rest|rest|a seat|the seat|seat)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return true;
  }

  if (/\b(?:in stride|in the sight|in the view|cover behind|cover under|hold of)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return true;
  }

  return false;
},

cleanItemPhrase(value) {
  let text = String(value || "")
    .replace(/[\r\n]+/g, " ")
    .replace(/[“”"'`]+/g, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  if (!text) return "";
  if (this.shouldRejectAcquisitionCapture(text)) return "";

  text = this.trimAcquisitionClause(text);

  text = text
    .replace(/^(?:up|out)\s+/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+(?:away|(?:in|into)\s+(?:your|the)\s+(?:pouch|pack|bag|sack|inventory|pocket))$/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+(?:and\s+)?(?:put|puts|place|places|placed|slip|slips|slipped|tuck|tucks|tucked)\s+(?:it|them)\s+(?:in|into|onto)\s+(?:your|the)\s+(?:pouch|pack|bag|sack|inventory|pocket|hand|hands).*$/i, "")
    .replace(/^(?:the|a|an|some|my|your)\s+/i, "")
    .trim();

  if (!text) return "";
  if (this.shouldRejectAcquisitionCapture(text)) return "";

  const tokens = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (!tokens.length) return "";

  const headIndex = this.findConcreteItemHeadIndex(tokens);
  if (headIndex === -1) return "";

  const secondToken = tokens.length > 1 ? String(tokens[1] || "").toLowerCase() : "";

  if (headIndex === 0 && secondToken === "of") {
    text = tokens.slice(0, Math.min(tokens.length, 5)).join(" ");
  } else {
    const start = Math.max(0, headIndex - 3);
    text = tokens.slice(start, headIndex + 1).join(" ");
  }

  text = text
    .replace(/^(?:another|other)\s+/i, "")
    .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  if (!text) return "";
  if (this.shouldRejectAcquisitionCapture(text)) return "";

  return text;
},

trimAcquisitionClause(value) {
  let text = String(value || "");

  text = text.split(/[.;!?]/, 1)[0];

  text = text.replace(
    /\s+(?:and then|then|but then|so then|and|as|while|because|when|before|after|with|using|into|onto|from|at|to|toward|towards|against|behind|beside|near|around|through|across|along|under|over|beneath|where|which|that|who|without|instead|rather than)\b[\s\S]*$/i,
    ""
  );

  text = text.replace(/\s*,[\s\S]*$/, "");
  return text.trim();
},

isItemHeadToken(value) {
  const raw = String(value || "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/g, "");

  if (!raw) return false;

  const singular = raw.length > 3 && raw.endsWith("s") ? raw.slice(0, -1) : raw;

  const itemHeads = new Set([
    "stick", "branch", "pole", "staff", "rod", "post", "spear", "lance",
    "sword", "greatsword", "longsword", "shortsword", "dagger", "knife", "blade",
    "axe", "mace", "hammer", "club", "cudgel", "bow", "crossbow", "wand",
    "shield", "torch", "rope", "cord", "chain", "key", "lockpick", "coin", "purse",
    "compass", "whistle", "chalk", "mirror", "lens", "spyglass", "tinderbox", "flint",
    "lamp", "candle", "stub", "gemstone", "set", "wheel",
    "cup", "bowl", "pot", "pan", "fork", "coil", "pen", "flute", "skillet", "kettle", "jug", "mug", "spoon", "comb", "spool", "net", "pick", "link", "spring", "pellet", "manacle", "chunk", "scrap", "fragment",
    "arrow", "arrows", "bolt", "bolts", "quiver", "kit", "tools", "hook", "wire", "pin",
    "badge", "seal", "keyring", "journal", "diary", "notebook", "ledger", "signet",
    "medallion", "buckle", "whetstone", "shovel", "pickaxe", "crowbar", "saw", "chisel",
    "file", "nail", "nails", "jar", "salve", "antidote", "antitoxin", "bedroll", "blanket",
    "tent", "ladder", "matchbox", "striker", "firestarter", "spellbook", "bead", "symbol",
    "idol", "shard", "dart", "oil", "bomb", "trap", "manacles", "bell", "horn", "drum",
    "lute", "harp", "gear", "apple", "fish", "cheese", "mushroom", "tube",
    "bag", "sack", "pack", "pouch", "satchel", "chest", "box", "case", "bundle",
    "cloak", "shirt", "tunic", "robe", "coat", "armor", "helm", "helmet", "hood",
    "belt", "strap", "sash",
    "boot", "boots", "glove", "gloves", "gauntlet", "gauntlets", "pant", "pants",
    "trouser", "trousers", "shoe", "shoes", "hat", "ring", "amulet", "necklace",
    "pendant", "bracelet", "charm", "token", "trinket", "potion", "tonic", "elixir", "vial",
    "herb", "ration", "bread", "meat", "flask", "bottle", "waterskin", "lantern",
    "book", "scroll", "map", "letter", "note", "paper", "document", "gem", "crystal",
    "orb", "relic", "artifact", "stone", "ore", "ingot", "tool", "crutch", "board", "plank",
    "needle", "thread", "cloth", "bandage", "feather", "fang", "claw", "scale", "shell"
  ]);

  return itemHeads.has(raw) || itemHeads.has(singular);
},

findConcreteItemHeadIndex(tokens) {
  if (!Array.isArray(tokens) || !tokens.length) return -1;

  const lastIndex = tokens.length - 1;
  if (this.isItemHeadToken(tokens[lastIndex])) {
    return lastIndex;
  }

  // Allow "flask of water" / "bottle of oil" style phrases where the
  // container is the actual item head.
  if (tokens.length >= 3 && String(tokens[1] || "").toLowerCase() === "of" && this.isItemHeadToken(tokens[0])) {
    return 0;
  }

  return -1;
},

isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(phrase) {
  const text = String(phrase || "").toLowerCase().trim();
  if (!text) return false;
  if (this.shouldRejectAcquisitionCapture(text)) return false;

  const abstractWords = new Set([
    "look", "glance", "effort", "pain", "nausea", "movement", "thought",
    "breath", "silence", "chance", "sound", "voice", "moment", "time",
    "focus", "haziness", "haze", "weight", "fear", "attention", "pulse",
    "cover", "seat", "rest", "damage", "hit", "stride", "view", "sight",
    "information", "news", "rumor", "rumour", "permission", "warning"
  ]);

  const sceneWords = new Set([
    "road", "path", "trail", "street", "lane", "room", "hall", "floor",
    "wall", "ceiling", "doorway", "market", "chapel", "smithy", "tavern",
    "inn", "forest", "woods", "cave", "cellar", "stairs", "bridge"
  ]);

  const tokens = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (!tokens.length) return false;
  if (tokens.length > 6) return false;

  for (let i = 0; i < tokens.length; i += 1) {
    const token = String(tokens[i] || "").replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/g, "");
    if (abstractWords.has(token)) return false;
  }

  const headIndex = this.findConcreteItemHeadIndex(tokens);
  if (headIndex === -1) return false;

  const headToken = String(tokens[headIndex] || "").replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/g, "");
  if (sceneWords.has(headToken)) return false;

  return true;
},

  queueNormalizationCandidate(phrase, holder, visibility, sourceType, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return false;
  this.init(root);

  const cleaned = this.cleanItemPhrase(phrase);
  if (!cleaned) return false;
  if (!this.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(cleaned)) return false;

  const pending = root.items.pendingNormalization;
  const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < pending.length; i += 1) {
    const row = pending[i];
    if (
      row &&
      row.phrase === cleaned &&
      row.holder === String(holder || "player") &&
      row.visibility === String(visibility || "packed") &&
      row.turn === turn
    ) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  pending.push({
    phrase: cleaned,
    holder: String(holder || "player"),
    visibility: String(visibility || "packed"),
    sourceType: String(sourceType || "unknown"),
    turn: turn
  });

  return true;
},

  normalizePending(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const pending = root.items.pendingNormalization;
    let changes = 0;

    while (pending.length > 0) {
      const candidate = pending.shift();
      if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") continue;

      const item = this.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase(
        candidate.phrase,
        candidate.visibility || "packed",
        candidate.sourceType || "story_gain",
        root
      );

      if (item) {
        changes += 1;
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase(phrase, visibility, sourceContext, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
  this.init(root);

  const cleaned = this.cleanItemPhrase(phrase);
  if (!cleaned) return null;
  if (!this.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(cleaned)) return null;

  const existing = this.findMatchingPlayerItem(cleaned, root);
  if (existing) {
    if (existing.ownership) {
      existing.ownership.holder = "player";
      if (visibility) existing.ownership.visibility = visibility;
    }
    return existing;
  }

  const seed = this.makeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase(cleaned, sourceContext);
  if (!seed.displayName) return null;

  const item = this.registerItem(seed, root);
  if (!item) return null;

  this.ensurePlayerOwns(item.id, root);

  if (item.ownership) {
    item.ownership.visibility = visibility || "packed";
  }

  const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
  if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
    U.markDirty("items", item.id, root);
  }

  return item;
},

  makeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase(phrase, sourceContext) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const cleaned = this.cleanPhrase(phrase);
    const lower = cleaned.toLowerCase();

    const baseType = this.detectBaseType(lower);
    const slot = this.inferSlotFromBaseType(baseType);
    const descriptors = this.extractDescriptors(cleaned, baseType);
    const materialTags = this.extractMaterialTags(lower);
    const quality = this.extractQuality(lower);

    return {
      displayName: cleaned,
      baseType: baseType,
      descriptors: descriptors,
      materialTags: materialTags,
      quality: quality,
      slot: slot,
      stackable: this.isLikelyStackable(baseType),
      quantity: 1,
      ownership: {
        holder: "player",
        visibility: "packed"
      },
      notes: {
        sourceContext: String(sourceContext || ""),
        observedChanges: []
      }
    };
  },

  detectBaseType(lowerPhrase) {
    const known = [
      "greatsword",
      "longsword",
      "shortsword",
      "dagger",
      "sword",
      "axe",
      "mace",
      "hammer",
      "spear",
      "staff",
      "wand",
      "bow",
      "crossbow",
      "shield",
      "lockpick",
      "lamp",
      "candle",
      "stub",
      "gemstone",
      "set",
      "wheel",
      "cup",
      "bowl",
      "pot",
      "pan",
      "fork",
      "coil",
      "pen",
      "flute",
      "skillet",
      "kettle",
      "jug",
      "mug",
      "spoon",
      "comb",
      "spool",
      "net",
      "pick",
      "link",
      "spring",
      "pellet",
      "manacle",
      "chunk",
      "scrap",
      "fragment",
      "arrow",
      "bolt",
      "bolts",
      "quiver",
      "kit",
      "tools",
      "hook",
      "wire",
      "pin",
      "badge",
      "seal",
      "keyring",
      "journal",
      "diary",
      "notebook",
      "ledger",
      "signet",
      "medallion",
      "buckle",
      "whetstone",
      "shovel",
      "pickaxe",
      "crowbar",
      "saw",
      "chisel",
      "file",
      "jar",
      "salve",
      "antidote",
      "antitoxin",
      "bedroll",
      "blanket",
      "tent",
      "ladder",
      "matchbox",
      "striker",
      "firestarter",
      "spellbook",
      "bead",
      "symbol",
      "idol",
      "shard",
      "dart",
      "oil",
      "bomb",
      "trap",
      "manacles",
      "bell",
      "horn",
      "drum",
      "lute",
      "harp",
      "gear",
      "apple",
      "fish",
      "cheese",
      "mushroom",
      "tube",
      "helmet",
      "helm",
      "hood",
      "armor",
      "robe",
      "cloak",
      "coat",
      "shirt",
      "gloves",
      "gauntlets",
      "pants",
      "trousers",
      "boots",
      "shoes",
      "ring",
      "amulet",
      "necklace",
      "potion",
      "vial",
      "flask",
      "scroll",
      "book",
      "key",
      "map",
      "coin",
      "ration",
      "bread",
      "meat",
      "herb",
      "gem",
      "clothes",
      "outfit"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < known.length; i += 1) {
      if (lowerPhrase.indexOf(known[i]) !== -1) {
        return known[i];
      }
    }

    const words = lowerPhrase.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    if (words.length) {
      return words[words.length - 1];
    }

    return "item";
  },

  inferSlotFromPhrase(phrase) {
    return this.inferSlotFromBaseType(this.detectBaseType(String(phrase || "").toLowerCase()));
  },

  inferSlotFromBaseType(baseType) {
    const key = String(baseType || "").toLowerCase();

    if (key === "sword" || key === "greatsword" || key === "longsword" || key === "shortsword" || key === "dagger" || key === "axe" || key === "mace" || key === "hammer" || key === "spear" || key === "staff" || key === "wand" || key === "bow" || key === "crossbow") {
      return "mainHand";
    }

    if (key === "shield") return "offHand";
