    if (key === "helmet" || key === "helm" || key === "hood") return "head";
    if (key === "armor" || key === "robe" || key === "cloak" || key === "coat" || key === "shirt" || key === "clothes" || key === "outfit") return "body";
    if (key === "gloves" || key === "gauntlets") return "hands";
    if (key === "pants" || key === "trousers") return "legs";
    if (key === "boots" || key === "shoes") return "feet";
    if (key === "ring") return "accessory1";
    if (key === "amulet" || key === "necklace") return "accessory2";

    return "";
  },

  inferSlotFromItem(item) {
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return "";
    if (item.slot && String(item.slot).trim()) return String(item.slot).trim();
    return this.inferSlotFromBaseType(item.baseType || "");
  },

  extractDescriptors(cleanedPhrase, baseType) {
    const lowerBase = String(baseType || "").toLowerCase();
    const words = String(cleanedPhrase || "").split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    const out = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < words.length; i += 1) {
      const w = words[i].toLowerCase();
      if (w === lowerBase) break;
      if (w === "a" || w === "an" || w === "the" || w === "my" || w === "some") continue;
      out.push(words[i]);
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractMaterialTags(lowerPhrase) {
    const known = ["iron", "steel", "wooden", "wood", "leather", "cloth", "bronze", "silver", "gold", "stone", "bone"];
    const out = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < known.length; i += 1) {
      if (lowerPhrase.indexOf(known[i]) !== -1 && out.indexOf(known[i]) === -1) {
        out.push(known[i]);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractQuality(lowerPhrase) {
    const known = ["rusty", "fine", "worn", "broken", "poor", "crude", "sturdy", "ornate", "simple"];
    for (let i = 0; i < known.length; i += 1) {
      if (lowerPhrase.indexOf(known[i]) !== -1) {
        return known[i];
      }
    }
    return "";
  },

  isLikelyStackable(baseType) {
    const key = String(baseType || "").toLowerCase();
    return key === "coin" || key === "ration" || key === "herb" || key === "gem" || key === "gemstone" || key === "potion" || key === "scroll";
  },

  findMatchingPlayerItem(query, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    this.init(root);

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const key = U.toKey(String(query || ""));
    if (!key) return null;

    const owned = root.items && root.items.ownership && Array.isArray(root.items.ownership.player)
      ? root.items.ownership.player
      : [];

    let best = null;
    let bestScore = -1;

    for (let i = 0; i < owned.length; i += 1) {
      const itemId = owned[i];
      const item = root.items.byId[itemId];
      if (!item || typeof item !== "object") continue;

      const displayKey = U.toKey(this.getItemDisplayName(item));
      const baseKey = U.toKey(item.baseType || "");

      let score = 0;

      if (displayKey === key) score = 100;
      else if (baseKey === key) score = 95;
      // Querying a shorter head should still find a longer owned item
      // ("potion" -> "small healing potion"), but a shorter owned item
      // must NOT swallow a longer concrete pickup
      // ("candle" must not match newly collected "candle stub").
      else if (displayKey && displayKey.indexOf(key) !== -1) score = 80;
      else if (baseKey && baseKey.indexOf(key) !== -1) score = 72;

      if (score > bestScore) {
        bestScore = score;
        best = item;
      }
    }

    // Phase 6/7u1 safety:
    // Never let a zero-score "best" match select the first owned item.
    // This was causing multi-item pickup to save only the first item, e.g.
    // "coins and a torch" became coins only because torch matched coins at score 0.
    return bestScore >= 60 ? best : null;
  },

  consumeItem(itemId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return false;
    this.init(root);

    const item = root.items.byId[itemId];
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return false;

    if (item.quantity > 1) {
      item.quantity -= 1;
    } else {
      this.removeItem(itemId, root);
    }

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("items", itemId, root);
    }

    return true;
  },

  removeItem(itemId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return false;
    this.init(root);

    const items = root.items;
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;

    if (!items.byId[itemId]) return false;

    U.removeValue(items.ownership.player, itemId);

    for (const slot in items.equipment) {
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(items.equipment, slot)) continue;
      if (items.equipment[slot] === itemId) {
        items.equipment[slot] = null;
      }
    }

    delete items.byId[itemId];
    this.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);
    return true;
  },

  applyStateTag(itemId, stateTag, durabilityLoss, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return false;
    this.init(root);

    const item = root.items.byId[itemId];
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return false;

    this.ensureItemShape(item);

    const tag = String(stateTag || "").trim();
    if (!tag) return false;

    if (item.condition.stateTags.indexOf(tag) === -1) {
      item.condition.stateTags.push(tag);
    }

    const loss = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(durabilityLoss || 0)));
    item.condition.durability = Math.max(0, item.condition.durability - loss);

    if (item.notes && Array.isArray(item.notes.observedChanges)) {
      item.notes.observedChanges.push(tag);
      while (item.notes.observedChanges.length > 12) {
        item.notes.observedChanges.shift();
      }
    }

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("items", itemId, root);
    }

    return true;
  },

  cleanPhrase(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .replace(/^(?:a|an|the|my|some)\s+/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+(?:from|for)\s+.+$/i, "")
      .trim();
  },

  createItemRecord(seed) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const data = seed && typeof seed === "object" ? seed : {};

    return {
      id: typeof data.id === "string" && data.id ? data.id : U.makeId("item"),
      displayName: typeof data.displayName === "string" ? data.displayName : "",
      baseType: typeof data.baseType === "string" ? data.baseType : "",
      descriptors: Array.isArray(data.descriptors) ? data.descriptors.slice() : [],
      materialTags: Array.isArray(data.materialTags) ? data.materialTags.slice() : [],
      quality: typeof data.quality === "string" ? data.quality : "",
      condition: {
        durability: data.condition && typeof data.condition.durability === "number" ? data.condition.durability : 100,
        stateTags: data.condition && Array.isArray(data.condition.stateTags) ? data.condition.stateTags.slice() : []
      },
      slot: typeof data.slot === "string" ? data.slot : "",
      stackable: data.stackable === true,
      quantity: typeof data.quantity === "number" && data.quantity > 0 ? Math.floor(data.quantity) : 1,
      stats: data.stats && typeof data.stats === "object" && !Array.isArray(data.stats) ? data.stats : {},
      ownership: {
        holder: data.ownership && typeof data.ownership.holder === "string" ? data.ownership.holder : "player",
        visibility: data.ownership && typeof data.ownership.visibility === "string" ? data.ownership.visibility : "packed"
      },
      notes: {
        sourceContext: data.notes && typeof data.notes.sourceContext === "string" ? data.notes.sourceContext : "",
        observedChanges: data.notes && Array.isArray(data.notes.observedChanges) ? data.notes.observedChanges.slice() : []
      }
    };
  },

  ensureItemShape(item) {
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return item;

    if (typeof item.id !== "string") item.id = "";
    if (typeof item.displayName !== "string") item.displayName = "";
    if (typeof item.baseType !== "string") item.baseType = "";
    if (!Array.isArray(item.descriptors)) item.descriptors = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(item.materialTags)) item.materialTags = [];
    if (typeof item.quality !== "string") item.quality = "";

    if (!item.condition || typeof item.condition !== "object" || Array.isArray(item.condition)) {
      item.condition = {};
    }
    if (typeof item.condition.durability !== "number") item.condition.durability = 100;
    if (!Array.isArray(item.condition.stateTags)) item.condition.stateTags = [];

    if (typeof item.slot !== "string") item.slot = "";
    if (typeof item.stackable !== "boolean") item.stackable = false;
    if (typeof item.quantity !== "number" || item.quantity <= 0) item.quantity = 1;

    if (!item.stats || typeof item.stats !== "object" || Array.isArray(item.stats)) {
      item.stats = {};
    }

    if (!item.ownership || typeof item.ownership !== "object" || Array.isArray(item.ownership)) {
      item.ownership = {};
    }
    if (typeof item.ownership.holder !== "string") item.ownership.holder = "player";
    if (typeof item.ownership.visibility !== "string") item.ownership.visibility = "packed";

    if (!item.notes || typeof item.notes !== "object" || Array.isArray(item.notes)) {
      item.notes = {};
    }
    if (typeof item.notes.sourceContext !== "string") item.notes.sourceContext = "";
    if (!Array.isArray(item.notes.observedChanges)) item.notes.observedChanges = [];

    return item;
  },

  ensureAllOwnedRecordsHaveShape(items) {
    if (!items || typeof items !== "object" || !items.byId) return;

    for (const itemId in items.byId) {
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(items.byId, itemId)) continue;
      this.ensureItemShape(items.byId[itemId]);
      if (!items.byId[itemId].id) {
        items.byId[itemId].id = itemId;
      }
    }
  },

  ensureEquipmentIntegrity(items) {
    if (!items || typeof items !== "object") return;

    const equipment = items.equipment || {};
    const byId = items.byId || {};

    for (const slot in equipment) {
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(equipment, slot)) continue;

      const itemId = equipment[slot];
      if (!itemId) continue;

      if (!byId[itemId] || typeof byId[itemId] !== "object") {
        equipment[slot] = null;
        continue;
      }

      byId[itemId].ownership.holder = "player";
      byId[itemId].ownership.visibility = "equipped";
    }
  },

  registerItem(seed, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    this.init(root);

    const items = root.items;
    const item = this.createItemRecord(seed);
    items.byId[item.id] = item;

    return item;
  },

  ensurePlayerOwns(itemId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return;
    this.init(root);

    const items = root.items;
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;

    if (!items.byId[itemId]) return;

    U.pushUnique(items.ownership.player, itemId);
    items.byId[itemId].ownership.holder = "player";

    if (!items.byId[itemId].ownership.visibility) {
      items.byId[itemId].ownership.visibility = "packed";
    }
  },

  equipItem(slot, itemId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return false;
    this.init(root);

    const items = root.items;
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;

    if (!items.equipment || !(slot in items.equipment)) return false;
    if (!items.byId[itemId]) return false;

    const oldItemId = items.equipment[slot];
    if (oldItemId && items.byId[oldItemId]) {
      items.byId[oldItemId].ownership.visibility = "packed";
    }

    items.equipment[slot] = itemId;
    this.ensurePlayerOwns(itemId, root);

    items.byId[itemId].slot = slot;
    items.byId[itemId].ownership.visibility = "equipped";

    U.markDirty("items", itemId, root);
    this.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);
    return true;
  },

  unequipSlot(slot, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return false;
    this.init(root);

    const items = root.items;
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;

    if (!items.equipment || !(slot in items.equipment)) return false;

    const itemId = items.equipment[slot];
    if (!itemId || !items.byId[itemId]) {
      items.equipment[slot] = null;
      return false;
    }

    items.equipment[slot] = null;
    items.byId[itemId].ownership.visibility = "packed";

    U.markDirty("items", itemId, root);
    this.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);
    return true;
  },

  addCurrency(type, amount, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const key = String(type || "").trim();
    const n = Math.floor(Number(amount || 0));
    if (!(key in root.items.currency.byType) || !Number.isFinite(n) || n === 0) {
      return 0;
    }

    root.items.currency.byType[key] += n;
    if (root.items.currency.byType[key] < 0) {
      root.items.currency.byType[key] = 0;
    }

    return root.items.currency.byType[key];
  },


  getContextInventorySummary(root = state.aidrpg, limit = 5) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
    this.init(root);

    const items = root.items || {};
    const byId = items.byId || {};
    const owned = items.ownership && Array.isArray(items.ownership.player) ? items.ownership.player : [];
    const max = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(limit || 5)));
    const names = [];

    for (let i = owned.length - 1; i >= 0 && names.length < max; i -= 1) {
      const itemId = owned[i];
      const item = byId[itemId];
      if (!item || typeof item !== "object") continue;
      const name = this.getItemDisplayName(item);
      if (!name || name === "(invalid item)") continue;
      if (names.indexOf(name) === -1) names.push(name);
    }

    return names.reverse().join(", ");
  },

  getVisibleCarryItems(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return [];
    this.init(root);

    const items = root.items;
    const byId = items.byId || {};
    const owned = Array.isArray(items.ownership.player) ? items.ownership.player : [];
    const out = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < owned.length; i += 1) {
      const itemId = owned[i];
      const item = byId[itemId];
      if (!item || typeof item !== "object") continue;

      const visibility = item.ownership && typeof item.ownership.visibility === "string"
        ? item.ownership.visibility
        : "packed";

      if (visibility === "equipped" || visibility === "carried") {
        out.push(item);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  getItemDisplayName(item) {
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return "(invalid item)";

    if (item.displayName && String(item.displayName).trim()) {
      return String(item.displayName).trim();
    }

    const parts = [];
    if (Array.isArray(item.descriptors) && item.descriptors.length) {
      for (let i = 0; i < item.descriptors.length; i += 1) {
        const part = String(item.descriptors[i] || "").trim();
        if (part) parts.push(part);
      }
    }

    const baseType = item.baseType && String(item.baseType).trim()
      ? String(item.baseType).trim()
      : "item";

    parts.push(baseType);
    return parts.join(" ");
  },

  refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";

    const visible = this.getVisibleCarryItems(root);
    const names = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < visible.length; i += 1) {
      names.push(this.getItemDisplayName(visible[i]));
    }

    const summary = names.length ? names.join(", ") : "nothing notable visible";

    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
      root.cache = {};
    }
    root.cache.visibleCarrySummary = summary;

    return summary;
  },

  getVisibleCarrySummary(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "nothing notable visible";
    this.init(root);

    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
      root.cache = {};
    }

    if (!root.cache.visibleCarrySummary || typeof root.cache.visibleCarrySummary !== "string") {
      return this.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);
    }

    return root.cache.visibleCarrySummary;
  }
},

    // === SCENE STATE SYSTEM ===
SceneStateSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

  if (!root.world || typeof root.world !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.world)) {
    root.world = {};
  }

  if (!root.world.currentScene || typeof root.world.currentScene !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.world.currentScene)) {
    root.world.currentScene = {};
  }

  const scene = root.world.currentScene;

  if (typeof scene.sceneRef !== "string") scene.sceneRef = "";
  if (typeof scene.displayName !== "string") scene.displayName = "";
  if (typeof scene.sceneType !== "string") scene.sceneType = "unknown";
  if (typeof scene.entityRef !== "string") scene.entityRef = "";
  if (typeof scene.parentLocationRef !== "string") scene.parentLocationRef = "";
  if (typeof scene.lastChangedTurn !== "number") scene.lastChangedTurn = 0;

  if (!Array.isArray(scene.localEntityIds)) scene.localEntityIds = [];
  if (!Array.isArray(scene.localActorIds)) scene.localActorIds = [];
  if (!Array.isArray(scene.localHazards)) scene.localHazards = [];
  if (!Array.isArray(scene.localExits)) scene.localExits = [];
  if (!Array.isArray(scene.conditionTags)) scene.conditionTags = [];

  if (!scene.localEntitiesById || typeof scene.localEntitiesById !== "object" || Array.isArray(scene.localEntitiesById)) {
    scene.localEntitiesById = {};
  }

  if (!scene.localActorsById || typeof scene.localActorsById !== "object" || Array.isArray(scene.localActorsById)) {
    scene.localActorsById = {};
  }

  if (typeof scene.sceneHintLabel !== "string") scene.sceneHintLabel = "";
  if (typeof scene.sceneHintStrength !== "number") scene.sceneHintStrength = 0;
  if (typeof scene.sceneHintTurn !== "number") scene.sceneHintTurn = 0;

  if (typeof scene.pendingSceneLabel !== "string") scene.pendingSceneLabel = "";
  if (typeof scene.pendingSceneStrength !== "number") scene.pendingSceneStrength = 0;
  if (typeof scene.pendingSceneCount !== "number") scene.pendingSceneCount = 0;
  if (typeof scene.pendingSceneTurn !== "number") scene.pendingSceneTurn = 0;

  if (typeof scene.sceneConfidence !== "number") scene.sceneConfidence = 0;

  if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
    root.cache = {};
  }

  if (typeof root.cache.sceneSummary !== "string") root.cache.sceneSummary = "";
  if (!Array.isArray(root.cache.localEntityIds)) root.cache.localEntityIds = [];
  if (!Array.isArray(root.cache.localActorIds)) root.cache.localActorIds = [];
  if (typeof root.cache.lastSceneHash !== "string") root.cache.lastSceneHash = "";

  if (root.player && typeof root.player === "object") {
    if (typeof root.player.sceneRef !== "string") root.player.sceneRef = "";
    if (typeof root.player.locationRef !== "string") root.player.locationRef = "";

    if (!scene.sceneRef && root.player.sceneRef) {
      scene.sceneRef = root.player.sceneRef;
    }
  }

  return scene;
},

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const U = A.Utils;
  const text = U && typeof U.normalizeSpaces === "function"
    ? U.normalizeSpaces(String(rawText || ""))
    : String(rawText || "").trim();

  if (!text) return 0;

  if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
    if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  const scene = root.world.currentScene;
  let changes = 0;
  let sceneChanged = 0;

  const sceneCandidate = this.extractSceneCandidate(text);
  if (sceneCandidate) {
    sceneChanged = this.applySceneCandidate(sceneCandidate, root);
    changes += sceneChanged;
  }

  const exits = this.extractLocalExits(text);
  if (sceneChanged || exits.length) {
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(scene.localExits, exits, 6);
  }

  const hazards = this.extractLocalHazards(text);
  if (sceneChanged || hazards.length) {
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(scene.localHazards, hazards, 6);
  }

  const conditionTags = this.extractConditionTags(text);
  if (sceneChanged || conditionTags.length) {
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(scene.conditionTags, conditionTags, 6);
  }

  const actorLabels = this.extractLocalActors(text);
  if (sceneChanged || actorLabels.length) {
    changes += this.syncLabeledRefs(scene.localActorsById, scene.localActorIds, actorLabels, "local_actor");
  }

  const entityLabels = this.extractLocalEntities(text);
  if (sceneChanged || entityLabels.length) {
    changes += this.syncLabeledRefs(scene.localEntitiesById, scene.localEntityIds, entityLabels, "local_entity");
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    this.refreshCache(root);

    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Scene updated: " + this.getCompactSummary(root));
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

  setCurrentSceneFromPhrase(phrase, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const cleaned = this.cleanSceneCandidate(phrase);
  if (!cleaned) return 0;

  return this.confirmSceneCandidate({
    label: cleaned,
    inferredType: this.inferSceneType(cleaned),
    strength: 3,
    sourceType: "forced_phrase"
  }, root);
},

  extractScenePhrase(text) {
  const candidate = this.extractSceneCandidate(text);
  if (!candidate || candidate.strength < 2) {
    return "";
  }

  return candidate.label;
},

pickSceneLabel(value) {
  const src = String(value || "").trim();
  if (!src) return "";

  const dungeonMatches = [
    { re: /\bbarrow\s+sanctum\b/i, label: "barrow sanctum" },
    { re: /\bsealed\s+sanctum\b/i, label: "sanctum" },
    { re: /\b(?:inner\s+)?sanctum\b/i, label: "sanctum" },
    { re: /\b(?:burial\s+)?chamber\b/i, label: "chamber" },
    { re: /\b(?:old\s+|sealed\s+|chapel\s+)?crypt\b/i, label: "crypt" },
    { re: /\b(?:old\s+|stone\s+)?shrine\b/i, label: "shrine" },
    { re: /\b(?:ancient\s+)?tomb\b/i, label: "tomb" },
    { re: /\bcatacombs?\b/i, label: "catacomb" }
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < dungeonMatches.length; i += 1) {
    if (dungeonMatches[i].re.test(src)) {
      const cleaned = this.cleanSceneCandidate(dungeonMatches[i].label);
      if (cleaned) return cleaned;
    }
  }

  const canonicalMatches = [
    { re: /\b(?:tavern|inn)\s+(?:common room|barroom|taproom)\b/i, label: "tavern common room" },
    { re: /\b(?:common room|barroom|taproom)\b/i, label: "common room" },
    { re: /\b(?:tavern\s+)?cellar\b/i, label: "cellar" },
    { re: /\bbarrel room\b/i, label: "cellar" },
    { re: /\bsmithy\s+forge\s+room\b|\bforge\s+room\b/i, label: "smithy forge room" },
    { re: /\b(?:smithy interior|smithy|forge|workshop)\b/i, label: "smithy" },
    { re: /\b(?:ruined\s+)?chapel\b/i, label: "chapel" },
    { re: /\bchapel\s+nave\b|\bnave\b/i, label: "chapel nave" },
    { re: /\bsanctuary\b/i, label: "chapel" },
    { re: /\bguild hall\b/i, label: "guild hall" },
    { re: /\bmain hall\b/i, label: "guild hall" },
    { re: /\bapothecary shop\b/i, label: "apothecary shop" },
    { re: /\bshop interior\b/i, label: "shop interior" },
    { re: /\bmarket lane\b/i, label: "market lane" },
    { re: /\bmarket square\b/i, label: "market square" }
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < canonicalMatches.length; i += 1) {
    if (canonicalMatches[i].re.test(src)) {
      const cleaned = this.cleanSceneCandidate(canonicalMatches[i].label);
      if (cleaned) return cleaned;
    }
  }

  const simplePatterns = [
    /\b(tavern|inn|cellar|smithy|chapel|guild hall|apothecary shop|shop interior|market lane|market square|square|lane|street|road|path|trail|courtyard|yard|bridge|gatehouse|gate|dock|pier|shore|beach|riverbank|river bank|boat|ship deck|deck|camp|forest|woods|swamp|marsh|jungle|cave|cavern|ruins|warehouse|granary|library|house|temple)\b/i
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < simplePatterns.length; i += 1) {
    const match = src.match(simplePatterns[i]);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      const cleaned = this.cleanSceneCandidate(match[1]);
      if (cleaned && !this.isWeakSceneCandidate(cleaned)) {
        return cleaned;
      }
    }
  }

  const cleaned = this.cleanSceneCandidate(src);
  if (!cleaned || this.isWeakSceneCandidate(cleaned)) {
    return "";
  }

  if (!/\b(tavern|inn|common room|barroom|taproom|cellar|barrel room|smithy|forge|workshop|chapel|nave|sanctuary|guild hall|main hall|apothecary|shop interior|market lane|market square|square|lane|street|road|path|trail|courtyard|yard|bridge|gatehouse|gate|dock|pier|shore|beach|riverbank|river bank|boat|ship deck|deck|camp|forest|woods|swamp|marsh|jungle|cave|cavern|ruins|warehouse|granary|library|house|temple)\b/i.test(cleaned)) {
    return "";
  }

  return cleaned;
},

sceneFamilyKey(label) {
  const lower = this.cleanSceneCandidate(label || "").toLowerCase();
  if (!lower) return "";

  if (/\bbarrow\s+sanctum\b/.test(lower)) return "barrow_sanctum";
  if (/\bsanctum\b/.test(lower)) return "sanctum";
  if (/\bchamber\b/.test(lower)) return "chamber";
  if (/\bcrypt\b/.test(lower)) return "crypt";
  if (/\bshrine\b/.test(lower)) return "shrine";
  if (/\btomb\b/.test(lower)) return "tomb";
  if (/\bcatacombs?\b/.test(lower)) return "catacomb";

  if ((/\b(tavern|inn)\b/.test(lower) && /\b(common room|barroom|taproom)\b/.test(lower)) || /\b(common room|barroom|taproom)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "tavern_common_room";
  }

  if (/\b(cellar|barrel room)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "cellar";
  }

  if (/\b(smithy|forge|workshop)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "smithy";
  }

  if (/\b(chapel|nave|sanctuary)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "chapel";
  }

  if (/\b(guild hall|main hall)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "guild_hall";
  }

  if (/\b(apothecary|shop interior)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "shop_interior";
  }

  if (/\bmarket lane\b/.test(lower) || /^lane$/.test(lower)) {
    return "market_lane";
  }

  if (/\bmarket square\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "market_square";
  }

  return "";
},

isGenericSceneLabel(label) {
  const lower = this.cleanSceneCandidate(label || "").toLowerCase();
  if (!lower) return true;

  return new Set([
    "lane",
    "room",
    "hall",
    "interior",
    "exterior",
    "outside",
    "inside",
    "place",
    "unknown",
    "unknown place"
  ]).has(lower);
},

labelsCompatible(a, b) {
  const left = this.cleanSceneCandidate(a || "").toLowerCase();
  const right = this.cleanSceneCandidate(b || "").toLowerCase();

  if (!left || !right) return false;
  if (left === right) return true;

  const leftFamily = this.sceneFamilyKey(left);
  const rightFamily = this.sceneFamilyKey(right);

  if (leftFamily && rightFamily && leftFamily === rightFamily) {
    return true;
  }

  if (left.indexOf(right) !== -1 || right.indexOf(left) !== -1) {
    if (!this.isGenericSceneLabel(left) || !this.isGenericSceneLabel(right)) {
      return true;
    }
  }

  return false;
},

preferSceneLabel(currentLabel, candidateLabel) {
  const current = this.cleanSceneCandidate(currentLabel || "");
  const candidate = this.cleanSceneCandidate(candidateLabel || "");

  if (!current) return candidate;
  if (!candidate) return current;
  if (!this.labelsCompatible(current, candidate)) return candidate;

  const family = this.sceneFamilyKey(current) || this.sceneFamilyKey(candidate);

  if (family === "tavern_common_room") {
    if (/\b(tavern|inn)\b/i.test(candidate)) return candidate;
    if (/\b(tavern|inn)\b/i.test(current)) return current;
  }

  if (family === "guild_hall") {
    if (/\bguild hall\b/i.test(candidate)) return candidate;
    if (/\bguild hall\b/i.test(current)) return current;
  }

  if (family === "chapel") {
    if (/\bchapel\b/i.test(candidate)) return candidate;
    if (/\bchapel\b/i.test(current)) return current;
  }

  if (family === "smithy") {
    if (/\bsmithy\b/i.test(candidate)) return candidate;
    if (/\bsmithy\b/i.test(current)) return current;
  }

  if (family === "shop_interior") {
    if (/\bapothecary\b/i.test(candidate)) return candidate;
    if (/\bapothecary\b/i.test(current)) return current;
  }

  if (family === "market_lane") {
    if (/\bmarket lane\b/i.test(candidate)) return candidate;
    if (/\bmarket lane\b/i.test(current)) return current;
  }

  if (this.isGenericSceneLabel(candidate) && !this.isGenericSceneLabel(current)) {
    return current;
  }

  if (this.isGenericSceneLabel(current) && !this.isGenericSceneLabel(candidate)) {
    return candidate;
  }

  return candidate.split(/\s+/).length > current.split(/\s+/).length ? candidate : current;
},

extractSceneCandidate(text) {
  const raw = String(text || "");
  const candidates = [];

  const hasWeakReferenceLanguage =
    /\b(?:visible from|can see|could see|seen from|nearby|down the street|down the road|beyond|in the distance|somewhere|only consider|consider heading|consider going|think about heading|talks about|talk about|mentions|mention|remembering|remembered|recalling|thinking about|visited before|notable locations? include)\b/i.test(raw);

  const hasExplicitArrivalLanguage =
    /\byou\s+(?:enter|step into|walk into|move into|head into|cross into|make your way into|arrive in|arrive at|return to|descend into|climb into|duck into|go inside|step inside|walk inside)\b/i.test(raw) ||
    /\byou\s+(?:push through|push open|slip through)\b/i.test(raw);

  const weakReferenceOnly = hasWeakReferenceLanguage && !hasExplicitArrivalLanguage;

  const pushCandidate = (value, strength, sourceType) => {
    let candidateValue = String(value || "");
    // v1.0.3: when narration says "you remain in Y while remembering X",
    // the remembered places are not current scenes. Trim them before label pick.
    if (String(sourceType || "") === "explicit_current_presence") {
      candidateValue = candidateValue
        .replace(/\s+while\s+(?:remembering|thinking\s+about|recalling|considering|mentioning)\b[\s\S]*$/i, "")
        .replace(/\s+(?:remembering|thinking\s+about|recalling|visited\s+before|notable\s+locations?\s+include)\b[\s\S]*$/i, "")
        .trim();
    }
    const picked = this.pickSceneLabel(candidateValue);
    if (!picked) return;

    candidates.push({
      label: picked,
      inferredType: this.inferSceneType(picked),
      strength: Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(strength) || 1)),
      sourceType: String(sourceType || "hint")
    });
  };

  // Phase 5 Recovery Patch 3:
  // Current-presence wording must extract only the place after "remain/stay/still in".
  // This prevents nearby/mentioned places earlier in the sentence from becoming current scene.
  const currentPresencePatterns = [
    /\byou\s+(?:remain|stay|wait|stand)\s+(?:inside|within|in|at|on)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]+)/i,
    /\byou\s+are\s+still\s+(?:inside|within|in|at|on)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]+)/i,
    /\bwhile\s+(?:staying|remaining)\s+(?:inside|within|in|at|on)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]+)/i,
    /\bbut\s+you\s+(?:remain|stay)\s+(?:inside|within|in|at|on)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]+)/i
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < currentPresencePatterns.length; i += 1) {
    const match = raw.match(currentPresencePatterns[i]);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      pushCandidate(match[1], 3, "explicit_current_presence");
    }
  }

  const arrivalPatterns = [
    /\byou\s+(?:enter|step into|walk into|move into|head into|cross into|make your way into|arrive in|arrive at|return to|descend into|climb into|duck into|go inside|step inside|walk inside)\b/i,
    /\byou\s+(?:push through|push open|slip through)\b/i
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < arrivalPatterns.length; i += 1) {
    if (arrivalPatterns[i].test(raw)) {
      pushCandidate(raw, 3, "explicit_arrival");
    }
  }

  const mediumPatterns = [
    /\b(?:inside|within|beneath|under|along|on|at)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;]+)/i,

    // "The market lane remains busy..." and "The tavern common room remains..."
    // are current-scene confirmations. Compound room labels must be listed
    // before shorter labels so they do not downgrade to weak mention-only hints.
    /\bthe\s+(tavern common room|inn common room|tavern barroom|inn barroom|tavern taproom|inn taproom|common room|barroom|taproom|storeroom|kitchen|workshop|smithy forge room|forge room|smithy|forge|loft|sleeping quarters|cellar|barrel room|guild hall|main hall|apothecary shop|shop interior|market lane|market square|square|lane|street|road|path|trail|courtyard|yard|bridge|gatehouse|gate|dock|pier|shore|beach|riverbank|river bank|tavern|inn|shop|temple|chapel|nave|camp|forest|woods|swamp|marsh|jungle|cave|cavern|ruins)\s+(?:is|lies|stretches|opens|remains|waits|stands|buzzes)\b/i,

    // In "the chapel is visible from the market lane", the current scene is
    // the "from" place, not the visible distant place.
    /\b(?:visible from|seen from|can be seen from)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;]+)/i
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < mediumPatterns.length; i += 1) {
    const match = raw.match(mediumPatterns[i]);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      pushCandidate(match[1], 2, "contextual_presence");
    }
  }

  // Do not allow weak nearby/future/distant mentions to become mention-only
  // scene candidates. They may appear in narration, but they are not arrival.
  if (
    !candidates.length &&
    !weakReferenceOnly &&
    /\b(tavern|inn|common room|cellar|smithy|forge|chapel|nave|guild hall|main hall|apothecary shop|shop interior|market lane|market square|bridge|gatehouse|dock|pier|forest|woods|swamp|marsh|jungle|cave|cavern|ruins)\b/i.test(raw)
  ) {
    pushCandidate(raw, 1, "mention_only");
  }

  if (
    !candidates.length &&
    !weakReferenceOnly &&
    /\b(barrow\s+sanctum|sealed\s+sanctum|sanctum|chamber|crypt|shrine|tomb|catacombs?)\b/i.test(raw)
  ) {
    pushCandidate(raw, hasExplicitArrivalLanguage ? 3 : 2, "dungeon_presence");
  }

  if (!candidates.length) {
    return null;
  }

  candidates.sort((a, b) => {
    if (b.strength !== a.strength) return b.strength - a.strength;
    return b.label.split(/\s+/).length - a.label.split(/\s+/).length;
  });

  return candidates[0];
},

applySceneCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object" || !candidate.label) {
    return 0;
  }

  const scene = root.world.currentScene;
  const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
  let changes = 0;

  if (scene.sceneHintLabel !== candidate.label) {
    scene.sceneHintLabel = candidate.label;
    changes += 1;
  }
  if (scene.sceneHintStrength !== candidate.strength) {
    scene.sceneHintStrength = candidate.strength;
    changes += 1;
  }
  if (scene.sceneHintTurn !== turn) {
    scene.sceneHintTurn = turn;
    changes += 1;
  }

  const currentLabel = this.cleanSceneCandidate(scene.displayName || "");
  const currentStable = currentLabel && !this.isWeakSceneCandidate(currentLabel);
  const noConfirmedScene = !scene.sceneRef || !currentStable || Number(scene.sceneConfidence || 0) < 2;
  const sameAsCurrent = currentStable && this.labelsCompatible(currentLabel, candidate.label);

  if (sameAsCurrent) {
    const preferred = this.preferSceneLabel(currentLabel, candidate.label);

    if (preferred && scene.displayName !== preferred) {
      scene.displayName = preferred;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const nextConfidence = Math.max(Number(scene.sceneConfidence || 0), candidate.strength);
    if (scene.sceneConfidence !== nextConfidence) {
      scene.sceneConfidence = nextConfidence;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if ((!scene.sceneType || scene.sceneType === "unknown") && candidate.inferredType) {
      scene.sceneType = candidate.inferredType;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (scene.pendingSceneLabel || scene.pendingSceneStrength || scene.pendingSceneCount || scene.pendingSceneTurn) {
      scene.pendingSceneLabel = "";
      scene.pendingSceneStrength = 0;
      scene.pendingSceneCount = 0;
      scene.pendingSceneTurn = 0;
      changes += 1;
    }

    return changes;
  }

  if (candidate.strength >= 3 || (candidate.strength >= 2 && noConfirmedScene)) {
    return changes + this.confirmSceneCandidate(candidate, root);
  }

  if (candidate.strength < 2) {
    return changes;
  }

  if (scene.pendingSceneLabel && this.labelsCompatible(scene.pendingSceneLabel, candidate.label)) {
    const preferredPending = this.preferSceneLabel(scene.pendingSceneLabel, candidate.label);
    const nextCount = Number(scene.pendingSceneCount || 0) + 1;
    const nextStrength = Math.max(Number(scene.pendingSceneStrength || 0), candidate.strength);

    if (scene.pendingSceneLabel !== preferredPending) {
      scene.pendingSceneLabel = preferredPending;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (scene.pendingSceneCount !== nextCount) {
      scene.pendingSceneCount = nextCount;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (scene.pendingSceneStrength !== nextStrength) {
      scene.pendingSceneStrength = nextStrength;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (scene.pendingSceneTurn !== turn) {
      scene.pendingSceneTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }
  } else {
    scene.pendingSceneLabel = candidate.label;
    scene.pendingSceneStrength = candidate.strength;
    scene.pendingSceneCount = 1;
    scene.pendingSceneTurn = turn;
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (scene.pendingSceneCount >= 2 && scene.pendingSceneStrength >= 2) {
    changes += this.confirmSceneCandidate({
      label: scene.pendingSceneLabel,
      inferredType: this.inferSceneType(scene.pendingSceneLabel),
      strength: scene.pendingSceneStrength,
      sourceType: "reinforced_presence"
    }, root);
  }

  return changes;
},

confirmSceneCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
  let label = this.cleanSceneCandidate(candidate && candidate.label ? candidate.label : "");
  if (!label) return 0;

  const scene = root.world.currentScene;
  const currentLabel = this.cleanSceneCandidate(scene.displayName || "");
  if (currentLabel && this.labelsCompatible(currentLabel, label)) {
    label = this.preferSceneLabel(currentLabel, label);
  }

  const key = U.toKey(label);
  if (!key) return 0;

  const nextSceneRef = "scene_" + key;
  const inferredType = candidate && candidate.inferredType
    ? candidate.inferredType
    : this.inferSceneType(label);
  const strength = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(candidate && candidate.strength ? candidate.strength : 2)));
  const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

  let changed = 0;

  if (scene.sceneRef !== nextSceneRef) {
    scene.sceneRef = nextSceneRef;
    scene.displayName = label;
    scene.sceneType = inferredType;
    scene.entityRef = "entity_" + key;
    scene.lastChangedTurn = turn;
    scene.sceneConfidence = strength;

    scene.localEntityIds = [];
    scene.localActorIds = [];
    scene.localHazards = [];
    scene.localExits = [];
    scene.conditionTags = [];
    scene.localEntitiesById = {};
    scene.localActorsById = {};

    changed = 1;

    if (root.player && typeof root.player === "object") {
      root.player.sceneRef = nextSceneRef;
    }

    if (U && typeof U.invalidateSceneCache === "function") {
      U.invalidateSceneCache(root);
    }
  } else {
    if (scene.displayName !== label) {
      scene.displayName = label;
      changed = 1;
    }

    if (scene.sceneType !== inferredType) {
      scene.sceneType = inferredType;
      changed = 1;
    }

    const nextConfidence = Math.max(Number(scene.sceneConfidence || 0), strength);
    if (scene.sceneConfidence !== nextConfidence) {
      scene.sceneConfidence = nextConfidence;
      changed = 1;
    }
  }

  if (scene.sceneHintLabel !== label) {
    scene.sceneHintLabel = label;
    changed = 1;
  }
  if (scene.sceneHintStrength !== strength) {
    scene.sceneHintStrength = strength;
    changed = 1;
  }
  if (scene.sceneHintTurn !== turn) {
    scene.sceneHintTurn = turn;
    changed = 1;
  }

  if (scene.pendingSceneLabel || scene.pendingSceneStrength || scene.pendingSceneCount || scene.pendingSceneTurn) {
    scene.pendingSceneLabel = "";
    scene.pendingSceneStrength = 0;
    scene.pendingSceneCount = 0;
    scene.pendingSceneTurn = 0;
    changed = 1;
  }

  return changed;
},

  extractLocalExits(text) {
    const out = [];
    const add = (value) => {
      const v = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!v) return;
      if (out.indexOf(v) === -1) out.push(v);
    };

    const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();

    if (/\bdoor\b/.test(lower)) add("door");
    if (/\bstairs?\b/.test(lower)) add("stairs");
    if (/\bgate\b/.test(lower)) add("gate");
    if (/\bhallway\b|\bcorridor\b/.test(lower)) add("hallway");
    if (/\bpath\b|\broad\b|\btrail\b/.test(lower)) add("path");
    if (/\bladder\b/.test(lower)) add("ladder");
    if (/\bwindow\b/.test(lower)) add("window");
    if (/\bbridge\b/.test(lower)) add("bridge");
    if (/\btunnel\b/.test(lower)) add("tunnel");

    if (/\bnorth\b/.test(lower)) add("north");
    if (/\bsouth\b/.test(lower)) add("south");
    if (/\beast\b/.test(lower)) add("east");
    if (/\bwest\b/.test(lower)) add("west");
    if (/\bupstairs\b|\bup\b/.test(lower)) add("up");
    if (/\bdownstairs\b|\bdown\b/.test(lower)) add("down");

    return out;
  },

  extractLocalHazards(text) {
    const out = [];
    const add = (value) => {
      const v = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!v) return;
      if (out.indexOf(v) === -1) out.push(v);
    };

    const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();

    if (/\bfire\b|\bflames?\b|\bburning\b/.test(lower)) add("fire");
    if (/\bsmoke\b|\bsmoky\b/.test(lower)) add("smoke");
    if (/\bdebris\b|\brubble\b/.test(lower)) add("debris");
    if (/\bcollaps(?:e|ing|ed)\b|\bunstable\b/.test(lower)) add("collapse");
    if (/\bflood(?:ed|ing)?\b|\bwaterlogged\b/.test(lower)) add("flood");
    if (/\bpoison\b|\btoxic\b|\bgas\b/.test(lower)) add("poison");
    if (/\btrap\b|\btrapped\b/.test(lower)) add("trap");
    if (/\bblood\b|\bbloody\b/.test(lower)) add("blood");
    if (/\bcorpse\b|\bbody\b|\bodies\b/.test(lower)) add("corpses");
    if (/\bdark\b|\bpitch-black\b/.test(lower)) add("darkness");

    return out;
  },

  extractConditionTags(text) {
    const out = [];
    const add = (value) => {
      const v = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!v) return;
      if (out.indexOf(v) === -1) out.push(v);
    };

    const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();

    if (/\bdamaged\b|\bbroken\b|\bcracked\b|\bburned\b|\bcharred\b/.test(lower)) add("damaged");
    if (/\bcrowded\b|\bpacked\b|\bfull of people\b/.test(lower)) add("crowded");
    if (/\babandoned\b|\bempty\b|\bdeserted\b/.test(lower)) add("empty");
    if (/\bwet\b|\bdamp\b/.test(lower)) add("wet");
    if (/\bsmoky\b|\bhazy\b/.test(lower)) add("obscured");
    if (/\bbloody\b/.test(lower)) add("violent_aftermath");
    if (/\bcold\b|\bfreezing\b/.test(lower)) add("cold");
    if (/\bhot\b|\bsweltering\b/.test(lower)) add("hot");

    return out;
  },

  extractLocalActors(text) {
    const out = [];
    const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();
    const actorTerms = [
      "guard",
      "merchant",
      "innkeeper",
      "bartender",
      "barkeep",
      "adventurer",
      "soldier",
      "bandit",
      "goblin",
      "wolf",
      "patron",
      "priest",
      "noble",
      "traveler",
      "mage",
      "knight",
      "child",
      "woman",
      "man",
      "beast"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < actorTerms.length; i += 1) {
      const term = actorTerms[i];
      const re = new RegExp("\\b(?:a|an|the|some)\\s+" + term + "s?\\b", "i");
      if (re.test(lower) && out.indexOf(term) === -1) {
        out.push(term);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractLocalEntities(text) {
    const out = [];
    const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();
    const entityTerms = [
      "door",
      "table",
      "bar",
      "counter",
      "chest",
      "altar",
      "bed",
      "window",
      "stairs",
      "ladder",
      "gate",
      "bookshelf",
      "shelf",
      "crate",
      "wagon",
      "cart",
      "fireplace"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < entityTerms.length; i += 1) {
      const term = entityTerms[i];
      const re = new RegExp("\\b(?:a|an|the|some)\\s+" + term + "s?\\b", "i");
      if (re.test(lower) && out.indexOf(term) === -1) {
        out.push(term);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  syncLabeledRefs(mapObj, idArray, labels, prefix) {
    if (!mapObj || typeof mapObj !== "object" || Array.isArray(mapObj)) return 0;
    if (!Array.isArray(idArray)) return 0;
    if (!Array.isArray(labels)) labels = [];

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const nextMap = {};
    const nextIds = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < labels.length && i < 8; i += 1) {
      const label = this.cleanPhrase(labels[i]);
      if (!label) continue;

      const key = U.toKey(label);
      if (!key) continue;

      const ref = String(prefix || "local") + "_" + key;
      nextMap[ref] = label;
      nextIds.push(ref);
    }

    let changed = 0;

    if (idArray.length !== nextIds.length) {
      changed = 1;
    } else {
      for (let i = 0; i < idArray.length; i += 1) {
        if (idArray[i] !== nextIds[i]) {
          changed = 1;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) {
      const oldKeys = Object.keys(mapObj);
      const newKeys = Object.keys(nextMap);
      if (oldKeys.length !== newKeys.length) {
        changed = 1;
      } else {
        for (let i = 0; i < newKeys.length; i += 1) {
          const key = newKeys[i];
          if (mapObj[key] !== nextMap[key]) {
            changed = 1;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) return 0;

    idArray.length = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < nextIds.length; i += 1) {
      idArray.push(nextIds[i]);
    }

    for (const key in mapObj) {
      if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(mapObj, key)) {
        delete mapObj[key];
      }
    }

    for (const key in nextMap) {
      if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(nextMap, key)) {
        mapObj[key] = nextMap[key];
      }
    }

    return 1;
  },

  replaceSimpleArray(targetArray, nextValues, maxCount) {
    if (!Array.isArray(targetArray)) return 0;
    if (!Array.isArray(nextValues)) nextValues = [];

    const cleaned = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < nextValues.length && i < (maxCount || 8); i += 1) {
      const value = String(nextValues[i] || "").trim();
      if (!value) continue;
      if (cleaned.indexOf(value) === -1) cleaned.push(value);
    }

    let changed = 0;
    if (targetArray.length !== cleaned.length) {
      changed = 1;
    } else {
      for (let i = 0; i < targetArray.length; i += 1) {
        if (targetArray[i] !== cleaned[i]) {
          changed = 1;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) return 0;

    targetArray.length = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < cleaned.length; i += 1) {
      targetArray.push(cleaned[i]);
    }

    return 1;
  },

  inferSceneType(label) {
  const lower = String(label || "").toLowerCase();

  if (/\b(barrow\s+sanctum|sealed\s+sanctum|sanctum|chamber|crypt|shrine|tomb|catacombs?)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "room";
  }

  if (/\b(common room|barroom|taproom|storeroom|kitchen|loft|sleeping quarters|cellar|room|hall|chamber)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "room";
  }

  if (/\b(tavern|inn|shop|temple|guildhall|house|building|library|warehouse|granary|smithy|forge|workshop|bar)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "interior";
  }

  if (/\b(market square|square|lane|street|road|path|trail|forest|field|bridge|gate|gatehouse|courtyard|yard|camp|dock|pier|shore|beach|riverbank|river bank|boat|ship deck|deck)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "outdoor";
  }

  return "scene";
},

  cleanSceneCandidate(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .replace(/^(?:the|an|a)\s+/i, "")
    .replace(/\s*,\s*(?:catching|taking|trying|struggling|still|while|as)\b.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+(?:where|when|while|and then|but then)\s+.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
    .trim();
},

isWeakSceneCandidate(value) {
  const lower = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!lower) return true;
  if (lower.length > 48) return true;
  if (/\b(catching your breath|looking around|trying to|beginning to|continuing to|still)\b/.test(lower)) return true;
  if (/^(there|here|somewhere|nearby|around you)$/.test(lower)) return true;
  return false;
},

cleanPhrase(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .replace(/^(?:the|an|a)\s+/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+(?:where|when|while|and|but)\s+.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
    .trim();
},

  refreshCache(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";

    const scene = this.init(root);

    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
      root.cache = {};
    }

    root.cache.localEntityIds = scene.localEntityIds.slice();
    root.cache.localActorIds = scene.localActorIds.slice();

    const summary = this.getCompactSummary(root);
    root.cache.sceneSummary = summary;
    root.cache.scenePacket = summary;

    if (globalThis.AIDRPG && globalThis.AIDRPG.TurnEngine && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.TurnEngine.stableHash === "function") {
      root.cache.lastSceneHash = globalThis.AIDRPG.TurnEngine.stableHash(summary);
    }

    return summary;
  },

  getCompactSummary(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || !root.world || !root.world.currentScene) {
      return "Scene unknown.";
    }

    const scene = root.world.currentScene;
    const parts = [];

    if (scene.displayName) {
      parts.push("Scene: " + scene.displayName);
    } else {
      parts.push("Scene: unknown");
    }

    if (scene.sceneType) {
      parts.push("Type: " + scene.sceneType);
    }

    if (Array.isArray(scene.conditionTags) && scene.conditionTags.length) {
      parts.push("State: " + scene.conditionTags.join(", "));
    }

    if (Array.isArray(scene.localHazards) && scene.localHazards.length) {
      parts.push("Hazards: " + scene.localHazards.join(", "));
    }

    if (Array.isArray(scene.localExits) && scene.localExits.length) {
      parts.push("Exits: " + scene.localExits.join(", "));
    }

    if (scene.localActorIds && scene.localActorIds.length && scene.localActorsById) {
      const names = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < scene.localActorIds.length; i += 1) {
        const ref = scene.localActorIds[i];
        if (scene.localActorsById[ref]) {
          names.push(scene.localActorsById[ref]);
        }
      }
      if (names.length) {
        parts.push("Actors: " + names.join(", "));
      }
    }

    if (scene.localEntityIds && scene.localEntityIds.length && scene.localEntitiesById) {
      const names = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < scene.localEntityIds.length; i += 1) {
        const ref = scene.localEntityIds[i];
        if (scene.localEntitiesById[ref]) {
          names.push(scene.localEntitiesById[ref]);
        }
      }
      if (names.length) {
        parts.push("Objects: " + names.join(", "));
      }
    }

    return parts.join(" | ");
  }
},

    // === WORLD ENTITY SYSTEM ===
WorldEntitySystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.world || typeof root.world !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.world)) {
      root.world = {};
    }

    const world = root.world;

    if (!world.locationsById || typeof world.locationsById !== "object" || Array.isArray(world.locationsById)) {
      world.locationsById = {};
    }

    if (!world.entitiesById || typeof world.entitiesById !== "object" || Array.isArray(world.entitiesById)) {
      world.entitiesById = {};
    }

    if (!world.factionsById || typeof world.factionsById !== "object" || Array.isArray(world.factionsById)) {
      world.factionsById = {};
    }

    if (!world.routesById || typeof world.routesById !== "object" || Array.isArray(world.routesById)) {
      world.routesById = {};
    }

    if (!world.currentScene || typeof world.currentScene !== "object" || Array.isArray(world.currentScene)) {
      world.currentScene = {};
    }

    if (!world.mapMemory || typeof world.mapMemory !== "object" || Array.isArray(world.mapMemory)) {
      world.mapMemory = {};
    }
    const map = world.mapMemory;
    if (!map.namedLocationsById || typeof map.namedLocationsById !== "object" || Array.isArray(map.namedLocationsById)) map.namedLocationsById = {};
    if (!map.dungeonLayoutsById || typeof map.dungeonLayoutsById !== "object" || Array.isArray(map.dungeonLayoutsById)) map.dungeonLayoutsById = {};
    if (!map.goalBindingsById || typeof map.goalBindingsById !== "object" || Array.isArray(map.goalBindingsById)) map.goalBindingsById = {};
    if (!Array.isArray(map.recentMapEvents)) map.recentMapEvents = [];
    if (typeof map.currentDungeonId !== "string") map.currentDungeonId = "";
    if (typeof map.lastDungeonRoomId !== "string") map.lastDungeonRoomId = "";

    if (!world.activeEncounter || typeof world.activeEncounter !== "object" || Array.isArray(world.activeEncounter)) {
      world.activeEncounter = {};
    }
    const enc = world.activeEncounter;
    if (typeof enc.enemyLabel !== "string") enc.enemyLabel = "";
    if (typeof enc.totalCount !== "number") enc.totalCount = 0;
    if (typeof enc.remainingCount !== "number") enc.remainingCount = 0;
    if (typeof enc.resolved !== "boolean") enc.resolved = false;
    if (!Array.isArray(enc.recentEvents)) enc.recentEvents = [];

    return world;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const U = A.Utils;
  const text = U && typeof U.normalizeSpaces === "function"
    ? U.normalizeSpaces(String(rawText || ""))
    : String(rawText || "").trim();

  if (!text) return 0;

  if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
    if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  let changes = 0;

  changes += this.syncCurrentSceneToWorld(root);

  const locationPhrase = this.extractLocationPhrase(text);
  if (locationPhrase) {
    changes += this.applyLocationPhrase(locationPhrase, root);
  }

  const ownerHints = this.extractOwnerHints(text);
  const materialHints = this.extractMaterialHints(text);
  const districtHints = this.extractDistrictHints(text);
  const factionHints = this.extractFactionHints(text);

  changes += this.applyHintsToCurrentScene(ownerHints, materialHints, districtHints, factionHints, root);
  changes += this.processMapContinuity(text, root);
  changes += this.applyValidatedConsequences(root);

  if (changes > 0) {
    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("World entity updates: " + changes);
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

processMapContinuity(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const text = String(rawText || "");
  if (!text.trim()) return 0;

  let changes = 0;
  changes += this.captureNamedLocationLayout(text, root);
  changes += this.captureDungeonLayout(text, root);
  changes += this.captureGoalLocationBinding(text, root);
  changes += this.processGroupCountTracking(text, root);
  changes += this.detectMapContradictions(text, root);

  if (changes > 0) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG && globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils ? globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils : null;
    if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") U.invalidatePackets(root);
  }

  return changes;
},


processGroupCountTracking(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const lower = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  if (!lower) return 0;

  const enc = root.world.activeEncounter || (root.world.activeEncounter = {});
  if (!Array.isArray(enc.recentEvents)) enc.recentEvents = [];
  const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

  const words = { one:1,two:2,three:3,four:4,five:5,six:6,seven:7,eight:8,nine:9,ten:10,eleven:11,twelve:12,thirteen:13,fourteen:14,fifteen:15,sixteen:16,seventeen:17,eighteen:18,nineteen:19,twenty:20 };
  const parseNum = (value) => /^\d+$/.test(String(value || "")) ? Math.floor(Number(value)) : (words[String(value || "").toLowerCase()] || 0);
  const singular = (value) => String(value || "enemy").toLowerCase().replace(/ies$/, "y").replace(/s$/, "");
  const pushEncEvent = (message) => {
    const msg = String(message || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!msg) return 0;
    const last = enc.recentEvents.length ? enc.recentEvents[enc.recentEvents.length - 1] : null;
    if (last && last.turn === turn && last.message === msg) return 0;
    enc.recentEvents.push({ turn, message: msg });
    while (enc.recentEvents.length > 10) enc.recentEvents.shift();
    this.pushMapEvent("Encounter: " + msg, root);
    return 1;
  };

  let changes = 0;

  let match = raw.match(/\b(?:there\s+are|you\s+face|you\s+see|before\s+you\s+stand|a\s+group\s+of|a\s+pack\s+of|a\s+swarm\s+of)?\s*(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty)\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{2,24}?)(?:s)?\b[^.!?]{0,80}\b(?:attack|attacks|ambush|ambushes|surround|surrounds|charge|charges|remain|stand|block|threaten|close\s+in|wait)/i);
  if (match) {
    const count = parseNum(match[1]);
    const label = singular(match[2]);
    if (count > 0 && label && !/^(?:minute|hour|day|year|coin|silver|gold)$/.test(label)) {
      if (enc.enemyLabel !== label || enc.totalCount !== count || enc.resolved === true) {
        enc.enemyLabel = label;
        enc.totalCount = count;
        enc.remainingCount = count;
        enc.resolved = false;
        enc.lastChangedTurn = turn;
        changes += 1 + pushEncEvent(count + " " + label + " enemies engaged; " + count + " remain.");
      }
    }
  }

  const reinforcement = raw.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty)\s+(?:more|additional|new)\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{2,24}?)(?:s)?\s+(?:arrive|join|rush\s+in|reinforce|appear)/i);
  if (reinforcement) {
    const add = parseNum(reinforcement[1]);
    const label = singular(reinforcement[2]);
    if (add > 0) {
      if (!enc.enemyLabel) enc.enemyLabel = label;
      enc.totalCount = Math.max(0, Number(enc.totalCount || 0)) + add;
      enc.remainingCount = Math.max(0, Number(enc.remainingCount || 0)) + add;
      enc.resolved = false;
      enc.lastChangedTurn = turn;
      changes += 1 + pushEncEvent(add + " reinforcements joined; " + enc.remainingCount + " " + (enc.enemyLabel || label || "enemies") + " remain.");
    }
  }

  const kill = raw.match(/\b(?:you\s+)?(?:kill|slay|drop|fell|defeat|cut\s+down|take\s+down)\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty)\s+(?:of\s+the\s+)?([a-z][a-z' -]{2,24}?)(?:s)?\b/i) ||
    raw.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty)\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{2,24}?)(?:s)?\s+(?:fall|die|are\s+slain|are\s+defeated)\b/i);
  if (kill) {
    const dead = parseNum(kill[1]);
    const label = singular(kill[2]);
    if (dead > 0 && (enc.enemyLabel || label)) {
      if (!enc.enemyLabel) enc.enemyLabel = label;
      if (!enc.totalCount) enc.totalCount = Math.max(dead, Number(enc.remainingCount || 0));
      const before = Math.max(0, Number(enc.remainingCount || enc.totalCount || 0));
      const after = Math.max(0, before - dead);
      if (enc.remainingCount !== after) {
        enc.remainingCount = after;
        enc.lastChangedTurn = turn;
        enc.resolved = after <= 0;
        changes += 1 + pushEncEvent(dead + " " + (enc.enemyLabel || label || "enemies") + " defeated; " + after + " remain.");
      }
    }
  }

  if (enc.enemyLabel && enc.remainingCount > 0 && /\b(?:all|every|the\s+last)\s+(?:enemy|enemies|foes?|goblins?|bandits?|wolves?|soldiers?)\s+(?:are\s+)?(?:dead|defeated|gone|finished)\b/.test(lower)) {
    changes += pushEncEvent("continuity holds: " + enc.remainingCount + " " + enc.enemyLabel + " remain unresolved.");
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG && globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils ? globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils : null;
    if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") U.invalidatePackets(root);
  }

  return changes;
},

getMapMemory(root = state.aidrpg) {
  this.init(root);
  return root && root.world ? root.world.mapMemory : null;
},

pushMapEvent(message, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const map = this.getMapMemory(root);
  if (!map) return 0;
  const msg = String(message || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  if (!msg) return 0;
  const turn = root && root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
  const last = map.recentMapEvents.length ? map.recentMapEvents[map.recentMapEvents.length - 1] : null;
  if (last && last.message === msg && last.turn === turn) return 0;
  map.recentMapEvents.push({ turn: turn, message: msg });
  while (map.recentMapEvents.length > 24) map.recentMapEvents.shift();
  const U = globalThis.AIDRPG && globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils ? globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils : null;
  if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") U.logEvent(msg);
  return 1;
},

extractNamedLocationLabels(rawText) {
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const out = [];
  const add = (value) => {
    const rawLabel = String(value || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "").trim();
    const clean = this.cleanPhrase(rawLabel);
    if (!clean) return;
    const label = /^The\s+[A-Z]/.test(rawLabel) ? rawLabel : clean;
    if (!/^The\s+[A-Z]/.test(rawLabel) && !/^[A-Z]/.test(clean)) return;
    if (out.indexOf(label) === -1) out.push(label);
  };

  const patterns = [
    /\b(The\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+){0,3})\b/g,
    /\b(?:at|inside|within|in|enter|return to|go to|meet at|pay at)\s+(The\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+){0,3})\b/g
  ];

  for (let p = 0; p < patterns.length; p += 1) {
    let match;
    while ((match = patterns[p].exec(raw))) add(match[1]);
  }

  return out.slice(0, 4);
},

ensureNamedLocationRecord(label, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const map = this.getMapMemory(root);
  if (!map) return null;
  const clean = this.cleanPhrase(label || "");
  if (!clean) return null;
  const id = "named_location_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(clean);
  const turn = root && root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

  if (!map.namedLocationsById[id] || typeof map.namedLocationsById[id] !== "object") {
    map.namedLocationsById[id] = { id, displayName: clean, layoutFacts: [], roomAssignment: "", goalRefs: [], firstSeenTurn: turn, lastSeenTurn: turn };
  }
  const rec = map.namedLocationsById[id];
  if (!Array.isArray(rec.layoutFacts)) rec.layoutFacts = [];
  if (!Array.isArray(rec.goalRefs)) rec.goalRefs = [];
  if (typeof rec.roomAssignment !== "string") rec.roomAssignment = "";
  rec.lastSeenTurn = turn;

  const locRef = "location_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(clean);
  const loc = this.ensureLocationRecord(locRef, clean, root);
  if (loc && loc.mutable) {
    if (!Array.isArray(loc.mutable.layoutFacts)) loc.mutable.layoutFacts = [];
    if (typeof loc.mutable.roomAssignment !== "string") loc.mutable.roomAssignment = "";
  }
  return rec;
},

extractLayoutFacts(rawText) {
  const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
  const facts = [];
  const add = (value) => { const clean = this.cleanPhrase(value || ""); if (clean && facts.indexOf(clean) === -1) facts.push(clean); };
  if (/\bcommon room\b/.test(lower)) add("common room");
  if (/\beast[- ]wall bar\b|\bbar\s+on\s+the\s+east\s+wall\b/.test(lower)) add("east-wall bar");
  if (/\bwest[- ]wall bar\b|\bbar\s+on\s+the\s+west\s+wall\b/.test(lower)) add("west-wall bar");
  if (/\bcentral\s+bar\b|\bround\s+bar\b|\bbar\s+in\s+the\s+center\b|\bbar\s+at\s+the\s+center\b/.test(lower)) add("central bar");
  if (/\brear stairs?\b|\bstairs?\s+at\s+the\s+rear\b/.test(lower)) add("rear stairs");
  if (/\bcellar hatch\b/.test(lower)) add(/\bbehind\s+the\s+bar\b/.test(lower) ? "cellar hatch behind the bar" : "cellar hatch");
  if (/\bupstairs\b/.test(lower) && /\bsecond\s+door\s+on\s+the\s+left\b/.test(lower)) add("rented room upstairs, second door on the left");
  else if (/\bsecond\s+door\s+on\s+the\s+left\b/.test(lower)) add("second door on the left");
  else if (/\brented room\b|\byour room\b/.test(lower)) {
    if (/\bupstairs\b/.test(lower)) add("rented room upstairs");
    if (/\bbasement\b/.test(lower)) add("rented room basement");
  }
  return facts.slice(0, 8);
},

captureNamedLocationLayout(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  let labels = this.extractNamedLocationLabels(raw);
  const scene = root.world && root.world.currentScene ? root.world.currentScene : {};

  if (!labels.length && scene.parentLocationRef && root.world.locationsById && root.world.locationsById[scene.parentLocationRef]) {
    const name = root.world.locationsById[scene.parentLocationRef].identity && root.world.locationsById[scene.parentLocationRef].identity.displayName;
    if (name && /^The\s+[A-Z]/.test(name)) labels.push(name);
  }

  const globalFacts = this.extractLayoutFacts(raw);
  if (!labels.length && !globalFacts.length) return 0;

  const lowerRaw = raw.toLowerCase();
  const labelSpans = labels.map((label, index) => ({
    label,
    index,
    start: lowerRaw.indexOf(String(label || "").toLowerCase())
  })).filter(row => row.start >= 0).sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);

  let changes = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < labels.length; i += 1) {
    const label = labels[i];
    const rec = this.ensureNamedLocationRecord(label, root);
    if (!rec) continue;

    const spanPos = labelSpans.findIndex(row => row.label === label);
    let segment = raw;
    if (spanPos >= 0) {
      const start = labelSpans[spanPos].start;
      const next = spanPos + 1 < labelSpans.length ? labelSpans[spanPos + 1].start : -1;
      segment = raw.slice(start, next > start ? next : Math.min(raw.length, start + 190));
    }

    const facts = labels.length > 1 ? this.extractLayoutFacts(segment) : globalFacts;
    if (!facts.length) continue;

    const locRef = "location_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(rec.displayName);
    const loc = root.world.locationsById[locRef];

    for (let f = 0; f < facts.length; f += 1) {
      const fact = facts[f];
      const isRoomAssignment = /\brented room\b|\bsecond door on the left\b/.test(fact);
      if (isRoomAssignment) {
        if (!rec.roomAssignment) {
          rec.roomAssignment = fact;
          changes += 1;
          this.pushMapEvent("Map memory: " + rec.displayName + " room is " + fact + ".", root);
        } else if (rec.roomAssignment !== fact && !this.hasValidatedMapChange(rawText)) {
          changes += this.pushMapEvent("Map contradiction: " + rec.displayName + " room placement preserved as " + rec.roomAssignment + ".", root);
          continue;
        } else if (rec.roomAssignment !== fact) {
          rec.roomAssignment = fact;
          changes += 1;
        }
        if (loc && loc.mutable && !loc.mutable.roomAssignment) loc.mutable.roomAssignment = rec.roomAssignment;
      } else if (rec.layoutFacts.indexOf(fact) === -1) {
        rec.layoutFacts.push(fact);
        while (rec.layoutFacts.length > 12) rec.layoutFacts.shift();
        changes += 1;
        this.pushMapEvent("Map memory: " + rec.displayName + " has " + fact + ".", root);
      }

      if (loc && loc.mutable && Array.isArray(loc.mutable.layoutFacts) && loc.mutable.layoutFacts.indexOf(fact) === -1) {
        loc.mutable.layoutFacts.push(fact);
        while (loc.mutable.layoutFacts.length > 12) loc.mutable.layoutFacts.shift();
      }
    }
  }
  return changes;
},

hasValidatedMapChange(rawText) {
  return /\b(?:moved|relocated|changed rooms|new room|different room|hidden passage|secret door|newly discovered|collapsed|blocked|detour|renovated|rebuilt|rearranged)\b/i.test(String(rawText || ""));
},

extractDungeonRooms(rawText) {
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const checks = [
    { re: /\bBarrow\s+Entry\s+Hall\b|\bEntry\s+Hall\b/i, label: "Barrow Entry Hall" },
    { re: /\bRuined\s+Armory\b|\bArmory\b/i, label: "Ruined Armory" },
    { re: /\bSealed\s+Sanctum\b/i, label: "Sealed Sanctum" },
    { re: /\bBarrow\s+Sanctum\b/i, label: "Barrow Sanctum" },
    { re: /\bSanctum\b/i, label: "Sealed Sanctum" }
  ];
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < checks.length; i += 1) if (checks[i].re.test(raw) && out.indexOf(checks[i].label) === -1) out.push(checks[i].label);
  return out;
},

ensureDungeonLayout(dungeonName, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const map = this.getMapMemory(root);
  if (!map) return null;
  const clean = this.cleanPhrase(dungeonName || "Old Barrow") || "Old Barrow";
  const id = "dungeon_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(clean);
  const turn = root && root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
  if (!map.dungeonLayoutsById[id] || typeof map.dungeonLayoutsById[id] !== "object") {
    map.dungeonLayoutsById[id] = { id, displayName: clean, rooms: [], path: [], adjacency: {}, contradictionNotes: [], firstSeenTurn: turn, lastSeenTurn: turn };
  }
  const layout = map.dungeonLayoutsById[id];
  if (!Array.isArray(layout.rooms)) layout.rooms = [];
  if (!Array.isArray(layout.path)) layout.path = [];
  if (!layout.adjacency || typeof layout.adjacency !== "object") layout.adjacency = {};
  if (!Array.isArray(layout.contradictionNotes)) layout.contradictionNotes = [];
  if (!layout.roomDetailsByRoom || typeof layout.roomDetailsByRoom !== "object" || Array.isArray(layout.roomDetailsByRoom)) layout.roomDetailsByRoom = {};
  layout.lastSeenTurn = turn;
  map.currentDungeonId = id;
  return layout;
},

extractDungeonRoomDetails(rawText) {
  const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
  const details = [];
  const add = (value) => { const clean = this.cleanPhrase(value || ""); if (clean && details.indexOf(clean) === -1) details.push(clean); };
  if (/\bbroken\s+weapon\s+racks?\b/.test(lower)) add("broken weapon rack");
  if (/\bweapon racks?\b/.test(lower)) add("weapon racks");
  if (/\bbone markings?\b|\bbone marks?\b/.test(lower)) add("bone markings");
  if (/\barmory door\b/.test(lower)) add("armory door");
  if (/\bwest wall\b/.test(lower)) add("west wall");
  if (/\bwest wall rack\b|\brack\s+on\s+the\s+west\s+wall\b/.test(lower)) add("west wall rack");
  if (/\bempty\s+shield\s+hook\b/.test(lower)) add("empty shield hook");
  if (/\bshield hook\b/.test(lower)) add("shield hook");
  if (/\bsealed door\b|\bstone door\b/.test(lower)) add("sealed door");
  return details.slice(0, 8);
},

captureDungeonLayout(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const scene = root.world && root.world.currentScene ? root.world.currentScene : {};
  const rawRooms = this.extractDungeonRooms(raw);
  const rooms = this.extractDungeonRooms(raw + " " + (scene.displayName || ""));
  const mentionsOldBarrow = /\bOld\s+Barrow\b|\bbarrow\b/i.test(raw) || rooms.length > 0;
  if (!mentionsOldBarrow && !rooms.length) return 0;
  const layout = this.ensureDungeonLayout("Old Barrow", root);
  if (!layout) return 0;
  let changes = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < rooms.length; i += 1) {
    const room = rooms[i];
    if (layout.rooms.indexOf(room) === -1) { layout.rooms.push(room); changes += 1; }
    if (layout.path.indexOf(room) === -1) { layout.path.push(room); changes += 1; }
  }
  const details = this.extractDungeonRoomDetails(raw);
  if (details.length) {
    const map = this.getMapMemory(root);
    const detailRoom = rawRooms.length ? rawRooms[rawRooms.length - 1] : (map.lastDungeonRoomId || layout.path[layout.path.length - 1] || "general");
    if (!layout.roomDetailsByRoom[detailRoom]) layout.roomDetailsByRoom[detailRoom] = [];
    for (let d = 0; d < details.length; d += 1) {
      if (layout.roomDetailsByRoom[detailRoom].indexOf(details[d]) === -1) {
        layout.roomDetailsByRoom[detailRoom].push(details[d]);
        while (layout.roomDetailsByRoom[detailRoom].length > 10) layout.roomDetailsByRoom[detailRoom].shift();
        changes += 1;
      }
    }
  }
  const pathSource = rooms.length >= 2 ? rooms : layout.path;
  for (let i = 0; i < pathSource.length - 1; i += 1) {
    const a = pathSource[i], b = pathSource[i + 1];
    if (!layout.adjacency[a]) layout.adjacency[a] = [];
    if (!layout.adjacency[b]) layout.adjacency[b] = [];
    if (layout.adjacency[a].indexOf(b) === -1) { layout.adjacency[a].push(b); changes += 1; }
    if (layout.adjacency[b].indexOf(a) === -1) { layout.adjacency[b].push(a); changes += 1; }
  }
  if (rooms.length === 1) {
    const map = this.getMapMemory(root);
    const last = map.lastDungeonRoomId;
    const current = rooms[0];
    if (last && last !== current) {
      if (!layout.adjacency[last]) layout.adjacency[last] = [];
      if (!layout.adjacency[current]) layout.adjacency[current] = [];
      if (layout.adjacency[last].indexOf(current) === -1) { layout.adjacency[last].push(current); changes += 1; }
      if (layout.adjacency[current].indexOf(last) === -1) { layout.adjacency[current].push(last); changes += 1; }
    }
    map.lastDungeonRoomId = current;
  }
  if (changes > 0) this.pushMapEvent("Map memory: " + layout.displayName + " path " + layout.path.slice(0, 5).join(" > ") + ".", root);
  return changes;
},

captureGoalLocationBinding(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const map = this.getMapMemory(root);
  if (!map) return 0;

  const patterns = [
    /\b(?:meet|find|pay|speak\s+with|return\s+to|bring\s+payment\s+to|deliver\s+to)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})?[^.!?]{0,60}\b(?:at|in|inside)\s+(The\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+){0,3}|Old\s+Barrow)\b/gi,
    /\b(?:goal|objective|quest|task|job|contract)\b[^.!?]{0,100}\b(?:at|in|inside|to)\s+(The\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+){0,3}|Old\s+Barrow)\b/gi,
    /\b(?:contact|merchant|informant|rendezvous)\b[^.!?]{0,80}\b(?:at|in|inside)\s+(The\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+){0,3}|Old\s+Barrow)\b/gi,
    /\breturn\s+to\s+your\s+rented\s+room\b[^.!?]{0,80}\b(?:at|in|inside)\s+(The\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+){0,3})\b/gi,
    /\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})\s+(?:waits|is\s+waiting|expects\s+you|will\s+meet\s+you|can\s+be\s+found)\b[^.!?]{0,60}\b(?:at|in|inside)\s+(The\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+){0,3}|Old\s+Barrow)\b/gi,
    /\b(?:payment|debt|coin|coins|reward)\b[^.!?]{0,50}\b(?:for|to)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})\b[^.!?]{0,60}\b(?:at|in|inside)\s+(The\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+){0,3}|Old\s+Barrow)\b/gi
  ];

  let changes = 0;
  const seen = {};
  for (let p = 0; p < patterns.length; p += 1) {
    let match;
    while ((match = patterns[p].exec(raw))) {
      const maybeActor = match[1] && !/^The\s+/i.test(match[1]) && !/^Old\s+Barrow$/i.test(match[1]) ? this.cleanPhrase(match[1]) : "";
      const rawPlace = String(match[2] || match[1] || "").replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
      const place = /^The\s+[A-Z]/.test(rawPlace) ? rawPlace : this.cleanPhrase(rawPlace);
      if (!place) continue;
      const goalLabel = maybeActor ? ("meet/pay " + maybeActor) : this.cleanPhrase(match[0]).slice(0, 80);
      const id = "goal_location_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(goalLabel + "_" + place);
      if (seen[id]) continue;
      seen[id] = true;
      if (!map.goalBindingsById[id]) {
        map.goalBindingsById[id] = {
          id,
          goal: goalLabel,
          locationName: place,
          locationRef: "location_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(place),
          firstSeenTurn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0
        };
        changes += 1;
        this.pushMapEvent("Goal location: " + goalLabel + " at " + place + ".", root);
      }
      const loc = /^The\s+/.test(place) ? this.ensureNamedLocationRecord(place, root) : null;
      if (loc && loc.goalRefs.indexOf(id) === -1) loc.goalRefs.push(id);
    }
  }
  return changes;
},

detectMapContradictions(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const map = this.getMapMemory(root);
  if (!map) return 0;
  let changes = 0;
  const layouts = map.dungeonLayoutsById || {};
  const dungeonIds = Object.keys(layouts);
  for (let i = 0; i < dungeonIds.length; i += 1) {
    const layout = layouts[dungeonIds[i]];
    if (!layout || !Array.isArray(layout.rooms) || layout.rooms.length < 3) continue;
    if (/\b(?:crystal observatory|flooded chapel|fourth chamber|new chamber|extra chamber|different armory)\b/i.test(raw) && !this.hasValidatedMapChange(raw)) {
      const note = "Map contradiction: unvalidated dungeon layout change rejected for " + layout.displayName + ".";
      if (layout.contradictionNotes.indexOf(note) === -1) layout.contradictionNotes.push(note);
      changes += this.pushMapEvent(note, root);
    }
  }
  const namedIds = Object.keys(map.namedLocationsById || {});
  for (let i = 0; i < namedIds.length; i += 1) {
    const rec = map.namedLocationsById[namedIds[i]];
    if (!rec || !rec.roomAssignment) continue;
    if (new RegExp("\\b" + this.escapeRegExp(rec.displayName) + "\\b", "i").test(raw) || /\byour\s+(?:rented\s+)?room\b/i.test(raw)) {
      if (/\b(?:basement|attic|third floor|back hall|different wing)\b/i.test(raw) && !this.hasValidatedMapChange(raw)) changes += this.pushMapEvent("Map contradiction: " + rec.displayName + " room remains " + rec.roomAssignment + ".", root);
      if (/\bbar\s+(?:is\s+)?(?:now\s+)?on\s+the\s+west\s+wall\b/i.test(raw) && rec.layoutFacts.indexOf("east-wall bar") !== -1 && !this.hasValidatedMapChange(raw)) changes += this.pushMapEvent("Map contradiction: " + rec.displayName + " bar remains east-wall.", root);
    }
  }
  return changes;
},

escapeRegExp(value) {
  return String(value || "").replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
},

getMapContextSummary(root = state.aidrpg, mode = "normal") {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
  this.init(root);
  const map = root.world && root.world.mapMemory ? root.world.mapMemory : null;
  if (!map) return "";
  const parts = [];

  const currentDungeon = map.currentDungeonId && map.dungeonLayoutsById ? map.dungeonLayoutsById[map.currentDungeonId] : null;
  if (currentDungeon && Array.isArray(currentDungeon.path) && currentDungeon.path.length) {
    const path = currentDungeon.path.slice(0, mode === "tiny" ? 3 : 6);
    const rev = path.slice().reverse();
    let dungeonText = "Dungeon " + currentDungeon.displayName + ": " + path.join(" > ");
    if (path.length > 1) dungeonText += " | backtrack " + rev.join(" > ");
    if (currentDungeon.roomDetailsByRoom && typeof currentDungeon.roomDetailsByRoom === "object") {
      const detailBits = [];
      const rooms = Object.keys(currentDungeon.roomDetailsByRoom);
      for (let i = 0; i < rooms.length && detailBits.length < 4; i += 1) {
        const list = currentDungeon.roomDetailsByRoom[rooms[i]];
        if (Array.isArray(list) && list.length) detailBits.push(rooms[i] + " has " + list.slice(-6).join(", "));
      }
      if (detailBits.length) dungeonText += " | " + detailBits.join("; ");
