    for (let i = 0; i < cleaned.length; i += 1) {
      targetArray.push(cleaned[i]);
    }

    return 1;
  },

  refreshCache(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";

    this.init(root);

    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
      root.cache = {};
    }

    const summary = this.getCompactSummary(root);
    root.cache.timeSummary = summary;

    return summary;
  },

  getCompactSummary(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || !root.time || !root.time.current) {
      return "Time unknown.";
    }

    const current = root.time.current;
    const parts = [];

    parts.push("Day " + current.day);
    parts.push(this.pad2(current.hour) + ":" + this.pad2(current.minute));

    if (current.season) {
      parts.push("Season: " + current.season);
    }

    if (current.weather) {
      parts.push("Weather: " + current.weather);
    }

    if (root.time.routes && root.time.routes.knownTravelTimes) {
      const routeRows = [];
      const seen = {};
      const keys = Object.keys(root.time.routes.knownTravelTimes);
      for (let i = 0; i < keys.length && routeRows.length < 4; i += 1) {
        const row = root.time.routes.knownTravelTimes[keys[i]];
        if (!row || typeof row.minutes !== "number") continue;
        const canon = this.makeCanonicalRouteKey(row.fromRef, row.toRef);
        if (seen[canon]) continue;
        seen[canon] = true;
        routeRows.push(this.formatRouteLabel(row.fromRef, row.toRef, root) + " " + row.minutes + "m base" + (row.lastDelayReason && row.lastActualMinutes && row.lastActualMinutes > row.minutes ? " (last " + row.lastActualMinutes + "m due to " + row.lastDelayReason + ")" : ""));
      }
      if (routeRows.length) parts.push("Routes: " + routeRows.join("; "));
    }

    if (root.time.routes && Array.isArray(root.time.routes.recentRouteEvents) && root.time.routes.recentRouteEvents.length) {
      const recentRoutes = [];
      const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
      for (let i = root.time.routes.recentRouteEvents.length - 1; i >= 0 && recentRoutes.length < 2; i -= 1) {
        const row = root.time.routes.recentRouteEvents[i] || {};
        const rowTurn = typeof row.turn === "number" ? row.turn : turn;
        if (turn - rowTurn > 10) continue;
        if (row.message) recentRoutes.push(String(row.message).slice(0, 120));
      }
      if (recentRoutes.length) parts.push("Route notes: " + recentRoutes.join(" | "));
    }

    if (root.time.contradictionFlags && root.time.contradictionFlags.length) {
      parts.push("Flags: " + root.time.contradictionFlags.join(", "));
    }

    return parts.join(" | ");
  },

  pad2(value) {
    const n = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(value || 0)));
    return n < 10 ? "0" + n : String(n);
  }
},

    // === ABILITY SYSTEM ===
AbilitySystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.abilities || typeof root.abilities !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.abilities)) {
      root.abilities = {};
    }

    if (!root.pending || typeof root.pending !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.pending)) {
      root.pending = {};
    }

    const abilities = root.abilities;

    if (!abilities.byId || typeof abilities.byId !== "object" || Array.isArray(abilities.byId)) {
      abilities.byId = {};
    }
    if (!abilities.byKey || typeof abilities.byKey !== "object" || Array.isArray(abilities.byKey)) {
      abilities.byKey = {};
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(abilities.allIds)) abilities.allIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(abilities.skillIds)) abilities.skillIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(abilities.talentIds)) abilities.talentIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(abilities.passiveIds)) abilities.passiveIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(abilities.recentEvents)) abilities.recentEvents = [];

    if (typeof root.pending.abilityCandidate === "undefined") {
      root.pending.abilityCandidate = null;
    }

    return abilities;
  },

  processInput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    this.init(root);

    const intent = root.pending && root.pending.inputIntent ? root.pending.inputIntent : null;
    const candidate = this.extractInputCandidate(rawText, intent);
    root.pending.abilityCandidate = candidate;
    return candidate;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const U = A.Utils;
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  if (!raw.trim()) return 0;

  if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
    if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  let changes = 0;
  const pending = root.pending ? root.pending.abilityCandidate : null;

  // Keep explicit input-driven ability confirmation.
  // This is structured and anchored to player intent, so it is the safe canon path for now.
  if (pending && !this.isClearlyFailedOutput(raw)) {
    changes += this.confirmCandidate(pending, raw, root);
  }

  // Do NOT create brand-new abilities directly from raw output prose here.
  // Raw output-only discovery will return later behind a validated consequence path.
  const revisionCandidates = this.extractRevisionCandidates(raw, root);
  for (let i = 0; i < revisionCandidates.length; i += 1) {
    changes += this.applyRevisionCandidate(revisionCandidates[i], raw, root);
  }

  changes += this.captureDerivedAbilityVariation(raw, root);

  // Existing abilities may still accumulate observed-use notes.
  // These are softer observation updates, not brand-new major canon records.
  changes += this.captureObservedUseSignals(raw, root);

  if (root.pending && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(root.pending, "abilityCandidate")) {
    root.pending.abilityCandidate = null;
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("abilities", "summary", root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
      U.invalidatePackets(root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Ability state updated through locked canon path.");
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

  extractInputCandidate(rawText, intent) {
  if (!intent || typeof intent !== "object") return null;

  const primaryType = String(intent.primaryType || "");
  const raw = String(rawText || "");

  const explicitTrainingInput =
    /^\s*(?:i\s+)?(?:train|practice|drill|exercise|meditate on|spar with|work on)\s+(?:my\s+|the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?/i.test(raw);

  const effectiveType = explicitTrainingInput ? "train" : primaryType;

  if (effectiveType !== "use_ability" && effectiveType !== "train") {
    return null;
  }

  let rawName = "";
  if (effectiveType === "train") {
    rawName = intent.trainingHint || intent.abilityHint || this.extractNamedPhrase(raw);
  } else if (intent.abilityHint) {
    rawName = intent.abilityHint;
  } else {
    rawName = this.extractNamedPhrase(raw);
  }

  const cleanName = this.cleanAbilityName(rawName);
  if (!cleanName) return null;

  const abilityType = this.inferAbilityType(cleanName, raw, intent);
  const intendedFunction = this.inferIntendedFunction(intent, raw);

  return {
    name: cleanName,
    type: abilityType,
    mode: effectiveType === "train" ? "train" : "use",
    confidence: effectiveType === "train" ? 0.74 : 0.68,
    originContext: raw,
    intendedFunction: intendedFunction,
    stableMeaningFlags: ["explicit_input", "structured_record"],
    keyPhrases: this.buildKeyPhrases(cleanName, intent),
    sourceIntentType: effectiveType,
    sourceTags: Array.isArray(intent.tags) ? intent.tags.slice(0, 6) : []
  };
},

  extractOutputCandidates(rawText) {
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const candidates = [];
  const patterns = [
    /\b(?:learn|learns|learned|develop|develops|developed|gain|gains|gained|acquire|acquires|acquired|unlock|unlocks|unlocked|manifest|manifests|manifested|awaken|awakens|awakened)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?(skill|talent|passive|ability)\s+(?:called\s+|known as\s+)?["“]?([^"”\n.,;:!?]{2,60})/gi,
    /\bnew\s+(skill|talent|passive|ability)\s*:\s*["“]?([^"”\n.,;:!?]{2,60})/gi,
    /\b(?:the|this)\s+(skill|talent|passive|ability)\s+(?:is|was)\s+called\s+["“]?([^"”\n.,;:!?]{2,60})/gi
  ];

  for (let p = 0; p < patterns.length; p += 1) {
    let match;
    while ((match = patterns[p].exec(raw))) {
      const typeWord = String(match[1] || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      const rawName = String(match[2] || "").trim();
      const cleanName = this.cleanAbilityName(rawName);
      if (!cleanName) continue;

      candidates.push({
        name: cleanName,
        type: typeWord === "ability" ? "skill" : typeWord,
        mode: "learn",
        confidence: 0.82,
        originContext: raw,
        intendedFunction: this.inferOutputIntendedFunction(raw, cleanName),
        stableMeaningFlags: ["explicit_output", "structured_record", "origin_preserved"],
        keyPhrases: this.buildKeyPhrases(cleanName, null),
        sourceIntentType: "output_discovery",
        sourceTags: this.extractSemanticTags(raw)
      });
    }
  }

  return candidates;
},

  confirmCandidate(candidate, rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
  const abilities = root.abilities;
  const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
  const record = this.findOrCreateRecord(candidate, root);
  if (!record) return 0;

  let changes = 0;
  const evidence = this.makeEvidenceSnippet(rawText || candidate.originContext || "");
  const observedFunction = this.inferOutputIntendedFunction(rawText || candidate.originContext || "", record.identity.name);
  const emergentTags = this.extractSemanticTags(rawText || candidate.originContext || "");

  if (!record.meaning.originContext && candidate.originContext) {
    record.meaning.originContext = String(candidate.originContext);
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (!record.meaning.originSignature) {
    record.meaning.originSignature = this.makeOriginSignature(candidate.originContext || record.identity.name);
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (candidate.intendedFunction && (!record.meaning.intendedFunction || record.meaning.intendedFunction === "general")) {
    record.meaning.intendedFunction = String(candidate.intendedFunction);
    changes += 1;
  }

  changes += this.mergeUnique(record.meaning.stableMeaningFlags, candidate.stableMeaningFlags);
  changes += this.mergeUnique(record.keys.keyPhrases, candidate.keyPhrases);
  changes += this.mergeUnique(record.source.sourceTags, candidate.sourceTags);
  changes += this.mergeUnique(record.observation.semanticTags, emergentTags);

  if (candidate.sourceIntentType && record.source.sourceIntentType !== candidate.sourceIntentType) {
    record.source.sourceIntentType = String(candidate.sourceIntentType);
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (record.observation.lastEvidence !== evidence) {
    record.observation.lastEvidence = evidence;
    changes += 1;
  }

  const nextConfidence = Math.max(Number(record.observation.confidence || 0), Number(candidate.confidence || 0));
  if (record.observation.confidence !== nextConfidence) {
    record.observation.confidence = nextConfidence;
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (record.progression.lastConfirmedTurn !== turn) {
    record.progression.lastConfirmedTurn = turn;
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (candidate.mode === "train") {
    record.progression.trainingCount += 1;
    record.progression.lastTrainedTurn = turn;
    record.progression.xp += 2;
    changes += 1;
  } else if (candidate.mode === "use") {
    record.progression.useCount += 1;
    record.progression.lastUsedTurn = turn;
    record.progression.xp += 1;
    changes += 1;
  } else if (candidate.mode === "learn") {
    record.progression.xp += 3;
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (observedFunction && observedFunction !== "general") {
    changes += this.pushUniqueNote(record.observation.observedFunctions, observedFunction, 8);
    if (record.meaning.intendedFunction && record.meaning.intendedFunction !== observedFunction) {
      changes += this.pushUniqueNote(record.meaning.emergentApplications, observedFunction, 8);
    }
  }

  const nextTier = this.tierFromXp(record.progression.xp);
  if (record.progression.tier !== nextTier) {
    record.progression.tier = nextTier;
    record.progression.masteryState = this.masteryLabelForTier(nextTier);
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    this.pushRecentEvent({
      turn: turn,
      type: record.identity.type,
      name: record.identity.name,
      mode: candidate.mode
    }, abilities);

    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("abilities", record.identity.id, root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Ability updated: " + record.identity.name + " [" + record.identity.type + "]");
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

  findOrCreateRecord(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
  this.init(root);

  const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
  const abilities = root.abilities;
  const normalized = this.normalizeAbilityKey(candidate.name);
  if (!normalized) return null;

  let abilityId = abilities.byKey[normalized] || "";
  if (abilityId && abilities.byId[abilityId]) {
    return abilities.byId[abilityId];
  }

  abilityId = U.makeId(candidate.type || "ability");
  const type = this.normalizeAbilityType(candidate.type);
  const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

  const record = {
    identity: {
      id: abilityId,
      name: String(candidate.name),
      type: type
    },
    keys: {
      normalizedName: normalized,
      aliases: [],
      keyPhrases: []
    },
    meaning: {
      originContext: "",
      originSignature: "",
      intendedFunction: String(candidate.intendedFunction || "general"),
      stableMeaningFlags: [],
      emergentApplications: []
    },
    progression: {
      tier: 1,
      xp: 0,
      useCount: 0,
      trainingCount: 0,
      masteryState: "novice",
      lastConfirmedTurn: turn,
      lastUsedTurn: 0,
      lastTrainedTurn: 0
    },
    observation: {
      confidence: Number(candidate.confidence || 0.55),
      lastEvidence: "",
      notes: [],
      observedFunctions: [],
      semanticTags: []
    },
    source: {
      firstSeenTurn: turn,
      sourceIntentType: "",
      sourceTags: []
    }
  };

  abilities.byId[abilityId] = record;
  abilities.byKey[normalized] = abilityId;
  abilities.allIds.push(abilityId);

  if (type === "skill") {
    abilities.skillIds.push(abilityId);
  } else if (type === "talent") {
    abilities.talentIds.push(abilityId);
  } else {
    abilities.passiveIds.push(abilityId);
  }

  return record;
},

  cleanAbilityName(value) {
  const text = String(value || "")
    .replace(/["'`]+/g, "")
    .replace(/^(?:my|the|a|an)\s+/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+(?:on|at|against|toward|towards|with|using|for|into|from|to|by|until|through|across|while|as)\b.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  if (!text) return "";
  if (text.length < 2 || text.length > 48) return "";
  if (text.split(" ").length > 6) return "";

  const lower = text.toLowerCase();
  const banned = new Set([
    "magic",
    "ability",
    "skill",
    "talent",
    "passive",
    "power",
    "attack",
    "move",
    "technique",
    "training",
    "it",
    "that",
    "this"
  ]);

  if (banned.has(lower)) return "";
  return text;
},

  extractNamedPhrase(rawText) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const patterns = [
      /\b(?:cast|channel|invoke|activate|use|unleash|focus|practice|train|drill|work on)\s+(?:my\s+|the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i,
      /["“]([^"”]{2,48})["”]/
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const match = raw.match(patterns[i]);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        return match[1];
      }
    }

    return "";
  },

  inferAbilityType(name, rawText, intent) {
    const lowerName = String(name || "").toLowerCase();
    const lowerRaw = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const primaryType = intent && intent.primaryType ? String(intent.primaryType) : "";

    if (/\b(passive|affinity|resistance|adaptation|instinct|attunement|constitution|tolerance|aura)\b/.test(lowerName) ||
        /\bpassive\b/.test(lowerRaw)) {
      return "passive";
    }

    if (/\b(talent|technique|art|style|form|method|stance)\b/.test(lowerName) ||
        /\btalent\b/.test(lowerRaw)) {
      return "talent";
    }

    if (primaryType === "train" && /\b(passive|talent)\b/.test(lowerRaw)) {
      return /\bpassive\b/.test(lowerRaw) ? "passive" : "talent";
    }

    return "skill";
  },

  normalizeAbilityType(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "skill").toLowerCase().trim();
    if (lower === "talent") return "talent";
    if (lower === "passive") return "passive";
    return "skill";
  },

  normalizeAbilityKey(value) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    return U.toKey(String(value || ""));
  },

  inferIntendedFunction(intent, rawText) {
    if (!intent || typeof intent !== "object") return "general";

    const parts = [];

    if (intent.primaryType === "use_ability") {
      parts.push("activated technique");
    } else if (intent.primaryType === "train") {
      parts.push("trained technique");
    }

    if (intent.targetHint) {
      parts.push("targeting " + String(intent.targetHint));
    }

    if (intent.styleHint) {
      parts.push("style " + String(intent.styleHint));
    }

    if (intent.trainingHint && intent.primaryType === "train") {
      parts.push("focus " + String(intent.trainingHint));
    }

    if (parts.length) {
      return parts.join(" | ");
    }

    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    if (/\b(defend|guard|brace|protect)\b/.test(lower)) return "defensive application";
    if (/\b(move|step|dash|rush|advance|retreat)\b/.test(lower)) return "mobility application";
    if (/\b(attack|strike|hit|slash|stab|shoot)\b/.test(lower)) return "offensive application";
    return "general";
  },

  buildKeyPhrases(name, intent) {
    const values = [];
    const push = function (value) {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!text) return;
      if (values.indexOf(text) === -1) values.push(text);
    };

    push(String(name || ""));
    push(this.normalizeAbilityKey(name));

    if (intent && intent.styleHint) push(intent.styleHint);
    if (intent && intent.targetHint) push(intent.targetHint);

    return values;
  },

  isClearlyFailedOutput(rawText) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    return /\b(fail to|fails to|failed to|cannot|can't|unable to|nothing happens|no effect|fizzles|misfires|does not activate|doesn't activate)\b/.test(lower);
  },

  tierFromXp(xp) {
    const value = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(xp || 0)));
    if (value >= 40) return 5;
    if (value >= 22) return 4;
    if (value >= 10) return 3;
    if (value >= 4) return 2;
    return 1;
  },

  masteryLabelForTier(tier) {
    const value = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(tier || 1)));
    if (value >= 5) return "mastered";
    if (value === 4) return "expert";
    if (value === 3) return "adept";
    if (value === 2) return "practiced";
    return "novice";
  },

  makeEvidenceSnippet(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim()
      .slice(0, 160);
  },

  makeOriginSignature(value) {
  const text = String(value || "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim()
    .slice(0, 120);

  let hash = 2166136261;
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i += 1) {
    hash ^= text.charCodeAt(i);
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 16777619);
    hash >>>= 0;
  }

  return "sig_" + String(hash);
},

inferOutputIntendedFunction(rawText, abilityName) {
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
  const lowerName = String(abilityName || "").toLowerCase();

  if (lowerName && lower.indexOf(lowerName) === -1 && !/\b(skill|talent|passive|ability)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "general";
  }

  if (/\b(block|brace|guard|shield|ward|protect|deflect)\b/.test(lower)) return "defensive application";
  if (/\b(dash|rush|step|blink|move|advance|retreat|evade)\b/.test(lower)) return "mobility application";
  if (/\b(strike|slash|stab|pierce|blast|burn|shock|smash|attack|hit)\b/.test(lower)) return "offensive application";
  if (/\b(scan|sense|track|inspect|read|detect|locate|survey)\b/.test(lower)) return "detection application";
  if (/\b(train|practice|drill|repeat|refine|hone)\b/.test(lower)) return "training application";
  if (/\b(endure|withstand|resist|stabilize|recover|heal)\b/.test(lower)) return "sustainment application";

  return "general";
},

extractSemanticTags(rawText) {
  const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
  const tags = [];

  const push = function (value) {
    if (tags.indexOf(value) === -1) tags.push(value);
  };

  if (/\bfire|burn|flame|ember|heat\b/.test(lower)) push("fire");
  if (/\bice|frost|cold|freeze\b/.test(lower)) push("ice");
  if (/\blightning|storm|shock|thunder\b/.test(lower)) push("lightning");
  if (/\bshadow|dark|veil|obscure\b/.test(lower)) push("shadow");
  if (/\blight|radiant|glow|holy\b/.test(lower)) push("light");
  if (/\bguard|block|brace|shield|ward\b/.test(lower)) push("defense");
  if (/\bdash|rush|evade|retreat|advance|blink\b/.test(lower)) push("mobility");
  if (/\bscan|detect|sense|track|survey|inspect\b/.test(lower)) push("detection");
  if (/\bheal|recover|restore|stabilize\b/.test(lower)) push("recovery");
  if (/\bweapon|blade|spear|arrow|bow|hammer|fist\b/.test(lower)) push("martial");

  return tags;
},


captureDerivedAbilityVariation(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  if (!raw.trim()) return 0;

  const patterns = [
    /\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}){0,3})\s+(?:becomes|became|forms?|formed|turns?\s+into|turned\s+into|expands?\s+into|expanded\s+into|shifts?\s+into|shifted\s+into|surges?\s+into|surged\s+into)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}){0,3})\b[^.!?]{0,160}/i,
    /\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}){0,3})\s+(?:becomes|became|forms?|formed|turns?\s+into|turned\s+into|expands?\s+into|expanded\s+into|shifts?\s+into|shifted\s+into)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([a-z][a-z'’-]{2,30}(?:\s+[a-z][a-z'’-]{2,30}){0,3})\b[^.!?]{0,160}/i
  ];

  let match = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
    match = raw.match(patterns[i]);
    if (match) break;
  }
  if (!match || !match[1] || !match[2]) return 0;

  const sourceName = this.cleanAbilityName(match[1]);
  let variationName = this.cleanAbilityName(match[2]);
  if (!sourceName || !variationName) return 0;
  if (variationName.split(/\s+/).length === 1 && sourceName.split(/\s+/).length > 1) {
    const sourceFirst = sourceName.split(/\s+/)[0];
    if (!new RegExp("^" + sourceFirst + "$", "i").test(variationName)) variationName = sourceFirst + " " + variationName;
  }
  if (variationName.toLowerCase() === sourceName.toLowerCase()) return 0;

  const abilities = root.abilities || {};
  const sourceKey = this.normalizeAbilityKey(sourceName);
  const sourceId = abilities.byKey ? abilities.byKey[sourceKey] : "";
  const sourceRecord = sourceId && abilities.byId ? abilities.byId[sourceId] : null;

  // Keep the hallucination gate: output-only variations hard-save only when they clearly derive from an existing ability.
  if (!sourceRecord) return 0;

  const candidate = {
    name: variationName,
    type: sourceRecord.identity && sourceRecord.identity.type ? sourceRecord.identity.type : "skill",
    mode: "learn",
    confidence: 0.78,
    originContext: raw,
    intendedFunction: this.inferOutputIntendedFunction(raw, variationName),
    stableMeaningFlags: ["derived_variation", "source_ability_preserved", "origin_preserved"],
    keyPhrases: [variationName, sourceName, this.normalizeAbilityKey(variationName)],
    sourceIntentType: "ability_variation",
    sourceTags: this.extractSemanticTags(raw)
  };

  const record = this.findOrCreateRecord(candidate, root);
  if (!record || !record.identity) return 0;

  let changes = this.confirmCandidate(candidate, raw, root);
  if (!record.meaning || typeof record.meaning !== "object") record.meaning = {};
  if (record.meaning.sourceAbilityRef !== sourceRecord.identity.id) { record.meaning.sourceAbilityRef = sourceRecord.identity.id; changes += 1; }
  if (record.meaning.sourceAbilityName !== sourceRecord.identity.name) { record.meaning.sourceAbilityName = sourceRecord.identity.name; changes += 1; }

  const reason = /\bprotect(?:ing)?\s+(?:allies|the\s+team|companions|party)\b/i.test(raw)
    ? "formed through ally/team protection"
    : /\bpractice|training|trained|refined|adapted\b/i.test(raw)
    ? "formed through practice/adaptation"
    : "formed as a derived application";
  if (record.meaning.variationReason !== reason) { record.meaning.variationReason = reason; changes += 1; }

  const limits = [];
  if (/\bcosts?\s+more\s+mana\b/i.test(raw)) limits.push("costs more mana");
  if (/\bweaker\s+against\s+([^.!?;,]+)/i.test(raw)) limits.push("weaker against " + raw.match(/\bweaker\s+against\s+([^.!?;,]+)/i)[1].trim());
  if (limits.length) {
    if (!Array.isArray(record.meaning.limitations)) record.meaning.limitations = [];
    changes += this.mergeUnique(record.meaning.limitations, limits);
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") U.markDirty("abilities", record.identity.id, root);
    if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") U.invalidatePackets(root);
    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") U.logEvent("Ability variation: " + variationName + " from " + sourceName + ".");
  }

  return changes;
},

extractRevisionCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return [];
  this.init(root);

  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
  const abilities = root.abilities || {};
  const ids = Array.isArray(abilities.allIds) ? abilities.allIds : [];
  const candidates = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i += 1) {
    const record = abilities.byId[ids[i]];
    if (!record || !record.identity) continue;

    const name = String(record.identity.name || "");
    const lowerName = name.toLowerCase();
    if (!lowerName) continue;
    if (lower.indexOf(lowerName) === -1) continue;

    const observedFunction = this.inferOutputIntendedFunction(raw, name);
    const semanticTags = this.extractSemanticTags(raw);

    candidates.push({
      abilityId: record.identity.id,
      observedFunction: observedFunction,
      semanticTags: semanticTags,
      evidence: this.makeEvidenceSnippet(raw)
    });
  }

  return candidates;
},

applyRevisionCandidate(candidate, rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const abilities = root.abilities || {};
  const record = abilities.byId && candidate.abilityId ? abilities.byId[candidate.abilityId] : null;
  if (!record) return 0;

  let changes = 0;

  if (candidate.observedFunction && candidate.observedFunction !== "general") {
    changes += this.pushUniqueNote(record.observation.observedFunctions, candidate.observedFunction, 8);

    if (record.meaning.intendedFunction && record.meaning.intendedFunction !== candidate.observedFunction) {
      changes += this.pushUniqueNote(record.meaning.emergentApplications, candidate.observedFunction, 8);
      changes += this.mergeUnique(record.meaning.stableMeaningFlags, ["emergent_application_seen"]);
    }
  }

  if (Array.isArray(candidate.semanticTags)) {
    changes += this.mergeUnique(record.observation.semanticTags, candidate.semanticTags);
  }

  if (candidate.evidence && record.observation.lastEvidence !== candidate.evidence) {
    record.observation.lastEvidence = candidate.evidence;
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    if (record.observation.lastObservedTurn !== turn) {
      record.observation.lastObservedTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

captureObservedUseSignals(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const abilities = root.abilities || {};
  const ids = Array.isArray(abilities.allIds) ? abilities.allIds : [];
  let changes = 0;

  const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i += 1) {
    const record = abilities.byId[ids[i]];
    if (!record || !record.identity) continue;

    const name = String(record.identity.name || "").toLowerCase();
    if (!name || raw.toLowerCase().indexOf(name) === -1) continue;

    let localChanges = 0;
    const fn = this.inferOutputIntendedFunction(raw, name);
    if (fn && fn !== "general") {
      localChanges += this.pushUniqueNote(record.observation.observedFunctions, fn, 8);
    }

    localChanges += this.mergeUnique(record.observation.semanticTags, this.extractSemanticTags(raw));

    if (localChanges > 0) {
      if (record.observation.lastObservedTurn !== turn) {
        record.observation.lastObservedTurn = turn;
        localChanges += 1;
      }
      changes += localChanges;
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

pushUniqueNote(list, value, maxEntries) {
  if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
  const text = String(value || "").trim();
  if (!text) return 0;
  if (list.indexOf(text) !== -1) return 0;

  list.push(text);

  const limit = Math.max(1, Number(maxEntries || 8));
  while (list.length > limit) {
    list.shift();
  }

  return 1;
},

  mergeUnique(target, values) {
    if (!Array.isArray(target) || !Array.isArray(values)) return 0;
    let changes = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < values.length; i += 1) {
      const text = String(values[i] || "").trim();
      if (!text) continue;
      if (target.indexOf(text) === -1) {
        target.push(text);
        changes += 1;
      }
    }
    return changes;
  },

  pushRecentEvent(eventRecord, abilities) {
    if (!abilities || !Array.isArray(abilities.recentEvents)) return;
    abilities.recentEvents.push(eventRecord);
    while (abilities.recentEvents.length > 12) {
      abilities.recentEvents.shift();
    }
  },

  getRelevantContextHint(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
    this.init(root);

    const pending = root.pending ? root.pending.abilityCandidate : null;
    if (pending && pending.name) {
      const parts = ["Ability focus: " + pending.name, "Type: " + this.normalizeAbilityType(pending.type)];
      if (pending.intendedFunction && pending.intendedFunction !== "general") {
        parts.push("Intent: " + pending.intendedFunction);
      }
      return parts.join(" | ");
    }

    const abilities = root.abilities || {};
    const ids = Array.isArray(abilities.allIds) ? abilities.allIds.slice() : [];
    if (!ids.length) return "";

    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    ids.sort(function (a, b) {
      const left = abilities.byId[a] || {};
      const right = abilities.byId[b] || {};
      const lo = left.observation || {};
      const ro = right.observation || {};
      const lp = left.progression || {};
      const rp = right.progression || {};
      const lt = Math.max(Number(lo.lastObservedTurn || 0), Number(lp.lastConfirmedTurn || 0), Number(lp.lastUsedTurn || 0), Number(lp.lastTrainedTurn || 0));
      const rt = Math.max(Number(ro.lastObservedTurn || 0), Number(rp.lastConfirmedTurn || 0), Number(rp.lastUsedTurn || 0), Number(rp.lastTrainedTurn || 0));
      return rt - lt;
    });

    for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i += 1) {
      const record = abilities.byId[ids[i]];
      if (!record || !record.identity || !record.observation) continue;
      const progression = record.progression || {};
      const recentTurn = Math.max(Number(record.observation.lastObservedTurn || 0), Number(progression.lastConfirmedTurn || 0), Number(progression.lastUsedTurn || 0), Number(progression.lastTrainedTurn || 0));
      if (recentTurn <= 0 || turn - recentTurn > 5) continue;

      const parts = ["Ability update: " + String(record.identity.name || ids[i])];
      const observed = Array.isArray(record.observation.observedFunctions) && record.observation.observedFunctions.length
        ? record.observation.observedFunctions[record.observation.observedFunctions.length - 1]
        : "";
      const emergent = record.meaning && Array.isArray(record.meaning.emergentApplications) && record.meaning.emergentApplications.length
        ? record.meaning.emergentApplications[record.meaning.emergentApplications.length - 1]
        : "";
      if (observed) parts.push(observed);
      if (emergent && emergent !== observed) parts.push("emergent: " + emergent);
      return parts.join(" | ");
    }

    return "";
  },

  getCompactSummary(root = state.aidrpg, maxCount = 2) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
  this.init(root);

  const abilities = root.abilities || {};
  const ids = Array.isArray(abilities.allIds) ? abilities.allIds.slice() : [];
  if (!ids.length) return "";

  ids.sort(function (a, b) {
    const recordA = abilities.byId[a];
    const recordB = abilities.byId[b];
    const turnA = recordA && recordA.progression ? Number(recordA.progression.lastConfirmedTurn || 0) : 0;
    const turnB = recordB && recordB.progression ? Number(recordB.progression.lastConfirmedTurn || 0) : 0;
    return turnB - turnA;
  });

  const names = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < ids.length && names.length < maxCount; i += 1) {
    const record = abilities.byId[ids[i]];
    if (!record || !record.identity || !record.progression) continue;

    const name = String(record.identity.name || "").trim();
    const mastery = String(record.progression.masteryState || "novice").trim();
    if (name) {
      names.push(name + " (" + mastery + ")");
    }
  }

  const parts = [
    "Abilities: " + ids.length,
    "Skills: " + (Array.isArray(abilities.skillIds) ? abilities.skillIds.length : 0),
    "Talents: " + (Array.isArray(abilities.talentIds) ? abilities.talentIds.length : 0),
    "Passives: " + (Array.isArray(abilities.passiveIds) ? abilities.passiveIds.length : 0)
  ];

  if (names.length) {
    parts.push("Recent: " + names.join(", "));
  }

  return parts.join(" | ");
},
},

    // === PERSISTENT TRAIT / CONDITION SYSTEM ===
PersistentTraitSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.player || typeof root.player !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.player)) {
      root.player = {};
    }

    const player = root.player;

    if (!player.conditions || typeof player.conditions !== "object" || Array.isArray(player.conditions)) {
      player.conditions = {};
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.active)) player.conditions.active = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.permanent)) player.conditions.permanent = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.vulnerabilities)) player.conditions.vulnerabilities = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.immunities)) player.conditions.immunities = [];

    if (!player.traitState || typeof player.traitState !== "object" || Array.isArray(player.traitState)) {
      player.traitState = {};
    }

    const traitState = player.traitState;

    if (!traitState.byId || typeof traitState.byId !== "object" || Array.isArray(traitState.byId)) {
      traitState.byId = {};
    }
    if (!traitState.byKey || typeof traitState.byKey !== "object" || Array.isArray(traitState.byKey)) {
      traitState.byKey = {};
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.allIds)) traitState.allIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.conditionIds)) traitState.conditionIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.traitIds)) traitState.traitIds = [];

    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.resistanceTags)) traitState.resistanceTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.immunityTags)) traitState.immunityTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.vulnerabilityTags)) traitState.vulnerabilityTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.adaptationTags)) traitState.adaptationTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.sensitivityTags)) traitState.sensitivityTags = [];

    if (!traitState.exposureByDomain || typeof traitState.exposureByDomain !== "object" || Array.isArray(traitState.exposureByDomain)) {
      traitState.exposureByDomain = {};
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.growthHooks)) traitState.growthHooks = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.recentEvents)) traitState.recentEvents = [];

    return traitState;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const U = A.Utils;
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  if (!raw.trim()) return 0;

  if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
    if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  let changes = 0;

  // Major condition canon now comes from validated consequences only.
  changes += this.applyValidatedConsequences(root);

  // Keep legacy visible arrays synchronized from the canonical trait/condition records.
  changes += this.syncLegacyPlayerArrays(root);

  // Allow low-risk exposure tracking only when it is anchored to already-validated player evidence.
  changes += this.captureValidatedExposureSignals(root);

  if (changes > 0) {
    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("player", true, root);
    }
    if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
      U.invalidatePackets(root);
    }
    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Persistent trait state updated through locked canon path.");
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

applyValidatedConsequences(root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const Guard = A.OperationGuard;
  const pending = root.pending || {};
  const validated = Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences) ? pending.validatedConsequences : [];
  let changes = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < validated.length; i += 1) {
    const candidate = validated[i];
    if (!candidate || candidate.category !== "player" || candidate._traitApplied === true) {
      continue;
    }

    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const status = String(payload.status || "").trim();

    const commit = () => {
      let localChanges = 0;

      if (candidate.type === "status_gain" && status) {
        localChanges += this.applyConditionCandidate({
          name: status,
          mode: "active",
          severity: this.validationSeverityForStatus(status)
        }, candidate.evidence || status, root);
      } else if (candidate.type === "status_loss" && status) {
        localChanges += this.removeConditionByName(status, root);
      }

      return localChanges;
    };

    const localChanges =
      Guard && typeof Guard.applyOnce === "function"
        ? Guard.applyOnce({
            kind: "trait_" + String(candidate.type || "change"),
            target: status || String(candidate.type || "trait"),
            payload: payload,
            sourceId: candidate.id || ""
          }, commit, root)
        : commit();

    changes += localChanges;
    candidate._traitApplied = true;
  }

  return changes;
},

captureValidatedExposureSignals(root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const pending = root.pending || {};
  const validated = Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences) ? pending.validatedConsequences : [];
  const textParts = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < validated.length; i += 1) {
    const candidate = validated[i];
    if (!candidate || candidate.category !== "player") continue;

    const payload = candidate.payload || {};

    if (typeof candidate.evidence === "string" && candidate.evidence.trim()) {
      textParts.push(candidate.evidence.trim());
    }

    if (typeof payload.status === "string" && payload.status.trim()) {
      textParts.push(payload.status.trim());
    }

    if (typeof payload.injuryTag === "string" && payload.injuryTag.trim()) {
      textParts.push(payload.injuryTag.trim());
    }

    if (typeof payload.bodyPart === "string" && payload.bodyPart.trim()) {
      textParts.push(payload.bodyPart.trim());
    }
  }

  if (!textParts.length) return 0;
  return this.captureExposureSignals(textParts.join(" | "), root);
},

validationSeverityForStatus(status) {
  const lower = String(status || "").toLowerCase();
  if (lower === "burned" || lower === "bleeding" || lower === "poisoned" || lower === "infected") {
    return "severe";
  }
  if (lower === "chilled" || lower === "soaked" || lower === "fatigued" || lower === "hungry") {
    return "moderate";
  }
  return "moderate";
},

  validationSeverityForStatus(status) {
  const lower = String(status || "").toLowerCase();

  if (
    lower === "burned" ||
    lower === "bleeding" ||
    lower === "poisoned" ||
    lower === "infected" ||
    lower === "exhausted"
  ) {
    return "severe";
  }

  if (
    lower === "chilled" ||
    lower === "soaked" ||
    lower === "fatigued" ||
    lower === "hungry" ||
    lower === "weakened"
  ) {
    return "moderate";
  }

  return "moderate";
},

  extractConditionRemovals(rawText) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const removals = [];
    const names = [
      "bleeding",
      "poisoned",
      "burned",
      "chilled",
      "frozen",
      "exhausted",
      "fatigued",
      "hungry",
      "soaked",
      "cursed",
      "diseased",
      "stunned",
      "dazed",
      "blinded",
      "deafened",
      "weakened",
      "infected"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < names.length; i += 1) {
      const name = names[i];
      const re = new RegExp(
        "\\b(?:no longer|without|free of|recovered from|recovers from|recovers from|cured of|cure[d]? of|ends|ended|fades|faded|stops|stopped|lifts|lifted|clears|cleared)\\b[^.!?]{0,30}\\b" + name + "\\b|\\b" + name + "\\b[^.!?]{0,20}\\b(?:ends|ended|fades|faded|stops|stopped|lifts|lifted|clears|cleared)\\b",
        "i"
      );

      if (re.test(raw)) {
        removals.push(name);
      }
    }

    return removals;
  },

  extractTraitCandidates(rawText) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const candidates = [];
    const patterns = [
      { category: "immunity", re: /\b(?:immune|immunity)\s+to\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\b/gi },
      { category: "resistance", re: /\b(?:resistant|resistance)\s+to\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\b/gi },
      { category: "vulnerability", re: /\b(?:vulnerable|susceptible|weak)\s+to\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\b/gi },
      { category: "adaptation", re: /\b(?:adapted|acclimated|accustomed|tolerant)\s+to\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\b/gi },
      { category: "sensitivity", re: /\b(?:sensitive)\s+to\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\b/gi },
      { category: "resistance", re: /\bresists?\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\b/gi }
    ];

    for (let p = 0; p < patterns.length; p += 1) {
      let match;
      while ((match = patterns[p].re.exec(raw))) {
        const domain = this.cleanDomainName(match[1]);
        if (!domain) continue;

        candidates.push({
          category: patterns[p].category,
          domain: domain
        });
      }
    }

    return candidates;
  },

  inferConditionMode(rawText, name) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const lowerName = String(name || "").toLowerCase();

    const permanentRe = new RegExp("\\b(?:permanent|chronic|lingering|lasting|lasting permanently|won't heal|will not heal)\\b[^.!?]{0,24}\\b" + lowerName + "\\b|\\b" + lowerName + "\\b[^.!?]{0,24}\\b(?:permanent|chronic|lingering|lasting)\\b", "i");
    if (permanentRe.test(raw)) return "permanent";

    return "active";
  },

  inferSeverity(rawText, name) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const lowerName = String(name || "").toLowerCase();

    const severeRe = new RegExp("\\b(?:severe|serious|deep|major|critical|grave)\\b[^.!?]{0,18}\\b" + lowerName + "\\b|\\b" + lowerName + "\\b[^.!?]{0,18}\\b(?:severe|serious|deep|major|critical|grave)\\b", "i");
    if (severeRe.test(raw)) return "severe";

    const minorRe = new RegExp("\\b(?:light|minor|slight|faint)\\b[^.!?]{0,18}\\b" + lowerName + "\\b|\\b" + lowerName + "\\b[^.!?]{0,18}\\b(?:light|minor|slight|faint)\\b", "i");
    if (minorRe.test(raw)) return "minor";

    return "moderate";
  },

  cleanDomainName(value) {
    const text = String(value || "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/^(?:the|an|a)\s+/i, "")
      .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();

    if (!text) return "";
    if (text.length > 24) return "";
    if (/\d/.test(text)) return "";
    if (text.split(" ").length > 3) return "";

    const banned = new Set([
      "it",
      "them",
      "that",
      "this",
      "everything",
      "anything"
    ]);

    if (banned.has(text)) return "";
    return text;
  },

  makeRecordName(category, domain) {
    const cleanDomain = this.cleanDomainName(domain);
    if (!cleanDomain) return "";

    if (category === "adaptation") return cleanDomain + " acclimation";
    if (category === "sensitivity") return cleanDomain + " sensitivity";
    if (category === "immunity") return cleanDomain + " immunity";
    if (category === "vulnerability") return cleanDomain + " vulnerability";
    return cleanDomain + " resistance";
  },

  findOrCreateRecord(name, kind, category, root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const traitState = root.player.traitState;
    const normalized = U.toKey(String(name || ""));
    if (!normalized) return null;

    let recordId = traitState.byKey[normalized] || "";
    if (recordId && traitState.byId[recordId]) {
      return traitState.byId[recordId];
    }

    recordId = U.makeId(kind === "trait" ? "trait" : "condition");
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

    const record = {
      identity: {
        id: recordId,
        name: String(name || ""),
        kind: kind === "trait" ? "trait" : "condition",
        category: String(category || (kind === "trait" ? "trait" : "condition"))
      },
      state: {
        mode: "active",
        severity: "moderate",
        stage: 1,
        startedTurn: turn,
        lastConfirmedTurn: turn,
        lastChangedTurn: turn
      },
      meaning: {
        originContext: "",
        stableFlags: [],
        domain: ""
      },
      progression: {
        exposureCount: 0,
        adaptationScore: 0,
        growthHooks: []
      }
    };

    traitState.byId[recordId] = record;
    traitState.byKey[normalized] = recordId;
    traitState.allIds.push(recordId);

    if (kind === "trait") {
      traitState.traitIds.push(recordId);
    } else {
      traitState.conditionIds.push(recordId);
    }

    return record;
  },

  applyConditionCandidate(candidate, rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const record = this.findOrCreateRecord(candidate.name, "condition", "condition", root);
    if (!record) return 0;

    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    let changes = 0;

    if (record.state.mode !== String(candidate.mode || "active")) {
      record.state.mode = String(candidate.mode || "active");
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.state.severity !== String(candidate.severity || "moderate")) {
      record.state.severity = String(candidate.severity || "moderate");
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.state.lastConfirmedTurn !== turn) {
      record.state.lastConfirmedTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.state.lastChangedTurn !== turn) {
      record.state.lastChangedTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const evidence = this.makeEvidenceSnippet(rawText);
    if (record.meaning.originContext !== evidence) {
      record.meaning.originContext = evidence;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.identity.category !== "condition") {
      record.identity.category = "condition";
      changes += 1;
    }

    return changes;
  },

  removeConditionByName(name, root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const traitState = root.player.traitState;
    const key = U.toKey(String(name || ""));
    if (!key) return 0;

    const recordId = traitState.byKey[key];
    if (!recordId || !traitState.byId[recordId]) return 0;

    const record = traitState.byId[recordId];
    if (record.identity.kind !== "condition") return 0;

    delete traitState.byId[recordId];
    delete traitState.byKey[key];

    this.removeValueLocal(traitState.allIds, recordId);
    this.removeValueLocal(traitState.conditionIds, recordId);

    return 1;
  },

  applyTraitCandidate(candidate, rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const traitState = root.player.traitState;
    const name = this.makeRecordName(candidate.category, candidate.domain);
    if (!name) return 0;

    const record = this.findOrCreateRecord(name, "trait", candidate.category, root);
    if (!record) return 0;

    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    let changes = 0;

    if (record.identity.category !== candidate.category) {
      record.identity.category = candidate.category;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.meaning.domain !== candidate.domain) {
      record.meaning.domain = candidate.domain;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.state.mode !== "permanent") {
      record.state.mode = "permanent";
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.state.lastConfirmedTurn !== turn) {
      record.state.lastConfirmedTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.state.lastChangedTurn !== turn) {
      record.state.lastChangedTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const evidence = this.makeEvidenceSnippet(rawText);
    if (record.meaning.originContext !== evidence) {
      record.meaning.originContext = evidence;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (candidate.category === "resistance") {
      changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(traitState.resistanceTags, candidate.domain);
    } else if (candidate.category === "immunity") {
      changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(traitState.immunityTags, candidate.domain);
    } else if (candidate.category === "vulnerability") {
      changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(traitState.vulnerabilityTags, candidate.domain);
    } else if (candidate.category === "adaptation") {
      changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(traitState.adaptationTags, candidate.domain);
    } else if (candidate.category === "sensitivity") {
      changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(traitState.sensitivityTags, candidate.domain);
    }

    return changes;
  },

  captureExposureSignals(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const raw = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const traitState = root.player.traitState;
    const domains = this.extractExposureDomains(raw);
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < domains.length; i += 1) {
      const domain = domains[i];

      if (!traitState.exposureByDomain[domain] || typeof traitState.exposureByDomain[domain] !== "object") {
        traitState.exposureByDomain[domain] = {
          count: 0,
          lastTurn: 0
        };
      }

      traitState.exposureByDomain[domain].count += 1;
      traitState.exposureByDomain[domain].lastTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number"
        ? root.meta.currentTurn
        : 0;
      changes += 1;

      if (traitState.exposureByDomain[domain].count >= 3) {
        changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(traitState.growthHooks, domain + "_adaptation_candidate");
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  extractExposureDomains(rawText) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const found = [];

    const push = function (value) {
      if (found.indexOf(value) === -1) found.push(value);
    };

    if (/\bfire|burn|flame|ember|heat\b/.test(lower)) push("fire");
    if (/\bcold|freeze|frost|ice|chill\b/.test(lower)) push("cold");
    if (/\bpoison|venom|toxic\b/.test(lower)) push("poison");
    if (/\blightning|storm|shock|thunder\b/.test(lower)) push("lightning");
    if (/\bshadow|dark|gloom|veil\b/.test(lower)) push("shadow");
    if (/\blight|radiant|sunlight|holy\b/.test(lower)) push("light");
    if (/\bwater|rain|soaked|drowned|flood\b/.test(lower)) push("water");
    if (/\bdisease|plague|sick|infect\b/.test(lower)) push("disease");
    if (/\brot|decay|wither|blight\b/.test(lower)) push("rot");
    if (/\bmagic|mana|arcane|spell\b/.test(lower)) push("magic");

    return found;
  },

  syncLegacyPlayerArrays(root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const player = root.player;
    const traitState = player.traitState;
    let changes = 0;

    const nextActive = [];
    const nextPermanent = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < traitState.conditionIds.length; i += 1) {
      const record = traitState.byId[traitState.conditionIds[i]];
      if (!record || !record.identity) continue;

      if (record.state.mode === "permanent") {
        nextPermanent.push(record.identity.name);
      } else {
        nextActive.push(record.identity.name);
      }
    }

    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(player.conditions.active, nextActive);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(player.conditions.permanent, nextPermanent);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(player.conditions.vulnerabilities, traitState.vulnerabilityTags);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(player.conditions.immunities, traitState.immunityTags);

    return changes;
  },

  getCompactSummary(root = state.aidrpg, maxCount = 2) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
    this.init(root);

    const player = root.player || {};
    const traitState = player.traitState || {};
    const parts = [];

    const active = Array.isArray(player.conditions.active) ? player.conditions.active.slice(0, maxCount) : [];
    const permanent = Array.isArray(player.conditions.permanent) ? player.conditions.permanent.slice(0, maxCount) : [];
    const resistances = Array.isArray(traitState.resistanceTags) ? traitState.resistanceTags.slice(0, maxCount) : [];
    const adaptations = Array.isArray(traitState.adaptationTags) ? traitState.adaptationTags.slice(0, maxCount) : [];
    const sensitivities = Array.isArray(traitState.sensitivityTags) ? traitState.sensitivityTags.slice(0, maxCount) : [];

    if (active.length) parts.push("Active: " + active.join(", "));
    if (permanent.length) parts.push("Permanent: " + permanent.join(", "));
    if (resistances.length) parts.push("Resist: " + resistances.join(", "));
    if (adaptations.length) parts.push("Adapt: " + adaptations.join(", "));
    if (sensitivities.length) parts.push("Sensitive: " + sensitivities.join(", "));

    return parts.join(" | ");
  },

  makeEvidenceSnippet(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim()
      .slice(0, 140);
  },

  replaceSimpleArray(target, nextValues) {
    if (!Array.isArray(target)) return 0;
    const next = Array.isArray(nextValues) ? nextValues.slice() : [];

    let changed = 0;
    if (target.length !== next.length) {
      changed = 1;
    } else {
      for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i += 1) {
        if (target[i] !== next[i]) {
          changed = 1;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) return 0;

    target.splice(0, target.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < next.length; i += 1) {
      target.push(next[i]);
    }

    return 1;
  },

  pushUniqueLocal(list, value) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
    const text = String(value || "").trim();
    if (!text) return 0;
    if (list.indexOf(text) !== -1) return 0;
    list.push(text);
    return 1;
  },

  removeValueLocal(list, value) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
    const idx = list.indexOf(value);
    if (idx === -1) return 0;
    list.splice(idx, 1);
    return 1;
  }
},

    // === CONSEQUENCE PARSER ===
ConsequenceParser: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.pending || typeof root.pending !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.pending)) {
      root.pending = {};
    }

    const pending = root.pending;

    if (!Array.isArray(pending.outputCandidates)) pending.outputCandidates = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(pending.unresolvedConsequences)) pending.unresolvedConsequences = [];
    if (!pending.lastConsequenceParse || typeof pending.lastConsequenceParse !== "object" || Array.isArray(pending.lastConsequenceParse)) {
      pending.lastConsequenceParse = {};
    }

    return pending;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;
    const pending = root.pending;
    const raw = String(rawText || "");

    pending.outputCandidates = [];
    pending.unresolvedConsequences = [];

    if (!raw.trim()) {
      return 0;
    }

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    const candidates = this.dedupeCandidates(
      []
        .concat(this.extractPlayerCandidates(raw, root))
        .concat(this.extractItemCandidates(raw, root))
        .concat(this.extractWorldCandidates(raw, root))
        .concat(this.extractActorCandidates(raw, root))
        .concat(this.extractTimeCandidates(raw, root))
        .concat(this.extractQuestCandidates(raw, root))
        .concat(this.extractReputationCandidates(raw, root))
    );

    for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i += 1) {
      const candidate = candidates[i];
      if (Number(candidate.confidence || 0) >= 0.72) {
        pending.outputCandidates.push(candidate);
      } else {
        pending.unresolvedConsequences.push(candidate);
      }
    }

    pending.lastConsequenceParse = {
      turn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0,
      candidateCount: candidates.length,
      committedCount: pending.outputCandidates.length,
      unresolvedCount: pending.unresolvedConsequences.length
    };

    if (candidates.length && U && typeof U.logParse === "function") {
      U.logParse(
        "ConsequenceParser: " +
        pending.outputCandidates.length +
        " high-confidence / " +
        pending.unresolvedConsequences.length +
        " tentative"
      );
    }

    return candidates.length;
  },

  extractPlayerCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
  const out = [];

  const add = (type, payload, confidence = 0.76) => {
    out.push(this.makeCandidate("player", type, payload, raw, confidence, root));
  };

  if (/\bno mp left\b|\bno mana left\b|\bout of mana\b|\bspent magic\b|\bmagic is gone\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("resource_state", { resource: "mp", state: "empty" }, 0.84);
  } else if (/\bmp is low\b|\bmana is low\b|\bmagic is low\b|\bnearly empty\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("resource_state", { resource: "mp", state: "low" }, 0.76);
  }

  // v1.0.3: direct HP/health resource wording. This is intentionally
  // resource-state only; actual death still goes through boundary validation.
  if (/\b(?:no hp left|no health left|hp is gone|health is gone|at 0 hp|zero hp|death[’']?s door|at death[’']?s door)\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("resource_state", { resource: "hp", state: "empty" }, 0.86);
  } else if (/\b(?:hp is low|health is low|badly hurt|badly wounded|near death|near-death|barely standing|critically hurt|critical health|health is critical)\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("resource_state", { resource: "hp", state: "low" }, 0.78);
  }

  if (/\bno ep left\b|\bfully exhausted\b|\bcan hardly stand\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("resource_state", { resource: "ep", state: "empty" }, 0.82);
  } else if (/\bep is dwindling\b|\benergy is dwindling\b|\bbreathing is ragged\b|\bexhausted\b|\bfatigued\b|\blegs shake\b|\bbreath gives out\b|\bwinded\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("resource_state", { resource: "ep", state: "low" }, 0.70);
  }

  if (/\bexhausted\b|\bfully exhausted\b|\bcan hardly stand\b|\blegs shake\b[^.!?]{0,40}\bexhausted\b|\bexhausted\b[^.!?]{0,40}\bclimb\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "exhausted" }, 0.78);
  }

  if (/\bfatigued\b|\bweary\b|\bworn out\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "fatigued" }, 0.72);
  }

  if (/\bburned\b|\bblistered\b|\bcharred\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "burned" }, 0.86);
  }

  if (/\bbleeding\b|\blosing blood\b|\bblood runs\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "bleeding" }, 0.84);
  }

  if (/\bpoisoned\b|\bpoisons?\b|\bvenom\b|\btoxins?\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "poisoned" }, 0.82);
  }

  if ((/\bshivering\b|\bchilled\b|\bfreezing\b|\bicy\b/.test(lower)) && /\brain\b|\bcold\b|\bfrost\b|\bice\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "chilled" }, 0.69);
  }

  if (/\bsoaked\b|\bdrenched\b|\brain-soaked\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "soaked" }, 0.71);
  }

  if (/\bno longer\b[^.!?]{0,24}\bburning\b|\bno longer\b[^.!?]{0,24}\bburned\b|\bthe burn fades\b|\bthe fire burned out\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_loss", { status: "burned" }, 0.68);
  }

  if (/\bno longer\b[^.!?]{0,24}\bbleeding\b|\bthe bleeding stops\b|\bblood loss ends\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_loss", { status: "bleeding" }, 0.80);
  }

  if (/\bno longer\b[^.!?]{0,24}\bexhausted\b|\bexhaustion fades\b|\brecovered from exhaustion\b|\bcatch your breath\b[^.!?]{0,30}\bsteady\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_loss", { status: "exhausted" }, 0.70);
  }

  if (/\barm\b[^.!?]{0,18}\bblistered\b|\barm\b[^.!?]{0,18}\bcharred\b|\bburned arm\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: "arm", injuryTag: "burn" }, 0.87);
  }

  if (/\bthroat is raw\b|\bcough\b[^.!?]{0,18}\bpainful\b|\bsmoke\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: "throat", injuryTag: "smoke_irritation" }, 0.66);
  }



  // C25u2 broadened injury/status capture for final adventure stress wording.
  if (/\b(?:flame\s+licks|acid\s+burns?|sears?|angry\s+burn|scalds?|scorches?)\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "burned" }, 0.84);
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: this.extractBodyPart(lower, "arm"), injuryTag: /\bacid\b/.test(lower) ? "acid_burn" : "burn" }, 0.82);
  }

  if (/\b(?:lightning\s+crawls?|shock(?:ed)?|electrified|numb)\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "numb" }, 0.74);
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: this.extractBodyPart(lower, "arm"), injuryTag: /\blightning\b/.test(lower) ? "shock" : "numb" }, 0.75);
  }

  if (/\b(?:ribs?\s+crack|cracked\s+ribs?|rib\s+injury)\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: "ribs", injuryTag: "cracked" }, 0.86);
  }

  if (/\b(?:toxic\s+gas|green\s+mist|poison\s+gas|choke|retch|smoke\s+fills\s+your\s+lungs|smoke\s+inhalation)\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: /\bsmoke\b/.test(lower) ? "lungs" : "throat", injuryTag: /\bsmoke\b/.test(lower) ? "smoke_inhalation" : "toxic_irritation" }, 0.78);
    if (/\btoxic|poison|green\s+mist\b/.test(lower)) add("status_gain", { status: "poisoned" }, 0.76);
  }

  if (/\b(?:freezing|frostbite|cold\s+bites?|icy\s+water)\b/.test(lower) && /\b(?:feet|foot|hands?|fingers?|numb|cold)\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "chilled" }, 0.74);
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: this.extractBodyPart(lower, "feet"), injuryTag: "cold_numbness" }, 0.73);
  }

  if (/\b(?:deep\s+gash|opens?\s+a\s+deep\s+gash|gash|bleeding\s+cut)\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "bleeding" }, 0.82);
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: this.extractBodyPart(lower, "thigh"), injuryTag: "gash" }, 0.82);
  }

  if (/\b(?:bruises?|bruise|bruised)\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: this.extractBodyPart(lower, "shoulder"), injuryTag: "bruise" }, 0.78);
  }

  if (/\b(?:claws?\s+tear|red\s+lines|scratches?|scratch)\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: this.extractBodyPart(lower, "cheek"), injuryTag: "scratch" }, 0.78);
  }

  if (/\b(?:healing\s+(?:potion|vial)|heal\s+bottle|minor\s+healing\s+vial)\b.*\b(?:eases?|closes?|knits?|mends?)\b/.test(lower) || /\b(?:eases?|closes?|knits?|mends?)\b.*\b(?:cracked\s+ribs?|cut|wound)\b/.test(lower)) {
    if (/\bribs?\b/.test(lower)) add("body_injury", { bodyPart: "ribs", injuryTag: "recovering_crack" }, 0.74);
    if (/\bcut|wound|gash\b/.test(lower)) add("body_injury", { bodyPart: this.extractBodyPart(lower, "arm"), injuryTag: "healing_cut" }, 0.74);
    add("status_gain", { status: "recovering" }, 0.70);
  }

  if (/\bsalve\b[^.!?]{0,50}\b(?:cools?|eases?|soothes?)\b[^.!?]{0,30}\bburn\b|\bburn\b[^.!?]{0,50}\b(?:cools?|eases?|soothes?|pain\s+fades?)\b|\bburn\s+pain\s+fades?\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: this.extractBodyPart(lower, "wrist"), injuryTag: "burn_recovering" }, 0.76);
    add("status_gain", { status: "recovering" }, 0.70);
  }

  if (/\b(?:fatigue|weariness)\s+eases?\b|\bno longer\b[^.!?]{0,24}\bfatigued\b|\brested\s+enough\s+to\s+recover\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_loss", { status: "fatigued" }, 0.78);
  }


  // C25u3 generated-suite phrase hardening: extra injury/status variants
  // from final stress testing. These stay player-anchored through validated
  // consequences and do not alter ability/time/scene logic.
  if (/\bneedle\s+(?:pricks?|poisons?)\s+your\s+thumb\b|\bpoison\s+numbs?\s+your\s+hand\b|\bpoison\s+in\s+your\s+(?:hand|thumb)\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "poisoned" }, 0.84);
    add("status_gain", { status: "numb" }, 0.72);
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: /\bthumb\b/.test(lower) ? "thumb" : "hand", injuryTag: "poison_numbness" }, 0.80);
  }

  if (/\blightning\s+snaps?\s+into\s+your\s+shoulder\b|\barm\s+twitching\b|\belectric(?:al)?\s+shock\b|\bshock\s+runs?\s+through\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "shocked" }, 0.78);
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: this.extractBodyPart(lower, "shoulder"), injuryTag: "electric_shock" }, 0.80);
  }

  if (/\bribs?\s+cracked\b|\bcracked\s+ribs?\b|\bevery\s+breath\s+painful\b|\bbreath\s+(?:is\s+)?painful\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: "ribs", injuryTag: "cracked" }, 0.86);
    add("status_gain", { status: "hurt" }, 0.66);
  }

  if (/\bhead\s+cracks?\s+against\b|\broom\s+spins\b|\bvision\s+swims\b|\bconcuss(?:ed|ion)\b|\bdizz(?:y|iness)\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "concussed" }, 0.78);
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: "head", injuryTag: "concussion" }, 0.82);
  }

  if (/\bfrost\s+blackens?\b|\bfrostbite\b|\bfingers?\s+(?:blacken|turn\s+black|go\s+numb)\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "chilled" }, 0.76);
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: this.extractBodyPart(lower, "fingers"), injuryTag: "frostbite" }, 0.80);
  }

  if (/\bacid\s+splashes?\s+across\s+your\s+hand\b|\bacid\s+eats?\s+through\b|\bacid\s+burns?\s+(?:your\s+)?(?:hand|skin|wrist)\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "burned" }, 0.82);
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: this.extractBodyPart(lower, "hand"), injuryTag: "acid_burn" }, 0.84);
  }

  if (/\b(?:ash|sparks?)\b[^.!?]{0,40}\b(?:fill|fills|get\s+in)\s+your\s+eyes\b|\bbriefly\s+blinded\b|\bleaves?\s+you\s+(?:briefly\s+)?blind(?:ed)?\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "blinded" }, 0.76);
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: "eye", injuryTag: "irritated_blindness" }, 0.76);
  }

  if (/\bantidote\b[^.!?]{0,60}\b(?:slows?|stabilizes?|checks?)\s+the\s+poison\b|\bnumbness\s+stops?\s+spreading\b|\bpoison\s+(?:is\s+)?stabiliz(?:ed|ing)\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "poison_stabilized" }, 0.78);
    if (/\bhand\s+is\s+weak\b|\bweak\s+hand\b/.test(lower)) {
      add("body_injury", { bodyPart: "hand", injuryTag: "weak_recovering" }, 0.72);
    }
  }

  // v1.0.3: sustained large ability/project strain. This catches the
  // AI describing a continued Earth Control scale-up without explicitly
  // saying "mana is low". It does not create/learn abilities; it only
  // records resource pressure from sustained effort.
  const sustainedProject =
    /\b(?:keep|keeps|continue|continues|continued|sustained|sustaining|for\s+hours?|hour\s+after\s+hour|ongoing)\b/.test(lower) &&
    /\b(?:earth\s+control|earth|stone|trench|wall|fortress|massive|enormous|huge|large\s+project|project|expands?|expanding|grows?|growing|build(?:ing)?|raise|pulling)\b/.test(lower);
  const severeSustainedProject = sustainedProject && /\b(?:for\s+hours?|massive|enormous|fortress\s+wall|whole\s+wall|huge|hour\s+after\s+hour)\b/.test(lower);
  if (sustainedProject) {
    add("resource_state", { resource: "mp", state: severeSustainedProject ? "empty" : "low" }, severeSustainedProject ? 0.78 : 0.70);
    add("resource_state", { resource: "ep", state: severeSustainedProject ? "empty" : "low" }, severeSustainedProject ? 0.76 : 0.68);
    if (severeSustainedProject) add("status_gain", { status: "exhausted" }, 0.70);
  }

  return out;
},


  extractBodyPart(lowerText, fallback = "body") {
    const lower = String(lowerText || "").toLowerCase();
    const parts = [
      "forearm", "shoulder", "ribs", "rib", "thigh", "cheek", "wrist", "thumb", "fingers", "finger", "arm", "hand", "hands",
      "feet", "foot", "leg", "legs", "lungs", "throat", "chest", "head", "eyes", "eye"
    ];
    for (let i = 0; i < parts.length; i += 1) {
      const part = parts[i];
      if (new RegExp("\\b" + part + "\\b", "i").test(lower)) {
        if (part === "rib") return "ribs";
        if (part === "hands") return "hand";
        if (part === "finger") return "fingers";
        if (part === "feet") return "feet";
        if (part === "legs") return "leg";
        if (part === "eyes") return "eye";
        return part;
      }
    }
    return String(fallback || "body");
  },

  extractItemCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const out = [];

    const add = (type, payload, confidence = 0.76) => {
      out.push(this.makeCandidate("item", type, payload, raw, confidence, root));
    };

    const currencyGainPatterns = [
      /\b(?:gain|gains|gained|receive|receives|received|earn|earns|earned|find|finds|found|take|takes|took)\s+(\d+)\s+(gold|silver|copper)\b/gi,
      /\b(\d+)\s+(gold|silver|copper)\s+(?:coins?\s+)?(?:is|are|was|were)?\s*(?:given|awarded|paid)\b/gi
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < currencyGainPatterns.length; i += 1) {
      let match;
      while ((match = currencyGainPatterns[i].exec(raw))) {
        add("currency_change", {
          currency: String(match[2] || "").toLowerCase(),
          amount: Number(match[1] || 0),
          direction: "gain"
        }, 0.88);
      }
    }

    const currencyLossPatterns = [
      /\b(?:pay|pays|paid|spend|spends|spent|lose|loses|lost)\s+(\d+)\s+(gold|silver|copper)\b/gi
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < currencyLossPatterns.length; i += 1) {
      let match;
      while ((match = currencyLossPatterns[i].exec(raw))) {
        add("currency_change", {
          currency: String(match[2] || "").toLowerCase(),
          amount: Number(match[1] || 0),
          direction: "loss"
        }, 0.88);
      }
    }

    if (/\bsleeve is gone\b|\bshirt is torn\b|\bcloak is torn\b|\barmor is cracked\b|\bcharred cloth\b|\bbag tears\b/.test(raw.toLowerCase())) {
      add("item_damage", { itemLabel: "worn gear", damageTag: "damaged" }, 0.74);
    }

    if (/\bdrink(?:s|ing| drank)?\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?(potion|tonic|elixir)\b/i.test(raw)) {
      const match = raw.match(/\bdrink(?:s|ing| drank)?\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?(potion|tonic|elixir)\b/i);
      add("item_consumed", { itemLabel: this.normalizeShortLabel(match && match[1]) }, 0.82);
    }

    if (/\beat(?:s|ing| ate)?\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?(ration|bread|stew|meat|herb)\b/i.test(raw)) {
      const match = raw.match(/\beat(?:s|ing| ate)?\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?(ration|bread|stew|meat|herb)\b/i);
      add("item_consumed", { itemLabel: this.normalizeShortLabel(match && match[1]) }, 0.78);
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractWorldCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    const add = (type, payload, confidence = 0.76) => {
      out.push(this.makeCandidate("world", type, payload, raw, confidence, root));
    };

    const targets = [
      "wall",
      "door",
      "gate",
      "gatehouse",
      "palisade",
      "granary",
      "warehouse",
      "tavern",
      "inn",
      "shop",
      "smithy",
      "bridge",
      "road",
      "trail",
      "cart",
      "stall",
      "wagon",
      "brush",
      "tree"
    ];

    const damageWords = [
      "burned",
      "scorched",
      "charred",
      "collapsed",
      "broken",
      "splintered",
      "wrecked",
      "blasted",
      "damaged",
      "crater"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < targets.length; i += 1) {
      const target = targets[i];
      const re = new RegExp(
        "\\b(" + damageWords.join("|") + ")\\b[^.!?]{0,24}\\b" + target + "\\b|\\b" + target + "\\b[^.!?]{0,24}\\b(" + damageWords.join("|") + ")\\b",
        "i"
      );

      const match = lower.match(re);
      if (match) {
        add("world_change", {
          targetLabel: target,
          changeTag: this.inferWorldChangeTag(match[0])
        }, 0.80);
      }
    }

    if (/\boccupied by\b|\bfull of\b|\bpacked with\b/.test(lower)) {
      add("occupancy_shift", { state: "occupied" }, 0.68);
    }

    if (/\babandoned\b|\bempty now\b|\bdeserted\b/.test(lower)) {
      add("occupancy_shift", { state: "abandoned" }, 0.68);
    }

    if (/\broad is blocked\b|\bbridge is out\b|\broute is cut off\b|\btrail is blocked\b/.test(lower)) {
      add("route_issue", { issue: "blocked" }, 0.82);
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractActorCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    const add = (type, payload, confidence = 0.74) => {
      out.push(this.makeCandidate("actor", type, payload, raw, confidence, root));
    };

    const roles = [
      "smith",
      "guard",
      "barkeep",
      "merchant",
      "hunter",
      "kid",
      "child",
      "man",
      "woman"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < roles.length; i += 1) {
      const role = roles[i];

      if (new RegExp("\\b" + role + "\\b[^.!?]{0,30}\\b(dies|dead|killed)\\b", "i").test(raw)) {
        add("actor_state_change", { actorLabel: role, state: "dead" }, 0.84);
      }

      if (new RegExp("\\b" + role + "\\b[^.!?]{0,30}\\b(injured|wounded|burned|bleeding)\\b", "i").test(raw)) {
        add("actor_state_change", { actorLabel: role, state: "injured" }, 0.76);
      }

      if (new RegExp("\\b" + role + "\\b[^.!?]{0,30}\\b(fled|retreated|backs away|backed away|runs away|ran away)\\b", "i").test(raw)) {
        add("actor_state_change", { actorLabel: role, state: "fled" }, 0.78);
      }
    }

    if (/\b(?:trusts|believes|fears|hates|respects)\s+you\b/i.test(raw)) {
      const match = raw.match(/\b(?:trusts|believes|fears|hates|respects)\s+you\b/i);
      add("relationship_shift", { relationTag: this.normalizeShortLabel(match && match[0]) }, 0.67);
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractTimeCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const out = [];

    const add = (type, payload, confidence = 0.78) => {
      out.push(this.makeCandidate("time", type, payload, raw, confidence, root));
    };

    let match = raw.match(/\b(\d+)\s+hours?\s+later\b/i);
    if (match) {
      add("time_advance", { amount: Number(match[1] || 0), unit: "hours" }, 0.90);
    }

    match = raw.match(/\bafter\s+(\d+)\s+hours?\b/i);
    if (match) {
      add("time_advance", { amount: Number(match[1] || 0), unit: "hours" }, 0.88);
    }

    match = raw.match(/\bafter\s+(\d+)\s+minutes?\b/i);
    if (match) {
      add("time_advance", { amount: Number(match[1] || 0), unit: "minutes" }, 0.86);
    }

    if (/\bovernight\b|\bthrough the night\b/.test(raw.toLowerCase())) {
      add("rest_completion", { label: "overnight" }, 0.84);
    }

    if (/\bnext morning\b|\bby dawn\b|\bat dawn\b/.test(raw.toLowerCase())) {
      add("time_advance", { label: "next_morning" }, 0.82);
    }

    if (/\btravel completed\b|\byou arrive\b|\byou reach\b/.test(raw.toLowerCase())) {
      add("travel_completion", { status: "arrived" }, 0.74);
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractQuestCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    const add = (type, payload, confidence = 0.78) => {
      out.push(this.makeCandidate("quest", type, payload, raw, confidence, root));
    };

    if (/\b(?:accept|accepted|agree to|agreed to|take on|took on)\b[^.!?]{0,30}\b(quest|job|bounty|contract|task)\b/.test(lower)) {
      add("quest_status", { status: "accepted" }, 0.84);
    }

    if (/\b(?:complete|completed|finish|finished|fulfill|fulfilled)\b[^.!?]{0,30}\b(quest|job|bounty|contract|task)\b/.test(lower)) {
      add("quest_status", { status: "completed" }, 0.84);
    }

    if (/\b(?:fail|failed|abandon|abandoned)\b[^.!?]{0,30}\b(quest|job|bounty|contract|task)\b/.test(lower)) {
      add("quest_status", { status: "failed" }, 0.82);
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractReputationCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    const add = (type, payload, confidence = 0.76) => {
      out.push(this.makeCandidate("reputation", type, payload, raw, confidence, root));
    };

    // Chunk 23 official reputation coverage:
    // Keep candidate extraction broad enough to notice player-facing social truth,
    // while ReputationSystem.shouldApplyReputationCandidate keeps the hard-save gate strict.
    const localGroup = "(?:locals|townsfolk|villagers|guards|patrons|crowd|people|citizens|merchants|traders|residents)";

    const negativeLocalRe = new RegExp(
      "\\b" + localGroup + "\\b[^.!?]{0,48}\\b(?:wary|afraid|hostile|suspicious|fear|fears|fearful|hateful|angry|resentful|distrustful|distrust|distrusts)\\b",
      "i"
    );

    const positiveLocalRe = new RegExp(
      "\\b" + localGroup + "\\b[^.!?]{0,48}\\b(?:respect|respects|respectful|trusting|trusted|grateful|thankful|cheer|cheers|cheering|welcoming|welcome|welcomes|praised|praise|praises|approving|friendly)\\b",
      "i"
    );

    if (negativeLocalRe.test(lower)) {
      add("reputation_shift", { direction: "negative", subject: "local_group" }, 0.76);
    }

    if (positiveLocalRe.test(lower)) {
