      add("reputation_shift", { direction: "positive", subject: "local_group" }, 0.76);
    }

    if (/\bpeople\s+(?:see|saw|view|viewed)\s+you\s+as\s+(?:trusted|respected|liked|beloved|welcome|welcomed|helpful|heroic|honest)\b/i.test(raw)) {
      add("reputation_shift", { direction: "positive", subject: "local_group" }, 0.78);
    }

    if (/\bpeople\s+(?:see|saw|view|viewed)\s+you\s+as\s+(?:feared|dangerous|threatening|hostile|cruel|notorious|infamous)\b/i.test(raw)) {
      add("reputation_shift", { direction: "negative", subject: "local_group" }, 0.78);
    }

    if (/\byou\s+(?:are|were|become|became)\s+(?:praised|respected|trusted|welcomed|liked|thanked)\s+by\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:locals|townsfolk|villagers|guards|patrons|crowd|people|citizens|merchants|traders|residents)\b/i.test(raw)) {
      add("reputation_shift", { direction: "positive", subject: "local_group" }, 0.78);
    }

    if (/\byou\s+(?:are|were|become|became)\s+(?:feared|hated|watched|avoided|suspected|resented)\s+by\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:locals|townsfolk|villagers|guards|patrons|crowd|people|citizens|merchants|traders|residents)\b/i.test(raw)) {
      add("reputation_shift", { direction: "negative", subject: "local_group" }, 0.78);
    }

    if (/\b(?:rumors?|rumours?)\b|\bwhispers?\b|\bwhispered\b|\bword spreads\b|\bnews spreads\b|\bpeople are saying\b|\btalk spreads\b/i.test(raw)) {
      add("reputation_shift", { direction: "rumor_activity", subject: "public" }, 0.76);
    }

    return out;
  },

  makeCandidate(category, type, payload, evidence, confidence, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const rounded = U && typeof U.round === "function"
      ? U.round(confidence, 2)
      : Number(confidence || 0);

    return {
      id: U && typeof U.makeId === "function" ? U.makeId("candidate") : String(Date.now()),
      category: String(category || ""),
      type: String(type || ""),
      confidence: rounded,
      evidence: this.makeEvidenceSnippet(evidence),
      payload: payload && typeof payload === "object" ? payload : {},
      turn: root && root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0
    };
  },

  dedupeCandidates(candidates) {
    const out = [];
    const seen = new Set();

    for (let i = 0; i < (Array.isArray(candidates) ? candidates.length : 0); i += 1) {
      const candidate = candidates[i];
      if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") continue;

      const signature = this.buildCandidateSignature(candidate);
      if (!signature || seen.has(signature)) continue;

      seen.add(signature);
      out.push(candidate);
    }

    return out;
  },

  buildCandidateSignature(candidate) {
    if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") return "";

    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    return [
      String(candidate.category || ""),
      String(candidate.type || ""),
      String(payload.resource || ""),
      String(payload.state || ""),
      String(payload.status || ""),
      String(payload.bodyPart || ""),
      String(payload.injuryTag || ""),
      String(payload.itemLabel || ""),
      String(payload.currency || ""),
      String(payload.direction || ""),
      String(payload.targetLabel || ""),
      String(payload.changeTag || ""),
      String(payload.actorLabel || ""),
      String(payload.amount || ""),
      String(payload.unit || ""),
      String(payload.label || ""),
      String(payload.subject || "")
    ].join("|").toLowerCase();
  },

  inferWorldChangeTag(text) {
    const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();
    if (/\bscorch|char|burn/.test(lower)) return "burn_damage";
    if (/\bcollapse|broken|splinter/.test(lower)) return "structural_damage";
    if (/\bcrater|blast/.test(lower)) return "blast_damage";
    if (/\bdamaged|wrecked/.test(lower)) return "general_damage";
    return "change";
  },

  normalizeShortLabel(value) {
    const text = String(value || "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();

    if (!text) return "";
    if (text.split(" ").length > 4) return "";
    return text;
  },

  makeEvidenceSnippet(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim()
      .slice(0, 180);
  }
},

    // === VALIDATION SYSTEM ===
ValidationSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.pending || typeof root.pending !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.pending)) {
      root.pending = {};
    }

    const pending = root.pending;

    if (!Array.isArray(pending.outputCandidates)) pending.outputCandidates = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(pending.unresolvedConsequences)) pending.unresolvedConsequences = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences)) pending.validatedConsequences = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(pending.tentativeConsequences)) pending.tentativeConsequences = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(pending.ignoredConsequences)) pending.ignoredConsequences = [];
    if (!pending.validationSummary || typeof pending.validationSummary !== "object" || Array.isArray(pending.validationSummary)) {
      pending.validationSummary = {};
    }

    return pending;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;
    const pending = root.pending;
    const raw = String(rawText || "");

    pending.validatedConsequences = [];
    pending.tentativeConsequences = [];
    pending.ignoredConsequences = [];

    if (!raw.trim()) {
      return 0;
    }

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    const source = []
      .concat(Array.isArray(pending.outputCandidates) ? pending.outputCandidates : [])
      .concat(Array.isArray(pending.unresolvedConsequences) ? pending.unresolvedConsequences : []);

    let processed = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < source.length; i += 1) {
      const candidate = source[i];
      const result = this.validateCandidate(candidate, root);
      if (!result || !result.action) continue;

      processed += 1;

      if (result.action === "hard_save") {
        pending.validatedConsequences.push(result.candidate);
      } else if (result.action === "tentative") {
        pending.tentativeConsequences.push(result.candidate);
      } else {
        pending.ignoredConsequences.push(result.candidate);
      }
    }

    pending.validationSummary = {
      turn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0,
      parsed: source.length,
      validated: pending.validatedConsequences.length,
      tentative: pending.tentativeConsequences.length,
      ignored: pending.ignoredConsequences.length
    };

    if (U && typeof U.logParse === "function") {
      U.logParse(
        "Validation: " +
        pending.validatedConsequences.length +
        " hard / " +
        pending.tentativeConsequences.length +
        " tentative / " +
        pending.ignoredConsequences.length +
        " ignored"
      );
    }

    return processed;
  },

  validateCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") {
      return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
    }

    const category = String(candidate.category || "");
    if (!category) {
      return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
    }

    if (category === "player") return this.validatePlayerCandidate(candidate, root);
    if (category === "item") return this.validateItemCandidate(candidate, root);
    if (category === "world") return this.validateWorldCandidate(candidate, root);
    if (category === "actor") return this.validateActorCandidate(candidate, root);
    if (category === "time") return this.validateTimeCandidate(candidate, root);
    if (category === "quest") return this.validateQuestCandidate(candidate, root);
    if (category === "reputation") return this.validateReputationCandidate(candidate, root);

    return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
  },

  validatePlayerCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const type = String(candidate.type || "");
    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const confidence = Number(candidate.confidence || 0);

    if (type === "resource_state") {
      const resource = String(payload.resource || "");
      const stateName = String(payload.state || "");

      if (!/^(hp|mp|ep)$/.test(resource)) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }
      if (!/^(empty|low)$/.test(stateName)) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      if (confidence >= 0.68) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
      }

      return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
    }

    if (type === "status_gain" || type === "status_loss") {
      const status = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.status);
      if (!status) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
      nextCandidate.payload.status = status;

      if (confidence >= 0.66) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    if (type === "body_injury") {
      const bodyPart = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.bodyPart);
      const injuryTag = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.injuryTag);

      if (!injuryTag) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
      nextCandidate.payload.bodyPart = bodyPart;
      nextCandidate.payload.injuryTag = injuryTag;

      if (confidence >= 0.72) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
  },

  validateItemCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const type = String(candidate.type || "");
    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const confidence = Number(candidate.confidence || 0);

    if (type === "currency_change") {
      const currency = String(payload.currency || "");
      const amount = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(payload.amount || 0)));
      const direction = String(payload.direction || "");

      if (!/^(gold|silver|copper)$/.test(currency) || amount <= 0 || !/^(gain|loss)$/.test(direction)) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
      nextCandidate.payload.currency = currency;
      nextCandidate.payload.amount = amount;
      nextCandidate.payload.direction = direction;

      if (confidence >= 0.82) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    if (type === "item_consumed") {
      const itemLabel = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.itemLabel);
      if (!itemLabel) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
      nextCandidate.payload.itemLabel = itemLabel;

      if (confidence >= 0.80) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    if (type === "item_damage") {
      const itemLabel = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.itemLabel);
      const damageTag = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.damageTag);
      if (!itemLabel && !damageTag) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
      nextCandidate.payload.itemLabel = itemLabel;
      nextCandidate.payload.damageTag = damageTag || "damaged";

      if (confidence >= 0.84) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
  },

  validateWorldCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const confidence = Number(candidate.confidence || 0);

    if (candidate.type === "world_change") {
      const targetLabel = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.targetLabel);
      const changeTag = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.changeTag);
      if (!targetLabel || !changeTag) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
      nextCandidate.payload.targetLabel = targetLabel;
      nextCandidate.payload.changeTag = changeTag;

      if (confidence >= 0.84) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      nextCandidate.payload.changeTag = "general_damage";
      return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    if (candidate.type === "route_issue") {
      const issue = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.issue);
      if (!issue) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
      nextCandidate.payload.issue = issue;

      if (confidence >= 0.82) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
  },

  validateActorCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const confidence = Number(candidate.confidence || 0);

    if (!this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.actorLabel) && !this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.relationTag)) {
      return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
    }

    if (confidence >= 0.82) {
      return { action: "hard_save", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
    }

    return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
  },

  validateTimeCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const confidence = Number(candidate.confidence || 0);

    if (candidate.type === "time_advance") {
      const amount = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(payload.amount || 0)));
      const unit = String(payload.unit || "");
      const label = String(payload.label || "");

      if ((amount > 0 && /^(minutes|hours)$/.test(unit)) || /^(next_morning)$/.test(label)) {
        const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);

        if (confidence >= 0.80) {
          return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
        }
        return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
    }

    if (candidate.type === "rest_completion") {
      const label = String(payload.label || "");
      if (label === "overnight") {
        if (confidence >= 0.82) {
          return { action: "hard_save", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
        }
        return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
      }

      return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
    }

    if (candidate.type === "travel_completion") {
      if (confidence >= 0.74) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
      }
      return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
    }

    return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
  },

  validateQuestCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const confidence = Number(candidate.confidence || 0);
    if (confidence >= 0.82) {
      return { action: "hard_save", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
    }
    return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
  },

  validateReputationCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const type = String(candidate.type || "");
    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const confidence = Number(candidate.confidence || 0);

    if (type !== "reputation_shift") {
      return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
    }

    const direction = String(payload.direction || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    const subject = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.subject || "local_group") || "local_group";

    if (!/^(positive|negative|rumor_activity)$/.test(direction)) {
      return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
    }

    const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
    nextCandidate.payload.direction = direction;
    nextCandidate.payload.subject = subject;

    if (confidence >= 0.65) {
      return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
  },

  cloneCandidate(candidate) {
    const copy = {};
    const keys = Object.keys(candidate || {});
    for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i += 1) {
      const key = keys[i];
      if (key === "payload" && candidate.payload && typeof candidate.payload === "object") {
        copy.payload = {};
        const payloadKeys = Object.keys(candidate.payload);
        for (let j = 0; j < payloadKeys.length; j += 1) {
          const payloadKey = payloadKeys[j];
          copy.payload[payloadKey] = candidate.payload[payloadKey];
        }
      } else {
        copy[key] = candidate[key];
      }
    }
    return copy;
  },

  normalizeShortLabel(value) {
    const text = String(value || "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();

    if (!text) return "";
    if (text.split(" ").length > 5) return "";
    return text;
  }
},

    // === SPECIALIZATION / MATCHUP SYSTEM ===
SpecializationSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.build || typeof root.build !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.build)) {
      root.build = {};
    }

    const build = root.build;

    if (!build.matchupProfile || typeof build.matchupProfile !== "object" || Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile)) {
      build.matchupProfile = {};
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.weakAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.weakAgainst = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst = [];

    if (!build.specializationState || typeof build.specializationState !== "object" || Array.isArray(build.specializationState)) {
      build.specializationState = {};
    }

    const spec = build.specializationState;

    if (!Array.isArray(spec.profileTags)) spec.profileTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(spec.profileSourceTags)) spec.profileSourceTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(spec.currentTargetTags)) spec.currentTargetTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(spec.currentEnvironmentTags)) spec.currentEnvironmentTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(spec.currentSituationTags)) spec.currentSituationTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(spec.recentAssessments)) spec.recentAssessments = [];

    if (typeof spec.currentEdge !== "string") spec.currentEdge = "none";
    if (typeof spec.currentPressure !== "string") spec.currentPressure = "none";
    if (typeof spec.currentSummary !== "string") spec.currentSummary = "";
    if (typeof spec.noveltyActive !== "boolean") spec.noveltyActive = false;
    if (typeof spec.lastAssessmentTurn !== "number") spec.lastAssessmentTurn = 0;

    return spec;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    if (!raw.trim()) return 0;

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    const build = root.build || {};
    const spec = build.specializationState || {};
    let changes = 0;

    const profile = this.derivePlayerProfile(root);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.profileTags, profile.tags);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.profileSourceTags, profile.sourceTags);

    const targetTags = this.extractTargetTags(raw, root);
    const envTags = this.extractEnvironmentTags(raw, root);
    const situationTags = this.extractSituationTags(raw, root);
    const combatLikely = this.isCombatRelevant(raw, targetTags, situationTags);

    if (!combatLikely) {
      changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.currentTargetTags, []);
      changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.currentEnvironmentTags, []);
      changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.currentSituationTags, []);
      if (spec.currentEdge !== "none") {
        spec.currentEdge = "none";
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (spec.currentPressure !== "none") {
        spec.currentPressure = "none";
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (spec.currentSummary !== "") {
        spec.currentSummary = "";
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (spec.noveltyActive !== false) {
        spec.noveltyActive = false;
        changes += 1;
      }
      return changes;
    }

    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.currentTargetTags, targetTags);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.currentEnvironmentTags, envTags);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.currentSituationTags, situationTags);

    const assessment = this.assessMatchup(profile, targetTags, envTags, situationTags, raw, root);

    if (spec.currentEdge !== assessment.edge) {
      spec.currentEdge = assessment.edge;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (spec.currentPressure !== assessment.pressure) {
      spec.currentPressure = assessment.pressure;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (spec.currentSummary !== assessment.summary) {
      spec.currentSummary = assessment.summary;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (spec.noveltyActive !== assessment.noveltyActive) {
      spec.noveltyActive = assessment.noveltyActive;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    if (spec.lastAssessmentTurn !== turn) {
      spec.lastAssessmentTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }

    changes += this.applyAssessmentToProfile(assessment, build.matchupProfile, root);
    changes += this.pushRecentAssessment(spec.recentAssessments, {
      turn: turn,
      edge: assessment.edge,
      pressure: assessment.pressure,
      targets: targetTags.slice(0, 4),
      environment: envTags.slice(0, 4),
      noveltyActive: assessment.noveltyActive,
      summary: assessment.summary
    });

    if (changes > 0) {
      if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
        U.markDirty("player", true, root);
      }
      if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
        U.invalidatePackets(root);
      }
      if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
        U.logEvent("Specialization/matchup assessment updated.");
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  derivePlayerProfile(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const player = root && root.player ? root.player : {};
    const stats = player.stats || {};
    const body = player.body || {};
    const appearance = player.appearance || {};
    const conditions = player.conditions || {};
    const build = root && root.build ? root.build : {};
    const sourceTags = this.collectSourceTags(root);

    const profileTags = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < sourceTags.length; i += 1) {
      const mapped = this.normalizeCapabilityTag(sourceTags[i]);
      if (mapped) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, mapped);
    }

    const atk = Number(stats.atk || 0);
    const def = Number(stats.def || 0);
    const spd = Number(stats.spd || 0);
    const intl = Number(stats.intl || 0);
    const maxHp = Number(stats.maxHp || 0);
    const maxMp = Number(stats.maxMp || 0);

    if (atk >= intl + 2) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, "martial");
    if (intl >= atk + 2 || maxMp >= 25) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, "magic");
    if (spd >= Math.max(atk, def)) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, "mobility");
    if (def >= Math.max(atk, intl)) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, "defense");
    if (maxHp >= 110 || def >= 8) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, "durable");

    if (Array.isArray(body.bodyTags)) {
      for (let i = 0; i < body.bodyTags.length; i += 1) {
        const tag = this.normalizeCapabilityTag(body.bodyTags[i]);
        if (tag) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, tag);
      }
    }

    if (Array.isArray(appearance.visibleTraits)) {
      for (let i = 0; i < appearance.visibleTraits.length; i += 1) {
        const tag = this.normalizeCapabilityTag(appearance.visibleTraits[i]);
        if (tag) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, tag);
      }
    }

    const active = Array.isArray(conditions.active) ? conditions.active : [];
    for (let i = 0; i < active.length; i += 1) {
      const tag = this.normalizeCapabilityTag(active[i]);
      if (tag) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, tag);
    }

    if (build.matchupProfile) {
      const strong = Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst) ? build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst : [];
      for (let i = 0; i < strong.length; i += 1) {
        const tag = this.normalizeCapabilityTag(strong[i]);
        if (tag) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, tag);
      }
    }

    return {
      tags: profileTags,
      sourceTags: sourceTags
    };
  },

  collectSourceTags(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const build = root && root.build ? root.build : {};
    const out = [];
    const buckets = [
      build.originTags,
      build.combatTags,
      build.methodTags,
      build.resourceTags,
      build.defenseTags,
      build.specializationTags,
      build.temperamentTags,
      build.styleTags
    ];

    for (let b = 0; b < buckets.length; b += 1) {
      const list = Array.isArray(buckets[b]) ? buckets[b] : [];
      for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) {
        this.pushUniqueLocal(out, String(list[i] || ""));
      }
    }

    const abilities = root && root.abilities ? root.abilities : {};
    const byId = abilities.byId || {};
    const allIds = Array.isArray(abilities.allIds) ? abilities.allIds : [];

    for (let i = 0; i < allIds.length; i += 1) {
      const record = byId[allIds[i]];
      if (!record || typeof record !== "object") continue;

      const semanticTags = record.observation && Array.isArray(record.observation.semanticTags)
        ? record.observation.semanticTags
        : [];

      for (let j = 0; j < semanticTags.length; j += 1) {
        this.pushUniqueLocal(out, String(semanticTags[j] || ""));
      }

      if (record.meaning && typeof record.meaning.intendedFunction === "string") {
        this.pushUniqueLocal(out, record.meaning.intendedFunction);
      }

      if (record.keys && Array.isArray(record.keys.keyPhrases)) {
        for (let j = 0; j < record.keys.keyPhrases.length; j += 1) {
          this.pushUniqueLocal(out, String(record.keys.keyPhrases[j] || ""));
        }
      }
    }

    const player = root && root.player ? root.player : {};
    const traitState = player.traitState || {};
    const traitBuckets = [
      traitState.resistanceTags,
      traitState.adaptationTags,
      traitState.sensitivityTags,
      traitState.immunityTags,
      traitState.vulnerabilityTags
    ];

    for (let b = 0; b < traitBuckets.length; b += 1) {
      const list = Array.isArray(traitBuckets[b]) ? traitBuckets[b] : [];
      for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) {
        this.pushUniqueLocal(out, String(list[i] || ""));
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  normalizeCapabilityTag(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[_-]+/g, " ")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();

    if (!lower) return "";

    if (/\bvery tall\b|\blarge frame\b|\bgiant\b|\btowering\b/.test(lower)) return "large_frame";
    if (/\bmobility\b|\bspeed\b|\bfast\b|\bagile\b|\bevasive\b|\bdash\b|\bstep\b/.test(lower)) return "mobility";
    if (/\bdefense\b|\bguard\b|\bshield\b|\bbrace\b|\btank\b|\bdurable\b|\bendure\b|\bresist\b/.test(lower)) return "defense";
    if (/\bmartial\b|\bblade\b|\bsword\b|\baxe\b|\bhammer\b|\bfist\b|\bmelee\b/.test(lower)) return "martial";
    if (/\bmagic\b|\barcane\b|\bmana\b|\bspell\b|\bcaster\b/.test(lower)) return "magic";
    if (/\branged\b|\barcher\b|\bbow\b|\bcrossbow\b|\bshot\b|\bprojectile\b/.test(lower)) return "ranged";
    if (/\bstealth\b|\bhidden\b|\bsilent\b|\bassassin\b/.test(lower)) return "stealth";
    if (/\bdetection\b|\btrack\b|\bsense\b|\bscout\b|\binspect\b|\bsurvey\b/.test(lower)) return "detection";
    if (/\bpierce\b|\bspear\b|\blance\b|\bthrust\b/.test(lower)) return "pierce";
    if (/\bfire\b|\bflame\b|\bember\b|\bburn\b/.test(lower)) return "fire";
    if (/\blightning\b|\bstorm\b|\bshock\b|\bthunder\b/.test(lower)) return "lightning";
    if (/\bice\b|\bfrost\b|\bcold\b/.test(lower)) return "cold";
    if (/\bshadow\b|\bdark\b|\bveil\b/.test(lower)) return "shadow";
    if (/\blight\b|\bholy\b|\bradiant\b/.test(lower)) return "light";
    if (/\bwater\b|\btide\b|\briver\b|\bsea\b|\baquatic\b/.test(lower)) return "water";
    if (/\bpoison\b|\bvenom\b|\btoxic\b/.test(lower)) return "poison";
    if (/\brot\b|\bdecay\b|\bwither\b|\bblight\b/.test(lower)) return "rot";
    if (/\brecovery\b|\bheal\b|\brestore\b|\bregenerat/.test(lower)) return "recovery";
    if (/\bburned\b|\bbleeding\b|\bpoisoned\b|\bchilled\b|\bsoaked\b|\bfatigued\b/.test(lower)) return "strained";

    return "";
  },

  extractTargetTags(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    const checks = [
      { re: /\bundead\b|\bskeleton\b|\bzombie\b|\bwraith\b|\brevenant\b|\bghoul\b/, tag: "undead" },
      { re: /\bdragon\b|\bdrake\b|\bwyvern\b|\bdraconic\b/, tag: "draconic" },
      { re: /\bbeast\b|\bpredator\b|\bwolf\b|\bboar\b|\bbear\b|\bhound\b/, tag: "beast" },
      { re: /\barmored\b|\bplate\b|\bmail\b|\bshielded\b|\bironclad\b/, tag: "armored" },
      { re: /\bmage\b|\bwizard\b|\bsorcerer\b|\bspellcaster\b|\bshaman\b|\bwarlock\b/, tag: "caster" },
      { re: /\barcher\b|\bbowman\b|\bcrossbowman\b|\bmarksman\b|\branged\b/, tag: "ranged" },
      { re: /\bwinged\b|\bflying\b|\bairborne\b|\bharpy\b|\boverhead\b/, tag: "flying" },
      { re: /\bhidden\b|\bconcealed\b|\binvisible\b|\bunseen\b|\bstealth\b|\bcamouflaged\b/, tag: "hidden" },
      { re: /\bpack\b|\bgroup\b|\bswarm\b|\bseveral\b|\bmultiple\b|\bcrowd\b/, tag: "pack" },
      { re: /\bgiant\b|\bhuge\b|\bmassive\b|\bogre\b|\btroll\b|\btowering\b|\bcolossal\b/, tag: "large" },
      { re: /\baquatic\b|\bmerfolk\b|\bwaterborne\b|\bsea[- ]?born\b|\bswimming\b/, tag: "aquatic" },
      { re: /\bfast\b|\bswift\b|\bquick\b|\bagile\b|\bnimble\b|\bblur\b/, tag: "fast" },
      { re: /\bbrute\b|\bhulking\b|\bheavy\b|\bthickset\b/, tag: "brute" }
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < checks.length; i += 1) {
      if (checks[i].re.test(lower)) {
        this.pushUniqueLocal(out, checks[i].tag);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractEnvironmentTags(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    const scene = root && root.world && root.world.currentScene ? root.world.currentScene : {};
    const sceneName = String(scene.displayName || "").toLowerCase();
    const sceneType = String(scene.sceneType || "").toLowerCase();

    if (sceneType === "interior" || sceneType === "room" || /\btavern\b|\binn\b|\bworkshop\b|\bsmithy\b|\bhall\b|\broom\b/.test(sceneName)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "interior");
    }
    if (sceneType === "outdoor" || /\bsquare\b|\blane\b|\broad\b|\btrail\b|\bbridge\b|\bgate\b/.test(sceneName)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "open");
    }

    if (/\bcramped\b|\bnarrow\b|\bconfined\b|\blow ceiling\b|\bdoorway\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "cramped");
    if (/\brain\b|\bstorm\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "rain");
    if (/\bmud\b|\bslippery\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "mud");
    if (/\bdark\b|\bnight\b|\bshadowed\b|\bgloom\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "darkness");
    if (/\bstream\b|\briver\b|\bwater\b|\bflood\b|\bsoaked\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "water");
    if (/\bfire\b|\bflame\b|\bember\b|\bsmoke\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "fire");
    if (/\bcold\b|\bfrost\b|\bice\b|\bfreezing\b|\bchill\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "cold");
    if (/\bcliff\b|\bhigh ground\b|\brooftop\b|\bbalcony\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "high_ground");
    if (/\bcover\b|\bbehind the cart\b|\bdoorframe\b|\bbarrel\b|\bwall\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "cover");

    return out;
  },

  extractSituationTags(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    if (/\bunfamiliar\b|\bstrange style\b|\bodd angle\b|\bunreadable\b|\bnot used to\b|\bcatches? .* off guard\b|\bunexpected\b/.test(lower)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "novelty_advantage");
    }

    if (/\bprepared\b|\bbraced\b|\bready\b|\bset stance\b|\bsteady\b|\baimed\b/.test(lower)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "prepared");
    }

    if (/\boutnumbered\b|\bsurrounded\b/.test(lower)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "outnumbered");
    }

    if (/\bopening\b|\bvulnerable\b|\bexposed\b|\breeling\b|\bstaggered\b|\boff balance\b/.test(lower)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "target_exposed");
    }

    if (/\boverwhelming\b|\bno opening\b|\bcan.?t keep up\b|\boutclassed\b/.test(lower)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "overwhelmed");
    }

    if (/\bambush\b|\bfrom behind\b|\bsurprise\b/.test(lower)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "surprise");
    }

    return out;
  },

  isCombatRelevant(rawText, targetTags, situationTags) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();

    if (Array.isArray(situationTags) && situationTags.length) return true;
    if (Array.isArray(targetTags) && targetTags.length && /\battack\b|\bfight\b|\bstrike\b|\bweapon\b|\bspell\b|\bcombat\b|\bduel\b|\bbattle\b|\bhostile\b|\bdefend\b|\bbrace\b|\bpressure\b|\bthreat\b/.test(lower)) {
      return true;
    }

    if (/\battack\b|\bfight\b|\bstrike\b|\bslash\b|\bstab\b|\bshoot\b|\bblast\b|\bspell\b|\bduel\b|\bbattle\b|\bhostile\b|\bthreat\b/.test(lower)) {
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  },

  hasPlayerPressureFlags(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const player = root && root.player ? root.player : {};
    const stats = player.stats || {};
    const conditions = player.conditions || {};

    const hp = Number(stats.hp || 0);
    const maxHp = Math.max(1, Number(stats.maxHp || 1));
    const mp = Number(stats.mp || 0);
    const maxMp = Math.max(1, Number(stats.maxMp || 1));
    const ep = Number(stats.ep || 0);
    const maxEp = Math.max(1, Number(stats.maxEp || 1));

    if (hp / maxHp <= 0.35) return true;
    if (mp / maxMp <= 0.25) return true;
    if (ep / maxEp <= 0.25) return true;

    const active = Array.isArray(conditions.active) ? conditions.active : [];
    for (let i = 0; i < active.length; i += 1) {
      const lower = String(active[i] || "").toLowerCase();
      if (/burned|bleeding|poisoned|infected|fatigued|chilled|soaked/.test(lower)) {
        return true;
      }
    }

    return false;
  },

  assessMatchup(profile, targetTags, envTags, situationTags, rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const profileTags = Array.isArray(profile && profile.tags) ? profile.tags : [];
    const hasTag = (tag) => profileTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;
    const targetHas = (tag) => Array.isArray(targetTags) && targetTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;
    const envHas = (tag) => Array.isArray(envTags) && envTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;
    const situHas = (tag) => Array.isArray(situationTags) && situationTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;

    let edgeScore = 0;
    let pressureScore = 0;

    if (this.hasPlayerPressureFlags(root)) {
      pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (/\bopening\b|\bvulnerable\b|\bexposed\b|\brecoils\b|\bstaggers?\b|\boff balance\b|\bcan.?t track\b|\bhesitates?\b/.test(lower)) {
      edgeScore += 2;
    }

    if (/\boverwhelming\b|\bshrugs off\b|\boutclassed\b|\bno opening\b|\btoo fast\b|\btoo strong\b|\bcan.?t keep up\b|\bpressure mounts\b/.test(lower)) {
      pressureScore += 2;
    }

    if (targetHas("armored")) {
      if (hasTag("magic") || hasTag("lightning") || hasTag("pierce")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("undead")) {
      if (hasTag("light") || hasTag("fire")) edgeScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("draconic") || targetHas("large")) {
      if (hasTag("ranged") || hasTag("magic") || hasTag("mobility")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("pack")) {
      if (hasTag("fire") || hasTag("lightning") || hasTag("large_frame")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("hidden")) {
      if (hasTag("detection") || hasTag("fire") || hasTag("lightning")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("ranged")) {
      if (hasTag("mobility") || hasTag("stealth")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("caster")) {
      if (hasTag("mobility") || hasTag("detection")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("flying")) {
      if (hasTag("ranged") || hasTag("magic") || hasTag("lightning")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("aquatic") && envHas("water")) {
      if (hasTag("water") || hasTag("ranged") || hasTag("magic")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("fast")) {
      if (hasTag("detection") || hasTag("mobility") || hasTag("defense")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (envHas("cramped") && hasTag("large_frame")) {
      pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (envHas("open") && (hasTag("ranged") || hasTag("mobility"))) {
      edgeScore += 1;
    }

    if (envHas("darkness")) {
      if (hasTag("detection") || hasTag("shadow")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (envHas("fire")) {
      if (hasTag("fire")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (envHas("cold")) {
      if (hasTag("cold")) edgeScore += 1;
      else if (this.hasPlayerPressureFlags(root)) pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (envHas("water")) {
      if (hasTag("water")) edgeScore += 1;
      else if (this.hasPlayerPressureFlags(root)) pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (situHas("novelty_advantage")) edgeScore += 1;
    if (situHas("target_exposed")) edgeScore += 1;
    if (situHas("prepared")) edgeScore += 1;
    if (situHas("outnumbered")) pressureScore += 1;
    if (situHas("overwhelmed")) pressureScore += 2;

    const diff = edgeScore - pressureScore;

    let edge = "even";
    if (diff >= 2) edge = "favorable";
    else if (diff === 1) edge = "slight_favor";
    else if (diff === -1) edge = "pressured";
    else if (diff <= -2) edge = "unfavorable";

    let pressure = "low";
    if (pressureScore >= 4) pressure = "overwhelming";
    else if (pressureScore >= 2) pressure = "high";
    else if (pressureScore >= 1) pressure = "moderate";

    const noveltyActive = situHas("novelty_advantage");
    const parts = [
      "Matchup: " + edge,
      "Pressure: " + pressure
    ];

    if (Array.isArray(targetTags) && targetTags.length) {
      parts.push("Target: " + targetTags.slice(0, 2).join(", "));
    }

    return {
      edge: edge,
      pressure: pressure,
      noveltyActive: noveltyActive,
      summary: parts.join(" | ")
    };
  },

  applyAssessmentToProfile(assessment, matchupProfile, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!assessment || !matchupProfile || typeof matchupProfile !== "object") return 0;

    let changes = 0;
    const spec = root && root.build ? root.build.specializationState || {} : {};
    const targetTags = Array.isArray(spec.currentTargetTags) ? spec.currentTargetTags : [];

    for (let i = 0; i < targetTags.length; i += 1) {
      const tag = String(targetTags[i] || "").trim();
      if (!tag) continue;

      if (assessment.edge === "favorable" || assessment.edge === "slight_favor") {
        changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(matchupProfile.strongAgainst, tag);
      }

      if (assessment.edge === "unfavorable" || assessment.pressure === "high" || assessment.pressure === "overwhelming") {
        changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(matchupProfile.weakAgainst, tag);
      }

      if (assessment.noveltyActive) {
        changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst, tag);
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  getRelevantContextHint(root = state.aidrpg, mode = "normal") {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
    this.init(root);

    const build = root.build || {};
    const spec = build.specializationState || {};
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

    if (!spec.currentSummary) return "";
    if (typeof spec.lastAssessmentTurn === "number" && turn - spec.lastAssessmentTurn > 1) {
      return "";
    }

    const parts = [
      "Matchup: " + String(spec.currentEdge || "none"),
      "Pressure: " + String(spec.currentPressure || "none")
    ];

    if (Array.isArray(spec.currentTargetTags) && spec.currentTargetTags.length) {
      parts.push("Target: " + spec.currentTargetTags.slice(0, 2).join(", "));
    }

    if (mode === "wide" && Array.isArray(spec.currentEnvironmentTags) && spec.currentEnvironmentTags.length) {
      parts.push("Terrain: " + spec.currentEnvironmentTags.slice(0, 2).join(", "));
    }

    if (spec.noveltyActive === true) {
      parts.push("Novelty edge active");
    }

    return parts.join(" | ");
  },

  replaceSimpleArray(target, nextValues) {
    if (!Array.isArray(target)) return 0;
    const next = Array.isArray(nextValues) ? nextValues.slice() : [];

    let changed = 0;
    if (target.length !== next.length) {
      changed = 1;
    } else {
      for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i += 1) {
        if (target[i] !== next[i]) {
          changed = 1;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) return 0;

    target.splice(0, target.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < next.length; i += 1) {
      target.push(next[i]);
    }

    return 1;
  },

  pushUniqueLocal(list, value) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
    const text = String(value || "").trim();
    if (!text) return 0;
    if (list.indexOf(text) !== -1) return 0;
    list.push(text);
    return 1;
  },

  pushRecentAssessment(list, record) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list) || !record || typeof record !== "object") return 0;

    const last = list.length ? list[list.length - 1] : null;
    const nextSummary = String(record.summary || "");

    if (last && String(last.summary || "") === nextSummary) {
      return 0;
    }

    list.push(record);

    while (list.length > 8) {
      list.shift();
    }

    return 1;
  }
},

    // === ACTOR PROFILE GENERATION SYSTEM ===
ActorProfileSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.actors || typeof root.actors !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.actors)) {
      root.actors = {};
    }

    const actors = root.actors;

    if (!actors.byId || typeof actors.byId !== "object" || Array.isArray(actors.byId)) {
      actors.byId = {};
    }
    if (!actors.bySceneKey || typeof actors.bySceneKey !== "object" || Array.isArray(actors.bySceneKey)) {
      actors.bySceneKey = {};
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(actors.allIds)) actors.allIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(actors.localIds)) actors.localIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(actors.pendingGeneration)) actors.pendingGeneration = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(actors.recentUpdates)) actors.recentUpdates = [];
    if (!actors.sightingsByKey || typeof actors.sightingsByKey !== "object" || Array.isArray(actors.sightingsByKey)) {
      actors.sightingsByKey = {};
    }

    return actors;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    if (!raw.trim()) return 0;

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    let changes = 0;

    changes += this.syncSceneActors(root);
    changes += this.applyValidatedConsequences(root);

    if (changes > 0) {
      if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
        const actors = root.actors || {};
        const localIds = Array.isArray(actors.localIds) ? actors.localIds : [];
        for (let i = 0; i < localIds.length; i += 1) {
          U.markDirty("actors", localIds[i], root);
        }
      }

      if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
        U.invalidatePackets(root);
      }

      if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
        U.logEvent("Actor profiles updated.");
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  syncSceneActors(root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const scene = root.world && root.world.currentScene ? root.world.currentScene : null;
    const actors = root.actors || {};
    if (!scene || !Array.isArray(scene.localActorIds) || !scene.localActorsById || typeof scene.localActorsById !== "object") {
      return this.replaceSimpleArray(actors.localIds, []);
    }

    const nextLocalIds = [];
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < scene.localActorIds.length; i += 1) {
      const sceneActorId = String(scene.localActorIds[i] || "").trim();
      if (!sceneActorId) continue;

      const rawActor = scene.localActorsById[sceneActorId];
      const candidate = this.buildCandidateFromScene(sceneActorId, rawActor, root);
      if (!candidate) continue;

      const record = this.findOrCreateRecord(candidate, root);
      if (!record) continue;

      changes += this.applyCandidateToRecord(candidate, record, root);
      nextLocalIds.push(record.identity.id);
    }

    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(actors.localIds, nextLocalIds);
    return changes;
  },

  buildCandidateFromScene(sceneActorId, rawActor, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;

    let displayName = "";
    if (rawActor && typeof rawActor === "object") {
      displayName =
        String(rawActor.displayName || rawActor.name || rawActor.label || rawActor.id || sceneActorId || "").trim();
    } else {
      displayName = String(sceneActorId || "").trim();
    }

    if (!displayName) return null;

    const roleTags = this.inferRoleTags(displayName);
    const rankTags = this.inferRankTags(displayName);
    const category = this.inferCategory(displayName);
    const sceneKey = U.toKey(displayName || sceneActorId);

    if (!sceneKey) return null;

    const importanceScore = this.computeImportanceScore(displayName, roleTags, rankTags, root);

    return {
      sceneKey: sceneKey,
      displayName: displayName,
      roleTags: roleTags,
      rankTags: rankTags,
      category: category,
      importanceScore: importanceScore,
      persistent: importanceScore >= 2,
      major: importanceScore >= 4
    };
  },

  findOrCreateRecord(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const actors = root.actors || {};
    const sceneKey = String(candidate.sceneKey || "").trim();
    if (!sceneKey) return null;

    const existingId = actors.bySceneKey && actors.bySceneKey[sceneKey] ? actors.bySceneKey[sceneKey] : "";
    if (existingId && actors.byId && actors.byId[existingId]) {
      return actors.byId[existingId];
    }

    const actorId = U.makeId("actor");
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

    const record = {
      identity: {
        id: actorId,
        name: String(candidate.displayName || ""),
        sceneKey: sceneKey,
        category: String(candidate.category || "humanoid")
      },
      roleTags: [],
      rankTags: [],
      factionRefs: [],
      levelProfile: {
        resolvedLevel: 1,
        bandMin: 1,
        bandMax: 3
      },
      stats: {
        hp: 0,
        maxHp: 0,
        atk: 0,
        def: 0,
        spd: 0,
        intl: 0
      },
      buildTags: [],
      abilities: [],
      equipment: [],
      relationshipToPlayer: 0,
      knownState: {
        alive: true,
        injured: [],
        mood: "",
        locationRef: "",
        lastSeenTurn: turn
      },
      importance: {
        persistent: false,
        major: false,
        score: 0,
        sightings: 0
      }
    };

    actors.byId[actorId] = record;
    actors.bySceneKey[sceneKey] = actorId;
    actors.allIds.push(actorId);

    return record;
  },

  applyCandidateToRecord(candidate, record, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!candidate || !record) return 0;

    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    let changes = 0;

    if (record.identity.name !== candidate.displayName) {
      record.identity.name = candidate.displayName;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.identity.category !== candidate.category) {
      record.identity.category = candidate.category;
      changes += 1;
    }

    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(record.roleTags, candidate.roleTags);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(record.rankTags, candidate.rankTags);

    const levelProfile = this.resolveLevelBand(candidate.roleTags, candidate.rankTags, candidate.category, root);
    if (record.levelProfile.bandMin !== levelProfile.bandMin) {
      record.levelProfile.bandMin = levelProfile.bandMin;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (record.levelProfile.bandMax !== levelProfile.bandMax) {
      record.levelProfile.bandMax = levelProfile.bandMax;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (record.levelProfile.resolvedLevel !== levelProfile.resolvedLevel) {
      record.levelProfile.resolvedLevel = levelProfile.resolvedLevel;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const nextBuildTags = this.inferBuildTags(candidate.roleTags, candidate.rankTags, candidate.category);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(record.buildTags, nextBuildTags);

    const nextAbilities = this.inferLikelyAbilities(nextBuildTags, candidate.roleTags, candidate.rankTags);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(record.abilities, nextAbilities);

    const nextEquipment = this.inferLikelyEquipment(candidate.roleTags, nextBuildTags);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(record.equipment, nextEquipment);

    const nextStats = this.deriveStats(levelProfile.resolvedLevel, nextBuildTags, candidate.category);
    changes += this.applyStatsObject(record.stats, nextStats);

    if (record.knownState.lastSeenTurn !== turn) {
      record.knownState.lastSeenTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const player = root.player || {};
    const locationRef = String(player.locationRef || "").trim();
    if (record.knownState.locationRef !== locationRef) {
      record.knownState.locationRef = locationRef;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const actors = root.actors || {};
    if (!actors.sightingsByKey[candidate.sceneKey] || typeof actors.sightingsByKey[candidate.sceneKey] !== "number") {
      actors.sightingsByKey[candidate.sceneKey] = 0;
    }

    actors.sightingsByKey[candidate.sceneKey] += 1;
    const sightings = actors.sightingsByKey[candidate.sceneKey];

    const nextScore = Math.max(Number(candidate.importanceScore || 0), sightings >= 3 ? 2 : 0);
    if (record.importance.score !== nextScore) {
      record.importance.score = nextScore;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.importance.sightings !== sightings) {
      record.importance.sightings = sightings;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const nextPersistent = candidate.persistent || sightings >= 3;
    if (record.importance.persistent !== nextPersistent) {
      record.importance.persistent = nextPersistent;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const nextMajor = candidate.major || sightings >= 5;
    if (record.importance.major !== nextMajor) {
      record.importance.major = nextMajor;
      changes += 1;
    }

    changes += this.pushRecentUpdate(root.actors.recentUpdates, {
      turn: turn,
      actorId: record.identity.id,
      name: record.identity.name
    });

    return changes;
  },


  processKnowledgeDelaySignals(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const reputation = this.init(root);
    if (!reputation) return 0;

    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const lower = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!lower) return 0;

    let changes = 0;
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

    const hiddenDeed = /\b(?:no one|nobody|none)\s+(?:sees|saw|witnesses|witnessed|knows|knew)\b|\bunwitnessed\b|\bin\s+secret\b|\bsecretly\b|\bhidden\s+deed\b|\balone\s+in\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:dungeon|crypt|barrow|forest|cave)\b/.test(lower);
    const deed = /\b(?:defeat|defeated|slay|slain|kill|killed|rescue|rescued|save|saved|steal|stole|theft|mercy|threat|cruelty|helped|aid)\b/.test(lower);
    const report = /\b(?:report|reported|tell|told|prove|proved|show|showed|bring|brought)\b[^.!?]{0,80}\b(?:guild|guards|town|council|captain|proof|trophy|body|token|evidence|witness)\b/.test(lower);
    const publicSpread = /\b(?:witness|witnesses|saw|seen|word spreads|news spreads|rumors? spread|messenger|announces?|publicly)\b/.test(lower);

    if (hiddenDeed && deed && !report && !publicSpread) {
      const label = this.cleanShortText(raw) || "hidden deed";
      const key = label.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_").slice(0, 48);
      let exists = false;
      for (let i = 0; i < reputation.pendingReports.length; i += 1) {
        if (reputation.pendingReports[i] && reputation.pendingReports[i].key === key) exists = true;
      }
      if (!exists) {
        reputation.pendingReports.push({ key, turn, label, status: "unreported", scope: "hidden" });
        while (reputation.pendingReports.length > 10) reputation.pendingReports.shift();
        changes += 1;
        if (globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils.logEvent === "function") {
          globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils.logEvent("Reputation delay: hidden deed held until report/proof/witness.");
        }
      }
    }

    if (report && reputation.pendingReports.length) {
      const pending = reputation.pendingReports[reputation.pendingReports.length - 1];
      if (pending && pending.status !== "reported") {
        pending.status = "reported";
        pending.reportedTurn = turn;
        const subject = /\bguild\b/.test(lower) ? this.resolveSubject("guild", root) : this.resolveSubject("local_group", root);
        changes += this.applyReputationShift(subject, "positive", "reported/proved deed: " + pending.label, root);
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  applyValidatedConsequences(root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const pending = root.pending || {};
    const validated = Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences) ? pending.validatedConsequences : [];
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < validated.length; i += 1) {
      const candidate = validated[i];
      if (!candidate || candidate.category !== "actor" || candidate._actorApplied === true) {
        continue;
      }

      const payload = candidate.payload || {};
      const actorLabel = String(payload.actorLabel || "").trim();
      if (!actorLabel) {
        candidate._actorApplied = true;
        continue;
      }

      const matchedIds = this.findMatchingActorIds(actorLabel, root);
      for (let j = 0; j < matchedIds.length; j += 1) {
        const record = root.actors && root.actors.byId ? root.actors.byId[matchedIds[j]] : null;
        if (!record) continue;

        if (candidate.type === "actor_state_change") {
          const nextState = String(payload.state || "").trim().toLowerCase();

          if (nextState === "dead" && record.knownState.alive !== false) {
            record.knownState.alive = false;
            changes += 1;
          }

          if (nextState === "injured") {
            changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(record.knownState.injured, "injured");
          }

          if (nextState === "fled" && record.knownState.mood !== "fled") {
            record.knownState.mood = "fled";
            changes += 1;
          }
        }

        if (candidate.type === "relationship_shift") {
          const relationTag = String(payload.relationTag || "").toLowerCase();
          let delta = 0;
          if (relationTag.indexOf("trusts") !== -1 || relationTag.indexOf("respects") !== -1) delta = 1;
          if (relationTag.indexOf("fears") !== -1 || relationTag.indexOf("hates") !== -1) delta = -1;

          if (delta !== 0) {
            const nextRel = Number(record.relationshipToPlayer || 0) + delta;
            if (record.relationshipToPlayer !== nextRel) {
              record.relationshipToPlayer = nextRel;
              changes += 1;
            }
          }
        }
      }

      candidate._actorApplied = true;
    }

    return changes;
  },

  findMatchingActorIds(actorLabel, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const actors = root.actors || {};
    const byId = actors.byId || {};
    const label = String(actorLabel || "").toLowerCase().trim();
    if (!label) return [];

    const out = [];
    const ids = Array.isArray(actors.allIds) ? actors.allIds : [];

    for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i += 1) {
      const record = byId[ids[i]];
      if (!record) continue;

      const name = String(record.identity && record.identity.name || "").toLowerCase();
      if (name === label || name.indexOf(label) !== -1 || label.indexOf(name) !== -1) {
        out.push(ids[i]);
        continue;
      }

      const roleTags = Array.isArray(record.roleTags) ? record.roleTags : [];
      for (let j = 0; j < roleTags.length; j += 1) {
        if (String(roleTags[j] || "").toLowerCase() === label) {
          out.push(ids[i]);
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  computeImportanceScore(displayName, roleTags, rankTags, root = state.aidrpg) {
    let score = 0;
    const name = String(displayName || "");

    if (/\b[A-Z][a-z]+(?:\s+[A-Z][a-z]+)+\b/.test(name)) {
      score += 2;
    }

    if (Array.isArray(rankTags) && rankTags.length) {
      score += 2;
    }

    if (Array.isArray(roleTags)) {
      const importantRoles = new Set([
        "guard",
        "captain",
        "commander",
        "guildmaster",
        "adventurer",
        "hunter",
        "mage",
        "priest",
        "merchant",
        "barkeep",
        "blacksmith",
        "smith",
        "knight",
        "noble",
        "bandit",
        "soldier"
      ]);

      for (let i = 0; i < roleTags.length; i += 1) {
        if (importantRoles.has(String(roleTags[i] || "").toLowerCase())) {
          score += 1;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    return score;
  },

  inferRoleTags(label) {
    const lower = String(label || "").toLowerCase();
    const out = [];
    const checks = [
      "guard",
      "captain",
      "commander",
      "guildmaster",
      "general",
      "sergeant",
      "queen",
      "king",
      "chancellor",
      "baron",
      "baroness",
      "duke",
      "duchess",
      "elder",
      "oracle",
      "archmage",
      "adventurer",
      "hunter",
      "merchant",
      "barkeep",
      "innkeeper",
      "blacksmith",
      "smith",
      "priest",
      "mage",
      "wizard",
      "knight",
      "soldier",
      "bandit",
      "noble",
      "scout",
      "archer",
      "beast",
      "monster",
      "undead",
      "wolf",
      "boar",
      "dragon"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < checks.length; i += 1) {
      const tag = checks[i];
      if (new RegExp("\\b" + tag.replace(/\s+/g, "\\s+") + "\\b", "i").test(lower)) {
        this.pushUniqueLocal(out, tag);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  inferRankTags(label) {
    const lower = String(label || "").toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    if (/\ba-rank\b|\bs-rank\b|\bhigh rank\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "elite");
    if (/\bveteran\b|\belite\b|\bseasoned\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "veteran");
    if (/\bcaptain\b|\bcommander\b|\bguildmaster\b|\blord\b|\blady\b|\bnoble\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "leader");
    if (/\bapprentice\b|\bnovice\b|\binitiate\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "novice");
    if (/\bchild\b|\bboy\b|\bgirl\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "child");

    return out;
  },

  inferCategory(label) {
    const lower = String(label || "").toLowerCase();

    if (/\bundead\b|\bskeleton\b|\bzombie\b|\bwraith\b/.test(lower)) return "undead";
    if (/\bdragon\b|\bdrake\b|\bwyvern\b/.test(lower)) return "draconic";
    if (/\bwolf\b|\bboar\b|\bbear\b|\bhound\b|\bbeast\b/.test(lower)) return "beast";
    if (/\bmonster\b|\bogre\b|\btroll\b|\bgoblin\b/.test(lower)) return "monster";
    return "humanoid";
  },

  resolveLevelBand(roleTags, rankTags, category, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const playerLevel =
      root && root.player && root.player.levelState && typeof root.player.levelState.level === "number"
        ? root.player.levelState.level
        : 1;

    let bandMin = 1;
    let bandMax = Math.max(3, playerLevel + 2);

    const hasRole = (tag) => Array.isArray(roleTags) && roleTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;
    const hasRank = (tag) => Array.isArray(rankTags) && rankTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;

    if (hasRole("guard") || hasRole("hunter") || hasRole("soldier") || hasRole("adventurer")) {
      bandMin = Math.max(1, playerLevel);
      bandMax = Math.max(bandMin + 2, playerLevel + 5);
    }

    if (hasRole("merchant") || hasRole("barkeep") || hasRole("innkeeper") || hasRole("blacksmith") || hasRole("smith")) {
      bandMin = Math.max(1, Math.max(1, playerLevel - 1));
      bandMax = Math.max(bandMin + 2, playerLevel + 3);
    }

    if (hasRole("mage") || hasRole("priest") || hasRole("knight")) {
      bandMin = Math.max(2, playerLevel + 1);
      bandMax = Math.max(bandMin + 2, playerLevel + 6);
    }

    if (category === "beast" || category === "monster" || category === "undead" || category === "draconic") {
      bandMin = Math.max(1, playerLevel - 1);
      bandMax = Math.max(bandMin + 3, playerLevel + 5);
    }

    if (hasRank("veteran")) {
      bandMin += 2;
      bandMax += 3;
    }

    if (hasRank("leader")) {
      bandMin += 4;
      bandMax += 6;
    }

    if (hasRank("elite")) {
      bandMin += 5;
      bandMax += 8;
    }

    if (hasRank("novice")) {
      bandMax = Math.max(2, bandMax - 2);
      bandMin = Math.min(bandMin, Math.max(1, bandMax - 1));
    }

    if (hasRank("child")) {
      bandMin = 1;
      bandMax = Math.min(2, bandMax);
    }

    if (bandMin > bandMax) bandMax = bandMin;
    const resolvedLevel = Math.max(bandMin, Math.floor((bandMin + bandMax) / 2));

    return {
      bandMin: bandMin,
      bandMax: bandMax,
      resolvedLevel: resolvedLevel
    };
  },

  inferBuildTags(roleTags, rankTags, category) {
    const out = [];
    const hasRole = (tag) => Array.isArray(roleTags) && roleTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;

    if (hasRole("guard") || hasRole("knight") || hasRole("soldier")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "martial");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "defense");
    }

    if (hasRole("hunter") || hasRole("archer") || hasRole("scout")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "ranged");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "detection");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "mobility");
    }

    if (hasRole("mage") || hasRole("wizard") || hasRole("priest")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "magic");
    }

    if (hasRole("merchant") || hasRole("barkeep") || hasRole("innkeeper") || hasRole("noble")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "social");
    }

    if (hasRole("blacksmith") || hasRole("smith")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "durable");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "martial");
    }

    if (category === "beast") {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "mobility");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "brute");
    }

    if (category === "monster" || category === "undead" || category === "draconic") {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "brute");
    }

    if (Array.isArray(rankTags) && rankTags.indexOf("leader") !== -1) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "command");
    }

    if (!out.length) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "generalist");
    }

    return out;
  },

  inferLikelyAbilities(buildTags, roleTags, rankTags) {
    const out = [];
    const hasBuild = (tag) => Array.isArray(buildTags) && buildTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;

    if (hasBuild("martial")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "weapon discipline");
    if (hasBuild("defense")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "guarded stance");
    if (hasBuild("ranged")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "aimed shot");
    if (hasBuild("detection")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "tracking sense");
    if (hasBuild("mobility")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "quick reposition");
    if (hasBuild("magic")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "basic spellcasting");
    if (hasBuild("command")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "command presence");
    if (hasBuild("social")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "social leverage");
    if (hasBuild("brute")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "overwhelming force");

    if (!out.length) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "practical action");
    }

    return out.slice(0, 4);
  },

  inferLikelyEquipment(roleTags, buildTags) {
    const out = [];
    const hasRole = (tag) => Array.isArray(roleTags) && roleTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;
    const hasBuild = (tag) => Array.isArray(buildTags) && buildTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;

    if (hasRole("guard") || hasRole("soldier")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "shield");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "sidearm");
    }
    if (hasRole("hunter") || hasRole("archer")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "bow");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "knife");
    }
    if (hasRole("blacksmith") || hasRole("smith")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "hammer");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "heavy apron");
    }
    if (hasRole("barkeep") || hasRole("innkeeper")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "work clothes");
    }
    if (hasRole("merchant")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "coin purse");
    }
    if (hasBuild("magic")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "focus");
    }
    if (hasBuild("brute")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "heavy weapon");
    }

    if (!out.length) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "ordinary gear");
    }

    return out.slice(0, 4);
  },

  deriveStats(level, buildTags, category) {
    const lvl = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(level || 1)));
    const stats = {
      hp: 70 + (lvl * 8),
      maxHp: 70 + (lvl * 8),
      atk: 4 + lvl,
      def: 4 + lvl,
      spd: 4 + lvl,
      intl: 4 + lvl
    };

    const hasBuild = (tag) => Array.isArray(buildTags) && buildTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;

    if (hasBuild("martial")) stats.atk += 2;
    if (hasBuild("defense")) stats.def += 2;
    if (hasBuild("mobility")) stats.spd += 2;
    if (hasBuild("magic")) stats.intl += 3;
    if (hasBuild("durable") || hasBuild("brute")) {
      stats.hp += 16;
      stats.maxHp += 16;
    }

    if (category === "draconic" || category === "monster") {
      stats.hp += 10;
      stats.maxHp += 10;
      stats.atk += 1;
    }

    if (category === "undead") {
      stats.def += 1;
    }

    return stats;
  },

  applyStatsObject(target, nextStats) {
    if (!target || typeof target !== "object") return 0;

    let changes = 0;
    const keys = Object.keys(nextStats || {});
    for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i += 1) {
      const key = keys[i];
      const next = Number(nextStats[key] || 0);
      if (target[key] !== next) {
        target[key] = next;
        changes += 1;
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  getContextSummary(root = state.aidrpg, mode = "normal") {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
    this.init(root);

    const actors = root.actors || {};
    const byId = actors.byId || {};
    const localIds = Array.isArray(actors.localIds) ? actors.localIds : [];
    if (!localIds.length) return "";

    const maxActors = mode === "tiny" ? 2 : (mode === "wide" ? 4 : 3);
    const parts = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < localIds.length && parts.length < maxActors; i += 1) {
      const record = byId[localIds[i]];
      if (!record || !record.identity) continue;

      const name = String(record.identity.name || "").trim();
      if (!name) continue;

      const role = Array.isArray(record.roleTags) && record.roleTags.length
        ? String(record.roleTags[0] || "")
        : String(record.identity.category || "actor");

      const band = record.levelProfile
        ? "lvl " + String(record.levelProfile.bandMin || 1) + "-" + String(record.levelProfile.bandMax || 1)
        : "";

      let summary = name;
      if (role) summary += " [" + role + "]";
      if (mode !== "tiny" && band) summary += " " + band;

      parts.push(summary);
    }

    if (!parts.length) return "";
    return "Nearby actors: " + parts.join(", ");
  },

  replaceSimpleArray(target, nextValues) {
    if (!Array.isArray(target)) return 0;
    const next = Array.isArray(nextValues) ? nextValues.slice() : [];

    let changed = 0;
    if (target.length !== next.length) {
      changed = 1;
    } else {
      for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i += 1) {
        if (target[i] !== next[i]) {
          changed = 1;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) return 0;

    target.splice(0, target.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < next.length; i += 1) {
      target.push(next[i]);
    }

    return 1;
  },

  pushUniqueLocal(list, value) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
    const text = String(value || "").trim();
    if (!text) return 0;
    if (list.indexOf(text) !== -1) return 0;
    list.push(text);
    return 1;
  },

  pushRecentUpdate(list, entry) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list) || !entry || typeof entry !== "object") return 0;

    const last = list.length ? list[list.length - 1] : null;
    if (last && last.actorId === entry.actorId && last.turn === entry.turn) {
      return 0;
    }

    list.push(entry);
    while (list.length > 10) {
      list.shift();
    }

    return 1;
  }
},

    // === REPUTATION SYSTEM ===
    ReputationSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.reputation || typeof root.reputation !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.reputation)) {
      root.reputation = {};
    }

    const reputation = root.reputation;

    if (!reputation.bySubject || typeof reputation.bySubject !== "object" || Array.isArray(reputation.bySubject)) {
      reputation.bySubject = {};
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(reputation.recentChanges)) reputation.recentChanges = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(reputation.rumors)) reputation.rumors = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(reputation.titles)) reputation.titles = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(reputation.pendingReports)) reputation.pendingReports = [];
    if (!reputation.tracking || typeof reputation.tracking !== "object" || Array.isArray(reputation.tracking)) {
      reputation.tracking = {};
    }

    if (typeof reputation.tracking.primarySubjectId !== "string") reputation.tracking.primarySubjectId = "";
    if (typeof reputation.tracking.summaryDirty !== "boolean") reputation.tracking.summaryDirty = false;
    if (typeof reputation.lastChangedTurn !== "number") reputation.lastChangedTurn = 0;

    return reputation;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    let changes = 0;
    changes += this.processKnowledgeDelaySignals(rawText, root);
    changes += this.applyValidatedConsequences(root);

    if (changes > 0) {
      root.reputation.tracking.summaryDirty = true;
      root.reputation.lastChangedTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

      if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
        U.markDirty("reputation", true, root);
      }
      if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
        U.invalidatePackets(root);
      }
      if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
        U.logEvent("Reputation updates: " + changes);
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  processKnowledgeDelaySignals(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const reputation = this.init(root);
    if (!reputation) return 0;

    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const lower = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!lower) return 0;

    let changes = 0;
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

    const hiddenDeed = /\b(?:no one|nobody|none)\s+(?:sees|saw|witnesses|witnessed|knows|knew)\b|\bunwitnessed\b|\bin\s+secret\b|\bsecretly\b|\bhidden\s+deed\b|\balone\s+in\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:dungeon|crypt|barrow|forest|cave)\b/.test(lower);
    const deed = /\b(?:defeat|defeated|slay|slain|kill|killed|rescue|rescued|save|saved|steal|stole|theft|mercy|threat|cruelty|helped|aid)\b/.test(lower);
    const report = /\b(?:report|reported|tell|told|prove|proved|show|showed|bring|brought)\b[^.!?]{0,80}\b(?:guild|guards|town|council|captain|proof|trophy|body|token|evidence|witness)\b/.test(lower);
    const publicSpread = /\b(?:witness|witnesses|saw|seen|word spreads|news spreads|rumors? spread|messenger|announces?|publicly)\b/.test(lower);

    if (hiddenDeed && deed && !report && !publicSpread) {
      const label = this.cleanShortText(raw) || "hidden deed";
      const key = label.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_").slice(0, 48);
      let exists = false;
      for (let i = 0; i < reputation.pendingReports.length; i += 1) {
        if (reputation.pendingReports[i] && reputation.pendingReports[i].key === key) exists = true;
      }
      if (!exists) {
        reputation.pendingReports.push({ key, turn, label, status: "unreported", scope: "hidden" });
        while (reputation.pendingReports.length > 10) reputation.pendingReports.shift();
        changes += 1;
        if (globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils.logEvent === "function") {
          globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils.logEvent("Reputation delay: hidden deed held until report/proof/witness.");
        }
      }
    }

    if (report && reputation.pendingReports.length) {
      const pending = reputation.pendingReports[reputation.pendingReports.length - 1];
      if (pending && pending.status !== "reported") {
        pending.status = "reported";
        pending.reportedTurn = turn;
        const subject = /\bguild\b/.test(lower) ? this.resolveSubject("guild", root) : this.resolveSubject("local_group", root);
        changes += this.applyReputationShift(subject, "positive", "reported/proved deed: " + pending.label, root);
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },


  applyValidatedConsequences(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const Guard = A.OperationGuard;
    const pending = root.pending || {};
    const validated = Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences) ? pending.validatedConsequences : [];
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < validated.length; i += 1) {
      const candidate = validated[i];
      if (!candidate || candidate.category !== "reputation" || candidate._reputationApplied === true) {
        continue;
      }

      const payload = candidate.payload || {};
      const subjectInfo = this.resolveSubject(payload.subject || "local_group", root);
      const direction = this.normalizeDirection(payload.direction || "");

      if (!this.shouldApplyReputationCandidate(direction, candidate.evidence || "", payload, root)) {
        candidate._reputationApplied = true;
        continue;
      }

      const target = subjectInfo.id || "reputation";

      const commit = () => {
        if (candidate.type === "reputation_shift") {
          return this.applyReputationShift(subjectInfo, direction, candidate.evidence || "", root);
        }
        return 0;
      };

      const localChanges =
        Guard && typeof Guard.applyOnce === "function"
          ? Guard.applyOnce({
              kind: "reputation_" + String(candidate.type || "change"),
              target: target,
              payload: {
                subject: subjectInfo.id,
                direction: direction,
                evidence: candidate.evidence || ""
              },
              sourceId: candidate.id || ""
            }, commit, root)
          : commit();

      changes += localChanges;
      candidate._reputationApplied = true;
    }

    return changes;
  },

  applyReputationShift(subjectInfo, direction, evidence, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const reputation = this.init(root);
    if (!reputation || !subjectInfo || !subjectInfo.id) return 0;

    const record = this.ensureSubjectRecord(subjectInfo, root);
    if (!record || !record.mutable) return 0;

    const delta = this.deltaForDirection(direction);
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    let changes = 0;

    if (direction === "rumor_activity") {
      const rumorLabel = this.cleanShortText(evidence) || "rumor activity";
      const rumor = {
        turn: turn,
        subjectId: subjectInfo.id,
        label: rumorLabel,
        text: rumorLabel
      };

      reputation.rumors.push(rumor);
      while (reputation.rumors.length > 20) reputation.rumors.shift();
      changes += 1;

      if (record.mutable.tags.indexOf("rumors_active") === -1) {
        record.mutable.tags.push("rumors_active");
        changes += 1;
      }
    }

    if (delta !== 0) {
      const current = typeof record.mutable.score === "number" ? record.mutable.score : 0;
      const next = this.clampScore(current + delta);
      if (record.mutable.score !== next) {
        record.mutable.score = next;
        changes += 1;
      }

      const nextStanding = this.standingForScore(next);
      if (record.mutable.standing !== nextStanding) {
        record.mutable.standing = nextStanding;
        changes += 1;
      }

      const tag = delta > 0 ? "positive_recent" : "negative_recent";
      if (record.mutable.tags.indexOf(tag) === -1) {
        record.mutable.tags.push(tag);
        changes += 1;
      }
    }

    if (changes > 0) {
      record.mutable.lastChangedTurn = turn;
      reputation.tracking.primarySubjectId = subjectInfo.id;

      reputation.recentChanges.push({
        turn: turn,
        subject: record.identity.displayName,
        subjectId: subjectInfo.id,
        direction: direction,
        delta: delta,
        evidence: this.cleanShortText(evidence)
      });

      while (reputation.recentChanges.length > 16) reputation.recentChanges.shift();
    }

    return changes;
  },

  ensureSubjectRecord(subjectInfo, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const reputation = this.init(root);
