    if (!reputation || !subjectInfo || !subjectInfo.id) return null;

    const id = subjectInfo.id;
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

    if (!reputation.bySubject[id] || typeof reputation.bySubject[id] !== "object" || Array.isArray(reputation.bySubject[id])) {
      reputation.bySubject[id] = {
        identity: {
          id: id,
          displayName: subjectInfo.displayName || id,
          scope: subjectInfo.scope || "local",
          createdTurn: turn
        },
        mutable: {
          score: 0,
          standing: "neutral",
          tags: [],
          notes: [],
          lastChangedTurn: turn
        }
      };
    }

    const record = reputation.bySubject[id];
    if (!record.identity || typeof record.identity !== "object" || Array.isArray(record.identity)) record.identity = {};
    if (!record.mutable || typeof record.mutable !== "object" || Array.isArray(record.mutable)) record.mutable = {};

    if (typeof record.identity.id !== "string") record.identity.id = id;
    if (typeof record.identity.displayName !== "string" || !record.identity.displayName) {
      record.identity.displayName = subjectInfo.displayName || id;
    }
    if (typeof record.identity.scope !== "string") record.identity.scope = subjectInfo.scope || "local";
    if (typeof record.identity.createdTurn !== "number") record.identity.createdTurn = turn;

    if (typeof record.mutable.score !== "number") record.mutable.score = 0;
    if (typeof record.mutable.standing !== "string") record.mutable.standing = this.standingForScore(record.mutable.score);
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.tags)) record.mutable.tags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.notes)) record.mutable.notes = [];
    if (typeof record.mutable.lastChangedTurn !== "number") record.mutable.lastChangedTurn = turn;

    record.mutable.score = this.clampScore(record.mutable.score);
    record.mutable.standing = this.standingForScore(record.mutable.score);

    return record;
  },

  resolveSubject(subject, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const raw = String(subject || "local_group").trim().toLowerCase();
    const scene = root && root.world && root.world.currentScene ? root.world.currentScene : {};
    const sceneLabel = String(scene.displayName || "").trim();
    const parentRef = String(scene.parentLocationRef || (root.player && root.player.locationRef) || "").trim();

    if (raw === "public") {
      return { id: "public", displayName: "public rumor", scope: "public" };
    }

    if (raw === "local_group" || raw === "locals" || raw === "local") {
      const label = sceneLabel || parentRef || "local area";
      const key = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(label) : label.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_");
      return {
        id: "local_" + (key || "area"),
        displayName: label + " locals",
        scope: "local"
      };
    }

    const clean = this.cleanShortText(raw) || "unknown group";
    const key = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(clean) : clean.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_");
    return { id: "subject_" + (key || "group"), displayName: clean, scope: "subject" };
  },

  shouldApplyReputationCandidate(direction, evidence, payload, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cleanDirection = this.normalizeDirection(direction);
    const raw = String(evidence || "");

    if (!raw.trim()) return false;

    if (cleanDirection === "rumor_activity") {
      return this.isStrongPlayerRumorEvidence(raw);
    }

    if (cleanDirection !== "positive" && cleanDirection !== "negative") {
      return false;
    }

    const lowerEvidence = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    const hiddenWithoutSpread = (/(?:no one|nobody|none)\s+(?:sees|saw|witnesses|witnessed|knows|knew)|unwitnessed|in\s+secret|secretly|alone\s+in\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:dungeon|crypt|barrow|forest|cave)/.test(lowerEvidence)) &&
      !/(?:report|reported|prove|proved|proof|witness|witnesses|word spreads|news spreads|rumors? spread|messenger|announces?|publicly)/.test(lowerEvidence);
    if (hiddenWithoutSpread) {
      return false;
    }

    if (cleanDirection === "negative" && this.hasNegatedReputationEvidence(raw)) {
      return false;
    }

    if (this.hasExternalEmotionTarget(raw)) {
      return false;
    }

    if (this.hasPlayerReputationAnchor(raw)) {
      return true;
    }

    // Phase 7u2: allow very clear positive local social reactions when no
    // external target exists. This preserves the rain/storm/bandits blockers
    // above while allowing lines such as "The villagers are respectful and grateful."
    if (cleanDirection === "positive" && this.hasClearLocalPositiveReception(raw)) {
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  },

  hasNegatedReputationEvidence(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!lower) return false;

    return (
      /\b(?:not|no longer|not anymore|no more)\s+(?:suspicious|hostile|afraid|wary|fearful|resentful|angry)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\b(?:less|no longer)\s+(?:wary|suspicious|hostile|afraid|fearful)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\b(?:suspicion|hostility|fear|wariness)\s+(?:fades|ends|lessens|eases)\b/.test(lower)
    );
  },

  hasExternalEmotionTarget(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!lower) return false;

    const match = lower.match(/\b(?:wary|afraid|hostile|suspicious|grateful|thankful|angry|fearful|respectful|trusting|distrustful)\s+(?:of|to|toward|towards|at|for)\s+([^.!?;,]+)/);
    if (!match || !match[1]) return false;

    const target = String(match[1] || "").trim();
    if (!target) return false;

    if (/^(?:you|your)\b/.test(target)) return false;

    return true;
  },

  hasPlayerReputationAnchor(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!lower) return false;

    const actionAnchorNouns = "reputation|name|deed|deeds|action|actions|help|defense|defence|rescue|return|threat|intimidation|violence|mercy|courage|bravery|kindness|cruelty|theft|honesty|dishonesty|warning|sacrifice|aid|service";

    return (
      /\b(?:of|toward|towards|at|to|for)\s+you\b/.test(lower) ||
      new RegExp("\\byour\\s+(?:" + actionAnchorNouns + ")\\b").test(lower) ||
      /\b(?:after|because of|following)\s+your\s+[a-z][a-z'-]*(?:\s+[a-z][a-z'-]*){0,3}\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\b(?:respect|respects|trust|trusts|praise|praises|thank|thanks|cheer|cheers|hail|hails|fear|fears|hate|hates|distrust|distrusts|suspect|suspects|watch|watches|avoid|avoids|welcome|welcomes)\s+you\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\byou\s+(?:are|become|became|seem|seemed|look|looked)\s+(?:trusted|respected|praised|feared|hated|welcomed|liked|known|notorious|infamous)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\bpeople\s+(?:see|saw|view|viewed)\s+you\s+as\b/.test(lower)
    );
  },

  hasClearLocalPositiveReception(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!lower) return false;

    if (/\b(?:of|for|toward|towards|at|to)\s+(?!you\b|your\b)[^.!?;,]+/.test(lower)) {
      return false;
    }

    return /\b(?:locals|townsfolk|villagers|guards|patrons|crowd|people|citizens|merchants)\s+(?:are|seem|become|became)\s+(?:[^.!?;,]{0,40}\b)?(?:grateful|thankful|respect|respects|respectful|trusting|cheer|cheers|cheering|welcoming|approving)\b/.test(lower);
  },

  isStrongPlayerRumorEvidence(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!lower) return false;

    if (!/\b(?:rumors?|rumours?|whispers?|whispered|word spreads|news spreads|people are saying|talk spreads)\b/.test(lower)) {
      return false;
    }

    return (
      /\b(?:rumors?|rumours?)\s+(?:spread|circulate|begin|start)\s+(?:about|of)\s+(?:you|your)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\bword\s+spreads\s+(?:that\s+)?(?:you|your)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\bword\s+spreads\s+(?:about|of)\s+your\s+(?:name|deed|deeds|action|actions|reputation|help|rescue|defense|defence|threat|return)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\bnews\s+(?:spreads\s+)?(?:of|about)\s+your\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\bwhispers?\s+(?:spread|circulate|rise|begin)?\s*(?:about|of)\s+(?:you|your)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\bpeople\s+are\s+saying\s+(?:you|your)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\byour\s+(?:name|deed|deeds|reputation|actions)\s+(?:spreads|is whispered|is discussed|is mentioned)\b/.test(lower)
    );
  },

  normalizeDirection(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (lower === "positive" || lower === "negative" || lower === "rumor_activity") return lower;
    if (/trust|respect|grateful|cheer|help|praise/.test(lower)) return "positive";
    if (/wary|afraid|hostile|suspicious|hate|fear|distrust/.test(lower)) return "negative";
    if (/rumor|whisper|word/.test(lower)) return "rumor_activity";
    return "rumor_activity";
  },

  deltaForDirection(direction) {
    const clean = this.normalizeDirection(direction);
    if (clean === "positive") return 8;
    if (clean === "negative") return -8;
    return 0;
  },

  clampScore(value) {
    const n = Number(value || 0);
    if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
    return Math.max(-100, Math.min(100, Math.round(n)));
  },

  standingForScore(score) {
    const n = this.clampScore(score);
    if (n >= 60) return "beloved";
    if (n >= 30) return "trusted";
    if (n >= 10) return "liked";
    if (n <= -60) return "hated";
    if (n <= -30) return "hostile";
    if (n <= -10) return "wary";
    return "neutral";
  },

  cleanShortText(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .replace(/[<>\[\]{}]/g, "")
      .replace(/[.!?;:]+$/g, "")
      .trim()
      .slice(0, 120);
  },

  getCompactSummary(root = state.aidrpg, mode = "normal") {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
    const reputation = this.init(root);
    if (!reputation || !reputation.bySubject) return "";

    const primaryId = reputation.tracking && reputation.tracking.primarySubjectId ? reputation.tracking.primarySubjectId : "";
    let record = primaryId && reputation.bySubject[primaryId] ? reputation.bySubject[primaryId] : null;

    if (!record) {
      const keys = Object.keys(reputation.bySubject);
      if (keys.length) record = reputation.bySubject[keys[0]];
    }

    if (!record || !record.identity || !record.mutable) return "";

    const name = record.identity.displayName || "local reputation";
    const standing = record.mutable.standing || this.standingForScore(record.mutable.score || 0);
    const score = typeof record.mutable.score === "number" ? record.mutable.score : 0;
    const parts = ["Reputation: " + name + " " + standing + " (" + score + ")"];

    if (mode === "wide" && Array.isArray(record.mutable.tags) && record.mutable.tags.length) {
      parts.push("Tags: " + record.mutable.tags.slice(0, 3).join(", "));
    }

    if (Array.isArray(reputation.pendingReports) && reputation.pendingReports.length) {
      const pending = reputation.pendingReports[reputation.pendingReports.length - 1];
      if (pending && pending.status === "unreported") parts.push("Hidden deed pending report/proof");
    }

    return parts.join(" | ");
  }
},

    // === QUEST LOG SYSTEM ===
    QuestLogSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || Array.isArray(root)) return null;

    if (!root.quests || typeof root.quests !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.quests)) {
      root.quests = {};
    }

    const quests = root.quests;

    if (!Array.isArray(quests.active)) quests.active = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(quests.archived)) quests.archived = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(quests.recentUpdates)) quests.recentUpdates = [];

    if (!quests.tracking || typeof quests.tracking !== "object" || Array.isArray(quests.tracking)) {
      quests.tracking = {};
    }

    if (typeof quests.tracking.primaryQuestId !== "string") quests.tracking.primaryQuestId = "";
    if (typeof quests.tracking.summaryDirty !== "boolean") quests.tracking.summaryDirty = false;
    if (typeof quests.tracking.lastChangedTurn !== "number") quests.tracking.lastChangedTurn = 0;

    this.repairQuestList(quests.active, root);
    this.repairQuestList(quests.archived, root);

    return quests;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    if (!raw.trim()) return 0;

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    let changes = 0;

    changes += this.applyValidatedConsequences(root);

    const events = this.extractDirectQuestEvents(raw, root);
    for (let i = 0; i < events.length; i += 1) {
      changes += this.applyQuestEvent(events[i], root);
    }

    if (changes > 0) {
      root.quests.tracking.summaryDirty = true;
      root.quests.tracking.lastChangedTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

      if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
        U.markDirty("quests", true, root);
      }
      if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
        U.invalidatePackets(root);
      }
      if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
        U.logEvent("Quest updates: " + changes);
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  applyValidatedConsequences(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const pending = root.pending || {};
    const validated = Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences) ? pending.validatedConsequences : [];
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < validated.length; i += 1) {
      const candidate = validated[i];
      if (!candidate || candidate.category !== "quest" || candidate._questApplied === true) {
        continue;
      }

      const payload = candidate.payload || {};
      const status = this.normalizeStatus(payload.status || "");
      if (!status) {
        candidate._questApplied = true;
        continue;
      }

      const evidence = String(candidate.evidence || "");
      if (this.hasQuestNegation(evidence, status)) {
        candidate._questApplied = true;
        continue;
      }

      const event = {
        kind: "status",
        status: status,
        title: this.cleanQuestTitle(payload.title || this.inferQuestTitleFromEvidence(evidence, status)),
        objective: this.cleanQuestObjective(payload.objective || this.inferQuestObjectiveFromEvidence(evidence, status)),
        progress: this.cleanShortText(payload.progress || ""),
        rewardSummary: this.cleanShortText(payload.rewardSummary || this.extractRewardSummary(evidence)),
        evidence: this.cleanShortText(evidence),
        confidence: Number(candidate.confidence || 0),
        sourceType: "validated_quest"
      };

      changes += this.applyQuestEvent(event, root);
      candidate._questApplied = true;
    }

    return changes;
  },

  extractDirectQuestEvents(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const out = [];

    const add = (status, title, objective, evidence, sourceType, confidence) => {
      const cleanStatus = this.normalizeStatus(status);
      if (!cleanStatus) return;

      const ev = String(evidence || raw);
      if (this.hasQuestNegation(ev, cleanStatus)) return;

      const cleanTitle = this.cleanQuestTitle(title || "");
      const cleanObjective = this.cleanQuestObjective(objective || title || "");
      const cleanProgress = cleanStatus === "progress" ? this.cleanShortText(objective || title || ev) : "";

      if ((cleanStatus === "accepted" || cleanStatus === "completed" || cleanStatus === "failed" || cleanStatus === "abandoned") && !cleanTitle && !this.getPrimaryQuest(root)) {
        return;
      }

      out.push({
        kind: cleanStatus === "progress" ? "progress" : "status",
        status: cleanStatus,
        title: cleanTitle,
        objective: cleanObjective,
        progress: cleanProgress,
        rewardSummary: this.cleanShortText(this.extractRewardSummary(ev)),
        evidence: this.cleanShortText(ev),
        confidence: typeof confidence === "number" ? confidence : 0.86,
        sourceType: String(sourceType || "direct_quest")
      });
    };

    const runPatterns = (patterns, status, sourceType) => {
      for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
        let match;
        const re = patterns[i];
        while ((match = re.exec(raw))) {
          const title = match[1] || "";
          add(status, title, title, match[0], sourceType, 0.88);
        }
      }
    };

    runPatterns([
      /\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:accepted|started|added)\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/gi,
      /\bnew\s+(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/gi,
      /\byou\s+(?:accept|accepted|take on|took on)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|job|bounty|contract|task)\s+(?:to|of|called|named)?\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/gi,
      /\byou\s+(?:agree|agreed)\s+to\s+([^.!?;\n]{3,100})\s+(?:as\s+)?(?:a\s+)?(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\b/gi,
      /\b(?:the\s+)?(?:innkeeper|guard captain|guard|captain|merchant|elder|villager|mayor|guildmaster|guild master|patron)\s+(?:asks|asked|hires|hired|tasks|tasked)\s+you\s+(?:to|with)\s+([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/gi
    ], "accepted", "direct_acceptance");

    runPatterns([
      /\b(?:objective|goal)\s+(?:updated|changed|set)\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/gi,
      /\bnext\s+(?:objective|goal)\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/gi,
      /\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:progress|updated|update)\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/gi
    ], "progress", "direct_progress");

    runPatterns([
      /\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:complete|completed|finished|fulfilled)\s*[:\-—]?\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/gi,
      /\byou\s+(?:complete|completed|finish|finished|fulfill|fulfilled)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/gi
    ], "completed", "direct_completion");

    runPatterns([
      /\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:failed|lost)\s*[:\-—]?\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/gi,
      /\byou\s+(?:fail|failed)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/gi
    ], "failed", "direct_failure");

    // Phase 8 u1:
    // Abandoned is terminal like failed, but it must keep its own status.
    // Do not group abandoned under failed or /questlog archives the quest incorrectly.
    runPatterns([
      /\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:abandoned)\s*[:\-—]?\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/gi,
      /\byou\s+(?:abandon|abandoned)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/gi
    ], "abandoned", "direct_abandonment");

    return this.dedupeEvents(out);
  },

  applyQuestEvent(event, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    if (!event || typeof event !== "object") return 0;

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const Guard = A.OperationGuard;
    const status = this.normalizeStatus(event.status || "");
    if (!status) return 0;

    const cleanEvent = {
      kind: String(event.kind || "status"),
      status: status,
      title: this.cleanQuestTitle(event.title || ""),
      objective: this.cleanQuestObjective(event.objective || ""),
      progress: this.cleanShortText(event.progress || ""),
      rewardSummary: this.cleanShortText(event.rewardSummary || ""),
      evidence: this.cleanShortText(event.evidence || ""),
      confidence: Number(event.confidence || 0),
      sourceType: String(event.sourceType || "quest_event")
    };

    if (this.shouldIgnoreQuestEvent(cleanEvent, root)) {
      return 0;
    }

    const target = cleanEvent.title || cleanEvent.objective || cleanEvent.status;
    const commit = () => this.commitQuestEvent(cleanEvent, root);

    return Guard && typeof Guard.applyOnce === "function"
      ? Guard.applyOnce({
          kind: "quest_" + cleanEvent.status,
          target: target,
          payload: cleanEvent,
          sourceId: cleanEvent.evidence
        }, commit, root)
      : commit();
  },

  commitQuestEvent(event, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const quests = this.init(root);
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    let changes = 0;

    if (event.status === "accepted") {
      const title = event.title || event.objective;
      if (!title) return 0;

      const quest = this.ensureQuestRecord(title, event.objective || title, event, root);
      if (!quest) return 0;

      if (quest.status !== "accepted" && quest.status !== "active") {
        quest.status = "accepted";
        changes += 1;
      }

      if (event.objective && quest.objective !== event.objective) {
        quest.objective = event.objective;
        changes += 1;
      }

      if (event.rewardSummary && quest.rewardSummary !== event.rewardSummary) {
        quest.rewardSummary = event.rewardSummary;
        changes += 1;
      }

      changes += this.pushProgressNote(quest, "accepted", event.evidence || event.objective || title, turn);
      changes += this.setPrimaryQuest(quest.id, root);
    } else if (event.status === "progress") {
      const quest = this.findMatchingQuest(event.title || event.objective, root) || this.getPrimaryQuest(root);
      if (!quest) return 0;

      if (event.objective && event.sourceType !== "direct_progress") {
        if (quest.objective !== event.objective) {
          quest.objective = event.objective;
          changes += 1;
        }
      } else if (event.objective && /objective|goal/i.test(event.evidence || "")) {
        if (quest.objective !== event.objective) {
          quest.objective = event.objective;
          changes += 1;
        }
      }

      changes += this.pushProgressNote(quest, "progress", event.progress || event.objective || event.evidence, turn);
    } else if (event.status === "completed" || event.status === "failed" || event.status === "abandoned") {
      let quest = this.findMatchingQuest(event.title, root) || this.getPrimaryQuest(root);

      if (!quest && event.title) {
        quest = this.ensureQuestRecord(event.title, event.objective || event.title, event, root);
      }

      if (!quest) return 0;

      if (quest.status !== event.status) {
        quest.status = event.status;
        changes += 1;
      }

      if (event.rewardSummary && quest.rewardSummary !== event.rewardSummary) {
        quest.rewardSummary = event.rewardSummary;
        changes += 1;
      }

      changes += this.pushProgressNote(quest, event.status, event.evidence || event.title, turn);
      changes += this.archiveQuestIfTerminal(quest.id, root);
    }

    if (changes > 0) {
      this.pushRecentUpdate({
        turn: turn,
        status: event.status,
        title: event.title || event.objective || "quest",
        evidence: event.evidence || ""
      }, root);
    }

    return changes;
  },

  shouldIgnoreQuestEvent(event, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!event || typeof event !== "object") return true;

    const status = this.normalizeStatus(event.status || "");
    if (!status) return true;

    const evidence = String(event.evidence || "");
    if (this.hasQuestNegation(evidence, status)) return true;

    if (status === "accepted") {
      const title = event.title || event.objective;
      if (!title || this.isBadQuestTitle(title)) return true;

      // Do not create quests from pure discussion or future intent.
      if (/\b(?:consider|considering|might|maybe|could|should|talk about|talks about|mentions|mention|heard about)\b/i.test(evidence) && !/\b(?:accepted|agree|agreed|take on|took on|quest accepted|new quest|tasks you|hires you|asks you)\b/i.test(evidence)) {
        return true;
      }
    }

    if ((status === "completed" || status === "failed" || status === "abandoned") && !event.title && !this.getPrimaryQuest(root)) {
      return true;
    }

    if (status === "progress" && !this.getPrimaryQuest(root) && !event.title) {
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  },

  ensureQuestRecord(title, objective, event, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const quests = this.init(root);
    const cleanTitle = this.cleanQuestTitle(title);
    if (!cleanTitle || this.isBadQuestTitle(cleanTitle)) return null;

    const existing = this.findMatchingQuest(cleanTitle, root, true);
    if (existing) {
      return existing;
    }

    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const id = "quest_" + (U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(cleanTitle) : this.fallbackKey(cleanTitle));

    const quest = {
      id: id,
      title: cleanTitle,
      objective: this.cleanQuestObjective(objective || cleanTitle),
      status: "accepted",
      rewardSummary: event && event.rewardSummary ? event.rewardSummary : "",
      tags: [],
      progressNotes: [],
      createdTurn: turn,
      lastChangedTurn: turn,
      sourceEvidence: event && event.evidence ? this.cleanShortText(event.evidence) : ""
    };

    quests.active.push(quest);
    return quest;
  },

  findMatchingQuest(query, root = state.aidrpg, activeOnly = false) {
    const quests = this.init(root);
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const key = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(query || "") : this.fallbackKey(query || "");
    if (!key) return null;

    const lists = activeOnly ? [quests.active] : [quests.active, quests.archived];
    let best = null;
    let bestScore = -1;

    for (let l = 0; l < lists.length; l += 1) {
      const list = Array.isArray(lists[l]) ? lists[l] : [];
      for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) {
        const quest = list[i];
        if (!quest || typeof quest !== "object") continue;

        const titleKey = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(quest.title || "") : this.fallbackKey(quest.title || "");
        const objectiveKey = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(quest.objective || "") : this.fallbackKey(quest.objective || "");
        let score = 0;

        if (titleKey === key) score = 100;
        else if (objectiveKey === key) score = 95;
        else if (titleKey && titleKey.indexOf(key) !== -1 && key.length >= 5) score = 82;
        else if (titleKey && key.indexOf(titleKey) !== -1 && titleKey.length >= 5) score = 78;
        else if (objectiveKey && objectiveKey.indexOf(key) !== -1 && key.length >= 5) score = 72;
        else if (objectiveKey && key.indexOf(objectiveKey) !== -1 && objectiveKey.length >= 5) score = 70;

        if (score > bestScore) {
          bestScore = score;
          best = quest;
        }
      }
    }

    return bestScore >= 70 ? best : null;
  },

  getPrimaryQuest(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const quests = this.init(root);
    const primaryId = quests.tracking && quests.tracking.primaryQuestId ? quests.tracking.primaryQuestId : "";

    if (primaryId) {
      for (let i = 0; i < quests.active.length; i += 1) {
        if (quests.active[i] && quests.active[i].id === primaryId) {
          return quests.active[i];
        }
      }
    }

    return quests.active.length && quests.active[0] ? quests.active[0] : null;
  },

  setPrimaryQuest(questId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const quests = this.init(root);
    const id = String(questId || "").trim();
    if (!id) return 0;
    if (quests.tracking.primaryQuestId === id) return 0;
    quests.tracking.primaryQuestId = id;
    return 1;
  },

  archiveQuestIfTerminal(questId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const quests = this.init(root);
    const id = String(questId || "").trim();
    if (!id) return 0;

    let index = -1;
    for (let i = 0; i < quests.active.length; i += 1) {
      if (quests.active[i] && quests.active[i].id === id) {
        index = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (index === -1) return 0;

    const quest = quests.active.splice(index, 1)[0];
    quest.lastChangedTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : quest.lastChangedTurn || 0;

    let archivedIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 0; i < quests.archived.length; i += 1) {
      if (quests.archived[i] && quests.archived[i].id === id) {
        archivedIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (archivedIndex === -1) {
      quests.archived.push(quest);
      while (quests.archived.length > 30) {
        quests.archived.shift();
      }
    } else {
      quests.archived[archivedIndex] = quest;
    }

    if (quests.tracking.primaryQuestId === id) {
      quests.tracking.primaryQuestId = quests.active.length && quests.active[0] ? quests.active[0].id : "";
    }

    return 1;
  },

  pushProgressNote(quest, status, note, turn) {
    if (!quest || typeof quest !== "object") return 0;
    if (!Array.isArray(quest.progressNotes)) quest.progressNotes = [];

    const text = this.cleanShortText(note || "");
    if (!text) return 0;

    const last = quest.progressNotes.length ? quest.progressNotes[quest.progressNotes.length - 1] : null;
    if (last && last.status === status && last.text === text) {
      return 0;
    }

    quest.progressNotes.push({
      turn: typeof turn === "number" ? turn : 0,
      status: String(status || "progress"),
      text: text
    });

    while (quest.progressNotes.length > 10) {
      quest.progressNotes.shift();
    }

    quest.lastChangedTurn = typeof turn === "number" ? turn : quest.lastChangedTurn || 0;
    return 1;
  },

  pushRecentUpdate(entry, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const quests = this.init(root);
    if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object") return 0;

    const clean = {
      turn: typeof entry.turn === "number" ? entry.turn : 0,
      status: this.normalizeStatus(entry.status || "progress") || "progress",
      title: this.cleanShortText(entry.title || "quest"),
      evidence: this.cleanShortText(entry.evidence || "")
    };

    const last = quests.recentUpdates.length ? quests.recentUpdates[quests.recentUpdates.length - 1] : null;
    if (last && last.turn === clean.turn && last.status === clean.status && last.title === clean.title) {
      return 0;
    }

    quests.recentUpdates.push(clean);
    while (quests.recentUpdates.length > 12) {
      quests.recentUpdates.shift();
    }
    return 1;
  },

  repairQuestList(list, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
    const turn = root && root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = list.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
      const quest = list[i];
      if (!quest || typeof quest !== "object") {
        list.splice(i, 1);
        changes += 1;
        continue;
      }

      if (typeof quest.id !== "string" || !quest.id) {
        quest.id = "quest_" + this.fallbackKey(quest.title || quest.objective || "quest_" + i);
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (typeof quest.title !== "string") quest.title = "";
      if (typeof quest.objective !== "string") quest.objective = quest.title || "";
      if (typeof quest.status !== "string") quest.status = "accepted";
      if (typeof quest.rewardSummary !== "string") quest.rewardSummary = "";
      if (!Array.isArray(quest.tags)) quest.tags = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(quest.progressNotes)) quest.progressNotes = [];
      if (typeof quest.createdTurn !== "number") quest.createdTurn = turn;
      if (typeof quest.lastChangedTurn !== "number") quest.lastChangedTurn = turn;
      if (typeof quest.sourceEvidence !== "string") quest.sourceEvidence = "";
    }

    return changes;
  },

  dedupeEvents(events) {
    const out = [];
    const seen = new Set();

    for (let i = 0; i < events.length; i += 1) {
      const ev = events[i] || {};
      const key = [ev.status || "", this.cleanQuestTitle(ev.title || ""), this.cleanQuestObjective(ev.objective || "")].join("|").toLowerCase();
      if (seen.has(key)) continue;
      seen.add(key);
      out.push(ev);
    }

    return out;
  },

  inferQuestTitleFromEvidence(evidence, status) {
    const raw = String(evidence || "");
    const cleanStatus = this.normalizeStatus(status || "");
    const patterns = [];

    if (cleanStatus === "accepted") {
      patterns.push(/\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:accepted|started|added)\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/i);
      patterns.push(/\bnew\s+(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/i);
      patterns.push(/\byou\s+(?:accept|accepted|take on|took on)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|job|bounty|contract|task)\s+(?:to|of|called|named)?\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/i);
    } else if (cleanStatus === "completed") {
      patterns.push(/\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:complete|completed|finished|fulfilled)\s*[:\-—]?\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/i);
    } else if (cleanStatus === "failed" || cleanStatus === "abandoned") {
      patterns.push(/\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:failed|abandoned|lost)\s*[:\-—]?\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/i);
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const match = raw.match(patterns[i]);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        return this.cleanQuestTitle(match[1]);
      }
    }

    return "";
  },

  inferQuestObjectiveFromEvidence(evidence, status) {
    const title = this.inferQuestTitleFromEvidence(evidence, status);
    if (title) return title;

    const raw = String(evidence || "");
    const match = raw.match(/\b(?:objective|goal)\s+(?:updated|changed|set)?\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return this.cleanQuestObjective(match[1]);
    }

    return "";
  },

  extractRewardSummary(evidence) {
    const raw = String(evidence || "");
    const match = raw.match(/\b(?:reward|payment)\s*[:\-—]?\s*([^.!?;\n]{2,80})/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return this.cleanShortText(match[1]);
    }
    return "";
  },

  hasQuestNegation(value, status) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!lower) return false;

    if (/\b(?:refuse|refused|decline|declined|turn down|turned down|reject|rejected)\b/.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/\b(?:not|never|no longer)\b[^.!?]{0,24}\b(?:accept|accepted|take on|took on|agree|agreed|complete|completed|finish|finished)\b/.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/\b(?:do not|don't|does not|doesn't|did not|didn't)\b[^.!?]{0,24}\b(?:accept|take on|complete|finish)\b/.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/\bnot\s+(?:a|your|the)\s+(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\b/.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/\b(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\s+(?:is|was)\s+not\s+(?:accepted|complete|completed|finished)\b/.test(lower)) return true;

    return false;
  },

  normalizeStatus(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().trim();
    if (!lower) return "";
    if (/^(accepted|accept|started|start|active|added)$/.test(lower)) return "accepted";
    if (/^(completed|complete|finished|finish|fulfilled|fulfill|done)$/.test(lower)) return "completed";
    if (/^(failed|fail|lost)$/.test(lower)) return "failed";
    if (/^(abandoned|abandon)$/.test(lower)) return "abandoned";
    if (/^(progress|updated|update|objective|goal)$/.test(lower)) return "progress";
    return "";
  },

  cleanQuestTitle(value) {
    let text = this.cleanShortText(value)
      .replace(/^(?:to|of|called|named)\s+/i, "")
      .replace(/^(?:the|an|a)\s+(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\s+(?:to|of)?\s*/i, "")
      .replace(/^(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\s+/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+reward\b[\s\S]*$/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+payment\b[\s\S]*$/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();

    if (text.length > 96) text = text.slice(0, 96).trim();
    return text;
  },

  cleanQuestObjective(value) {
    let text = this.cleanShortText(value)
      .replace(/^(?:objective|goal)\s+(?:updated|changed|set)?\s*[:\-—]?\s*/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+reward\b[\s\S]*$/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+payment\b[\s\S]*$/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();

    if (text.length > 120) text = text.slice(0, 120).trim();
    return text;
  },

  cleanShortText(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .replace(/[<>\[\]{}]/g, "")
      .replace(/[.!?;:]+$/g, "")
      .trim()
      .slice(0, 140);
  },

  isBadQuestTitle(value) {
    const lower = this.cleanShortText(value).toLowerCase();
    if (!lower) return true;
    if (lower.length < 3) return true;
    if (lower.split(/\s+/).length > 14) return true;

    const banned = new Set([
      "quest", "task", "job", "contract", "bounty", "the quest", "the task",
      "it", "that", "this", "nothing", "none", "unknown", "accepted", "complete",
      "completed", "failed", "abandoned"
    ]);

    if (banned.has(lower)) return true;
    if (/\b(?:maybe|perhaps|might|could|consider|considering|rumor|rumour|story|tale)\b/.test(lower)) return true;

    return false;
  },

  fallbackKey(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_")
      .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "")
      .replace(/_+/g, "_");
  },

  getCompactSummary(root = state.aidrpg, mode = "normal") {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
    const quests = this.init(root);
    if (!quests || !Array.isArray(quests.active) || !quests.active.length) return "";

    const quest = this.getPrimaryQuest(root) || quests.active[0];
    if (!quest || typeof quest !== "object") return "";

    const parts = ["Quest: " + (quest.title || "active task")];
    if (quest.status) parts.push("Status: " + quest.status);
    if (mode !== "tiny" && quest.objective) parts.push("Goal: " + quest.objective);

    if (mode === "wide" && Array.isArray(quest.progressNotes) && quest.progressNotes.length) {
      const last = quest.progressNotes[quest.progressNotes.length - 1];
      if (last && last.text) parts.push("Latest: " + last.text);
    }

    return parts.join(" | ");
  }
},

    // === CARD SYNC SYSTEM ===
    CardSyncSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || Array.isArray(root)) return null;

    if (!root.cards || typeof root.cards !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cards)) {
      root.cards = {};
    }

    const cards = root.cards;

    if (!cards.config || typeof cards.config !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.config)) {
      cards.config = {};
    }

    const config = cards.config;
    if (typeof config.namespacePrefix !== "string" || !config.namespacePrefix) config.namespacePrefix = "SYS:AIDRPG";
    if (typeof config.liveWrites !== "boolean") config.liveWrites = true;
    if (typeof config.strictCardSync !== "boolean") config.strictCardSync = true;
    if (typeof config.cardSafeMode !== "boolean") config.cardSafeMode = false;
    if (typeof config.maxWritesPerTurn !== "number") config.maxWritesPerTurn = 14;
    if (typeof config.maxCardTextChars !== "number") config.maxCardTextChars = 1200;
    if (typeof config.maxQueueSize !== "number") config.maxQueueSize = 80;

    if (!cards.index || typeof cards.index !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.index)) {
      cards.index = {};
    }
    if (!cards.index.byNamespace || typeof cards.index.byNamespace !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.index.byNamespace)) cards.index.byNamespace = {};
    if (!cards.index.byCardId || typeof cards.index.byCardId !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.index.byCardId)) cards.index.byCardId = {};
    if (typeof cards.index.lastRefreshedTurn !== "number") cards.index.lastRefreshedTurn = 0;

    if (!Array.isArray(cards.dirtyQueue)) cards.dirtyQueue = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(cards.pendingWrites)) cards.pendingWrites = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(cards.recentOperations)) cards.recentOperations = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(cards.dryRunLog)) cards.dryRunLog = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(cards.duplicateRefs)) cards.duplicateRefs = [];
    if (!cards.lastWriteHashes || typeof cards.lastWriteHashes !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.lastWriteHashes)) cards.lastWriteHashes = {};
    if (typeof cards.lastSyncTurn !== "number") cards.lastSyncTurn = 0;

    return cards;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    // Chunk 24 serializes dirty canon state into dry-run specs by default.
    // Live Story Card writes remain double-gated behind /cardsync live on and /cardsync safe off.
    this.refreshIndex(root);
    const queued = this.queueDirtyFromMeta(root);
    const scheduled = this.processDirtyQueue(root);
    return queued + scheduled;
  },

  getStoryCardApi() {
    try {
      if (typeof storyCards !== "undefined" && storyCards) return storyCards;
    } catch (_error) {}

    const g = globalThis || {};
    if (g.storyCards) return g.storyCards;

    try {
      if (typeof info === "object" && info && info.storyCards) return info.storyCards;
    } catch (_error2) {}

    return null;
  },

  getAllCards() {
    const api = this.getStoryCardApi();
    if (!api) return [];

    try {
      if (Array.isArray(api)) return api.slice();
      if (Array.isArray(api.cards)) return api.cards.slice();
      if (typeof api.getCards === "function") {
        const value = api.getCards();
        return Array.isArray(value) ? value.slice() : [];
      }
      if (typeof api.getAll === "function") {
        const value = api.getAll();
        return Array.isArray(value) ? value.slice() : [];
      }
    } catch (_error) {
      return [];
    }

    const out = [];
    try {
      const keys = Object.keys(api);
      for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i += 1) {
        const row = api[keys[i]];
        if (row && typeof row === "object") out.push(row);
      }
    } catch (_error2) {}

    return out;
  },

  makeNamespace(family, id, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root) || {};
    const prefix = cards.config && cards.config.namespacePrefix ? cards.config.namespacePrefix : "SYS:AIDRPG";
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const cleanFamily = U.toKey(String(family || "misc")).toUpperCase();
    const cleanId = U.toKey(String(id || "summary")).toUpperCase();
    if (!cleanFamily || !cleanId) return "";
    return prefix + ":" + cleanFamily + ":" + cleanId;
  },

  makeTitle(namespace, displayName) {
    const ns = String(namespace || "").trim();
    const label = this.cleanCardText(displayName || "", 80);
    if (!ns) return label;
    return label ? ns + " | " + label : ns;
  },

  extractNamespaceFromText(value, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root) || {};
    const prefix = cards.config && cards.config.namespacePrefix ? cards.config.namespacePrefix : "SYS:AIDRPG";
    const text = String(value || "");
    const escaped = prefix.replace(/[-\/\\^$*+?.()|[\]{}]/g, "\\$&");
    const re = new RegExp("\\b" + escaped + ":[A-Z0-9_]+:[A-Z0-9_]+\\b", "i");
    const match = text.match(re);
    return match && match[0] ? match[0].toUpperCase() : "";
  },

  extractNamespaceFromCard(card, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!card || typeof card !== "object") return "";

    const fields = [
      card.title,
      card.name,
      card.entry,
      card.text,
      card.description,
      Array.isArray(card.keys) ? card.keys.join(" ") : card.keys,
      Array.isArray(card.keywords) ? card.keywords.join(" ") : card.keywords
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < fields.length; i += 1) {
      const ns = this.extractNamespaceFromText(fields[i], root);
      if (ns) return ns;
    }

    return "";
  },

  isManagedCard(card, root = state.aidrpg) {
    return !!this.extractNamespaceFromCard(card, root);
  },

  normalizeCardRecord(card, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = card && typeof card === "object" ? card : {};
    const id = String(raw.id || raw.cardId || raw.uuid || raw._id || "").trim();
    const title = String(raw.title || raw.name || "").trim();
    const entry = String(raw.entry || raw.text || raw.description || "").trim();
    const keysValue = Array.isArray(raw.keys) ? raw.keys : Array.isArray(raw.keywords) ? raw.keywords : [];
    const keys = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < keysValue.length; i += 1) {
      const key = String(keysValue[i] || "").trim();
      if (key) keys.push(key);
    }

    return {
      id: id,
      title: title,
      entry: entry,
      keys: keys,
      namespace: this.extractNamespaceFromCard(raw, root),
      raw: raw
    };
  },

  refreshIndex(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards) return 0;

    const all = this.getAllCards();
    const byNamespace = {};
    const byCardId = {};
    const duplicateRefs = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < all.length; i += 1) {
      const normalized = this.normalizeCardRecord(all[i], root);
      if (!normalized.namespace) continue;

      const key = normalized.namespace;
      if (!byNamespace[key]) byNamespace[key] = [];
      byNamespace[key].push({
        id: normalized.id,
        title: normalized.title,
        keys: normalized.keys.slice(0, 8),
        hash: this.stableHash(normalized.entry || normalized.title || key)
      });

      if (normalized.id) {
        byCardId[normalized.id] = key;
      }
    }

    const namespaces = Object.keys(byNamespace);
    for (let i = 0; i < namespaces.length; i += 1) {
      const ns = namespaces[i];
      if (byNamespace[ns].length > 1) duplicateRefs.push(ns);
    }

    cards.index.byNamespace = byNamespace;
    cards.index.byCardId = byCardId;
    cards.index.lastRefreshedTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    cards.duplicateRefs = duplicateRefs;

    return namespaces.length;
  },

  cleanCardText(value, maxChars) {
    const limit = Math.max(40, Math.floor(Number(maxChars || 1200)));
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]+/g, " ")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .replace(/[<>]/g, "")
      .trim()
      .slice(0, limit);
  },

  stableHash(value) {
    if (globalThis.AIDRPG && globalThis.AIDRPG.TurnEngine && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.TurnEngine.stableHash === "function") {
      return globalThis.AIDRPG.TurnEngine.stableHash(value);
    }

    const str = String(value || "");
    let hash = 5381;
    for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i += 1) {
      hash = ((hash << 5) + hash) ^ str.charCodeAt(i);
      hash = hash >>> 0;
    }
    return String(hash);
  },

  makeCardSpec(family, id, displayName, body, keys, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root) || {};
    const namespace = this.makeNamespace(family, id, root);
    const cleanName = this.cleanCardText(displayName || id || family, 80);
    const cleanBody = this.cleanCardText(body || "", cards.config ? cards.config.maxCardTextChars : 1200);
    const cleanKeys = [namespace];

    if (Array.isArray(keys)) {
      for (let i = 0; i < keys.length && cleanKeys.length < 8; i += 1) {
        const key = this.cleanCardText(keys[i], 40);
        if (key && cleanKeys.indexOf(key) === -1) cleanKeys.push(key);
      }
    }

    return {
      namespace: namespace,
      family: String(family || "misc"),
      id: String(id || "summary"),
      title: this.makeTitle(namespace, cleanName),
      entry: namespace + "\n" + cleanBody,
      keys: cleanKeys,
      hash: this.stableHash(cleanBody + "|" + cleanKeys.join(","))
    };
  },

  isLiveWritesEnabled(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards || !cards.config) return false;
    return cards.config.liveWrites === true && cards.config.cardSafeMode === false;
  },

  setLiveWrites(enabled, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards || !cards.config) return 0;

    const next = enabled === true;
    if (cards.config.liveWrites === next) return 0;

    cards.config.liveWrites = next;
    this.recordOperation("setLiveWrites", {
      mode: next ? "armed" : "dry_run",
      message: next ? "live writes armed; safe mode must also be off" : "live writes disabled"
    }, root);
    return 1;
  },

  setCardSafeMode(enabled, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards || !cards.config) return 0;

    const next = enabled !== false;
    if (cards.config.cardSafeMode === next) return 0;

    cards.config.cardSafeMode = next;
    this.recordOperation("setCardSafeMode", {
      mode: next ? "safe" : "armed",
      message: next ? "safe mode enabled" : "safe mode disabled; live writes require liveWrites on"
    }, root);
    return 1;
  },

  buildStoryCardPayload(spec, existingCard) {
    const cleanSpec = spec && typeof spec === "object" ? spec : {};
    const existing = existingCard && typeof existingCard === "object" ? existingCard : {};
    const raw = existing.raw && typeof existing.raw === "object" ? existing.raw : existing;
    const id = String(existing.id || raw.id || raw.cardId || raw.uuid || raw._id || "").trim();
    const title = String(cleanSpec.title || existing.title || raw.title || raw.name || cleanSpec.namespace || "").trim();
    const entry = String(cleanSpec.entry || existing.entry || raw.entry || raw.text || raw.description || "").trim();
    const keys = Array.isArray(cleanSpec.keys) ? cleanSpec.keys.slice(0, 8) : [];

    const payload = {
      title: title,
      name: title,
      entry: entry,
      text: entry,
      description: entry,
      keys: keys,
      keywords: keys.slice(),
      namespace: String(cleanSpec.namespace || existing.namespace || ""),
      aidrpgManaged: true
    };

    if (id) {
      payload.id = id;
      payload.cardId = id;
    }

    return payload;
  },

  callStoryCardMethod(methodNames, argSets, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const sources = [];
    const api = this.getStoryCardApi();
    if (api && typeof api === "object") sources.push({ label: "storyCards", api: api });

    try {
      if (typeof globalThis === "object" && globalThis) sources.push({ label: "globalThis", api: globalThis });
    } catch (_error) {}

    try {
      if (typeof info === "object" && info && info.storyCards && info.storyCards !== api) {
        sources.push({ label: "info.storyCards", api: info.storyCards });
      }
    } catch (_error2) {}

    const names = Array.isArray(methodNames) ? methodNames : [];
    const calls = Array.isArray(argSets) ? argSets : [];
    const errors = [];

    for (let s = 0; s < sources.length; s += 1) {
      const source = sources[s];
      for (let n = 0; n < names.length; n += 1) {
        const name = names[n];
        const fn = source.api && source.api[name];
        if (typeof fn !== "function") continue;

        for (let a = 0; a < calls.length; a += 1) {
          const args = Array.isArray(calls[a]) ? calls[a] : [calls[a]];
          try {
            const result = fn.apply(source.api, args);
            return {
              ok: true,
              result: result,
              method: String(name || ""),
              source: source.label
            };
          } catch (error) {
            errors.push(String(name || "method") + ": " + (error && error.message ? error.message : String(error)));
          }
        }
      }
    }

    return {
      ok: false,
      error: errors.length ? errors.slice(-2).join(" | ") : "no compatible Story Card write method found"
    };
  },

  tryLiveAddCard(spec, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const payload = this.buildStoryCardPayload(spec, null);
    return this.callStoryCardMethod(
      ["addStoryCard", "addCard", "createStoryCard", "createCard", "create", "add"],
      [[payload]],
      root
    );
  },

  tryLiveUpdateCard(card, spec, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const payload = this.buildStoryCardPayload(spec, card);
    const id = String(payload.id || payload.cardId || "").trim();
    const argSets = id ? [[id, payload], [payload]] : [[payload]];

    return this.callStoryCardMethod(
      ["updateStoryCard", "updateCard", "editStoryCard", "editCard", "update", "set"],
      argSets,
      root
    );
  },

  tryLiveRemoveCard(card, reason, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const normalized = this.normalizeCardRecord(card, root);
    const raw = normalized.raw || card;
    const id = String(normalized.id || "").trim();
    const argSets = id ? [[id], [raw]] : [[raw]];

    return this.callStoryCardMethod(
      ["removeStoryCard", "removeCard", "deleteStoryCard", "deleteCard", "remove"],
      argSets,
      root
    );
  },

  recordOperation(action, detail, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards) return 0;

    const row = detail && typeof detail === "object" ? detail : {};
    cards.recentOperations.push({
      turn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0,
      action: String(action || "operation"),
      namespace: String(row.namespace || ""),
      cardId: String(row.cardId || row.id || ""),
      mode: String(row.mode || (this.isLiveWritesEnabled(root) ? "live" : "dry_run")),
      message: this.cleanCardText(row.message || "", 140)
    });

    while (cards.recentOperations.length > 24) cards.recentOperations.shift();
    return 1;
  },

  pushPendingWrite(kind, spec, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards || !spec || typeof spec !== "object" || !spec.namespace) return 0;

    const hash = spec.hash || this.stableHash((spec.entry || "") + "|" + (spec.title || ""));
    for (let i = 0; i < cards.pendingWrites.length; i += 1) {
      const row = cards.pendingWrites[i];
      if (row && row.kind === kind && row.namespace === spec.namespace && row.hash === hash) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    const row = {
      turn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0,
      kind: String(kind || "write"),
      namespace: spec.namespace,
      title: spec.title || "",
      hash: hash
    };

    cards.pendingWrites.push(row);

    cards.dryRunLog.push({
      turn: row.turn,
      kind: row.kind,
      namespace: row.namespace,
      title: row.title,
      hash: row.hash,
      mode: "dry_run"
    });

    while (cards.pendingWrites.length > 80) cards.pendingWrites.shift();
    while (cards.dryRunLog.length > 80) cards.dryRunLog.shift();
    return 1;
  },

  safeFindCardsByNamespace(namespace, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const ns = String(namespace || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!ns) return [];

    const all = this.getAllCards();
    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < all.length; i += 1) {
      const normalized = this.normalizeCardRecord(all[i], root);
      if (normalized.namespace === ns) out.push(normalized);
    }
    return out;
  },

  safeFindCard(namespace, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const matches = this.safeFindCardsByNamespace(namespace, root);
    if (!matches.length) return null;
    return matches[0];
  },

  safeAddCard(spec, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cleanSpec = spec && typeof spec === "object" ? spec : null;
    if (!cleanSpec || !cleanSpec.namespace) return null;

    if (!this.isLiveWritesEnabled(root)) {
      this.pushPendingWrite("add", cleanSpec, root);
      this.recordOperation("safeAddCard", { namespace: cleanSpec.namespace, mode: "dry_run", message: "live writes disabled" }, root);
      return null;
    }

    const liveResult = this.tryLiveAddCard(cleanSpec, root);
    if (liveResult && liveResult.ok) {
      this.recordOperation("safeAddCard", {
        namespace: cleanSpec.namespace,
        mode: "live",
        message: "created via " + liveResult.source + "." + liveResult.method
      }, root);
      this.refreshIndex(root);
      return liveResult.result || null;
    }

    this.pushPendingWrite("add", cleanSpec, root);
    this.recordOperation("safeAddCard", {
      namespace: cleanSpec.namespace,
      mode: "blocked",
      message: liveResult && liveResult.error ? liveResult.error : "live add failed"
    }, root);
    return null;
  },

  safeUpdateCard(card, spec, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cleanSpec = spec && typeof spec === "object" ? spec : null;
    if (!cleanSpec || !cleanSpec.namespace) return null;

    if (!this.isLiveWritesEnabled(root)) {
      this.pushPendingWrite("update", cleanSpec, root);
      this.recordOperation("safeUpdateCard", { namespace: cleanSpec.namespace, cardId: card && card.id, mode: "dry_run", message: "live writes disabled" }, root);
      return null;
    }

    const liveResult = this.tryLiveUpdateCard(card, cleanSpec, root);
    if (liveResult && liveResult.ok) {
      this.recordOperation("safeUpdateCard", {
        namespace: cleanSpec.namespace,
        cardId: card && card.id,
        mode: "live",
        message: "updated via " + liveResult.source + "." + liveResult.method
      }, root);
      this.refreshIndex(root);
      return liveResult.result || card || null;
    }

    this.pushPendingWrite("update", cleanSpec, root);
    this.recordOperation("safeUpdateCard", {
      namespace: cleanSpec.namespace,
      cardId: card && card.id,
      mode: "blocked",
      message: liveResult && liveResult.error ? liveResult.error : "live update failed"
    }, root);
    return null;
  },

  safeRemoveCard(card, reason, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const normalized = this.normalizeCardRecord(card, root);
    if (!normalized.namespace && !normalized.id) return 0;

    if (!this.isLiveWritesEnabled(root)) {
      const spec = { namespace: normalized.namespace || String(normalized.id || ""), title: normalized.title || "", hash: this.stableHash(reason || "remove") };
      this.pushPendingWrite("remove", spec, root);
      this.recordOperation("safeRemoveCard", { namespace: normalized.namespace, cardId: normalized.id, mode: "dry_run", message: reason || "remove requested" }, root);
      return 0;
    }

    const liveResult = this.tryLiveRemoveCard(card, reason, root);
    if (liveResult && liveResult.ok) {
      this.recordOperation("safeRemoveCard", {
        namespace: normalized.namespace,
        cardId: normalized.id,
        mode: "live",
        message: "removed via " + liveResult.source + "." + liveResult.method
      }, root);
      this.refreshIndex(root);
      return 1;
    }

    const spec = { namespace: normalized.namespace || String(normalized.id || ""), title: normalized.title || "", hash: this.stableHash(reason || "remove") };
    this.pushPendingWrite("remove", spec, root);
    this.recordOperation("safeRemoveCard", {
      namespace: normalized.namespace,
      cardId: normalized.id,
      mode: "blocked",
      message: liveResult && liveResult.error ? liveResult.error : "live remove failed"
    }, root);
    return 0;
  },

  safeRenameDuplicateCard(card, namespace, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const ns = String(namespace || this.extractNamespaceFromCard(card, root) || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!ns) return 0;

    this.recordOperation("safeRenameDuplicateCard", { namespace: ns, cardId: card && (card.id || card.cardId || ""), mode: "dry_run", message: "duplicate managed card detected" }, root);
    const spec = { namespace: ns, title: ns + " | duplicate pending manual review", hash: this.stableHash("duplicate:" + ns) };
    return this.pushPendingWrite("rename_duplicate", spec, root);
  },

  safeEnsureSingletonCard(spec, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cleanSpec = spec && typeof spec === "object" ? spec : null;
    if (!cleanSpec || !cleanSpec.namespace) return null;

    const matches = this.safeFindCardsByNamespace(cleanSpec.namespace, root);
    if (!matches.length) {
      this.safeAddCard(cleanSpec, root);
      return null;
    }

    const primary = matches[0];
    for (let i = 1; i < matches.length; i += 1) {
      this.safeRenameDuplicateCard(matches[i].raw || matches[i], cleanSpec.namespace, root);
    }

    const currentHash = this.stableHash(primary.entry || "");
    if (currentHash !== cleanSpec.hash) {
      this.safeUpdateCard(primary, cleanSpec, root);
    }

    return primary;
  },

  queueDirty(family, id, reason, payload, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards) return 0;

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const cleanFamily = U.toKey(String(family || "misc"));
    const cleanId = U.toKey(String(id || "summary"));
    if (!cleanFamily || !cleanId) return 0;

    const key = cleanFamily + ":" + cleanId;
    for (let i = 0; i < cards.dirtyQueue.length; i += 1) {
      const row = cards.dirtyQueue[i];
      if (row && row.key === key) {
        row.reason = String(reason || row.reason || "dirty");
        row.turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
        return 0;
      }
    }

    cards.dirtyQueue.push({
      key: key,
      family: cleanFamily,
      id: cleanId,
      namespace: this.makeNamespace(cleanFamily, cleanId, root),
      reason: String(reason || "dirty"),
      turn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0,
      payloadHash: this.stableHash(payload == null ? "" : JSON.stringify(payload))
    });

    while (cards.dirtyQueue.length > cards.config.maxQueueSize) cards.dirtyQueue.shift();
    return 1;
  },

  queueDirtyFromMeta(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const dirty = root.meta && root.meta.dirty && typeof root.meta.dirty === "object" ? root.meta.dirty : {};
    let changes = 0;

    if (dirty.player === true) changes += this.queueDirty("player", "summary", "player_dirty", null, root);
    if (dirty.reputation === true) changes += this.queueDirty("reputation", "summary", "reputation_dirty", null, root);
    if (dirty.quests === true) changes += this.queueDirty("quest", "summary", "quests_dirty", null, root);
    if (dirty.time === true) changes += this.queueDirty("time", "summary", "time_dirty", null, root);

    const arrayMappings = [
      { key: "abilities", family: "ability" },
      { key: "items", family: "item" },
      { key: "actors", family: "actor" },
      { key: "entities", family: "entity" },
      { key: "routes", family: "route" }
    ];

    for (let m = 0; m < arrayMappings.length; m += 1) {
      const map = arrayMappings[m];
      const list = Array.isArray(dirty[map.key]) ? dirty[map.key] : [];
      for (let i = 0; i < list.length && i < 24; i += 1) {
        changes += this.queueDirty(map.family, list[i], map.key + "_dirty", null, root);
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },


  processDirtyQueue(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards || !Array.isArray(cards.dirtyQueue) || !cards.dirtyQueue.length) return 0;

    const max = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(cards.config && cards.config.maxWritesPerTurn ? cards.config.maxWritesPerTurn : 2)));
    let processed = 0;
    let scheduled = 0;
    const remaining = [];

    while (cards.dirtyQueue.length) {
      const row = cards.dirtyQueue.shift();
      if (!row || typeof row !== "object") continue;

      if (processed >= max) {
        remaining.push(row);
        continue;
      }

      const spec = this.buildCardSpecForDirtyTarget(row, root);
      processed += 1;

      if (spec && spec.namespace) {
        this.safeEnsureSingletonCard(spec, root);
        this.clearDirtySourceForRow(row, root);
        scheduled += 1;
      } else {
        this.recordOperation("processDirtyQueue", {
          namespace: String(row.namespace || ""),
          mode: "skipped",
          message: "no serializable canon state for " + String(row.family || "unknown") + ":" + String(row.id || "unknown")
        }, root);
      }
    }

    while (remaining.length) {
      cards.dirtyQueue.unshift(remaining.pop());
    }

    if (scheduled > 0) {
      cards.lastSyncTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    }

    return scheduled;
  },

  clearDirtySourceForRow(row, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || !row || typeof row !== "object") return 0;

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    if (U && typeof U.ensureDirtyShape === "function") {
      U.ensureDirtyShape(root);
    }

    const dirty = root.meta && root.meta.dirty && typeof root.meta.dirty === "object" ? root.meta.dirty : null;
    if (!dirty) return 0;

    const family = String(row.family || "").toLowerCase();
    const id = String(row.id || "").trim();
    const cleanId = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(id) : id;
    let changes = 0;

    const clearBoolean = (key) => {
      if (dirty[key] === true) {
        dirty[key] = false;
        changes += 1;
      }
    };

    const clearArrayValue = (key, value) => {
      if (!Array.isArray(dirty[key])) return;

      const before = dirty[key].length;
      const target = String(value || "");
      const targetKey = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(target) : target;

      dirty[key] = dirty[key].filter(function (entry) {
        const entryText = String(entry || "");
        const entryKey = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(entryText) : entryText;
        return entryText !== target && entryKey !== targetKey;
      });

      if (dirty[key].length !== before) {
        changes += 1;
      }
    };

    if (family === "player") {
      clearBoolean("player");
    } else if (family === "reputation") {
      clearBoolean("reputation");
    } else if (family === "quest") {
      clearBoolean("quests");
    } else if (family === "time") {
      clearBoolean("time");
    } else if (family === "ability") {
      clearArrayValue("abilities", cleanId || id);
    } else if (family === "item") {
      clearArrayValue("items", cleanId || id);
    } else if (family === "actor") {
      clearArrayValue("actors", cleanId || id);
    } else if (family === "entity") {
      clearArrayValue("entities", cleanId || id);
    } else if (family === "route") {
      clearArrayValue("routes", cleanId || id);
    }

    return changes;
  },

  buildCardSpecForDirtyTarget(row, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const target = row && typeof row === "object" ? row : {};
    const family = String(target.family || "").toLowerCase();
    const id = String(target.id || "summary");

    if (!family) return null;

    if (family === "player") return this.serializePlayerCard(root);
    if (family === "reputation") return this.serializeReputationCard(root);
    if (family === "quest") return this.serializeQuestCard(root);
    if (family === "time") return this.serializeTimeCard(root);
    if (family === "ability") return this.serializeAbilityCard(id, root);
    if (family === "item") return this.serializeItemCard(id, root);
    if (family === "actor") return this.serializeActorCard(id, root);
    if (family === "entity") return this.serializeEntityCard(id, root);
    if (family === "route") return this.serializeRouteCard(id, root);

    return null;
  },

  serializePlayerCard(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
    const lines = [];

    if (A.PlayerSystem && typeof A.PlayerSystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      lines.push(A.PlayerSystem.getCompactSummary(root.player));
    }
    if (A.BuildIdentitySystem && typeof A.BuildIdentitySystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      lines.push(A.BuildIdentitySystem.getCompactSummary(root.build));
    }
    if (A.AbilitySystem && typeof A.AbilitySystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      const abilityLine = A.AbilitySystem.getCompactSummary(root, 3);
      if (abilityLine) lines.push(abilityLine);
    }
    if (A.PersistentTraitSystem && typeof A.PersistentTraitSystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      const traitLine = A.PersistentTraitSystem.getCompactSummary(root, 3);
      if (traitLine) lines.push(traitLine);
    }
    if (A.InventorySystem && typeof A.InventorySystem.getVisibleCarrySummary === "function") {
      lines.push("Visible Carry: " + A.InventorySystem.getVisibleCarrySummary(root));
    }
    if (A.SceneStateSystem && typeof A.SceneStateSystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      lines.push(A.SceneStateSystem.getCompactSummary(root));
    }

    return this.serializeSummaryCard("player", "summary", "Player Summary", lines, root);
  },

  serializeReputationCard(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
    const lines = [];

    if (A.ReputationSystem && typeof A.ReputationSystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      const summary = A.ReputationSystem.getCompactSummary(root, "wide");
      if (summary) lines.push(summary);
    }

    const reputation = root.reputation || {};
    const rumors = Array.isArray(reputation.rumors) ? reputation.rumors : [];
    if (rumors.length) {
      const recent = rumors.slice(Math.max(0, rumors.length - 3)).map(function (r) {
        return r && typeof r === "object" ? (r.label || r.text || "") : String(r || "");
      }).filter(Boolean);
      if (recent.length) lines.push("Recent Rumors: " + recent.join(" | "));
    }

    if (!lines.length) return null;
    return this.serializeSummaryCard("reputation", "summary", "Reputation Summary", lines, root);
  },

  serializeQuestCard(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
    const lines = [];

    if (A.QuestLogSystem && typeof A.QuestLogSystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      const summary = A.QuestLogSystem.getCompactSummary(root, "wide");
      if (summary) lines.push(summary);
    }

    const quests = root.quests || {};
    const archived = Array.isArray(quests.archived) ? quests.archived : [];
    if (archived.length) {
      const last = archived[archived.length - 1] || {};
      if (last.title) lines.push("Last Archived: " + last.title + " (" + (last.status || "archived") + ")");
    }

    if (!lines.length) return null;
    return this.serializeSummaryCard("quest", "summary", "Quest Summary", lines, root);
  },

  serializeTimeCard(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
    const lines = [];

    if (A.TimeTravelSystem && typeof A.TimeTravelSystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      const summary = A.TimeTravelSystem.getCompactSummary(root);
      if (summary) lines.push(summary);
    }

    if (!lines.length) return null;
    return this.serializeSummaryCard("time", "summary", "Time Summary", lines, root);
  },

  serializeAbilityCard(id, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const abilities = root.abilities || {};
    const byId = abilities.byId || {};
    let record = byId[id];

    if ((!record || typeof record !== "object") && id === "summary") {
      const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
      const summary = A.AbilitySystem && typeof A.AbilitySystem.getCompactSummary === "function"
        ? A.AbilitySystem.getCompactSummary(root, 5)
        : "";
      return summary ? this.serializeSummaryCard("ability", "summary", "Ability Summary", [summary], root) : null;
    }

    if (!record || typeof record !== "object") return null;

    const identity = record.identity || {};
    const meaning = record.meaning || {};
    const progression = record.progression || {};
    const observation = record.observation || {};
    const name = identity.name || id;
    const lines = [
      "Name: " + name,
      "Type: " + (identity.type || identity.kind || "ability"),
      "Mastery: " + (progression.masteryState || "novice")
    ];

    if (meaning.intendedFunction) lines.push("Function: " + meaning.intendedFunction);
    if (Array.isArray(meaning.keyPhrases) && meaning.keyPhrases.length) lines.push("Phrases: " + meaning.keyPhrases.slice(0, 5).join(", "));
    if (Array.isArray(observation.semanticTags) && observation.semanticTags.length) lines.push("Observed: " + observation.semanticTags.slice(0, 5).join(", "));

    return this.serializeSummaryCard("ability", id, name, lines, root);
  },

  serializeItemCard(id, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const items = root.items || {};
    const byId = items.byId || {};

    if (id === "summary" || id === "currency") {
      const owned = items.ownership && Array.isArray(items.ownership.player) ? items.ownership.player : [];
      const names = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < owned.length && names.length < 8; i += 1) {
        const item = byId[owned[i]];
        if (!item || typeof item !== "object") continue;
        names.push(item.displayName || item.baseType || owned[i]);
      }
      const currency = items.currency && items.currency.byType ? items.currency.byType : {};
      const lines = [
        "Owned Items: " + (names.length ? names.join(", ") : "none"),
        "Currency: copper " + (currency.copper || 0) + ", silver " + (currency.silver || 0) + ", gold " + (currency.gold || 0)
      ];
      return this.serializeSummaryCard("item", "summary", "Inventory Summary", lines, root);
    }

    const item = byId[id];
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return null;

    const display = item.displayName || item.baseType || id;
    const condition = item.condition || {};
    const ownership = item.ownership || {};
    const lines = [
      "Item: " + display,
      "Base: " + (item.baseType || "item"),
      "Quantity: " + (item.quantity || 1),
      "Holder: " + (ownership.holder || "player"),
      "Visibility: " + (ownership.visibility || "packed")
    ];

    if (item.quality) lines.push("Quality: " + item.quality);
    if (Array.isArray(item.descriptors) && item.descriptors.length) lines.push("Descriptors: " + item.descriptors.slice(0, 5).join(", "));
    if (Array.isArray(condition.stateTags) && condition.stateTags.length) lines.push("Condition: " + condition.stateTags.slice(0, 5).join(", "));

    return this.serializeSummaryCard("item", id, display, lines, root);
  },

  serializeActorCard(id, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const actors = root.actors || {};
    const byId = actors.byId || {};
    const actor = byId[id];
    if (!actor || typeof actor !== "object") return null;

    const identity = actor.identity || actor;
    const mutable = actor.mutable || {};
    const name = identity.displayName || identity.name || id;
    const lines = ["Actor: " + name];
    if (identity.kind) lines.push("Kind: " + identity.kind);
    if (Array.isArray(mutable.tags) && mutable.tags.length) lines.push("Tags: " + mutable.tags.slice(0, 8).join(", "));
    if (Array.isArray(mutable.relationshipTags) && mutable.relationshipTags.length) lines.push("Relations: " + mutable.relationshipTags.slice(0, 6).join(", "));

    if (globalThis.AIDRPG.MajorActorMemorySystem && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.MajorActorMemorySystem.getMemorySummaryForActor === "function") {
      const memoryLine = globalThis.AIDRPG.MajorActorMemorySystem.getMemorySummaryForActor(id, root, "card");
      if (memoryLine) lines.push(memoryLine);
    }

    return this.serializeSummaryCard("actor", id, name, lines, root);
  },

  serializeEntityCard(id, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const world = root.world || {};
    const entities = world.entitiesById || {};
    const locations = world.locationsById || {};
    const record = entities[id] || locations[id];
    if (!record || typeof record !== "object") return null;

    const identity = record.identity || {};
    const mutable = record.mutable || {};
    const links = record.links || {};
    const name = identity.displayName || id;
    const lines = [
      "Entity: " + name,
      "Kind: " + (identity.kind || "entity")
    ];

    if (links.parentLocationRef) lines.push("Parent: " + links.parentLocationRef);
    if (Array.isArray(mutable.conditionTags) && mutable.conditionTags.length) lines.push("State: " + mutable.conditionTags.slice(0, 8).join(", "));
    if (Array.isArray(mutable.hazardTags) && mutable.hazardTags.length) lines.push("Hazards: " + mutable.hazardTags.slice(0, 8).join(", "));
    if (Array.isArray(mutable.exitHints) && mutable.exitHints.length) lines.push("Exits: " + mutable.exitHints.slice(0, 8).join(", "));

    return this.serializeSummaryCard("entity", id, name, lines, root);
  },

  serializeRouteCard(id, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const world = root.world || {};
    const routes = world.routesById || {};
    const route = routes[id];
    if (!route || typeof route !== "object") return null;

    const identity = route.identity || {};
    const mutable = route.mutable || {};
    const name = identity.id || id;
    const lines = [
      "Route: " + name,
      "From: " + (mutable.fromRef || "unknown"),
      "To: " + (mutable.toRef || "unknown")
    ];
    if (Array.isArray(mutable.travelHints) && mutable.travelHints.length) lines.push("Travel: " + mutable.travelHints.slice(0, 5).join(", "));
    if (Array.isArray(mutable.hazardTags) && mutable.hazardTags.length) lines.push("Hazards: " + mutable.hazardTags.slice(0, 5).join(", "));

    return this.serializeSummaryCard("route", id, name, lines, root);
  },

  clearDirtyQueue(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards) return 0;
    const count = cards.dirtyQueue.length;
    cards.dirtyQueue = [];
    return count;
  },

  serializeSummaryCard(family, id, displayName, lines, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const safeLines = [];
    if (Array.isArray(lines)) {
      for (let i = 0; i < lines.length && i < 12; i += 1) {
        const line = this.cleanCardText(lines[i], 180);
        if (line) safeLines.push(line);
      }
    }

    return this.makeCardSpec(family, id, displayName, safeLines.join("\n"), [displayName], root);
  }
},

    // === CONTEXT PACKET SYSTEM ===
ContextPacketSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
      root.cache = {};
    }

    if (!root.meta || typeof root.meta !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.meta)) {
      root.meta = {};
    }

    if (typeof root.meta.mode !== "string" || !root.meta.mode) {
      root.meta.mode = "normal";
    }

    if (typeof root.cache.priorityPacket !== "string") root.cache.priorityPacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.scenePacket !== "string") root.cache.scenePacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.playerPacket !== "string") root.cache.playerPacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.timePacket !== "string") root.cache.timePacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.actorPacket !== "string") root.cache.actorPacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.reputationPacket !== "string") root.cache.reputationPacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.questPacket !== "string") root.cache.questPacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.worldPacket !== "string") root.cache.worldPacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.mapPacket !== "string") root.cache.mapPacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.recentEventPacket !== "string") root.cache.recentEventPacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.contextPacket !== "string") root.cache.contextPacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.packetMode !== "string") root.cache.packetMode = "normal";

    return root.cache;
  },

  buildContext(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return String(rawText || "");
    this.init(root);

    const mode = this.resolveMode(rawText, root);
    root.meta.mode = mode;
    root.cache.packetMode = mode;

    this.refreshCache(mode, root);

    const packetText = this.composePacketText(mode, rawText, root);
    if (!packetText) {
      return String(rawText || "");
    }

    return packetText + "\n\n" + String(rawText || "");
  },

  resolveMode(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
