    const preferred = root.meta && typeof root.meta.mode === "string"
      ? root.meta.mode.trim().toLowerCase()
      : "";

    if (preferred === "tiny" || preferred === "normal" || preferred === "wide") {
      return preferred;
    }

    const maxChars = this.getMaxChars();
    if (maxChars <= 1400) return "tiny";
    if (maxChars >= 6000) return "wide";
    return "normal";
  },

  getMaxChars() {
    if (typeof info === "object" && info && typeof info.maxChars === "number" && Number.isFinite(info.maxChars)) {
      return Math.max(800, Math.floor(Math.abs(info.maxChars)));
    }
    return 2000;
  },

  getBudget(mode, rawText) {
    const maxChars = this.getMaxChars();
    const baseLen = String(rawText || "").length;
    const freeSpace = Math.max(120, maxChars - baseLen);

    let ratio = 0.32;
    let hardMin = 360;
    let hardMax = 960;

    if (mode === "tiny") {
      ratio = 0.22;
      hardMin = 240;
      hardMax = 520;
    } else if (mode === "wide") {
      ratio = 0.42;
      hardMin = 560;
      hardMax = 1500;
    }

    let budget = Math.floor(maxChars * ratio);
    if (budget < hardMin) budget = hardMin;
    if (budget > hardMax) budget = hardMax;

    const freeCap = Math.max(160, Math.floor(freeSpace * 0.72));
    if (budget > freeCap) budget = freeCap;

    return budget;
  },

  refreshCache(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return;
    this.init(root);

    root.cache.priorityPacket = this.makePriorityPacket(mode, root);
    root.cache.scenePacket = this.makeScenePacket(mode, root);
    root.cache.playerPacket = this.makePlayerPacket(mode, root);
    root.cache.timePacket = this.makeTimePacket(mode, root);
    root.cache.actorPacket = this.makeActorPacket(mode, root);
    root.cache.reputationPacket = this.makeReputationPacket(mode, root);
    root.cache.questPacket = this.makeQuestPacket(mode, root);
    root.cache.worldPacket = this.makeWorldPacket(mode, root);
    root.cache.mapPacket = this.makeMapPacket(mode, root);
    root.cache.recentEventPacket = this.makeRecentEventPacket(mode, root);
  },

  composePacketText(mode, rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";

    const budget = this.getBudget(mode, rawText);
    const packets = this.getPacketPriorityList(mode, root);

    const lines = ["Current truth:"];
    let used = lines[0].length;

    for (let i = 0; i < packets.length; i += 1) {
      const text = String(packets[i] || "").trim();
      if (!text) continue;

      const nextLen = used + 1 + text.length;
      if (nextLen > budget) {
        continue;
      }

      lines.push(text);
      used = nextLen;
    }

    if (lines.length === 1) {
      return "";
    }

    const out = lines.join("\n");
    root.cache.contextPacket = out;
    return out;
  },

  getPacketPriorityList(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const packets = [];
    const pushIf = (value) => {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (text) packets.push(text);
    };

    const actorPacket = root && root.cache ? String(root.cache.actorPacket || "").trim() : "";
    const hasMajorActorMemory = /\bMajor actor memory\b/i.test(actorPacket);

    // C25u4: current player danger, recent ability change, and major actor
    // memory must survive clutter. Put compact priority truth first, then the
    // larger packets.
    pushIf(root.cache.priorityPacket);
    pushIf(root.cache.playerPacket);

    if (hasMajorActorMemory) {
      pushIf(actorPacket);
    }

    pushIf(root.cache.questPacket);
    pushIf(root.cache.mapPacket);
    pushIf(root.cache.recentEventPacket);
    pushIf(root.cache.timePacket);
    pushIf(root.cache.scenePacket);
    pushIf(root.cache.reputationPacket);

    if (!hasMajorActorMemory) {
      pushIf(actorPacket);
    }

    if (mode === "wide") {
      pushIf(root.cache.worldPacket);
    }

    return packets;
  },

  makePriorityPacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
    const parts = [];
    const player = root.player || {};
    const conditions = player.conditions || {};
    const body = player.body || {};
    const active = Array.isArray(conditions.active) ? conditions.active.filter(Boolean) : [];
    const injuries = Array.isArray(body.injuries) ? body.injuries.filter(Boolean) : [];

    if (active.length) {
      parts.push("Cond " + active.slice(Math.max(0, active.length - 4)).join(", "));
    }
    if (injuries.length) {
      parts.push("Inj " + injuries.slice(Math.max(0, injuries.length - 3)).join(", "));
    }

    const abilityHint = globalThis.AIDRPG.AbilitySystem && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.AbilitySystem.getRelevantContextHint === "function"
      ? globalThis.AIDRPG.AbilitySystem.getRelevantContextHint(root)
      : "";
    if (abilityHint) parts.push(abilityHint);

    const logs = root.logs || {};
    const events = Array.isArray(logs.recentEvents) ? logs.recentEvents : [];
    if (events.length) {
      const recent = [];
      const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
      for (let i = events.length - 1; i >= 0 && recent.length < (mode === "tiny" ? 1 : 2); i -= 1) {
        const row = events[i] || {};
        const msg = String(row.message || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
        if (!msg) continue;
        const rowTurn = typeof row.turn === "number" ? row.turn : turn;
        if (turn - rowTurn > 5) continue;
        if (!/^(?:Actor note|Ability note|Death rumor rejected|Failed action)/i.test(msg)) continue;
        recent.push(msg.slice(0, 110));
      }
      if (recent.length) parts.push("Recent " + recent.join("; "));
    }

    const timeSummary = globalThis.AIDRPG.TimeTravelSystem && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.TimeTravelSystem.getCompactSummary === "function"
      ? globalThis.AIDRPG.TimeTravelSystem.getCompactSummary(root)
      : "";
    if (/\bRoutes?:\b|\broute_time_conflict\b/i.test(timeSummary)) {
      parts.push(timeSummary.replace(/^Day\s+\d+\s+\|\s+\d{2}:\d{2}\s+\|\s*/i, ""));
    }

    const memory = root.actors && root.actors.majorMemory ? root.actors.majorMemory : null;
    const recentEvents = memory && Array.isArray(memory.recentEvents) ? memory.recentEvents : [];
    if (recentEvents.length) {
      const recent = [];
      const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
      for (let i = recentEvents.length - 1; i >= 0 && recent.length < (mode === "tiny" ? 1 : 2); i -= 1) {
        const row = recentEvents[i] || {};
        const rowTurn = typeof row.turn === "number" ? row.turn : turn;
        if (turn - rowTurn > 5) continue;
        const name = String(row.name || row.actorId || "actor").trim();
        const detail = String(row.text || row.kind || "memory").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
        if (name && detail) recent.push(name + ": " + detail.slice(0, 90));
      }
      if (recent.length) parts.push("Actor " + recent.join("; "));
    }

    return parts.length ? "Priority: " + parts.join(" | ") : "";
  },

  makeRecentEventPacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const logs = root && root.logs ? root.logs : {};
    const events = Array.isArray(logs.recentEvents) ? logs.recentEvents : [];
    if (!events.length) return "";

    const picked = [];
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    for (let i = events.length - 1; i >= 0 && picked.length < (mode === "wide" ? 3 : 2); i -= 1) {
      const row = events[i] || {};
      const msg = String(row.message || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
      if (!msg) continue;
      const rowTurn = typeof row.turn === "number" ? row.turn : turn;
      if (turn - rowTurn > 8) continue;
      if (!/^(?:Purchase failed|Combat result|Boundary|Death rumor rejected|Failed action|Actor note|Ability note|XP \+)/i.test(msg)) continue;
      picked.push(msg.slice(0, 120));
    }

    return picked.length ? "Recent: " + picked.join(" | ") : "";
  },

  makeScenePacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const scene = root.world && root.world.currentScene ? root.world.currentScene : null;
  if (!scene || typeof scene !== "object") {
    return "";
  }

  const world = root.world || {};
  const locationsById = world.locationsById || {};

  const parts = [];
  const name = scene.displayName && String(scene.displayName).trim()
    ? String(scene.displayName).trim()
    : "unknown place";

  parts.push("Scene: " + name);

  if (scene.parentLocationRef && locationsById[scene.parentLocationRef] && locationsById[scene.parentLocationRef].identity) {
    const locationName = locationsById[scene.parentLocationRef].identity.displayName || scene.parentLocationRef;
    parts.push("Location: " + locationName);
  }

  if (scene.sceneType) {
    parts.push("Type: " + scene.sceneType);
  }

  const factLimit = mode === "tiny" ? 3 : (mode === "wide" ? 6 : 4);
  const facts = [];

  facts.push.apply(facts, this.takeDistinct(scene.conditionTags, factLimit));
  facts.push.apply(facts, this.takeDistinct(scene.localHazards, factLimit));
  facts.push.apply(facts, this.takeDistinct(scene.localExits, factLimit));

  const trimmedFacts = this.takeDistinct(facts, factLimit);
  if (trimmedFacts.length) {
    parts.push("Facts: " + trimmedFacts.join(", "));
  }

  if (mode !== "tiny" && scene.localEntityIds && scene.localEntityIds.length && scene.localEntitiesById) {
    const names = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < scene.localEntityIds.length && names.length < 3; i += 1) {
      const ref = scene.localEntityIds[i];
      if (scene.localEntitiesById[ref]) {
        names.push(scene.localEntitiesById[ref]);
      }
    }
    if (names.length) {
      parts.push("Objects: " + names.join(", "));
    }
  }

  return parts.join(" | ");
},

  makePlayerPacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const playerSummary = globalThis.AIDRPG.PlayerSystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.PlayerSystem.getCompactSummary === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.PlayerSystem.getCompactSummary(root.player)
    : "";

  const inventoryContext = globalThis.AIDRPG.InventorySystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.InventorySystem.getContextInventorySummary === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.InventorySystem.getContextInventorySummary(root, mode === "wide" ? 8 : mode === "tiny" ? 3 : 5)
    : "";

  const visibleCarry = globalThis.AIDRPG.InventorySystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.InventorySystem.getVisibleCarrySummary === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.InventorySystem.getVisibleCarrySummary(root)
    : "";

  const buildSummary = globalThis.AIDRPG.BuildIdentitySystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.BuildIdentitySystem.getCompactSummary === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.BuildIdentitySystem.getCompactSummary(root.build)
    : "";

  const abilityFocus = globalThis.AIDRPG.AbilitySystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.AbilitySystem.getRelevantContextHint === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.AbilitySystem.getRelevantContextHint(root)
    : "";

  const abilitySummary = globalThis.AIDRPG.AbilitySystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.AbilitySystem.getCompactSummary === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.AbilitySystem.getCompactSummary(root, mode === "wide" ? 3 : 1)
    : "";

  const traitSummary = globalThis.AIDRPG.PersistentTraitSystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.PersistentTraitSystem.getCompactSummary === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.PersistentTraitSystem.getCompactSummary(root, mode === "wide" ? 3 : 1)
    : "";

  const specializationHint = globalThis.AIDRPG.SpecializationSystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.SpecializationSystem.getRelevantContextHint === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.SpecializationSystem.getRelevantContextHint(root, mode)
    : "";

  const parts = [];

  if (playerSummary) {
    parts.push("Player: " + playerSummary);
  }

  // C25u2: AI Dungeon needs packed/current inventory truth in the next Context,
  // not just visible/equipped gear. Keep this short so it survives pressure.
  if (inventoryContext) {
    parts.push("Inventory: " + inventoryContext);
  } else if (visibleCarry && visibleCarry !== "nothing notable visible") {
    parts.push("Visible gear: " + visibleCarry);
  }

  if (abilityFocus) {
    parts.push(abilityFocus);
  } else if (mode === "wide" && abilitySummary) {
    parts.push(abilitySummary);
  }

  if (specializationHint) {
    parts.push(specializationHint);
  }

  if (traitSummary) {
    parts.push("Traits: " + traitSummary);
  }

  if (mode === "wide" && buildSummary && buildSummary !== "Build summary unavailable.") {
    parts.push(buildSummary);
  }

  return parts.join(" | ");
},

  makeTimePacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const summary = globalThis.AIDRPG.TimeTravelSystem &&
      typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.TimeTravelSystem.getCompactSummary === "function"
      ? globalThis.AIDRPG.TimeTravelSystem.getCompactSummary(root)
      : "";

    if (!summary || summary === "Time unknown.") {
      return "";
    }

    return "Time: " + summary;
  },

  makeActorPacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const actorSummary = globalThis.AIDRPG.ActorProfileSystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.ActorProfileSystem.getContextSummary === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.ActorProfileSystem.getContextSummary(root, mode)
    : "";

  const memorySummary = globalThis.AIDRPG.MajorActorMemorySystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.MajorActorMemorySystem.getContextSummary === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.MajorActorMemorySystem.getContextSummary(root, mode)
    : "";

  const parts = [];
  if (actorSummary) parts.push(actorSummary);
  if (memorySummary) parts.push(memorySummary);

  return parts.join(" | ");
},

  makeReputationPacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const summary = globalThis.AIDRPG.ReputationSystem &&
      typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.ReputationSystem.getCompactSummary === "function"
      ? globalThis.AIDRPG.ReputationSystem.getCompactSummary(root, mode)
      : "";

    return summary || "";
  },

  makeQuestPacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (globalThis.AIDRPG.QuestLogSystem && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.QuestLogSystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      const summary = globalThis.AIDRPG.QuestLogSystem.getCompactSummary(root, mode);
      if (summary) return summary;
    }

    const quests = root.quests || {};
    const active = Array.isArray(quests.active) ? quests.active : [];
    const archived = Array.isArray(quests.archived) ? quests.archived : [];
    const tracking = quests.tracking && typeof quests.tracking === "object" ? quests.tracking : {};

    let quest = null;

    if (tracking.primaryQuestId) {
      for (let i = 0; i < active.length; i += 1) {
        if (active[i] && active[i].id === tracking.primaryQuestId) {
          quest = active[i];
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!quest && active.length) {
      quest = active[0];
    }

    if (!quest || typeof quest !== "object") {
      if (archived.length) {
        const recentQuest = archived[archived.length - 1] || {};
        const recentTitle = String(recentQuest.title || recentQuest.objective || "recent quest").trim();
        const recentStatus = String(recentQuest.status || "archived").trim();
        return recentTitle ? ("Recent quest: " + recentTitle + " | Status: " + recentStatus) : "";
      }
      return "";
    }

    const title = quest.title ? String(quest.title).trim() : "active task";
    const status = quest.status ? String(quest.status).trim() : "";
    const objective = quest.objective ? String(quest.objective).trim() : "";

    const parts = ["Quest: " + title];

    if (status) {
      parts.push("Status: " + status);
    }

    if (mode !== "tiny" && objective) {
      parts.push("Goal: " + objective);
    }

    return parts.join(" | ");
  },

  makeMapPacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const summary = globalThis.AIDRPG.WorldEntitySystem &&
      typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.WorldEntitySystem.getMapContextSummary === "function"
      ? globalThis.AIDRPG.WorldEntitySystem.getMapContextSummary(root, mode)
      : "";
    return summary || "";
  },

  makeWorldPacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (mode !== "wide") return "";

    const scene = root.world && root.world.currentScene ? root.world.currentScene : null;
    const world = root.world || {};
    if (!scene || !world.entitiesById) return "";

    const entityRef = scene.entityRef || "";
    const entity = entityRef && world.entitiesById[entityRef] ? world.entitiesById[entityRef] : null;
    if (!entity || typeof entity !== "object") {
      return "";
    }

    const parts = [];

    if (entity.mutable && Array.isArray(entity.mutable.materialHints) && entity.mutable.materialHints.length) {
      parts.push("Materials: " + entity.mutable.materialHints.join(", "));
    }

    if (entity.mutable && Array.isArray(entity.mutable.ownerHints) && entity.mutable.ownerHints.length) {
      parts.push("Owners: " + entity.mutable.ownerHints.join(", "));
    }

    if (entity.mutable && Array.isArray(entity.mutable.districtHints) && entity.mutable.districtHints.length) {
      parts.push("District: " + entity.mutable.districtHints.join(", "));
    }

    return parts.length ? ("Linked detail: " + parts.join(" | ")) : "";
  },

  takeDistinct(values, maxCount) {
    const out = [];
    const list = Array.isArray(values) ? values : [];

    for (let i = 0; i < list.length && out.length < (maxCount || 4); i += 1) {
      const clean = String(list[i] || "").trim();
      if (!clean) continue;
      if (out.indexOf(clean) === -1) {
        out.push(clean);
      }
    }

    return out;
  }
},

    // === MAJOR ACTOR MEMORY SYSTEM ===
    MajorActorMemorySystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || Array.isArray(root)) return null;

    if (!root.actors || typeof root.actors !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.actors)) {
      root.actors = {};
    }

    const actors = root.actors;

    if (!actors.majorMemory || typeof actors.majorMemory !== "object" || Array.isArray(actors.majorMemory)) {
      actors.majorMemory = {};
    }

    const memory = actors.majorMemory;

    if (!memory.byActorId || typeof memory.byActorId !== "object" || Array.isArray(memory.byActorId)) memory.byActorId = {};
    if (!Array.isArray(memory.activeIds)) memory.activeIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(memory.recentEvents)) memory.recentEvents = [];
    if (!memory.config || typeof memory.config !== "object" || Array.isArray(memory.config)) memory.config = {};

    const config = memory.config;
    if (typeof config.maxMajorActors !== "number") config.maxMajorActors = 12;
    if (typeof config.maxGoals !== "number") config.maxGoals = 6;
    if (typeof config.maxGrudges !== "number") config.maxGrudges = 6;
    if (typeof config.maxNotes !== "number") config.maxNotes = 8;
    if (typeof config.maxEvents !== "number") config.maxEvents = 40;
    if (typeof config.activationSightings !== "number") config.activationSightings = 5;

    return memory;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
    const U = A.Utils;
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    if (!raw.trim()) return 0;

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) return 0;
    }

    let changes = 0;
    changes += this.discoverNamedMajorActors(raw, root);
    changes += this.syncMajorActors(root);
    changes += this.applyMemoryFromText(raw, root);
    changes += this.rebuildActiveIds(raw, root);
    changes += this.pruneMajorMemory(root);

    if (changes > 0) {
      if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") U.invalidatePackets(root);
      if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") U.logEvent("Major actor memory updated.");
    }

    return changes;
  },

  discoverNamedMajorActors(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
    const ActorSystem = A.ActorProfileSystem;
    if (!ActorSystem || typeof ActorSystem.findOrCreateRecord !== "function") return 0;

    const found = [];
    const addName = (value, reason) => {
      const name = this.cleanShortText(value || "");
      if (!name || this.isBannedActorLabel(name)) return;
      if (!/\b[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}\b/.test(name)) return;
      for (let i = 0; i < found.length; i += 1) {
        if (found[i].name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase()) return;
      }
      found.push({ name: name, reason: String(reason || "named_major_actor") });
    };

    const titledPatterns = [
      /\b(High\s+Priestess|High\s+Priest|Archdruid|Archmage|Master|Queen|King|Sergeant|General|Commander|Chancellor|Baroness|Baron|Duchess|Duke|Elder|Oracle|Captain|Guildmaster|Guild\s+Master|Lord|Lady|Sir|Dame|Priestess|Priest|Mage|Wizard|Knight|Hunter|Barkeep|Innkeeper|Blacksmith|Smith)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})(?:\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}))?\b/g
    ];

    for (let p = 0; p < titledPatterns.length; p += 1) {
      let match;
      while ((match = titledPatterns[p].exec(raw))) {
        addName([match[1], match[2], match[3]].filter(Boolean).join(" "), "titled_major_actor");
      }
    }

    const appositivePatterns = [
      /\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})\s*,\s+(?:your\s+)?(?:companion|ally|friend|mentor|rival|guide|apothecary|scout|healer|bodyguard|protector|merchant|trader|contact|informant)\b/g,
      /\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})\s+the\s+(?:wounded\s+|old\s+|young\s+|loyal\s+|scarred\s+)?(?:scout|apothecary|companion|ally|guide|oracle|healer|priestess|priest|captain|sergeant|general|mage|archmage|merchant|trader|contact|informant|cultist|acolyte)\b/g,
      /\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})\b[^.!?]{0,50}\b(?:promises?|vows?|remembers?|trusts?|fears?|warns?|offers?\s+you\s+(?:an?\s+)?alliance|offers?\s+fair\s+trade|trades?\s+fairly|warms?\s+to\s+you|betrays?|swears?)\b/g,
      /\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})\b[^.!?]{0,70}\b(?:is\s+wounded|is\s+injured|is\s+hurt|bleeds?|is\s+bleeding|is\s+poisoned|stays\s+with\s+the\s+party|stays\s+with\s+you|joins\s+the\s+party|follows\s+you|guards\s+you|protects\s+you)\b/g,
      /\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})\b[^.!?]{0,80}\b(?:merchant|trader|fair\s+trade|trades?\s+fairly|warms?\s+to\s+you|cult|cultist|betrays?|betrayal|fearful|afraid)\b/g
    ];

    for (let p = 0; p < appositivePatterns.length; p += 1) {
      let match;
      while ((match = appositivePatterns[p].exec(raw))) {
        addName(match[1], "important_named_actor");
      }
    }

    const namedCreaturePatterns = [
      /you\s+name\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([a-z][a-z' -]{2,30})\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})/g,
      /(?:the\s+)?([a-z][a-z' -]{2,30})\s+(?:is\s+)?(?:named|called)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})/g
    ];
    for (let p = 0; p < namedCreaturePatterns.length; p += 1) {
      let match;
      while ((match = namedCreaturePatterns[p].exec(raw))) {
        const kind = String(match[1] || "creature").replace(/^(?:blue|gray|grey|small|large|young|old|nameless)\s+/i, "").trim();
        const name = String(match[2] || "").trim();
        if (!name || this.isBannedActorLabel(name)) continue;
        found.push({ name: name, reason: "named_creature", creatureKind: kind || "creature" });
      }
    }

    let changes = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < found.length; i += 1) {
      const name = found[i].name;
      const sceneKey = A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.toKey === "function" ? A.Utils.toKey(name) : name.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "_");
      if (!sceneKey) continue;

      const roleTags = typeof ActorSystem.inferRoleTags === "function" ? ActorSystem.inferRoleTags(name) : [];
      const rankTags = typeof ActorSystem.inferRankTags === "function" ? ActorSystem.inferRankTags(name) : [];
      const category = found[i].creatureKind ? "creature" : (typeof ActorSystem.inferCategory === "function" ? ActorSystem.inferCategory(name) : "humanoid");
      if (found[i].creatureKind && roleTags.indexOf("companion") === -1) roleTags.push("companion");
      if (found[i].creatureKind && roleTags.indexOf(found[i].creatureKind) === -1) roleTags.push(found[i].creatureKind);

      const candidate = {
        sceneKey: sceneKey,
        displayName: found[i].creatureKind ? (name + " the " + found[i].creatureKind) : name,
        roleTags: roleTags,
        rankTags: rankTags.length ? rankTags : ["leader"],
        category: category,
        importanceScore: 5,
        persistent: true,
        major: true
      };

      const record = ActorSystem.findOrCreateRecord(candidate, root);
      if (!record || !record.identity || !record.identity.id) continue;

      if (typeof ActorSystem.applyCandidateToRecord === "function") {
        changes += ActorSystem.applyCandidateToRecord(candidate, record, root) || 0;
      }

      changes += this.ensureMemoryForActor(record.identity.id, found[i].reason, root) ? 1 : 0;
    }

    return changes;
  },

  syncMajorActors(root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const actors = root.actors || {};
    const byId = actors.byId || {};
    const allIds = Array.isArray(actors.allIds) ? actors.allIds : [];
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < allIds.length; i += 1) {
      const actorId = allIds[i];
      const actor = byId[actorId];
      if (!actor || typeof actor !== "object") continue;

      if (this.shouldActorHaveMemory(actor, root)) {
        changes += this.ensureMemoryForActor(actorId, "profile_major_actor", root) ? 1 : 0;
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  shouldActorHaveMemory(actor, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!actor || typeof actor !== "object") return false;

    const identity = actor.identity || {};
    const name = this.getActorName(actor);
    if (!name || this.isBannedActorLabel(name)) return false;

    const importance = actor.importance || {};
    const score = Number(importance.score || 0);
    const sightings = Number(importance.sightings || 0);
    const rankTags = Array.isArray(actor.rankTags) ? actor.rankTags : [];
    const roleTags = Array.isArray(actor.roleTags) ? actor.roleTags : [];

    if (importance.major === true) return true;
    if (score >= 4) return true;
    if (sightings >= this.getConfig(root).activationSightings) return true;

    for (let i = 0; i < rankTags.length; i += 1) {
      const tag = String(rankTags[i] || "").toLowerCase();
      if (tag === "leader" || tag === "elite" || tag === "veteran" || tag === "royalty") return true;
    }

    const importantRoles = new Set(["captain", "commander", "guildmaster", "general", "sergeant", "queen", "king", "chancellor", "baron", "baroness", "duke", "duchess", "elder", "oracle", "archmage", "priest", "priestess", "apothecary", "scout", "merchant", "trader", "companion", "ally"]);
    for (let i = 0; i < roleTags.length; i += 1) {
      const tag = String(roleTags[i] || "").toLowerCase();
      if (importantRoles.has(tag)) return true;
    }

    if (/\b(High Priestess|High Priest|Archmage|Queen|King|Sergeant|General|Commander|Chancellor|Baroness|Baron|Duchess|Duke|Elder|Oracle|Captain|Guildmaster|Lord|Lady|Sir|Dame)\b/.test(String(identity.name || identity.displayName || name || ""))) {
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  },

  ensureMemoryForActor(actorId, reason, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory) return false;

    const id = String(actorId || "").trim();
    if (!id) return false;

    const actor = root.actors && root.actors.byId ? root.actors.byId[id] : null;
    const name = actor ? this.getActorName(actor) : id;
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

    if (!memory.byActorId[id] || typeof memory.byActorId[id] !== "object") {
      memory.byActorId[id] = {
        actorId: id,
        name: name,
        createdTurn: turn,
        lastSeenTurn: turn,
        lastUpdatedTurn: turn,
        relationshipStance: "neutral",
        goals: [],
        grudges: [],
        attitudeNotes: [],
        longTermNotes: [],
        recentEvents: [],
        activation: {
          reason: String(reason || "major_actor"),
          activationCount: 0
        }
      };

      this.pushRecentEvent("activated", name, id, String(reason || "major_actor"), root);
      this.markActorDirty(id, root);
      return true;
    }

    const rec = memory.byActorId[id];
    let changed = false;
    if (rec.name !== name && name) {
      rec.name = name;
      changed = true;
    }
    if (!rec.activation || typeof rec.activation !== "object") {
      rec.activation = { reason: String(reason || "major_actor"), activationCount: 0 };
      changed = true;
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(rec.goals)) { rec.goals = []; changed = true; }
    if (!Array.isArray(rec.grudges)) { rec.grudges = []; changed = true; }
    if (!Array.isArray(rec.attitudeNotes)) { rec.attitudeNotes = []; changed = true; }
    if (!Array.isArray(rec.longTermNotes)) { rec.longTermNotes = []; changed = true; }
    if (!Array.isArray(rec.recentEvents)) { rec.recentEvents = []; changed = true; }
    if (typeof rec.relationshipStance !== "string") { rec.relationshipStance = "neutral"; changed = true; }

    if (changed) {
      rec.lastUpdatedTurn = turn;
      this.markActorDirty(id, root);
    }

    return changed;
  },

  applyMemoryFromText(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory) return 0;

    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const actorIds = Object.keys(memory.byActorId || {});
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < actorIds.length; i += 1) {
      const actorId = actorIds[i];
      const actor = root.actors && root.actors.byId ? root.actors.byId[actorId] : null;
      const rec = memory.byActorId[actorId];
      if (!rec || typeof rec !== "object") continue;

      const referenced = this.isActorReferencedInText(actor, rec, raw);
      const local = this.isActorCurrentlyLocal(actorId, root);
      if (!referenced && !local) continue;

      changes += this.touchMemory(rec, actorId, referenced ? "mentioned" : "local", root);
      if (!referenced) continue;

      const candidates = this.extractMemoryCandidatesForActor(raw, actor, rec, root);
      for (let c = 0; c < candidates.length; c += 1) {
        changes += this.applyMemoryCandidate(actorId, candidates[c], root);
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  extractMemoryCandidatesForActor(rawText, actor, rec, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const labels = this.getActorReferenceLabels(actor, rec);
    const out = [];

    const add = (kind, text, confidence) => {
      const clean = this.cleanShortText(text);
      if (!kind || !clean) return;
      out.push({ kind: kind, text: clean, confidence: Number(confidence || 0.75) });
    };

    for (let i = 0; i < labels.length; i += 1) {
      const label = labels[i];
      const reLabel = this.escapeRegExp(label);
      if (!reLabel) continue;

      let match;
      const goalPatterns = [
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,24}\\b(?:wants|intends|plans|hopes|needs|tries|is determined)\\s+to\\s+([^.!?;,]{3,100})", "i"),
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,24}\\b(?:goal|aim|plan)\\s+(?:is|becomes)\\s+to\\s+([^.!?;,]{3,100})", "i")
      ];

      for (let g = 0; g < goalPatterns.length; g += 1) {
        match = raw.match(goalPatterns[g]);
        if (match && match[1]) add("goal", match[1], 0.82);
      }

      const grudgePatterns = [
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,36}\\b(?:swears|vows|promises)\\s+(?:revenge|to make you pay|to remember this)", "i"),
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,36}\\b(?:holds|bears|keeps)\\s+(?:a\\s+)?grudge", "i"),
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,36}\\b(?:will not forget|won't forget)\\s+(?:what you did|this)", "i")
      ];

      for (let gr = 0; gr < grudgePatterns.length; gr += 1) {
        match = raw.match(grudgePatterns[gr]);
        if (match) add("grudge", match[0], 0.84);
      }

      const positiveRe = new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,60}\\b(?:trusts|respects|thanks|supports|protects|is grateful to|is loyal to|offers? you (?:an? )?alliance|offers? fair trade|trades? fairly|warms? to you|allies? with)\\s*(?:you)?\\b", "i");
      const allianceRe = new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,48}\\boffers?\\s+you\\s+(?:an?\\s+)?alliance\\b", "i");
      const negativeRe = new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,48}\\b(?:fears|hates|distrusts|resents|suspects|is wary of|is suspicious of|is hostile to)\\s+(?:you|your\\b[^.!?]{0,24})", "i");
      const playerEarnedRe = new RegExp("\\byou\\s+(?:earn|earned|gain|gained)\\s+" + reLabel + "'?s?\\s+(trust|respect|gratitude|fear|hatred)\\b", "i");

      match = raw.match(positiveRe) || raw.match(allianceRe);
      if (match) add("relationship", "friendly: " + match[0], 0.86);

      match = raw.match(negativeRe);
      if (match) add("relationship", "hostile: " + match[0], 0.86);

      const hostileActionRe = new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,60}\\b(?:bars|blocks|denies|refuses|orders?\\s+(?:the\\s+)?guards?\\s+to\\s+(?:hold|stop|watch))\\b[^.!?]{0,80}\\b(?:you|your|gate|entry|passage)\\b", "i");
      match = raw.match(hostileActionRe);
      if (match) add("relationship", "hostile: " + match[0], 0.80);

      match = raw.match(playerEarnedRe);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        const value = String(match[1] || "").toLowerCase();
        const stance = value === "fear" || value === "hatred" ? "hostile" : "friendly";
        add("relationship", stance + ": " + match[0], 0.84);
      }

      const notePatterns = [
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,60}\\b(?:remembers|recognizes|owes|promises|warns|forgives|blames|trades? fairly|warms? to you|betrays?|fears?)\\b[^.!?]{0,100}", "i"),
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,48}\\b(?:asks you to return|offers to help|refuses to help|keeps your secret)\\b[^.!?]{0,80}", "i"),
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,60}\\b(?:is wounded|is injured|is hurt|limps|bleeds|is bleeding|is poisoned|needs help|follows you|stays with you|stays with the party|joins the party|guards you|protects you|stands beside you)\\b[^.!?]{0,80}", "i"),
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,60}\\b(?:orders?|commands?|instructs?)\\b[^.!?]{0,100}", "i"),
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,60}\\b(?:merchant|trader|fair trade|trades? fairly|warms? to you|cult|betrays?|betrayal|fearful|afraid)\\b[^.!?]{0,100}", "i"),
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,60}\\b(?:bars|blocks|denies|refuses)\\b[^.!?]{0,80}\\b(?:gate|entry|passage|you)\\b[^.!?]{0,80}", "i")
      ];

      for (let n = 0; n < notePatterns.length; n += 1) {
        match = raw.match(notePatterns[n]);
        if (match) add("note", match[0], 0.76);
      }
    }

    return this.dedupeCandidates(out);
  },

  applyMemoryCandidate(actorId, candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory || !candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") return 0;

    const rec = memory.byActorId[String(actorId || "")];
    if (!rec) return 0;

    const kind = String(candidate.kind || "note").toLowerCase();
    const text = this.cleanShortText(candidate.text || "");
    if (!text) return 0;

    let changes = 0;

    if (kind === "goal") {
      changes += this.pushCappedUnique(rec.goals, text, this.getConfig(root).maxGoals);
    } else if (kind === "grudge") {
      changes += this.pushCappedUnique(rec.grudges, text, this.getConfig(root).maxGrudges);
      if (rec.relationshipStance !== "hostile") {
        rec.relationshipStance = "hostile";
        changes += 1;
      }
    } else if (kind === "relationship") {
      const stance = text.indexOf("hostile:") === 0 ? "hostile" : text.indexOf("friendly:") === 0 ? "friendly" : "mixed";
      if (rec.relationshipStance !== stance) {
        rec.relationshipStance = stance;
        changes += 1;
      }
      changes += this.pushCappedUnique(rec.attitudeNotes, text.replace(/^(?:friendly|hostile|mixed):\s*/i, ""), this.getConfig(root).maxNotes);
    } else {
      changes += this.pushCappedUnique(rec.attitudeNotes, text, this.getConfig(root).maxNotes);
    }

    if (changes > 0) {
      const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
      rec.lastUpdatedTurn = turn;
      this.pushCappedUnique(rec.recentEvents, kind + ": " + text, 8);
      this.pushRecentEvent(kind, rec.name || actorId, actorId, text, root);
      this.markActorDirty(actorId, root);
    }

    return changes;
  },

  rebuildActiveIds(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory) return 0;

    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const byActorId = memory.byActorId || {};
    const ids = Object.keys(byActorId);
    const active = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i += 1) {
      const id = ids[i];
      const actor = root.actors && root.actors.byId ? root.actors.byId[id] : null;
      const rec = byActorId[id];
      if (!rec) continue;

      if (this.isActorCurrentlyLocal(id, root) || this.isActorReferencedInText(actor, rec, raw)) {
        active.push(id);
      }
    }

    active.sort((a, b) => {
      const left = byActorId[a] || {};
      const right = byActorId[b] || {};
      return Number(right.lastSeenTurn || 0) - Number(left.lastSeenTurn || 0);
    });

    return this.replaceArray(memory.activeIds, active.slice(0, 4));
  },

  touchMemory(rec, actorId, reason, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!rec || typeof rec !== "object") return 0;

    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    let changes = 0;

    if (rec.lastSeenTurn !== turn) {
      rec.lastSeenTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (!rec.activation || typeof rec.activation !== "object") {
      rec.activation = { reason: String(reason || "seen"), activationCount: 0 };
      changes += 1;
    }

    rec.activation.activationCount = Math.max(0, Number(rec.activation.activationCount || 0)) + 1;

    if (changes > 0) {
      this.markActorDirty(actorId, root);
    }

    return changes;
  },

  pruneMajorMemory(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory) return 0;

    const max = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(this.getConfig(root).maxMajorActors || 12)));
    const byActorId = memory.byActorId || {};
    const ids = Object.keys(byActorId);
    if (ids.length <= max) return 0;

    ids.sort((a, b) => {
      const left = byActorId[a] || {};
      const right = byActorId[b] || {};
      return Number(right.lastUpdatedTurn || right.lastSeenTurn || 0) - Number(left.lastUpdatedTurn || left.lastSeenTurn || 0);
    });

    let changes = 0;
    for (let i = max; i < ids.length; i += 1) {
      delete byActorId[ids[i]];
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (changes > 0) {
      const keep = ids.slice(0, max);
      memory.activeIds = Array.isArray(memory.activeIds) ? memory.activeIds.filter((id) => keep.indexOf(id) !== -1) : [];
    }

    return changes;
  },

  getContextSummary(root = state.aidrpg, mode = "normal") {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory) return "";

    const byActorId = memory.byActorId || {};
    const activeIds = Array.isArray(memory.activeIds) ? memory.activeIds.slice() : [];
    const maxActors = mode === "wide" ? 3 : (mode === "tiny" ? 1 : 2);
    const recentIds = Object.keys(byActorId).sort((a, b) => {
      const left = byActorId[a] || {};
      const right = byActorId[b] || {};
      return Number(right.lastUpdatedTurn || right.lastSeenTurn || 0) - Number(left.lastUpdatedTurn || left.lastSeenTurn || 0);
    });

    const ids = activeIds.slice();
    for (let i = 0; i < recentIds.length && ids.length < maxActors; i += 1) {
      if (ids.indexOf(recentIds[i]) === -1) ids.push(recentIds[i]);
    }

    if (!ids.length) return "";
    const parts = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < ids.length && parts.length < maxActors; i += 1) {
      const summary = this.getMemorySummaryForActor(ids[i], root, mode);
      if (summary) parts.push(summary);
    }

    return parts.length ? "Major actor memory: " + parts.join(" || ") : "";
  },

  getMemorySummaryForActor(actorId, root = state.aidrpg, mode = "normal") {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory) return "";

    const rec = memory.byActorId[String(actorId || "")];
    if (!rec || typeof rec !== "object") return "";

    const parts = [];
    const name = rec.name || String(actorId || "actor");
    parts.push(name);

    if (rec.relationshipStance && rec.relationshipStance !== "neutral") {
      parts.push("stance: " + rec.relationshipStance);
    }

    if (Array.isArray(rec.goals) && rec.goals.length) {
      parts.push("goal: " + rec.goals[rec.goals.length - 1]);
    }

    if (Array.isArray(rec.grudges) && rec.grudges.length) {
      parts.push("grudge: " + rec.grudges[rec.grudges.length - 1]);
    }

    // C25u3: one compact attitude note is current actor truth and helps AI
    // Dungeon preserve wounds, orders, gate bars, and protection details under
    // context pressure. Wide/card modes still include the same latest note.
    if (Array.isArray(rec.attitudeNotes) && rec.attitudeNotes.length) {
      parts.push("note: " + rec.attitudeNotes[rec.attitudeNotes.length - 1]);
    }

    return parts.join("; ");
  },

  markActorDirty(actorId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG && globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils ? globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils : null;
    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("actors", String(actorId || ""), root);
    }
  },

  pushRecentEvent(kind, name, actorId, text, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory) return 0;

    const event = {
      turn: root && root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0,
      kind: String(kind || "memory"),
      actorId: String(actorId || ""),
      name: this.cleanShortText(name || actorId || "actor"),
      text: this.cleanShortText(text || "")
    };

    memory.recentEvents.push(event);
    const max = Math.max(8, Math.floor(Number(this.getConfig(root).maxEvents || 40)));
    while (memory.recentEvents.length > max) memory.recentEvents.shift();
    return 1;
  },

  getConfig(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    return memory && memory.config ? memory.config : {};
  },

  getActorName(actor) {
    if (!actor || typeof actor !== "object") return "";
    const identity = actor.identity || {};
    return this.cleanShortText(identity.displayName || identity.name || actor.name || actor.id || "");
  },

  getActorReferenceLabels(actor, rec) {
    const out = [];
    const add = (value) => {
      const clean = this.cleanShortText(value);
      if (!clean || clean.length < 3) return;
      if (this.isBannedActorLabel(clean)) return;
      if (out.indexOf(clean) === -1) out.push(clean);
    };

    if (rec && rec.name) add(rec.name);
    const actorName = this.getActorName(actor);
    if (actorName) add(actorName);

    if (actor && Array.isArray(actor.roleTags)) {
      for (let i = 0; i < actor.roleTags.length && out.length < 5; i += 1) {
        add(actor.roleTags[i]);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  isActorReferencedInText(actor, rec, rawText) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    if (!raw) return false;

    const labels = this.getActorReferenceLabels(actor, rec);
    for (let i = 0; i < labels.length; i += 1) {
      const label = labels[i];
      const re = new RegExp("\\b" + this.escapeRegExp(label) + "\\b", "i");
      if (re.test(raw)) return true;
    }

    return false;
  },

  isActorCurrentlyLocal(actorId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const actors = root && root.actors ? root.actors : {};
    const localIds = Array.isArray(actors.localIds) ? actors.localIds : [];
    return localIds.indexOf(String(actorId || "")) !== -1;
  },

  isBannedActorLabel(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().trim();
    if (!lower) return true;
    if (lower.length > 80) return true;

    return new Set([
      "the", "a", "an", "someone", "somebody", "anyone", "everyone", "people",
      "crowd", "locals", "townsfolk", "patrons", "guards", "merchants", "bandits",
      "you", "your", "player", "storm", "rain", "fire", "market", "street",
      "captain", "commander", "general", "sergeant", "queen", "king", "lord", "lady",
      "priest", "priestess", "archdruid", "archmage", "master", "mage", "wizard", "elder", "oracle"
    ]).has(lower);
  },

  cleanShortText(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]+/g, " ")
      .replace(/[<>]/g, "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .replace(/^[\s:;,.!?-]+|[\s:;,.!?-]+$/g, "")
      .trim()
      .slice(0, 160);
  },

  escapeRegExp(value) {
    return String(value || "").replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
  },

  pushCappedUnique(list, value, maxCount) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
    const clean = this.cleanShortText(value);
    if (!clean) return 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) {
      if (String(list[i] || "").toLowerCase() === clean.toLowerCase()) return 0;
    }

    list.push(clean);
    const max = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(maxCount || 8)));
    while (list.length > max) list.shift();
    return 1;
  },

  replaceArray(target, next) {
    if (!Array.isArray(target) || !Array.isArray(next)) return 0;

    let changed = target.length !== next.length;
    if (!changed) {
      for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i += 1) {
        if (target[i] !== next[i]) {
          changed = true;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) return 0;

    target.length = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < next.length; i += 1) target.push(next[i]);
    return 1;
  },

  dedupeCandidates(list) {
    const out = [];
    const seen = new Set();
    const input = Array.isArray(list) ? list : [];

    for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i += 1) {
      const row = input[i];
      if (!row || typeof row !== "object") continue;
      const key = String(row.kind || "") + "|" + this.cleanShortText(row.text || "").toLowerCase();
      if (seen.has(key)) continue;
      seen.add(key);
      out.push(row);
    }

    return out;
  }
}
  };
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------
  // AIDRPG BETA FINAL PLAYTEST PATCH
  // Narrow final-canon polishing layer. This patch intentionally avoids
  // broad rewrites of the stable systems and only extends the remaining
  // weak edges found by the v1.0.3 testers.
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------
  (function installAIDRPGBetaPatch(A) {
    "use strict";
    if (!A || A.__betaPatchInstalled === true) return;
    A.__betaPatchInstalled = true;
    A.VERSION = "1.0.4-beta";
    A.BUILD_STAGE = "AIDRPGbeta-final-playtest";

    const P = A.BetaFinalPatch = {
      normalizeSpaces(value) {
        return String(value == null ? "" : value).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
      },

      key(value) {
        const U = A.Utils || {};
        return U.toKey ? U.toKey(value) : String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_").replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
      },

      log(root, message) {
        const U = A.Utils || {};
        if (U.logEvent) U.logEvent(String(message || ""));
      },

      dirty(root, kind, value) {
        const U = A.Utils || {};
        if (U.markDirty) U.markDirty(kind, value, root);
        if (U.invalidatePackets) U.invalidatePackets(root);
      },

      pushUnique(list, value, max) {
        if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
        const clean = this.normalizeSpaces(value);
        if (!clean) return 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) {
          if (String(list[i] || "").toLowerCase() === clean.toLowerCase()) return 0;
        }
        list.push(clean);
        const cap = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(max || 12)));
        while (list.length > cap) list.shift();
        return 1;
      },

      numberWord(value) {
        const words = {
          one:1,two:2,three:3,four:4,five:5,six:6,seven:7,eight:8,nine:9,ten:10,
          eleven:11,twelve:12,thirteen:13,fourteen:14,fifteen:15,sixteen:16,seventeen:17,eighteen:18,nineteen:19,twenty:20,
          thirty:30,forty:40,fifty:50,sixty:60,seventy:70,eighty:80,ninety:90,hundred:100
        };
        const raw = String(value || "").toLowerCase().trim();
        if (/^\d+$/.test(raw)) return Math.floor(Number(raw));
        if (words[raw]) return words[raw];
        const parts = raw.split(/[ -]+/).filter(Boolean);
        let sum = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < parts.length; i += 1) sum += words[parts[i]] || 0;
        return sum;
      },

      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      // Player HP / transformation / aging
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      applyNumericHpDamage(rawText, root) {
        if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
        if (!A.PlayerSystem || !A.PlayerSystem.init) return 0;
        A.PlayerSystem.init(root);
        const raw = String(rawText || "");
        const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
        const player = root.player || {};
        const stats = player.stats || {};
        if (typeof stats.hp !== "number" || typeof stats.maxHp !== "number") return 0;

        let amount = 0;
        const patterns = [
          /\byou\s+(?:take|took|suffer|suffered|receive|received)\s+(\d{1,4})\s+(?:points?\s+of\s+)?damage\b/i,
          /\b(?:hits?|strikes?|smashes?|slashes?|stabs?|blasts?|burns?|shocks?)\s+you\s+(?:for\s+)?(\d{1,4})\s+(?:points?\s+of\s+)?damage\b/i,
          /\bfor\s+(\d{1,4})\s+(?:points?\s+of\s+)?damage\b/i
        ];
        for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
          const m = raw.match(patterns[i]);
          if (m && m[1]) { amount = Math.max(amount, Math.floor(Number(m[1]))); }
        }

        const hpLow = /\b(?:your\s+)?(?:hp|health)\s+is\s+low\b|\bbadly\s+hurt\b|\bnear\s+death\b|\bbarely\s+standing\b/i.test(raw);
        const hpEmpty = /\b(?:no\s+hp\s+left|0\s*hp|at\s+death['’]?s\s+door)\b/i.test(raw);

        let changes = 0;
        const commit = () => {
          let local = 0;
          if (amount > 0) {
            const before = stats.hp;
            stats.hp = Math.max(0, Math.min(stats.maxHp, stats.hp - amount));
            if (stats.hp !== before) {
              local += 1;
              P.log(root, "HP damage: -" + amount + " HP.");
            }
          }
          if (hpLow && stats.hp > Math.max(1, Math.floor(stats.maxHp * 0.35))) {
            stats.hp = Math.max(1, Math.floor(stats.maxHp * 0.25));
            local += 1;
            P.log(root, "HP state: low health preserved.");
          }
          if (hpEmpty && stats.hp !== 0) {
            stats.hp = 0;
            local += 1;
            P.log(root, "HP state: empty/critical preserved.");
          }
          if (local > 0) {
            if (A.PlayerSystem.recomputeDerived) A.PlayerSystem.recomputeDerived(player);
            P.dirty(root, "player", true);
          }
          return local;
        };

        if (amount <= 0 && !hpLow && !hpEmpty) return 0;
        const Guard = A.OperationGuard;
        changes += Guard && Guard.applyOnce ? Guard.applyOnce({ kind:"beta_hp_damage", target:"player", payload:{ amount, hpLow, hpEmpty, text:raw.slice(0,160) } }, commit, root) : commit();
        return changes;
      },

      applyTransformationCanon(rawText, root) {
        if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
        if (!A.PlayerSystem || !A.PlayerSystem.init) return 0;
        A.PlayerSystem.init(root);
        const raw = String(rawText || "");
        const lower = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
        if (!raw.trim()) return 0;
        if (/\b(?:temporary|temporarily|illusion|illusory|disguise|dream|vision|seems|appears|as if|pretend|mask)\b/.test(lower)) return 0;

        const player = root.player || {};
        const body = player.body || (player.body = {});
        const appearance = player.appearance || (player.appearance = {});
        const conditions = player.conditions || (player.conditions = {});
        if (!Array.isArray(body.mutations)) body.mutations = [];
        if (!Array.isArray(body.speciesTraits)) body.speciesTraits = [];
        if (!Array.isArray(body.bodyTags)) body.bodyTags = [];
        if (!Array.isArray(appearance.visibleTraits)) appearance.visibleTraits = [];
        if (!Array.isArray(conditions.permanent)) conditions.permanent = [];
        if (!Array.isArray(player.titles)) player.titles = [];
        if (!player.history || typeof player.history !== "object") player.history = {};
        if (!Array.isArray(player.history.majorEvents)) player.history.majorEvents = [];

        let changes = 0;
        const addTrait = (v) => { changes += P.pushUnique(body.speciesTraits, v, 20); changes += P.pushUnique(appearance.visibleTraits, v, 20); };
        const addMutation = (v) => { changes += P.pushUnique(body.mutations, v, 20); changes += P.pushUnique(appearance.visibleTraits, v, 20); };
        const addPerm = (v) => { changes += P.pushUnique(conditions.permanent, v, 20); };

        if (/\b(?:changed|changes|transformed|transforms|becomes|became|made)\s+(?:you\s+)?(?:into\s+)?(?:a\s+|an\s+)?drakekin\b|\bdrakekin\b[^.!?]{0,80}\b(?:horn|claw|scale|silver)\b/.test(lower)) {
          if (player.species !== "drakekin") { player.species = "drakekin"; changes += 1; }
          addTrait("drakekin");
          if (/\bsilver\b/.test(lower)) addTrait("silver drakekin scales");
          if (/\bhorns?\b/.test(lower)) addTrait("horns");
          if (/\bclaws?\b/.test(lower)) addTrait("claws");
        }

        if (/\bfae[- ]blooded\b|\b(?:changed|cursed|transformed|becomes|made)\b[^.!?]{0,80}\bfae\b/.test(lower)) {
          if (player.species !== "fae-blooded") { player.species = "fae-blooded"; changes += 1; }
          addTrait("fae-blooded");
          if (/\b(?:iron|cold iron)\b[^.!?]{0,60}\b(?:weak|weakness|vulnerable|burns?)\b|\b(?:weak|vulnerable)\b[^.!?]{0,60}\b(?:iron|cold iron)\b/.test(lower)) addPerm("cold iron weakness");
          if (/\b(?:magic|glamou?r|wild magic|fae magic|affinity)\b/.test(lower)) addPerm("fae magic affinity");
        }

        if (/\bcrystal\s+(?:right\s+|left\s+)?arm\b|\b(?:right|left)\s+arm\b[^.!?]{0,60}\bcrystal\b/.test(lower)) {
          const side = /\bleft\s+arm\b/.test(lower) ? "left" : /\bright\s+arm\b/.test(lower) ? "right" : "";
          const label = (side ? side + " " : "") + "crystal arm";
          addMutation(label);
          if (!body.limbs || typeof body.limbs !== "object") body.limbs = {};
          if (side === "left") body.limbs.leftArm = "crystal arm";
          else if (side === "right") body.limbs.rightArm = "crystal arm";
          else { body.limbs.rightArm = body.limbs.rightArm || "crystal arm"; }
        }

        if (/\bstar[- ]sight\s+eyes?\b|\beyes?\b[^.!?]{0,80}\b(?:star[- ]sight|see\s+stars|see\s+starlight|read\s+the\s+stars)\b/.test(lower)) {
          addMutation("star-sight eyes");
          addPerm("star-sight vision");
        }

        const titleMatches = [
          raw.match(/\b(?:earn|earned|gain|gained|receive|received|granted|given)\s+(?:the\s+)?title\s+(?:of\s+)?["“]?([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{2,40})(?:["”])?/),
          raw.match(/\b(?:known\s+as|named|called)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{3,40})\b/)
        ];
        for (let i = 0; i < titleMatches.length; i += 1) {
          const m = titleMatches[i];
          if (!m) continue;
          if (m[2] && !player.name) { player.name = m[1]; changes += 1; }
          const title = m[2] || m[1];
          if (title && !/^(?:the|a|an|you|your)$/i.test(title)) changes += P.pushUnique(player.titles, title, 8);
        }

        if (changes > 0) {
          P.pushUnique(player.history.majorEvents, "Transformation/body canon: " + raw.slice(0, 140), 20);
          P.log(root, "Player canon updated: transformation/body/title.");
          P.dirty(root, "player", true);
        }
        return changes;
      },

      applyLongTimeSkipAging(rawText, root) {
        if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
        if (!A.PlayerSystem || !A.PlayerSystem.init) return 0;
        A.PlayerSystem.init(root);
        const raw = String(rawText || "");
        const lower = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
        const m = raw.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)(?:\s+full|\s+long)?\s+years?\s+(?:pass|passes|passed|go\s+by|went\s+by|elapse|elapsed|later|roll\s+by)\b/i) ||
          raw.match(/\b(?:after|through|across)\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)(?:\s+full|\s+long)?\s+years?\b/i);
        if (!m || !m[1]) return 0;
        const years = this.numberWord(m[1]);
        if (years <= 0) return 0;
        const player = root.player || {};
        const immortal = /\b(?:immortal|undead|ageless|time\s*frozen|does\s+not\s+age|do\s+not\s+age)\b/.test(lower) || /\b(?:undead|ageless|immortal)\b/i.test(String(player.species || ""));
        if (!player.history || typeof player.history !== "object") player.history = {};
        if (!Array.isArray(player.history.timeSkips)) player.history.timeSkips = [];
        const sig = this.key(years + ":" + raw.slice(0, 100));
        if (player.history.lastBetaTimeSkipSig === sig) return 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < player.history.timeSkips.length; i += 1) {
          const row = player.history.timeSkips[i] || {};
          if (row.betaSig === sig) return 0;
        }
        let changes = 0;
        player.history.lastBetaTimeSkipSig = sig;
        player.history.timeSkips.push({ turn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0, years: years, betaSig: sig, text: raw.slice(0, 140) });
        while (player.history.timeSkips.length > 12) player.history.timeSkips.shift();
        changes += 1;
        if (!immortal && typeof player.age === "number" && player.age > 0) {
          player.age += years;
          changes += 1;
          const cond = player.conditions || (player.conditions = {});
          if (!Array.isArray(cond.permanent)) cond.permanent = [];
          changes += P.pushUnique(cond.permanent, "aged by " + years + " years", 20);
        } else {
          const cond = player.conditions || (player.conditions = {});
          if (!Array.isArray(cond.permanent)) cond.permanent = [];
          changes += P.pushUnique(cond.permanent, years + " year time skip survived", 20);
        }
        P.log(root, "Long time skip: " + years + " years preserved" + (player.age ? "; age " + player.age : "") + ".");
        P.dirty(root, "player", true);
        P.dirty(root, "time", true);
        return changes;
      },

      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      // Inventory / named item promotion
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      patchItemVocabulary() {
        if (!A.InventorySystem) return;
        const Inv = A.InventorySystem;
        const oldHead = Inv.isItemHeadToken;
        Inv.isItemHeadToken = function(value) {
          const raw = String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/g, "");
          const singular = raw.length > 3 && raw.endsWith("s") ? raw.slice(0, -1) : raw;
          const extra = new Set(["belt", "strap", "sash", "lamp", "candle", "stub", "gemstone", "set", "wheel"]);
          if (extra.has(raw) || extra.has(singular)) return true;
          return oldHead ? oldHead.call(this, value) : false;
        };
        const oldDetect = Inv.detectBaseType;
        Inv.detectBaseType = function(lowerPhrase) {
          const lower = String(lowerPhrase || "").toLowerCase();
          const betaKnown = ["leather belt", "belt", "candle stub", "lockpick set", "cheese wheel", "oil lamp", "gold amulet", "plain iron sword", "iron sword", "silver ring", "ring"];
          for (let i = 0; i < betaKnown.length; i += 1) {
            if (lower.indexOf(betaKnown[i]) !== -1) {
              const words = betaKnown[i].split(/\s+/);
              return words[words.length - 1];
            }
          }
          return oldDetect ? oldDetect.call(this, lowerPhrase) : "item";
        };
        const oldFind = Inv.findMatchingPlayerItem;
        Inv.findMatchingPlayerItem = function(query, root) {
          const item = oldFind ? oldFind.call(this, query, root) : null;
          if (!item) return null;
          const U = A.Utils || {};
          const key = U.toKey ? U.toKey(String(query || "")) : String(query || "").toLowerCase();
          const displayKey = U.toKey ? U.toKey(this.getItemDisplayName ? this.getItemDisplayName(item) : (item.displayName || "")) : String(item.displayName || "").toLowerCase();
          // Do not let a shorter existing item swallow a longer concrete item.
          if (displayKey && key && key.indexOf(displayKey + "_") === 0) return null;
          return item;
        };
      },

      promoteNamedItem(rawText, root) {
        if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || !A.InventorySystem) return 0;
        A.InventorySystem.init(root);
        const raw = String(rawText || "");
        if (!raw.trim()) return 0;
        const patterns = [
          /\byou\s+(?:name|named|call|called)\s+(?:your\s+|the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,60}?)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{2,40})\b/i,
          /\b(?:your\s+|the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,60}?)\s+(?:is\s+)?(?:named|called)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{2,40})\b/i
        ];
        let m = null;
        for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) { m = raw.match(patterns[i]); if (m) break; }
        if (!m || !m[1] || !m[2]) return 0;
        let itemPhrase = this.normalizeSpaces(m[1]).replace(/^(?:plain|old|newly|personal)\s+/i, "").trim();
        const personalName = this.normalizeSpaces(m[2]);
        if (!itemPhrase || !personalName || /^(?:it|item|thing|weapon)$/i.test(itemPhrase)) return 0;
        const item = A.InventorySystem.findMatchingPlayerItem(itemPhrase, root) || A.InventorySystem.findMatchingPlayerItem(itemPhrase.split(/\s+/).slice(-2).join(" "), root);
        if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return 0;
        const baseDisplay = item.baseDisplayName || item.originalDisplayName || item.displayName || itemPhrase;
        let changes = 0;
        if (item.personalName !== personalName) { item.personalName = personalName; changes += 1; }
        if (!item.baseDisplayName) { item.baseDisplayName = baseDisplay; changes += 1; }
        const nextDisplay = personalName + " (" + item.baseDisplayName + ")";
        if (item.displayName !== nextDisplay) { item.displayName = nextDisplay; changes += 1; }
        item.personalItem = true;
        if (!item.notes || typeof item.notes !== "object") item.notes = {};
        if (!Array.isArray(item.notes.observedChanges)) item.notes.observedChanges = [];
        if (/\b(?:glows?|reveals?|protects?|opens?|unlocks?|warns?|points?|guides?|heals?|stores?|contains?|lets\s+you|allows?\s+you|can\s+)\b/i.test(raw)) {
          const fn = raw.replace(/^[\s\S]*?\b(?:and|;|,)\s*/i, "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().slice(0, 140);
          changes += this.pushUnique(item.notes.observedChanges, "Function: " + fn, 8);
        }
        if (changes > 0) {
          P.log(root, "Named item: " + item.displayName + ".");
          P.dirty(root, "items", item.id || item.identity && item.identity.id || "summary");
        }
        return changes;
      },

      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      // Ability variation / CardSync mirror
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      queueAbilityVariationCard(record, root) {
        if (!record || !record.identity || !A.CardSyncSystem) return 0;
        A.CardSyncSystem.init(root);
        const C = A.CardSyncSystem;
        const spec = C.serializeAbilityCard ? C.serializeAbilityCard(record.identity.id, root) : null;
        if (spec && spec.namespace && C.safeEnsureSingletonCard) {
          C.safeEnsureSingletonCard(spec, root);
          P.log(root, "Ability card mirror queued: " + (record.identity.name || record.identity.id) + ".");
          return 1;
        }
        return C.queueDirty ? C.queueDirty("ability", record.identity.id, "ability_variation_mirror", null, root) : 0;
      },

      patchAbilityCardSerialization() {
        if (!A.CardSyncSystem || !A.CardSyncSystem.serializeAbilityCard) return;
        const oldSerialize = A.CardSyncSystem.serializeAbilityCard;
        A.CardSyncSystem.serializeAbilityCard = function(id, root) {
          const spec = oldSerialize.call(this, id, root);
          if (!spec || !root || !root.abilities || !root.abilities.byId) return spec;
          const rec = root.abilities.byId[id];
          if (!rec || !rec.meaning) return spec;
          const extra = [];
          if (rec.meaning.sourceAbilityName) extra.push("Source: " + rec.meaning.sourceAbilityName);
          if (rec.meaning.variationReason) extra.push("Variation Reason: " + rec.meaning.variationReason);
          if (Array.isArray(rec.meaning.limitations) && rec.meaning.limitations.length) extra.push("Limits: " + rec.meaning.limitations.slice(0, 4).join(", "));
          if (Array.isArray(rec.meaning.emergentApplications) && rec.meaning.emergentApplications.length) extra.push("Applications: " + rec.meaning.emergentApplications.slice(0, 4).join(", "));
          if (!extra.length) return spec;
          spec.entry = String(spec.entry || "") + "\n" + extra.join("\n");
          spec.hash = this.stableHash ? this.stableHash(spec.entry) : spec.entry;
          return spec;
        };
      },

      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      // Named creature / actor promotion
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      promoteNamedCreature(rawText, root) {
        if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || !A.ActorProfileSystem || !A.MajorActorMemorySystem) return 0;
        const raw = String(rawText || "");
        const patterns = [
          /\byou\s+(?:name|named|call|called)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?((?:nameless|blue|grey|gray|small|large|young|old|hostile|friendly|wounded|loyal)\s+)?(slime|wolf|fox|dog|cat|hawk|raven|spider|goblin|drake|wyrmling|beast|creature)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{2,40})\b/i,
          /\b(?:the\s+)?((?:blue|grey|gray|small|large|hostile|friendly|wounded|loyal)\s+)?(slime|wolf|fox|dog|cat|hawk|raven|spider|goblin|drake|wyrmling|beast|creature)\s+(?:is\s+)?(?:named|called)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{2,40})\b/i
        ];
        let m = null;
        for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) { m = raw.match(patterns[i]); if (m) break; }
        if (!m) return 0;
        const desc = this.normalizeSpaces(m[1] || "");
        const kind = this.normalizeSpaces(m[2] || "creature").toLowerCase();
        const name = this.normalizeSpaces(m[3] || "");
        if (!name || !kind) return 0;
        A.ActorProfileSystem.init(root);
        A.MajorActorMemorySystem.init(root);
        const sceneKey = this.key(name);
        const candidate = {
          sceneKey: sceneKey,
          displayName: name + " the " + (desc ? desc + " " : "") + kind,
          roleTags: ["named_creature", "companion", kind].filter(Boolean),
          rankTags: ["named"],
          category: "creature",
          importanceScore: 5,
          persistent: true,
          major: true
        };
        const rec = A.ActorProfileSystem.findOrCreateRecord(candidate, root);
        if (!rec || !rec.identity || !rec.identity.id) return 0;
        if (A.ActorProfileSystem.applyCandidateToRecord) A.ActorProfileSystem.applyCandidateToRecord(candidate, rec, root);
        A.MajorActorMemorySystem.ensureMemoryForActor(rec.identity.id, "named_creature_companion", root);
        const mem = root.actors && root.actors.majorMemory && root.actors.majorMemory.byActorId ? root.actors.majorMemory.byActorId[rec.identity.id] : null;
        if (mem) {
          mem.name = candidate.displayName;
          if (!Array.isArray(mem.attitudeNotes)) mem.attitudeNotes = [];
          P.pushUnique(mem.attitudeNotes, "named " + kind + "; follows/recurs with the player", 8);
          mem.relationshipStance = /\bhostile\b/i.test(raw) ? "hostile" : "friendly";
          mem.lastUpdatedTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
          A.MajorActorMemorySystem.pushRecentEvent("named_creature", candidate.displayName, rec.identity.id, "named " + kind + " companion/recurring creature", root);
          A.MajorActorMemorySystem.markActorDirty(rec.identity.id, root);
        }
        P.log(root, "Named creature promoted: " + candidate.displayName + ".");
        P.dirty(root, "actors", rec.identity.id);
        return 1;
      },

      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      // Group encounter counts
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      processGroupCount(rawText, root) {
        if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
        if (!root.world || typeof root.world !== "object") root.world = {};
        const enc = root.world.activeEncounter || (root.world.activeEncounter = {});
        if (!Array.isArray(enc.recentEvents)) enc.recentEvents = [];
        const raw = String(rawText || "");
        const lower = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
        const num = "(\\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty)";
        const parse = (v) => P.numberWord(v);
        const singular = (v) => String(v || "enemy").toLowerCase().replace(/ies$/, "y").replace(/s$/, "").trim();
        const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
        const event = (msg) => {
          const clean = P.normalizeSpaces(msg);
          if (!clean) return 0;
          const last = enc.recentEvents.length ? enc.recentEvents[enc.recentEvents.length - 1] : null;
          if (last && last.turn === turn && last.message === clean) return 0;
          enc.recentEvents.push({ turn, message: clean });
          while (enc.recentEvents.length > 12) enc.recentEvents.shift();
          if (A.WorldEntitySystem && A.WorldEntitySystem.pushMapEvent) A.WorldEntitySystem.pushMapEvent("Encounter: " + clean, root);
          else P.log(root, "Encounter: " + clean);
          return 1;
        };
        let changes = 0;
        const startRe = new RegExp("\\b(?:there\\s+are|you\\s+face|you\\s+see|before\\s+you\\s+stand|a\\s+group\\s+of|a\\s+pack\\s+of|a\\s+swarm\\s+of|a\\s+band\\s+of)\\s+" + num + "\\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{2,28}?)(?:s)?\\b", "i");
        let m = raw.match(startRe);
        if (m && /\b(?:attack|ambush|surround|charge|remain|stand|block|threaten|close|wait|advance|guard|fight|bar|barred|hold)\b/i.test(raw)) {
          const count = parse(m[1]); const label = singular(m[2]);
          if (count > 0 && label && !/^(?:minute|hour|day|year|coin|silver|gold)$/.test(label)) {
            enc.enemyLabel = label; enc.totalCount = count; enc.remainingCount = count; enc.resolved = false; enc.lastChangedTurn = turn;
            changes += 1 + event(count + " " + label + " enemies engaged; " + count + " remain.");
          }
        }
        const reinRe = new RegExp("\\b" + num + "\\s+(?:more|additional|new|reinforcing)\\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{2,28}?)(?:s)?\\s+(?:arrive|join|rush\\s+in|appear|reinforce)", "i");
        m = raw.match(reinRe);
        if (m) {
          const add = parse(m[1]); const label = singular(m[2]);
          if (add > 0) {
            if (!enc.enemyLabel) enc.enemyLabel = label || "enemy";
            enc.totalCount = Math.max(0, Number(enc.totalCount || 0)) + add;
            enc.remainingCount = Math.max(0, Number(enc.remainingCount || 0)) + add;
            enc.resolved = false; enc.lastChangedTurn = turn;
            changes += 1 + event(add + " reinforcements joined; " + enc.remainingCount + " " + enc.enemyLabel + " remain.");
          }
        }
        const killRes = [
          new RegExp("\\b(?:you\\s+)?(?:kill|slay|drop|fell|defeat|cut\\s+down|take\\s+down|destroy|rout)\\s+" + num + "\\s+(?:of\\s+the\\s+)?([a-z][a-z' -]{2,28}?)(?:s)?\\b", "i"),
          new RegExp("\\b" + num + "\\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{2,28}?)(?:s)?\\s+(?:fall|die|are\\s+slain|are\\s+defeated|collapse|flee|surrender|are\\s+routed)\\b", "i")
        ];
        for (let i = 0; i < killRes.length; i += 1) {
          m = raw.match(killRes[i]);
          if (!m) continue;
          const dead = parse(m[1]); const label = singular(m[2]);
          if (dead > 0) {
            const priorSameTurn = Array.isArray(enc.recentEvents) && enc.recentEvents.some(function(row) { return row && row.turn === turn && new RegExp("\\b" + dead + "\\s+" + (enc.enemyLabel || label || "") + "\\b.*(?:defeated|remain)", "i").test(String(row.message || "")); });
            if (priorSameTurn) { break; }
            if (!enc.enemyLabel) enc.enemyLabel = label || "enemy";
            if (!enc.totalCount) enc.totalCount = Math.max(dead, Number(enc.remainingCount || 0));
            const before = Math.max(0, Number(enc.remainingCount || enc.totalCount || 0));
            const after = Math.max(0, before - dead);
            enc.remainingCount = after; enc.resolved = after <= 0; enc.lastChangedTurn = turn;
            changes += 1 + event(dead + " " + enc.enemyLabel + " defeated/resolved; " + after + " remain.");
            break;
          }
        }
        if (enc.enemyLabel && Number(enc.remainingCount || 0) > 0 && /\b(?:all|every|the\s+last)\s+(?:enemy|enemies|foes?|goblins?|bandits?|wolves?|soldiers?|orcs?|skeletons?)\s+(?:are\s+)?(?:dead|defeated|gone|finished)\b/i.test(raw)) {
          changes += event("continuity holds: " + enc.remainingCount + " " + enc.enemyLabel + " remain unresolved.");
        }
        if (changes > 0) P.dirty(root, "entities", "active_encounter");
        return changes;
      },

      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      // Reputation delay and reporting
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      processReputationDelay(rawText, root) {
        if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
        if (!A.ReputationSystem || !A.ReputationSystem.init) return 0;
        A.ReputationSystem.init(root);
        const raw = String(rawText || "");
        const lower = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
        const rep = root.reputation || (root.reputation = {});
        if (!Array.isArray(rep.pendingDeeds)) rep.pendingDeeds = [];
        let changes = 0;
        const deed = /\b(?:defeat|defeated|clear|cleared|slay|slain|kill|killed|rescue|rescued|save|saved|recover|recovered|steal|stole|theft)\b/.test(lower);
        const hidden = /\b(?:hidden|alone|unseen|no\s+witness|without\s+witness|no\s+one\s+saw|deep\s+in\s+the\s+dungeon|in\s+the\s+barrow)\b/.test(lower);
        if (deed && hidden && !/\b(?:word\s+spreads|rumou?rs?\s+spread|report|reported|tell|told|proof)\b/.test(lower)) {
          const label = this.normalizeSpaces(raw).slice(0, 140) || "hidden deed";
          let exists = false;
          for (let i = 0; i < rep.pendingDeeds.length; i += 1) if (String(rep.pendingDeeds[i].label || "") === label) exists = true;
          if (!exists) {
            rep.pendingDeeds.push({ turn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0, label, scope:"hidden", status:"pending_report" });
            while (rep.pendingDeeds.length > 12) rep.pendingDeeds.shift();
            P.log(root, "Reputation delay: hidden deed held until report/proof/witness.");
            changes += 1;
          }
        }
        if (/\b(?:report|reported|tell|told|show|shown|prove|proved|bring\s+proof|turn\s+in)\b[^.!?]{0,80}\b(?:guild|guard|captain|mayor|town|locals|authority)\b|\b(?:guild|guard|captain|mayor|authority)\b[^.!?]{0,80}\b(?:learns?|hears?|accepts?|confirms?)\b/.test(lower)) {
          const pending = rep.pendingDeeds.length ? rep.pendingDeeds[rep.pendingDeeds.length - 1] : null;
          if (pending && pending.status !== "reported") {
            pending.status = "reported"; pending.reportTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
            P.log(root, "Reputation report: hidden deed now known to guild/local authority.");
            if (A.ReputationSystem.applyReputationChange) {
              const subject = /\bguild\b/.test(lower) ? A.ReputationSystem.resolveSubject("guild", root) : A.ReputationSystem.resolveSubject("local_group", root);
              A.ReputationSystem.applyReputationChange(subject, "positive", "reported hidden deed", raw, root);
            }
            changes += 1;
          }
        }
        if (/\b(?:remote|distant|far)\s+(?:town|city|village)\b/.test(lower) && /\b(?:does\s+not|doesn't|has\s+not|hasn't|cannot|can't)\s+(?:know|heard|learned)\b/.test(lower)) {
          P.log(root, "Reputation scope: remote town does not know local deed yet.");
          changes += 1;
        }
        if (changes > 0) P.dirty(root, "reputation", true);
        return changes;
      },

      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      // Goal/location exact binding
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      reinforceGoalLocation(rawText, root) {
        if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
        const raw = String(rawText || "");
        if (!raw.trim()) return 0;
        if (!A.WorldEntitySystem || !A.WorldEntitySystem.processMapContinuity) return 0;
        let changes = 0;
        // Re-run the existing binder, then add route/map-aware explicit bindings for common objective wording.
        changes += A.WorldEntitySystem.captureGoalLocationBinding ? (A.WorldEntitySystem.captureGoalLocationBinding(raw, root) || 0) : 0;
        const map = A.WorldEntitySystem.getMapMemory ? A.WorldEntitySystem.getMapMemory(root) : null;
        if (!map) return changes;
        if (!map.goalBindingsById || typeof map.goalBindingsById !== "object") map.goalBindingsById = {};
        const re = /\b(?:meet|pay|return\s+to|bring|deliver|find|speak\s+to)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,60}?)\s+at\s+(The\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+){0,4}|[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+){1,4})\b/g;
        let m;
        while ((m = re.exec(raw))) {
          const goal = this.normalizeSpaces(m[0]);
          const place = this.normalizeSpaces(m[2]);
          const id = "goal_" + this.key(goal + "_" + place);
          const locRef = "location_" + this.key(place);
          map.goalBindingsById[id] = { goal: goal, locationName: place, locationRef: locRef, firstSeenTurn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0 };
          if (A.WorldEntitySystem.ensureLocationRecord) A.WorldEntitySystem.ensureLocationRecord(locRef, place, root);
          changes += 1;
        }
        if (changes > 0) P.dirty(root, "quests", true);
        return changes;
      },

      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      // Intent corrections
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------
      refineIntent(intent, rawText) {
        if (!intent || typeof intent !== "object") return intent;
        const raw = String(rawText || "");
        const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
        const setPrimary = (type) => {
          if (intent.primaryType === type) return;
