          const old = intent.primaryType;
          intent.primaryType = type;
          intent.confidence = Math.max(Number(intent.confidence || 0.55), 0.78);
          if (!Array.isArray(intent.alternates)) intent.alternates = [];
          if (old && old !== type) intent.alternates.unshift({ type: old, confidence: 0.65 });
          if (!Array.isArray(intent.tags)) intent.tags = [];
          if (intent.tags.indexOf("intent:" + type) === -1) intent.tags.push("intent:" + type);
        };
        if (/^\s*(?:i\s+)?(?:ask|tell|say|speak|talk|whisper|reply)\b/.test(lower)) setPrimary("talk");
        if (/\b(?:strap|buckle|fasten|sling)\s+on\b[^.!?]{0,60}\b(?:shield|armor|armour|belt|helm|helmet|cloak)\b|\b(?:shield|armor|armour|belt|helm|helmet|cloak)\b[^.!?]{0,40}\b(?:strap|buckled|fastened)\b/.test(lower)) setPrimary("equip");
        const routeMatch = raw.match(/\bfrom\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;,]+?)\s+to\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;,]+?)(?:[.!?,;]|$)/i);
        if (routeMatch && routeMatch[2]) intent.destinationHint = P.normalizePlaceLabel(routeMatch[2]);
        return intent;
      }
    };

    // ---- Install wrappers ----
    P.patchItemVocabulary();
    P.patchAbilityCardSerialization();

    if (A.IntentParser && A.IntentParser.parseInput) {
      const oldParseInput = A.IntentParser.parseInput;
      A.IntentParser.parseInput = function(rawText, root) {
        const intent = oldParseInput.call(this, rawText, root);
        const next = P.refineIntent(intent, rawText);
        if (root && root.pending) root.pending.inputIntent = next;
        return next;
      };
    }

    if (A.PlayerSystem && A.PlayerSystem.processOutput) {
      const oldPlayerOutput = A.PlayerSystem.processOutput;
      A.PlayerSystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
        root = root || state.aidrpg;
        let changes = oldPlayerOutput.call(this, rawText, root) || 0;
        changes += P.applyNumericHpDamage(rawText, root);
        changes += P.applyTransformationCanon(rawText, root);
        changes += P.applyLongTimeSkipAging(rawText, root);
        return changes;
      };
      if (A.PlayerSystem.getCompactSummary) {
        const oldPlayerSummary = A.PlayerSystem.getCompactSummary;
        A.PlayerSystem.getCompactSummary = function(player) {
          let out = oldPlayerSummary.call(this, player);
          const body = player && player.body ? player.body : {};
          const traits = [];
          if (Array.isArray(body.speciesTraits)) traits.push.apply(traits, body.speciesTraits.slice(-3));
          if (Array.isArray(body.mutations)) traits.push.apply(traits, body.mutations.slice(-3));
          if (player && player.conditions && Array.isArray(player.conditions.permanent) && player.conditions.permanent.length) {
            traits.push.apply(traits, player.conditions.permanent.slice(-3));
          }
          if (traits.length) out += " | Body: " + traits.slice(-5).join(", ");
          return out;
        };
      }
    }

    if (A.InventorySystem && A.InventorySystem.processOutput) {
      const oldInvOutput = A.InventorySystem.processOutput;
      A.InventorySystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
        root = root || state.aidrpg;
        let changes = oldInvOutput.call(this, rawText, root) || 0;
        changes += P.promoteNamedItem(rawText, root);
        return changes;
      };
    }

    if (A.AbilitySystem) {
      const oldExtractInputCandidate = A.AbilitySystem.extractInputCandidate;
      if (oldExtractInputCandidate) {
        A.AbilitySystem.extractInputCandidate = function(rawText, intent) {
          const c = oldExtractInputCandidate.call(this, rawText, intent);
          if (c && /\bEarth\s+Control\b/i.test(String(rawText || ""))) c.name = "Earth Control";
          return c;
        };
      }
      const oldAbilityOutput = A.AbilitySystem.processOutput;
      if (oldAbilityOutput) {
        A.AbilitySystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
          root = root || state.aidrpg;
          const beforeIds = root && root.abilities && Array.isArray(root.abilities.allIds) ? root.abilities.allIds.slice() : [];
          let changes = oldAbilityOutput.call(this, rawText, root) || 0;
          const afterIds = root && root.abilities && Array.isArray(root.abilities.allIds) ? root.abilities.allIds : [];
          for (let i = 0; i < afterIds.length; i += 1) {
            if (beforeIds.indexOf(afterIds[i]) !== -1) continue;
            const rec = root.abilities.byId[afterIds[i]];
            if (rec && rec.meaning && rec.meaning.sourceAbilityRef) P.queueAbilityVariationCard(rec, root);
          }
          return changes;
        };
      }
    }



    if (A.PersistentTraitSystem && A.PersistentTraitSystem.processOutput) {
      const oldTraitOutput = A.PersistentTraitSystem.processOutput;
      A.PersistentTraitSystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
        root = root || state.aidrpg;
        let changes = oldTraitOutput.call(this, rawText, root) || 0;
        changes += P.applyTransformationCanon(rawText, root);
        changes += P.applyLongTimeSkipAging(rawText, root);
        return changes;
      };
    }

    if (A.MajorActorMemorySystem && A.MajorActorMemorySystem.processOutput) {
      const oldActorMemOutput = A.MajorActorMemorySystem.processOutput;
      A.MajorActorMemorySystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
        root = root || state.aidrpg;
        let changes = oldActorMemOutput.call(this, rawText, root) || 0;
        changes += P.promoteNamedCreature(rawText, root);
        return changes;
      };
    }

    if (A.WorldEntitySystem && A.WorldEntitySystem.processOutput) {
      const oldWorldOutput = A.WorldEntitySystem.processOutput;
      A.WorldEntitySystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
        root = root || state.aidrpg;
        let changes = oldWorldOutput.call(this, rawText, root) || 0;
        changes += P.processGroupCount(rawText, root);
        changes += P.reinforceGoalLocation(rawText, root);
        return changes;
      };
    }

    if (A.TimeTravelSystem) {
      const T = A.TimeTravelSystem;
      const oldMakeLocationRef = T.makeLocationRef;
      T.makeLocationRef = function(label) {
        const clean = P.normalizePlaceLabel ? P.normalizePlaceLabel(label) : String(label || "");
        return oldMakeLocationRef ? oldMakeLocationRef.call(this, clean) : ("location_" + P.key(clean));
      };
      P.normalizePlaceLabel = function(label) {
        let clean = P.normalizeSpaces(label).replace(/^(?:the|an|a)\s+/i, "").replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "");
        clean = clean.replace(/\s+(?:usually|normally|typically|takes?|took|lasts?|lasted|is|are|was|were|with|without|because|after|before|and|but|delays?|delayed|adds?)\b[\s\S]*$/i, "").trim();
        const key = clean.toLowerCase().replace(/[’']/g, "");
        const aliases = {
          "guild":"Adventurers Guild", "guild hall":"Adventurers Guild", "adventurer guild":"Adventurers Guild", "adventurers guild":"Adventurers Guild", "adventurers guild hall":"Adventurers Guild",
          "old barrow":"Old Barrow", "barrow":"Old Barrow", "the old barrow":"Old Barrow",
          "market lane":"Market Lane", "ruined chapel":"Ruined Chapel", "drunken huntsman":"The Drunken Huntsman", "the drunken huntsman":"The Drunken Huntsman", "rusted lantern":"The Rusted Lantern", "the rusted lantern":"The Rusted Lantern"
        };
        return aliases[key] || clean;
      };
      P.extractRouteFromText = function(text) {
        const raw = P.normalizeSpaces(text);
        const num = "(\\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve)";
        const durRe = new RegExp(num + "\\s+(minutes?|hours?)", "i");
        const parseDuration = (m) => m ? P.numberWord(m[1]) * (/hour/i.test(m[2]) ? 60 : 1) : 0;
        const patterns = [
          new RegExp("\\bit\\s+takes\\s+" + num + "\\s+(minutes?|hours?)\\s+to\\s+travel\\s+from\\s+(?:the\\s+)?([^.!?;,]+?)\\s+to\\s+(?:the\\s+)?([^.!?;,]+?)(?=\\s*(?:[.!?,;]|$))", "i"),
          new RegExp("\\b(?:the\\s+)?(?:trip|route|road|path|trail)\\s+from\\s+(?:the\\s+)?([^.!?;,]+?)\\s+to\\s+(?:the\\s+)?([^.!?;,]+?)\\s+(?:usually\\s+|normally\\s+|typically\\s+)?takes\\s+" + num + "\\s+(minutes?|hours?)", "i"),
          new RegExp("\\bfrom\\s+(?:the\\s+)?([^.!?;,]+?)\\s+to\\s+(?:the\\s+)?([^.!?;,]+?)\\s+(?:usually\\s+|normally\\s+|typically\\s+)?takes\\s+" + num + "\\s+(minutes?|hours?)", "i")
        ];
        for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
          const m = raw.match(patterns[i]);
          if (!m) continue;
          if (i === 0) return { from:P.normalizePlaceLabel(m[3]), to:P.normalizePlaceLabel(m[4]), baseMinutes:P.numberWord(m[1]) * (/hour/i.test(m[2]) ? 60 : 1) };
          return { from:P.normalizePlaceLabel(m[1]), to:P.normalizePlaceLabel(m[2]), baseMinutes:P.numberWord(m[3]) * (/hour/i.test(m[4]) ? 60 : 1) };
        }
        // If the route endpoints are present but duration is elsewhere in the sentence.
        const endpoint = raw.match(/\b(?:travel|trip|route|road|path|journey|return)\s+from\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;,]+?)\s+to\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;,]+?)(?=\s+(?:usually|normally|typically|takes?|took|with|without|because|but|and|,|\.|$)|[.!?,;]|$)/i) || raw.match(/\bfrom\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;,]+?)\s+to\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;,]+?)(?=\s+(?:usually|normally|typically|takes?|took|with|without|because|but|and|,|\.|$)|[.!?,;]|$)/i);
        const dm = raw.match(durRe);
        if (endpoint && dm) return { from:P.normalizePlaceLabel(endpoint[1]), to:P.normalizePlaceLabel(endpoint[2]), baseMinutes:parseDuration(dm) };
        return null;
      };
      P.routeDelayFromText = function(text) {
        const raw = String(text || "").toLowerCase();
        const reason = /\brain\b/.test(raw) ? "rain" : /\bstorm\b/.test(raw) ? "storm" : /\bmud\b/.test(raw) ? "mud" : /\b(?:fight|ambush|wolf|bandit|encounter|battle)\b/.test(raw) ? "encounter" : /\b(?:injur|wounded|limp|escort|heavy loot|scout|detour|roadblock|blocked|obstruction)\b/.test(raw) ? "slow travel" : "";
        const m = raw.match(/\b(?:delays?|delayed|slows?|slowed|adds?|added|costs?)\s+(?:you\s+|the\s+party\s+|the\s+trip\s+)?(?:by\s+)?(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve)\s+(minutes?|hours?)\b/) || raw.match(/\b(?:rain|storm|mud|fight|ambush|encounter|detour|injury|scouting|roadblock)[^.!?]{0,60}\b(?:by\s+)?(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve)\s+(minutes?|hours?)\b/);
        const minutes = m ? P.numberWord(m[1]) * (/hour/i.test(m[2]) ? 60 : 1) : 0;
        return { reason, minutes };
      };
      const oldExtractRoute = T.extractRouteMention;
      T.extractRouteMention = function(text, root) {
        const direct = P.extractRouteFromText(text);
        if (direct && direct.from && direct.to) return { from: direct.from, to: direct.to };
        const old = oldExtractRoute ? oldExtractRoute.call(this, text, root) : null;
        if (old) { if (old.from) old.from = P.normalizePlaceLabel(old.from); if (old.to) old.to = P.normalizePlaceLabel(old.to); }
        return old;
      };
      const oldResolve = T.resolveRouteTiming;
      T.resolveRouteTiming = function(pending, text, root) {
        if (pending && String(pending.kind || "") === "travel") {
          const direct = P.extractRouteFromText(text);
          if (direct && direct.from && direct.to && direct.baseMinutes > 0) {
            const fromRef = this.makeLocationRef(direct.from);
            const toRef = this.makeLocationRef(direct.to);
            const known = this.getKnownRouteDuration(fromRef, toRef, root);
            const delay = P.routeDelayFromText(text);
            let base = direct.baseMinutes;
            let actual = base + (delay.minutes || 0);
            if (known && known.minutes > 0 && Math.abs(base - known.minutes) > 15 && !delay.reason) {
              this.pushRouteEvent("Route contradiction: " + this.formatRouteLabel(fromRef, toRef, root) + " remains " + known.minutes + " minutes; rejected " + base + " minutes.", root);
              this.pushContradictionFlag("route_time_conflict", root);
              base = known.minutes; actual = known.minutes;
            } else if (known && known.minutes > 0 && delay.reason) {
              base = known.minutes; actual = base + (delay.minutes || Math.max(0, direct.baseMinutes - base));
            }
            return { minutes:actual, baseMinutes:base, delayReason:delay.reason, delayMinutes:delay.minutes, fromRef, toRef, toLabel:direct.to };
          }
        }
        if (pending && String(pending.kind || "") === "travel") {
          const rawText = String(text || "");
          const dm = rawText.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve)\s+(minutes?|hours?)\b/i);
          const explicit = dm ? P.numberWord(dm[1]) * (/hour/i.test(dm[2]) ? 60 : 1) : 0;
          const fromRef = String(pending.startLocationRef || "");
          const toRef = this.makeLocationRef(pending.destinationHint || "");
          const known = fromRef && toRef ? this.getKnownRouteDuration(fromRef, toRef, root) : null;
          const delay = P.routeDelayFromText(rawText);
          if (explicit > 0 && known && known.minutes > 0 && Math.abs(explicit - known.minutes) > 15 && !delay.reason) {
            this.pushRouteEvent("Route contradiction: " + this.formatRouteLabel(fromRef, toRef, root) + " remains " + known.minutes + " minutes; rejected " + explicit + " minutes.", root);
            this.pushContradictionFlag("route_time_conflict", root);
            return { minutes: known.minutes, baseMinutes: known.minutes, rejectedMinutes: explicit, delayReason: "", fromRef: fromRef, toRef: toRef, toLabel: pending.destinationHint || "" };
          }
        }
        return oldResolve ? oldResolve.call(this, pending, text, root) : null;
      };
      const oldCapture = T.captureRouteTimingFromText;
      T.captureRouteTimingFromText = function(text, root) {
        let changes = oldCapture ? oldCapture.call(this, text, root) || 0 : 0;
        const direct = P.extractRouteFromText(text);
        if (direct && direct.from && direct.to && direct.baseMinutes > 0) {
          const fromRef = this.makeLocationRef(direct.from), toRef = this.makeLocationRef(direct.to);
          const known = this.getKnownRouteDuration(fromRef, toRef, root);
          const delay = P.routeDelayFromText(text);
          let base = direct.baseMinutes;
          let actual = base + (delay.minutes || 0);
          if (known && known.minutes > 0 && Math.abs(base - known.minutes) > 15 && !delay.reason) {
            changes += this.pushRouteEvent("Route contradiction: " + this.formatRouteLabel(fromRef, toRef, root) + " remains " + known.minutes + " minutes; rejected " + base + " minutes.", root);
            changes += this.pushContradictionFlag("route_time_conflict", root);
            return changes;
          }
          if (known && known.minutes > 0 && delay.reason) { base = known.minutes; actual = base + (delay.minutes || Math.max(0, direct.baseMinutes - base)); }
          this.rememberRouteDuration(fromRef, toRef, base, root, { actualMinutes:actual, delayReason:delay.reason, rawText:text });
          changes += 1;
        }
        return changes;
      };
    }

    if (A.ReputationSystem && A.ReputationSystem.processOutput) {
      const oldRepOutput = A.ReputationSystem.processOutput;
      A.ReputationSystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
        root = root || state.aidrpg;
        let changes = oldRepOutput.call(this, rawText, root) || 0;
        changes += P.processReputationDelay(rawText, root);
        return changes;
      };
    }

    if (A.ContextPacketSystem && A.ContextPacketSystem.makePriorityPacket) {
      const oldPriority = A.ContextPacketSystem.makePriorityPacket;
      A.ContextPacketSystem.makePriorityPacket = function(mode, root) {
        let base = oldPriority.call(this, mode, root) || "";
        const extras = [];
        const player = root && root.player ? root.player : {};
        if (player && player.history && Array.isArray(player.history.timeSkips) && player.history.timeSkips.length) {
          const last = player.history.timeSkips[player.history.timeSkips.length - 1];
          if (last && last.years) extras.push("Time skip " + last.years + "y" + (player.age ? "; age " + player.age : ""));
        }
        const enc = root && root.world ? root.world.activeEncounter : null;
        if (enc && enc.enemyLabel && Number(enc.remainingCount || 0) > 0 && enc.resolved !== true) extras.push("Encounter " + enc.remainingCount + "/" + enc.totalCount + " " + enc.enemyLabel + " remain");
        const rep = root && root.reputation ? root.reputation : {};
        if (Array.isArray(rep.pendingDeeds) && rep.pendingDeeds.length) {
          const last = rep.pendingDeeds[rep.pendingDeeds.length - 1];
          if (last && last.status) extras.push("Rep deed " + last.status + ": " + String(last.label || "").slice(0, 70));
        }
        if (extras.length) base = (base ? base + " | " : "Priority: ") + extras.join(" | ");
        return base;
      };
    }
  })(g.AIDRPG);


  // -----------------------------------------------------------------
  // AIDRPG BETA 2 CONFLICT / CANON POLISH PATCH
  // Targets: distinct item identity, city-scoped locations, unique item
  // functions/CardSync, species growth bias, named creatures, and route notes.
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------
  (function installAIDRPGBeta2Patch(A) {
    "use strict";
    if (!A || A.__beta2PatchInstalled === true) return;
    A.__beta2PatchInstalled = true;
    A.VERSION = "1.0.5-beta";
    A.BUILD_STAGE = "AIDRPGbeta2-conflict-canon-polish";

    const P2 = A.Beta2ConflictPatch = {
      norm(v) { return String(v == null ? "" : v).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim(); },
      key(v) { const U = A.Utils || {}; return U.toKey ? U.toKey(v) : String(v || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_").replace(/^_+|_+$/g, ""); },
      log(root, msg) { if (A.Utils && A.Utils.logEvent) A.Utils.logEvent(String(msg || "")); },
      dirty(root, kind, value) { if (A.Utils && A.Utils.markDirty) A.Utils.markDirty(kind, value, root); if (A.Utils && A.Utils.invalidatePackets) A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root); },
      pushUnique(list, value, max) {
        if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
        const clean = this.norm(value);
        if (!clean) return 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) if (String(list[i] || "").toLowerCase() === clean.toLowerCase()) return 0;
        list.push(clean);
        while (list.length > Math.max(1, Number(max || 12))) list.shift();
        return 1;
      },
      sourceId(text, sourceContext) {
        const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();
        const source = String(sourceContext || "").toLowerCase();
        if (/\b(player[- ]made|self[- ]made|homemade|crafted|craft\s+and\s+keep|brewed|brew)\b/.test(lower) || /craft|brew/.test(source)) return "player-made";
        if (/\b(store[- ]bought|shop[- ]bought|merchant[- ]bought|bought\s+from|from\s+the\s+merchant)\b/.test(lower) || /buy|bought|purchase|barter/.test(source)) return "store-bought";
        if (/\b(gifted|given|presented|awarded)\b/.test(lower) || /given|gift|award/.test(source)) return "gifted";
        if (/\b(found|recovered|looted|salvaged)\b/.test(lower) || /loot|found|recover/.test(source)) return "found";
        return "";
      },
      forceNew(text) { return /\b(another|second|different|separate|new|normal|plain|spare)\b/i.test(String(text || "")); },
      special(text) {
        let raw = this.norm(text).replace(/[“”"`]/g, "");
        if (!raw) return "";
        raw = raw.replace(/\s+(and\s+keep(?:s|ing)?\s+moving|from\s+the\s+(merchant|shop)|into\s+your\s+(pouch|pack|bag|pocket)).*$/i, "");
        const pats = [
          /\b(?:that|which)\s+([^.!?;,]{3,160})/i,
          /\b((?:opens?|unlocks?)\s+(?:the\s+)?[^.!?;,]{3,120}(?:\s+only\s+(?:at|during|under|when)\s+[^.!?;,]{2,80})?)/i,
          /\b((?:glows?|shines?|lights?)\s+when\s+[^.!?;,]{3,100})/i,
          /\b((?:cuts?|severs?|binds?|detects?|points?\s+toward|mutes?|silences?|hides?)\s+[^.!?;,]{3,120})/i
        ];
        for (let i = 0; i < pats.length; i += 1) {
          const m = raw.match(pats[i]);
          if (m && m[1]) return this.norm(m[1]).replace(/\s+(and|but|then)\s+.*$/i, "").replace(/[.!?;,]+$/g, "").slice(0, 160);
        }
        return "";
      },
      stripSpecial(text) {
        return this.norm(text)
          .replace(/\b(?:that|which)\s+[^.!?;,]{3,180}$/i, "")
          .replace(/\s+(and\s+keep(?:s|ing)?\s+moving|from\s+the\s+(merchant|shop)).*$/i, "")
          .trim();
      },
      cleanItem(text, Inv) {
        let raw = this.norm(text).replace(/[“”"'`]/g, "");
        raw = raw.replace(/^\s*(you\s+)?(pick(ed)?\s+up|grab(bed)?|take|takes|took|collect(ed)?|gather(ed)?|loot(ed)?|snatch(ed)?|snag(ged)?|nab(bed)?|yoink(ed)?|scoop(ed)?\s+up|pocket(ed)?|stash(ed)?|retrieve(d)?|recover(ed)?|find|found|buy|buys|bought|purchase(d)?|receive(d)?|accept(ed)?|craft(ed)?|brew(ed)?|make|made)\s+/i, "");
        raw = raw.replace(/^\s*and\s+keep\s+/i, "");
        const sp = this.special(raw);
        let base = this.stripSpecial(raw)
          .replace(/^(the|a|an|some|my|your)\s+/i, "")
          .replace(/^(another|second|different|separate|new|normal|plain|spare)\s+/i, "")
          .replace(/\s+(from|for)\s+(the\s+)?(merchant|shop|vendor|seller).*$/i, "")
          .replace(/\s+(and|then|but|while|as)\b.*$/i, "")
          .replace(/[.!?;,]+$/g, "")
          .trim();
        if (!base) return "";
        const tokens = base.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
        let head = Inv && Inv.findConcreteItemHeadIndex ? Inv.findConcreteItemHeadIndex(tokens) : tokens.length - 1;
        if (head < 0) head = tokens.length - 1;
        if (tokens[1] && String(tokens[1]).toLowerCase() === "of") base = tokens.slice(0, Math.min(tokens.length, 5)).join(" ");
        else base = tokens.slice(Math.max(0, head - 4), head + 1).join(" ");
        base = this.norm(base);
        if (sp && /^potion$/i.test(base)) return base + " that " + sp;
        return base;
      },
      same(a, b) { return this.norm(a).toLowerCase() === this.norm(b).toLowerCase(); },
      detectCity(raw, root) {
        const m = String(raw || "").match(/\b(Stoneford|Greybridge|Ravenfall|Highmere|Eastport|Westhaven|Ashford|Bramblewick)\b/i);
        if (m) return m[1].replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase());
        const loc = root && root.player ? String(root.player.locationRef || "") : "";
        const k = loc.replace(/^location_/, "").replace(/_/g, " ").toLowerCase();
        const known = ["stoneford","greybridge","ravenfall","highmere","eastport","westhaven","ashford","bramblewick"];
        for (let i = 0; i < known.length; i += 1) if (k === known[i]) return known[i].replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase());
        return "";
      },
      detectBuilding(raw) {
        const lower = String(raw || "").toLowerCase();
        if (/\badventurers? guild\b|\bguild hall\b/.test(lower)) return "Adventurers Guild";
        if (/\btavern common room\b|\bcommon room\b/.test(lower)) return "tavern common room";
        if (/\bmarket square\b/.test(lower)) return "market square";
        if (/\bblacksmith\b|\bsmithy\b/.test(lower)) return "blacksmith";
        if (/\binn\b/.test(lower)) return "inn";
        if (/\btavern\b/.test(lower)) return "tavern";
        return "";
      },
      scopeCityScene(rawText, root) {
        if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || !A.WorldEntitySystem || !A.SceneStateSystem) return 0;
        A.WorldEntitySystem.init(root); A.SceneStateSystem.init(root);
        const raw = String(rawText || "");
        let changes = 0;
        const cityOnly = raw.match(/\b(?:arrive|arrives|return|returns|travel|travels)\s+(?:in|to)\s+(?:the\s+)?(Stoneford|Greybridge|Ravenfall|Highmere|Eastport|Westhaven|Ashford|Bramblewick)\b/i);
        if (cityOnly) {
          const cRef = "location_" + this.key(cityOnly[1]);
          if (A.WorldEntitySystem.ensureLocationRecord) A.WorldEntitySystem.ensureLocationRecord(cRef, cityOnly[1], root);
          if (root.player && root.player.locationRef !== cRef) { root.player.locationRef = cRef; changes += 1; }
        }
        const city = this.detectCity(raw, root);
        const building = this.detectBuilding(raw);
        if (!city || !building) return changes;
        const cityRef = "location_" + this.key(city);
        const key = this.key(city + " " + building);
        const sceneRef = "scene_location_" + key;
        const entityRef = "entity_location_" + key;
        const display = city + " " + building;
        const scene = root.world.currentScene || (root.world.currentScene = {});
        if (A.WorldEntitySystem.ensureLocationRecord) A.WorldEntitySystem.ensureLocationRecord(cityRef, city, root);
        const ent = A.WorldEntitySystem.ensureEntityRecord ? A.WorldEntitySystem.ensureEntityRecord(entityRef, display, building, root) : null;
        if (ent && ent.links) { ent.links.parentLocationRef = cityRef; ent.links.sceneRef = sceneRef; }
        if (root.player) { root.player.locationRef = cityRef; root.player.sceneRef = sceneRef; }
        if (scene.sceneRef !== sceneRef) { scene.sceneRef = sceneRef; changes += 1; }
        if (scene.entityRef !== entityRef) { scene.entityRef = entityRef; changes += 1; }
        if (scene.displayName !== display) { scene.displayName = display; changes += 1; }
        if (scene.parentLocationRef !== cityRef) { scene.parentLocationRef = cityRef; changes += 1; }
        scene.sceneType = /inn|tavern|guild|blacksmith/.test(building.toLowerCase()) ? "building" : "location";
        scene.sceneConfidence = Math.max(Number(scene.sceneConfidence || 0), 3);
        const facts = [];
        if (/\bblue sign\b/i.test(raw)) facts.push("blue sign");
        if (/\bcarved wolf\b/i.test(raw)) facts.push("carved wolf");
        if (/\bred sign\b/i.test(raw)) facts.push("red sign");
        if (/\bbrass lion\b/i.test(raw)) facts.push("brass lion");
        if (ent && ent.mutable && Array.isArray(ent.mutable.objectHints)) for (let i = 0; i < facts.length; i += 1) this.pushUnique(ent.mutable.objectHints, facts[i], 12);
        if (A.Utils && A.Utils.invalidateSceneCache) A.Utils.invalidateSceneCache(root);
        if (changes > 0) this.log(root, "Scoped location: " + display + ".");
        return changes;
      },
      promoteCreature(rawText, root) {
        if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || !A.ActorProfileSystem || !A.MajorActorMemorySystem) return 0;
        const raw = String(rawText || "");
        const m1 = raw.match(/\byou\s+(?:name|named|call|called)\s+it\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{2,40})\b/i);
        const m2 = raw.match(/\byou\s+(?:name|named|call|called)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?((?:nameless|blue|green|grey|gray|small|large|young|old|hostile|friendly|wounded|loyal)\s+)?(slime|wolf|fox|dog|cat|hawk|raven|spider|goblin|drake|wyrmling|beast|creature)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{2,40})\b/i);
        let name = "", kind = "creature", desc = "";
        if (m2) { desc = this.norm(m2[1]); kind = this.norm(m2[2]).toLowerCase(); name = this.norm(m2[3]); }
        else if (m1) { name = this.norm(m1[1]); const k = raw.match(/\b(blue|green|grey|gray|small|large|nameless)?\s*(slime|wolf|fox|dog|cat|hawk|raven|spider|goblin|drake|wyrmling|beast|creature)\b/i); desc = k && k[1] ? this.norm(k[1]) : ""; kind = k && k[2] ? this.norm(k[2]).toLowerCase() : "creature"; }
        if (!name || /^(The|A|An|You|Your|It)$/i.test(name)) return 0;
        A.ActorProfileSystem.init(root); A.MajorActorMemorySystem.init(root);
        const display = name + " the " + (desc ? desc + " " : "") + kind;
        const candidate = { sceneKey:this.key(name), displayName:display, roleTags:["named_creature","companion",kind], rankTags:["named"], category:"creature", importanceScore:6, persistent:true, major:true };
        const rec = A.ActorProfileSystem.findOrCreateRecord(candidate, root);
        if (!rec || !rec.identity) return 0;
        if (A.ActorProfileSystem.applyCandidateToRecord) A.ActorProfileSystem.applyCandidateToRecord(candidate, rec, root);
        A.MajorActorMemorySystem.ensureMemoryForActor(rec.identity.id, "named_creature_companion", root);
        const mem = root.actors && root.actors.majorMemory && root.actors.majorMemory.byActorId ? root.actors.majorMemory.byActorId[rec.identity.id] : null;
        if (mem) {
          mem.name = display;
          if (!Array.isArray(mem.attitudeNotes)) mem.attitudeNotes = [];
          this.pushUnique(mem.attitudeNotes, "named " + kind + "; companion/recurring creature", 10);
          mem.relationshipStance = /\b(hostile|enemy|attacks?)\b/i.test(raw) ? "hostile" : "friendly";
          mem.lastSeenTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
          mem.lastUpdatedTurn = mem.lastSeenTurn;
          if (A.MajorActorMemorySystem.pushRecentEvent) A.MajorActorMemorySystem.pushRecentEvent("named_creature", display, rec.identity.id, "named creature remembered", root);
          if (A.MajorActorMemorySystem.markActorDirty) A.MajorActorMemorySystem.markActorDirty(rec.identity.id, root);
        }
        this.dirty(root, "actors", rec.identity.id);
        this.log(root, "Named creature promoted: " + display + ".");
        return 1;
      },
      speciesGrowth(root) {
        if (!root || !root.player || !root.player.stats) return 0;
        const p = root.player, s = p.stats, species = String(p.species || "human").toLowerCase();
        let changes = 0;
        const inc = (k, n) => { if (typeof s[k] === "number") { s[k] += n; changes += 1; } };
        if (/\b(elf|fae|fae-blooded|spirit|elemental|fire elemental)\b/.test(species)) { inc("maxMp", 4); s.mp = s.maxMp; inc("intl", 1); }
        else if (/\b(orc|ogre|troll)\b/.test(species)) { inc("maxHp", 8); s.hp = s.maxHp; inc("atk", 1); }
        else if (/\b(giant|goliath|titan)\b/.test(species)) { inc("maxHp", 14); s.hp = s.maxHp; inc("def", 1); inc("atk", 1); }
        else if (/\b(drakekin|dragonkin)\b/.test(species)) { inc("maxHp", 6); s.hp = s.maxHp; inc("atk", 1); inc("maxMp", 2); s.mp = s.maxMp; }
        else if (/\bhuman\b/.test(species)) { inc("lck", 1); }
        if (changes && A.PlayerSystem && A.PlayerSystem.recomputeDerived) A.PlayerSystem.recomputeDerived(p);
        return changes;
      }
    };

    if (A.InventorySystem) {
      const I = A.InventorySystem;
      const oldHead = I.isItemHeadToken;
      I.isItemHeadToken = function(v) { const r = String(v || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/g, ""); if (/^(belt|potion|potions|lamp|candle|stub|gemstone|set|wheel)$/.test(r)) return true; return oldHead ? oldHead.call(this, v) : false; };
      I.detectBaseType = function(phrase) {
        const words = String(phrase || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9\s_-]+/g, " ").split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
        const combos = [["lockpick","set"],["healing","potion"],["oil","lamp"],["candle","stub"],["cheese","wheel"],["leather","belt"],["black","gate","key"],["iron","sword"],["silver","ring"]];
        for (let c = 0; c < combos.length; c += 1) for (let i = 0; i <= words.length - combos[c].length; i += 1) if (combos[c].every((w, k) => words[i + k] === w)) return combos[c][combos[c].length - 1];
        const pri = ["potion","keyring","key","sword","ring","amulet","belt","lamp","candle","stub","gemstone","set","wheel","kit","salve","antidote","shield","bolt","arrow","coin","map","book","scroll","cup","bowl","pot","pan"];
        for (let i = 0; i < pri.length; i += 1) if (words.indexOf(pri[i]) !== -1) return pri[i];
        for (let i = words.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) if (this.isItemHeadToken(words[i])) return words[i];
        return words.length ? words[words.length - 1] : "item";
      };
      I.cleanItemPhrase = function(v) { return P2.cleanItem(v, this); };
      I.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase = function(phrase) {
        const base = P2.stripSpecial(phrase).toLowerCase();
        const toks = base.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
        if (!toks.length || toks.length > 7) return false;
        const hi = this.findConcreteItemHeadIndex(toks);
        if (hi < 0) return false;
        return !(new Set(["road","path","trail","street","lane","room","hall","market","chapel","smithy","tavern","inn","forest","cave","bridge"])).has(toks[hi]);
      };
      const oldExtract = I.extractAcquisitionPhrases;
      I.extractAcquisitionPhrases = function(text, root) {
        const out = oldExtract ? (oldExtract.call(this, text, root) || []) : [];
        const raw = String(text || "");
        if (this.hasAcquisitionNegation && this.hasAcquisitionNegation(raw, root)) return out;
        const add = (v) => { const c = this.cleanItemPhrase(v); if (c && this.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(c) && out.indexOf(c) === -1) out.push(c); };
        const pats = [/\byou\s+(?:craft|crafted|brew|brewed|make|made)\s+(?:and\s+keep\s+)?([^.!?;\n]+)/gi, /\byou\s+(?:take|took|pick\s+up|picked\s+up|grab|grabbed|accept|accepted|buy|bought|collect|collected)\s+([^.!?;\n]*?\b(?:that|which)\b[^.!?;\n]+)/gi];
        for (let p = 0; p < pats.length; p += 1) { let m; while ((m = pats[p].exec(raw))) if (m[1]) add(m[1]); }
        return out;
      };
      I.queueNormalizationCandidate = function(phrase, holder, visibility, sourceType, root) {
        if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return false; this.init(root);
        const raw = P2.norm(phrase), clean = this.cleanItemPhrase(raw); if (!clean || !this.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(clean)) return false;
        const row = { phrase:clean, rawPhrase:raw, holder:String(holder || "player"), visibility:String(visibility || "packed"), sourceType:String(sourceType || "unknown"), turn:root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0, specialFunction:P2.special(raw), sourceIdentity:P2.sourceId(raw, sourceType), forceDistinct:P2.forceNew(raw) };
        const pending = root.items.pendingNormalization;
        for (let i = 0; i < pending.length; i += 1) if (pending[i] && pending[i].phrase === row.phrase && pending[i].turn === row.turn && P2.same(pending[i].specialFunction || "", row.specialFunction || "") && P2.same(pending[i].sourceIdentity || "", row.sourceIdentity || "")) return false;
        pending.push(row); return true;
      };
      I.normalizePending = function(root) { if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0; this.init(root); let changes = 0; const p = root.items.pendingNormalization; while (p.length) { const c = p.shift(); const item = this.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase(c.rawPhrase || c.phrase, c.visibility || "packed", c.sourceType || "story_gain", root, c); if (item) changes += 1; } return changes; };
      I.makeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase = function(phrase, sourceContext) {
        const raw = P2.norm(phrase), clean = this.cleanItemPhrase(raw), basePhrase = P2.stripSpecial(clean), sp = P2.special(raw) || P2.special(clean), source = P2.sourceId(raw, sourceContext);
        const baseType = this.detectBaseType((basePhrase + " " + raw).toLowerCase());
        const display = sp && /^potion\b/i.test(basePhrase) && !/\bthat\b/i.test(basePhrase) ? basePhrase + " that " + sp : basePhrase;
        const seed = { displayName:display || clean, baseType, descriptors:this.extractDescriptors ? this.extractDescriptors(display || clean, baseType) : [], materialTags:this.extractMaterialTags ? this.extractMaterialTags(raw.toLowerCase()) : [], quality:this.extractQuality ? this.extractQuality(raw.toLowerCase()) : "", slot:this.inferSlotFromBaseType ? this.inferSlotFromBaseType(baseType) : "", stackable:this.isLikelyStackable ? this.isLikelyStackable(baseType) : false, quantity:1, ownership:{ holder:"player", visibility:"packed" }, notes:{ sourceContext:String(sourceContext || ""), observedChanges:[], sourceIdentity:source, specialFunction:sp, uniqueFunctionItem:Boolean(sp) } };
        if (source && seed.descriptors.indexOf(source) === -1) seed.descriptors.unshift(source);
        if (sp) seed.notes.observedChanges.push("Function: " + sp);
        return seed;
      };
      I.findMatchingPlayerItem = function(query, root, options) {
        if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null; this.init(root);
        const U = A.Utils || {}, meta = options || {}, raw = P2.norm(meta.rawPhrase || query);
        if (meta.forceDistinct || P2.forceNew(raw)) return null;
        const clean = this.cleanItemPhrase(raw || query), basePhrase = P2.stripSpecial(clean), qKey = U.toKey ? U.toKey(basePhrase || clean) : P2.key(basePhrase || clean), qBase = this.detectBaseType((basePhrase || clean).toLowerCase()), qSpecial = P2.special(raw) || P2.special(clean) || String(meta.specialFunction || ""), qSource = P2.sourceId(raw, meta.sourceType || "") || String(meta.sourceIdentity || "");
        if (!qKey) return null;
        const owned = root.items && root.items.ownership && Array.isArray(root.items.ownership.player) ? root.items.ownership.player : [];
        let best = null, scoreBest = -1, qWords = qKey.split("_").filter(Boolean).length;
        for (let i = 0; i < owned.length; i += 1) {
          const item = root.items.byId[owned[i]]; if (!item) continue; this.ensureItemShape(item);
          const notes = item.notes || {}, itemSpecial = String(notes.specialFunction || ""), itemSource = String(notes.sourceIdentity || ""), personal = String(notes.personalName || "");
          if (notes.namedPersonalItem === true && personal && raw.toLowerCase().indexOf(personal.toLowerCase()) === -1) continue;
          if (qSpecial && !P2.same(qSpecial, itemSpecial)) continue; if (!qSpecial && itemSpecial) continue;
          if (qSource && itemSource && !P2.same(qSource, itemSource)) continue; if (qSource && !itemSource) continue;
          const display = this.getItemDisplayName(item), displayKey = U.toKey ? U.toKey(P2.stripSpecial(display)) : P2.key(P2.stripSpecial(display)), baseKey = U.toKey ? U.toKey(item.baseType || "") : P2.key(item.baseType || ""), dWords = displayKey.split("_").filter(Boolean).length;
          let score = 0; if (displayKey === qKey) score = 100; else if (baseKey === qKey && dWords <= 1) score = 92; else if (displayKey.indexOf(qKey) !== -1 && qWords >= 2) score = 84; else if (baseKey === P2.key(qBase) && qWords <= 2 && dWords <= 2 && !qSource && !itemSource) score = 74;
          if (score > scoreBest) { scoreBest = score; best = item; }
        }
        return scoreBest >= 80 ? best : null;
      };
      I.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase = function(phrase, visibility, sourceContext, root, options) {
        if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null; this.init(root);
        const raw = P2.norm(phrase), clean = this.cleanItemPhrase(raw); if (!clean || !this.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(clean)) return null;
        const meta = Object.assign({}, options || {}, { rawPhrase:raw, sourceType:sourceContext });
        const existing = this.findMatchingPlayerItem(clean, root, meta);
        if (existing) { existing.ownership = existing.ownership || {}; existing.ownership.holder = "player"; if (visibility) existing.ownership.visibility = visibility; return existing; }
        const item = this.registerItem(this.makeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase(raw || clean, sourceContext), root); if (!item) return null;
        this.ensurePlayerOwns(item.id, root); item.ownership.visibility = visibility || "packed"; if (A.Utils && A.Utils.markDirty) A.Utils.markDirty("items", item.id, root); return item;
      };
      const oldName = I.getItemDisplayName;
      I.getItemDisplayName = function(item) { if (!item) return "(invalid item)"; const n = item.notes || {}; if (n.personalName) return String(n.personalName) + " (" + String(n.baseDisplayName || item.baseType || "item").replace(/^\(|\)$/g, "") + ")"; const base = oldName ? oldName.call(this, item) : (item.displayName || item.baseType || "item"); return n.specialFunction && String(base).toLowerCase().indexOf(String(n.specialFunction).toLowerCase()) === -1 ? base + " [" + n.specialFunction + "]" : base; };
    }

    if (A.CardSyncSystem && A.CardSyncSystem.serializeItemCard) {
      const old = A.CardSyncSystem.serializeItemCard;
      A.CardSyncSystem.serializeItemCard = function(id, root) { const spec = old.call(this, id, root); const item = root && root.items && root.items.byId ? root.items.byId[id] : null; if (!spec || !item || !item.notes) return spec; const lines = []; if (item.notes.personalName) lines.push("Personal Name: " + item.notes.personalName); if (item.notes.baseDisplayName) lines.push("Base Identity: " + item.notes.baseDisplayName); if (item.notes.sourceIdentity) lines.push("Source Identity: " + item.notes.sourceIdentity); if (item.notes.specialFunction) lines.push("Special Function: " + item.notes.specialFunction); if (Array.isArray(item.notes.observedChanges) && item.notes.observedChanges.length) lines.push("Observed: " + item.notes.observedChanges.slice(-4).join("; ")); if (lines.length) { spec.entry = String(spec.entry || "") + "\n" + lines.join("\n"); spec.hash = this.stableHash ? this.stableHash(spec.entry) : spec.entry; } return spec; };
    }

    if (A.SceneStateSystem && A.SceneStateSystem.processOutput) { const old = A.SceneStateSystem.processOutput; A.SceneStateSystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) { root = root || state.aidrpg; let ch = old.call(this, rawText, root) || 0; ch += P2.scopeCityScene(rawText, root); return ch; }; }
    if (A.MajorActorMemorySystem && A.MajorActorMemorySystem.processOutput) { const old = A.MajorActorMemorySystem.processOutput; A.MajorActorMemorySystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) { root = root || state.aidrpg; let ch = old.call(this, rawText, root) || 0; ch += P2.promoteCreature(rawText, root); return ch; }; }
    if (A.ProgressionSystem && A.ProgressionSystem.applyLevelUpBenefits) { const old = A.ProgressionSystem.applyLevelUpBenefits; A.ProgressionSystem.applyLevelUpBenefits = function(root) { old.call(this, root); P2.speciesGrowth(root || state.aidrpg); }; }
    if (A.TimeTravelSystem && A.TimeTravelSystem.captureRouteTimingFromText) { const old = A.TimeTravelSystem.captureRouteTimingFromText; A.TimeTravelSystem.captureRouteTimingFromText = function(text, root) { root = root || state.aidrpg; let ch = old.call(this, text, root) || 0; const B = A.BetaFinalPatch || {}; if (B.extractRouteFromText && B.routeDelayFromText) { const direct = B.extractRouteFromText(text), delay = B.routeDelayFromText(text); if (direct && delay && delay.reason) { const fromRef = this.makeLocationRef(direct.from), toRef = this.makeLocationRef(direct.to), known = this.getKnownRouteDuration(fromRef, toRef, root), base = known && known.minutes ? known.minutes : direct.baseMinutes, actual = base + (delay.minutes || Math.max(0, direct.baseMinutes - base)); this.rememberRouteDuration(fromRef, toRef, base, root, { actualMinutes:actual, delayReason:delay.reason, rawText:text }); if (this.pushRouteEvent) this.pushRouteEvent("Route delay: " + this.formatRouteLabel(fromRef, toRef, root) + " base " + base + "m; actual " + actual + "m due to " + delay.reason + ".", root); ch += 1; } } return ch; }; }
    if (A.AbilitySystem && A.AbilitySystem.processOutput) { const old = A.AbilitySystem.processOutput; A.AbilitySystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) { root = root || state.aidrpg; let ch = old.call(this, rawText, root) || 0; const m = String(rawText || "").match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+)?)\s+(?:becomes|became|forms|formed|turns\s+into|expands\s+into)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+){0,3})?\s*([^.!?;]{3,100})/); if (m && root.abilities && root.abilities.byId) { const source = P2.norm(m[1]), name = P2.norm(m[2] || (source + " Variation")), fn = P2.norm(m[3] || "adapted use"), id = "ability_" + P2.key(name); let rec = root.abilities.byId[id]; if (!rec) { rec = root.abilities.byId[id] = { identity:{ id, name, type:"skill", kind:"variation" }, meaning:{ intendedFunction:fn, keyPhrases:[name], sourceAbilityName:source, variationReason:"emergent adaptation", emergentApplications:[fn] }, progression:{ masteryState:"novice" }, observation:{ semanticTags:["variation","derived"] } }; ch += 1; } else { rec.meaning = rec.meaning || {}; rec.meaning.sourceAbilityName = rec.meaning.sourceAbilityName || source; rec.meaning.intendedFunction = rec.meaning.intendedFunction || fn; if (!Array.isArray(rec.meaning.emergentApplications)) rec.meaning.emergentApplications = []; P2.pushUnique(rec.meaning.emergentApplications, fn, 8); } if (A.Utils && A.Utils.markDirty) A.Utils.markDirty("abilities", id, root); if (A.CardSyncSystem && A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty) A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty("ability", id, "ability_variation", null, root); } return ch; }; }

  })(g.AIDRPG);

  // -----------------------------------------------------------------
  // AIDRPG BETA 2 HOTFIX: attach special item functions even when an
  // earlier broad acquisition pass saved the plain base item first.
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------
  (function installAIDRPGBeta2Hotfix(A) {
    "use strict";
    if (!A || A.__beta2HotfixInstalled === true) return;
    A.__beta2HotfixInstalled = true;
    const P2 = A.Beta2ConflictPatch;
    if (!P2 || !A.InventorySystem) return;

    P2.upgradeSpecialItemsFromText = function(rawText, root) {
      if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || !A.InventorySystem) return 0;
      const I = A.InventorySystem;
      I.init(root);
      const raw = String(rawText || "");
      const clauses = [];
      const patterns = [
        /\byou\s+(?:take|took|pick\s+up|picked\s+up|grab|grabbed|accept|accepted|collect|collected|loot|looted|pocket|pocketed)\s+([^.!?;\n]*?\b(?:that|which)\b[^.!?;\n]+)/gi,
        /\b([^.!?;\n]{1,90}?\b(?:that|which)\b[^.!?;\n]{3,140})\s+(?:is|are)\s+(?:placed|pressed|dropped|slipped|tucked)\s+(?:into|in|onto)\s+your\b/gi
      ];
      for (let p = 0; p < patterns.length; p += 1) { let m; while ((m = patterns[p].exec(raw))) if (m[1]) clauses.push(m[1]); }
      let changes = 0;
      for (let c = 0; c < clauses.length; c += 1) {
        const phrase = P2.norm(clauses[c]);
        const special = P2.special(phrase);
        if (!special) continue;
        const baseName = I.cleanItemPhrase(phrase);
        const baseKey = P2.key(P2.stripSpecial(baseName));
        if (!baseKey) continue;
        const owned = root.items && root.items.ownership && Array.isArray(root.items.ownership.player) ? root.items.ownership.player : [];
        let item = null;
        for (let i = owned.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
          const rec = root.items.byId[owned[i]];
          if (!rec) continue;
          const display = I.getItemDisplayName ? I.getItemDisplayName(rec) : (rec.displayName || rec.baseType || "");
          const dKey = P2.key(P2.stripSpecial(display));
          const rawDisplayKey = P2.key(rec.displayName || rec.baseType || "");
          if (dKey === baseKey || rawDisplayKey === baseKey) { item = rec; break; }
        }
        if (!item) item = I.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase(phrase, "packed", "story_gain", root, { rawPhrase:phrase, specialFunction:special, forceDistinct:false });
        if (!item) continue;
        I.ensureItemShape(item);
        item.notes = item.notes || {};
        if (!Array.isArray(item.notes.observedChanges)) item.notes.observedChanges = [];
        if (item.notes.specialFunction !== special) { item.notes.specialFunction = special; changes += 1; }
        item.notes.uniqueFunctionItem = true;
        P2.pushUnique(item.notes.observedChanges, "Function: " + special, 12);
        if (/^potion$/i.test(P2.stripSpecial(baseName)) && !/\bthat\b/i.test(String(item.displayName || ""))) {
          item.displayName = "potion that " + special;
          changes += 1;
        }
        if (A.Utils && A.Utils.markDirty) A.Utils.markDirty("items", item.id, root);
        if (A.CardSyncSystem && A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty) A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty("item", item.id, "special_function", null, root);
      }
      if (changes > 0) {
        if (I.refreshVisibleCarrySummary) I.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);
        if (A.Utils && A.Utils.invalidatePackets) A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
        P2.log(root, "Unique item function preserved.");
      }
      return changes;
    };

    const oldOutput = A.InventorySystem.processOutput;
    A.InventorySystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
      root = root || state.aidrpg;
      let changes = oldOutput.call(this, rawText, root) || 0;
      changes += P2.upgradeSpecialItemsFromText(rawText, root);
      return changes;
    };
  })(g.AIDRPG);
})();

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// AIDRPG BETA 3 HOTFIX: final-playtest canon polish.
// Applies even if the main library guard returns on rerun.
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
(function installAIDRPGBeta3Patch(A) {
  "use strict";
  if (!A || A.__beta3PatchInstalled === true) return;
  A.__beta3PatchInstalled = true;
  A.VERSION = "1.0.6-beta";
  A.BUILD_STAGE = "AIDRPGbeta3-final-playtest-polish";

  const P = A.Beta3FinalPatch = A.Beta3FinalPatch || {};
  P.norm = function(value) { return String(value || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim(); };
  P.key = function(value) { return P.norm(value).toLowerCase().replace(/[’'´"]/g, "").replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_").replace(/^_+|_+$/g, ""); };
  P.pushUnique = function(list, value, cap) {
    const text = P.norm(value);
    if (!Array.isArray(list) || !text) return 0;
    const low = text.toLowerCase();
    for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) if (String(list[i]).toLowerCase() === low) return 0;
    list.push(text);
    while (cap && list.length > cap) list.shift();
    return 1;
  };
  P.numberWord = function(value) {
    const raw = String(value || "").toLowerCase().trim();
    if (/^\d+$/.test(raw)) return Math.floor(Number(raw));
    const words = { one:1,two:2,three:3,four:4,five:5,six:6,seven:7,eight:8,nine:9,ten:10,eleven:11,twelve:12,thirteen:13,fourteen:14,fifteen:15,sixteen:16,seventeen:17,eighteen:18,nineteen:19,twenty:20,thirty:30,forty:40,fifty:50,sixty:60 };
    return words[raw] || 0;
  };

  P.extractLocationDescriptors = function(rawText) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const facts = [];
    const add = (v) => P.pushUnique(facts, v, 16);
    let m;
    const colorObj = /\b(blue|red|black|white|green|gold|golden|silver|brass|bronze|copper|oak|stone|wooden|iron)\s+(sign|hearth|fountain|anvil|door|banner|counter|bar|gate|statue|lantern|window|roof|arch|pillar|stairs?)\b/g;
    while ((m = colorObj.exec(lower))) add(m[1] + " " + m[2]);
    const carved = /\bcarved\s+(wolf|lion|stag|owl|dragon|raven|fish|bear|fox|serpent|horse|boar)\b/g;
    while ((m = carved.exec(lower))) add("carved " + m[1]);
    const emblem = /\b(brass|bronze|silver|gold|iron|stone|wooden|ivory)\s+(lion|wolf|stag|owl|dragon|raven|fish|bear|fox|serpent|horse|boar|anvil|fountain|hearth|bell|sign)\b/g;
    while ((m = emblem.exec(lower))) add(m[1] + " " + m[2]);
    const paired = /\b(fish|stone|owl|stag|wolf|lion|raven|boar|dragon)\s+(fountain|anvil|sign|banner|statue|hearth)\b/g;
    while ((m = paired.exec(lower))) add(m[1] + " " + m[2]);
    return facts.slice(0, 12);
  };

  P.attachLocationDescriptors = function(rawText, root) {
    root = root || state.aidrpg;
    if (!root || !root.world || !A.WorldEntitySystem) return 0;
    const W = A.WorldEntitySystem;
    if (typeof W.init === "function") W.init(root);
    const scene = root.world.currentScene || {};
    const facts = P.extractLocationDescriptors(rawText);
    if (!facts.length || !scene.entityRef) return 0;
    let changes = 0;
    let rec = root.world.entitiesById && root.world.entitiesById[scene.entityRef];
    if (!rec && W.ensureEntityRecord) rec = W.ensureEntityRecord(scene.entityRef, scene.displayName || scene.entityRef, scene.sceneType || "location", root);
    if (rec) {
      rec.mutable = rec.mutable || {};
      if (!Array.isArray(rec.mutable.layoutFacts)) rec.mutable.layoutFacts = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(rec.mutable.descriptiveFacts)) rec.mutable.descriptiveFacts = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(rec.mutable.objectHints)) rec.mutable.objectHints = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < facts.length; i += 1) {
        changes += P.pushUnique(rec.mutable.layoutFacts, facts[i], 18);
        changes += P.pushUnique(rec.mutable.descriptiveFacts, facts[i], 18);
        changes += P.pushUnique(rec.mutable.objectHints, facts[i], 18);
      }
    }
    if (scene.displayName && W.ensureNamedLocationRecord) {
      const named = W.ensureNamedLocationRecord(scene.displayName, root);
      if (named) {
        if (!Array.isArray(named.layoutFacts)) named.layoutFacts = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < facts.length; i += 1) changes += P.pushUnique(named.layoutFacts, facts[i], 18);
      }
    }
    if (changes > 0) {
      if (W.pushMapEvent) W.pushMapEvent("Location detail: " + (scene.displayName || scene.entityRef) + " has " + facts.join(", ") + ".", root);
      if (A.Utils && A.Utils.markDirty) A.Utils.markDirty("entities", scene.entityRef, root);
      if (A.Utils && A.Utils.invalidatePackets) A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
    }
    return changes;
  };

  P.singularEnemy = function(value) {
    let s = P.norm(value).toLowerCase().replace(/^(?:the|a|an|of|remaining|last)\s+/, "");
    s = s.replace(/\b(?:enemies|enemy|foes|foe|creatures|creature)\b/g, "").trim();
    return (s.replace(/ies$/, "y").replace(/ves$/, "f").replace(/s$/, "").trim() || "enemy");
  };

  P.processGroupCountRobust = function(rawText, root) {
    root = root || state.aidrpg;
    if (!root || !root.world || !A.WorldEntitySystem) return 0;
    A.WorldEntitySystem.init(root);
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const enc = root.world.activeEncounter || (root.world.activeEncounter = {});
    if (!Array.isArray(enc.recentEvents)) enc.recentEvents = [];
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    let changes = 0;
    const event = (msg) => {
      msg = P.norm(msg);
      if (!msg) return 0;
      const last = enc.recentEvents.length ? enc.recentEvents[enc.recentEvents.length - 1] : null;
      if (last && last.turn === turn && last.message === msg) return 0;
      enc.recentEvents.push({ turn, message: msg });
      while (enc.recentEvents.length > 12) enc.recentEvents.shift();
      if (A.WorldEntitySystem.pushMapEvent) A.WorldEntitySystem.pushMapEvent("Encounter: " + msg, root);
      return 1;
    };
    const num = "(\\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty)";
    const setEncounter = (count, label) => {
      count = P.numberWord(count); label = P.singularEnemy(label || enc.enemyLabel || "enemy");
      if (count > 0 && (!enc.enemyLabel || enc.resolved === true || Number(enc.totalCount || 0) === 0)) {
        enc.enemyLabel = label; enc.totalCount = count; enc.remainingCount = count; enc.resolved = false; enc.lastChangedTurn = turn;
        changes += 1 + event(count + " " + label + " enemies engaged; " + count + " remain.");
      }
    };
    let m = raw.match(new RegExp("\\b(?:you\\s+face|you\\s+see|there\\s+are|before\\s+you\\s+stand|a\\s+group\\s+of|a\\s+pack\\s+of|a\\s+band\\s+of)\\s+" + num + "\\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{2,30}?)(?:s)?\\b[^.!?]{0,100}\\b(?:attack|ambush|surround|charge|rush|advance|block|stand|wait|threaten|close\\s+in|guard)", "i")) || raw.match(new RegExp("\\b" + num + "\\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{2,30}?)(?:s)?\\s+(?:attack|ambush|surround|charge|rush|advance|block|stand|wait|threaten|close\\s+in|guard|emerge|appear)", "i"));
    if (m) setEncounter(m[1], m[2]);
    m = raw.match(new RegExp("\\b" + num + "\\s+(?:more|additional|new|reinforcing)\\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{2,30}?)(?:s)?\\s+(?:arrive|join|rush\\s+in|appear|enter|reinforce)", "i"));
    if (m) {
      const add = P.numberWord(m[1]);
      if (add > 0) { if (!enc.enemyLabel) enc.enemyLabel = P.singularEnemy(m[2]); enc.totalCount = Number(enc.totalCount || 0) + add; enc.remainingCount = Number(enc.remainingCount || 0) + add; enc.resolved = false; changes += 1 + event(add + " reinforcements joined; " + enc.remainingCount + " " + enc.enemyLabel + " remain."); }
    }
    m = raw.match(new RegExp("\\b(?:you\\s+)?(?:kill|killed|slay|slain|defeat|defeated|destroy|destroyed|drop|dropped|fell|felled|cut\\s+down|take\\s+down|took\\s+down)\\s+" + num + "(?:\\s+of\\s+the)?(?:\\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{2,30}?)(?:s)?)?\\b", "i")) || raw.match(new RegExp("\\b" + num + "\\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{2,30}?)(?:s)?\\s+(?:fall|fell|die|died|are\\s+slain|are\\s+defeated|drop|collapse)", "i"));
    if (m) {
      const dead = P.numberWord(m[1]);
      if (dead > 0) { if (!enc.enemyLabel) enc.enemyLabel = P.singularEnemy(m[2]); if (!Number(enc.totalCount || 0)) enc.totalCount = Math.max(dead, Number(enc.remainingCount || 0)); const before = Number(enc.remainingCount || enc.totalCount || 0); const after = Math.max(0, before - dead); enc.remainingCount = after; enc.resolved = after <= 0; enc.lastChangedTurn = turn; changes += 1 + event(dead + " " + enc.enemyLabel + " defeated; " + after + " remain."); }
    }
    m = raw.match(new RegExp("\\b" + num + "\\s+(?:remain|are\\s+left|survive|still\\s+stand|continue\\s+fighting)\\b", "i")) || raw.match(new RegExp("\\bleaving\\s+" + num + "\\s+(?:left|remaining|to\\s+fight|still\\s+standing)?\\b", "i"));
    if (m && enc.enemyLabel) { const n = P.numberWord(m[1]); enc.remainingCount = n; enc.resolved = n <= 0; enc.lastChangedTurn = turn; changes += 1 + event(n + " " + enc.enemyLabel + " remain confirmed."); }
    if (enc.enemyLabel && Number(enc.remainingCount || 0) > 0 && /\b(?:all|every|the\s+last)\s+(?:enemy|enemies|foes?|goblins?|skeletons?|bandits?|wolves?|soldiers?|raiders?|cultists?)\s+(?:are\s+)?(?:dead|defeated|gone|finished|slain)\b/i.test(raw)) changes += event("continuity holds: " + enc.remainingCount + " " + enc.enemyLabel + " remain unresolved.");
    if (changes > 0 && A.Utils && A.Utils.invalidatePackets) A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
    return changes;
  };

  P.parseRouteDelay = function(rawText) {
    const raw = P.norm(rawText);
    const num = "(\\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty)";
    const fromTo = raw.match(/\bfrom\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;,]+?)\s+to\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;,]+?)(?=\s+(?:usually|normally|typically|takes?|took|is|are|with|but|and|,|\.|$)|[.!?,;]|$)/i);
    const duration = raw.match(new RegExp("\\b(?:usually\\s+|normally\\s+|typically\\s+)?(?:takes?|took|lasts?|lasted|is)\\s+" + num + "\\s+(minutes?|hours?)", "i"));
    const delayDuration = raw.match(new RegExp("\\b(?:delays?|delayed|slows?|slowed|adds?|added|costs?|takes\\s+an\\s+extra)\\s+(?:you\\s+|the\\s+party\\s+|the\\s+trip\\s+|travel\\s+)?(?:by\\s+)?" + num + "\\s+(minutes?|hours?)", "i")) || raw.match(new RegExp("\\b(?:extra|another|additional)\\s+" + num + "\\s+(minutes?|hours?)", "i"));
    let reason = "";
    const low = raw.toLowerCase();
    if (/\brain|downpour|drizzle\b/.test(low)) reason = "rain";
    else if (/\bstorm|thunder\b/.test(low)) reason = "storm";
    else if (/\bmud|muddy\b/.test(low)) reason = "mud";
    else if (/\b(?:break|rest|pause|camp)\b/.test(low)) reason = "break";
    else if (/\b(?:injur|wounded|limp|escort|companion|mira)\b/.test(low)) reason = "injured companion";
    else if (/\bscout|scouting|searching ahead\b/.test(low)) reason = "scouting";
    else if (/\bdetour|alternate route|wrong road\b/.test(low)) reason = "detour";
    else if (/\bfight|battle|ambush|bandit|wolf|encounter\b/.test(low)) reason = "encounter";
    const base = duration ? P.numberWord(duration[1]) * (/hour/i.test(duration[2]) ? 60 : 1) : 0;
    const extra = delayDuration ? P.numberWord(delayDuration[1]) * (/hour/i.test(delayDuration[2]) ? 60 : 1) : 0;
    return { from: fromTo ? fromTo[1] : "", to: fromTo ? fromTo[2] : "", baseMinutes: base, delayMinutes: extra, reason: reason };
  };
  P.applyRouteDelayMemory = function(rawText, root) {
    root = root || state.aidrpg;
    const T = A.TimeTravelSystem;
    if (!T || !root) return 0;
    if (typeof T.init === "function") T.init(root);
    const data = P.parseRouteDelay(rawText);
    if (!data || !data.from || !data.to || (!data.baseMinutes && !data.reason)) return 0;
    const fromRef = T.makeLocationRef ? T.makeLocationRef(data.from) : "location_" + P.key(data.from);
    const toRef = T.makeLocationRef ? T.makeLocationRef(data.to) : "location_" + P.key(data.to);
    const known = T.getKnownRouteDuration ? T.getKnownRouteDuration(fromRef, toRef, root) : null;
    const base = known && known.minutes ? known.minutes : data.baseMinutes;
    if (!base) return 0;
    const actual = base + (data.delayMinutes || 0);
    const reason = data.reason || (data.delayMinutes ? "delay" : "");
    if (T.rememberRouteDuration) T.rememberRouteDuration(fromRef, toRef, base, root, { actualMinutes: actual, delayReason: reason, rawText: rawText });
    const label = T.formatRouteLabel ? T.formatRouteLabel(fromRef, toRef, root) : (P.norm(data.from) + " <-> " + P.norm(data.to));
    if (T.pushRouteEvent) T.pushRouteEvent("Route memory: " + label + " base " + base + "m" + (reason ? "; latest actual " + actual + "m due to " + reason : "") + ".", root);
    if (A.Utils && A.Utils.markDirty) A.Utils.markDirty("time", true, root);
    if (A.Utils && A.Utils.invalidatePackets) A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
    return 1;
  };

  P.repairNamedItemFunction = function(rawText, root) {
    root = root || state.aidrpg;
    const I = A.InventorySystem;
    if (!I || !root) return 0;
    I.init(root);
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const owned = root.items && root.items.ownership && Array.isArray(root.items.ownership.player) ? root.items.ownership.player : [];
    let changes = 0;
    const specialMatch = raw.match(/\b(?:can|could|will|does|lets?\s+you|allows?\s+you|makes?\s+your|mutes?|silences?|hides?|reveals?|detects?|glows?|opens?|protects?|cuts?|binds?|points?)\s+([^.!?;]{3,100})/i);
    const special = specialMatch ? P.norm(specialMatch[0]).replace(/[.!?;]+$/g, "").slice(0, 100) : "";
    const nameMatch = raw.match(/\b(?:name|named|call|called|dub|dubbed)\s+(?:the\s+|my\s+|your\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;]{2,60}?)\s+(?:as\s+|to\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{2,40})\b/) || raw.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{2,40})\s+(?:is\s+)?(?:the\s+)?(?:name|title)\s+(?:of|for)\s+(?:the\s+|my\s+|your\s+)?([^.!?;]{2,60})/i);
    let target = null;
    if (nameMatch) {
      const base = /^[A-Z]/.test(nameMatch[2] || "") ? P.norm(nameMatch[1]) : P.norm(nameMatch[2]);
      const personal = /^[A-Z]/.test(nameMatch[2] || "") ? P.norm(nameMatch[2]) : P.norm(nameMatch[1]);
      const baseKey = P.key(base.replace(/^(?:the|my|your|a|an)\s+/i, ""));
      for (let i = owned.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
        const item = root.items.byId[owned[i]]; if (!item) continue;
        const display = P.key((item.notes && item.notes.baseDisplayName) || item.displayName || item.baseType || "");
        const type = P.key(item.baseType || "");
        if (display.indexOf(baseKey) !== -1 || baseKey.indexOf(display) !== -1 || (type && baseKey.indexOf(type) !== -1)) { target = item; break; }
      }
      if (!target && base && I.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase) target = I.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase(base, "packed", "story_gain", root, { forceDistinct:true });
      if (target) {
        target.notes = target.notes || {}; if (!Array.isArray(target.notes.observedChanges)) target.notes.observedChanges = [];
        const baseDisplay = target.notes.baseDisplayName || target.displayName || base || target.baseType || "item";
        target.notes.personalName = personal; target.notes.baseDisplayName = baseDisplay; target.notes.namedPersonalItem = true; target.displayName = personal + " (" + baseDisplay.replace(/^\(|\)$/g, "") + ")";
        changes += 1;
      }
    }
    if (!target && special && owned.length) target = root.items.byId[owned[owned.length - 1]];
    if (target && special) {
      target.notes = target.notes || {}; if (!Array.isArray(target.notes.observedChanges)) target.notes.observedChanges = [];
      target.notes.specialFunction = special; target.notes.uniqueFunctionItem = true;
      changes += P.pushUnique(target.notes.observedChanges, "Function: " + special, 12);
    }
    if (changes > 0 && target) {
      if (A.Utils && A.Utils.markDirty) A.Utils.markDirty("items", target.id, root);
      if (A.CardSyncSystem && A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty) A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty("item", target.id, "named_item_function", null, root);
      if (I.refreshVisibleCarrySummary) I.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);
      if (A.Utils && A.Utils.invalidatePackets) A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
    }
    return changes;
  };

  P.forceAbilityMirror = function(rawText, root) {
    root = root || state.aidrpg;
    const C = A.CardSyncSystem;
    if (!root || !root.abilities || !root.abilities.byId || !C) return 0;
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const m = raw.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+)?)\s+(?:becomes|became|forms|formed|turns\s+into|expands\s+into|shifts\s+into)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]+){0,3})?\s*([^.!?;]{3,120})/);
    if (!m) return 0;
    const source = P.norm(m[1]); const name = P.norm(m[2] || (source + " Variation")); const fn = P.norm(m[3] || "adapted use"); const id = "ability_" + P.key(name);
    let rec = root.abilities.byId[id];
    if (!rec) rec = root.abilities.byId[id] = { identity:{ id, name, type:"skill", kind:"variation" }, meaning:{ intendedFunction:fn, keyPhrases:[name], sourceAbilityName:source, variationReason:"emergent adaptation", emergentApplications:[fn] }, progression:{ masteryState:"novice" }, observation:{ semanticTags:["variation","derived"] } };
    rec.meaning = rec.meaning || {}; rec.meaning.sourceAbilityName = rec.meaning.sourceAbilityName || source; rec.meaning.intendedFunction = rec.meaning.intendedFunction || fn;
    if (!Array.isArray(rec.meaning.emergentApplications)) rec.meaning.emergentApplications = [];
    P.pushUnique(rec.meaning.emergentApplications, fn, 8);
    if (A.Utils && A.Utils.markDirty) A.Utils.markDirty("abilities", id, root);
    if (C.queueDirty) C.queueDirty("ability", id, "ability_variation", null, root);
    if (C.serializeAbilityCard && C.safeAddCard) { const spec = C.serializeAbilityCard(id, root); if (spec) C.safeAddCard(spec, root); }
    if (A.Utils && A.Utils.invalidatePackets) A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
    return 1;
  };

  P.strengthenReputationDelay = function(rawText, root) {
    root = root || state.aidrpg;
    const R = A.ReputationSystem; if (!R || !root) return 0;
    const rep = R.init ? R.init(root) : (root.reputation || (root.reputation = {}));
    if (!Array.isArray(rep.pendingReports)) rep.pendingReports = [];
    const raw = String(rawText || ""); const lower = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
    let changes = 0; const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    const deed = /\b(?:clear|cleared|defeat|defeated|slay|slain|kill|killed|rescue|rescued|save|saved|recover|recovered|complete|completed|destroy|destroyed|seal|sealed)\b/.test(lower);
    const hidden = /\b(?:no one|nobody|unwitnessed|alone|in secret|secretly|without witnesses|no witnesses|no townsfolk|far from town)\b/.test(lower);
    const report = /\b(?:report|reported|tell|told|prove|proved|show|showed|bring|brought|present|presented|claim|claimed)\b[^.!?]{0,90}\b(?:guild|captain|guard|council|mayor|proof|token|trophy|evidence|witness)\b/.test(lower);
    if (hidden && deed && !report) { const label = P.norm(raw).slice(0, 140) || "hidden deed"; const key = "pending_" + P.key(label).slice(0, 48); if (!rep.pendingReports.some(x => x && x.key === key)) { rep.pendingReports.push({ key, turn, label, status:"unreported", scope:"hidden" }); changes += 1; } }
    if (report) { let pending = rep.pendingReports.length ? rep.pendingReports[rep.pendingReports.length - 1] : null; if (!pending && deed) { pending = { key:"reported_" + turn, turn, label:P.norm(raw).slice(0,140), status:"unreported", scope:"reported" }; rep.pendingReports.push(pending); changes += 1; } if (pending && pending.status !== "reported") { pending.status = "reported"; pending.reportedTurn = turn; const subject = /\bguild\b/.test(lower) && R.resolveSubject ? R.resolveSubject("guild", root) : (R.resolveSubject ? R.resolveSubject("local_group", root) : { id:"reputation_local", displayName:"locals" }); if (R.applyReputationShift) changes += R.applyReputationShift(subject, "positive", "reported/proved deed: " + (pending.label || "deed"), root) || 0; } }
    if (/\bremote\s+(?:town|city|village)|\bfar\s+(?:town|city|village)|\banother\s+(?:town|city|village)\b/.test(lower) && /\b(?:does\s+not|doesn't|do\s+not|don't|has\s+not|hasn't|have\s+not|haven't)\s+(?:know|heard|learned)\b/.test(lower)) { if (!Array.isArray(rep.rumors)) rep.rumors = []; rep.rumors.push({ turn, label:"Remote area has not heard local deed yet", scope:"remote_unaware", text:P.norm(raw).slice(0,140) }); changes += 1; }
    if (changes > 0) { rep.tracking = rep.tracking || {}; rep.tracking.summaryDirty = true; rep.lastChangedTurn = turn; if (A.Utils && A.Utils.markDirty) A.Utils.markDirty("reputation", true, root); if (A.Utils && A.Utils.invalidatePackets) A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root); }
    return changes;
  };

  if (A.WorldEntitySystem && A.WorldEntitySystem.processOutput) {
    const oldWorld = A.WorldEntitySystem.processOutput;
    A.WorldEntitySystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) { root = root || state.aidrpg; let ch = oldWorld.call(this, rawText, root) || 0; ch += P.attachLocationDescriptors(rawText, root); ch += P.processGroupCountRobust(rawText, root); return ch; };
  }
  if (A.TimeTravelSystem && A.TimeTravelSystem.processOutput) {
    const oldTime = A.TimeTravelSystem.processOutput;
    A.TimeTravelSystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) { root = root || state.aidrpg; let ch = oldTime.call(this, rawText, root) || 0; ch += P.applyRouteDelayMemory(rawText, root); return ch; };
  }
  if (A.ReputationSystem && A.ReputationSystem.processOutput) {
    const oldRep = A.ReputationSystem.processOutput;
    A.ReputationSystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) { root = root || state.aidrpg; let ch = oldRep.call(this, rawText, root) || 0; ch += P.strengthenReputationDelay(rawText, root); return ch; };
  }
  if (A.InventorySystem && A.InventorySystem.processOutput) {
    const oldInv = A.InventorySystem.processOutput;
    A.InventorySystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) { root = root || state.aidrpg; let ch = oldInv.call(this, rawText, root) || 0; ch += P.repairNamedItemFunction(rawText, root); return ch; };
  }
  if (A.AbilitySystem && A.AbilitySystem.processOutput) {
    const oldAb = A.AbilitySystem.processOutput;
    A.AbilitySystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) { root = root || state.aidrpg; let ch = oldAb.call(this, rawText, root) || 0; ch += P.forceAbilityMirror(rawText, root); return ch; };
  }
})(globalThis.AIDRPG);


// -----------------------------------------------------------------
// AIDRPG FINAL STORY CARD DEFAULTS + PHYSICAL RESOURCE PATCH
// v1.0.7-final
// Adds default live managed Story Card generation/update behavior,
// starter cards for core RPG categories, safer live Story Card call
// signatures, and physical-skill EP strain parsing.
// -----------------------------------------------------------------
(function installAIDRPGFinalStoryCardDefaults(A) {
  "use strict";
  if (!A || A.__finalStoryCardDefaultsInstalled === true) return;
  A.__finalStoryCardDefaultsInstalled = true;
  A.VERSION = "1.0.7-final";
  A.BUILD_STAGE = "AIDRPG-final-storycard-defaults";

  const F = A.FinalStoryCardDefaults = {
    norm(value) {
      return String(value == null ? "" : value).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    },

    key(value) {
      const U = A.Utils || {};
      return U.toKey ? U.toKey(value) : String(value || "")
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_")
        .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
    },

    pushUnique(list, value, max) {
      if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
      const clean = this.norm(value);
      if (!clean) return 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) {
        if (String(list[i] || "").toLowerCase() === clean.toLowerCase()) return 0;
      }
      list.push(clean);
      const cap = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(max || 12)));
      while (list.length > cap) list.shift();
      return 1;
    },

    ensureFinalCardConfig(root) {
      if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;

      if (!root.cards || typeof root.cards !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cards)) {
        root.cards = {};
      }

      const cards = root.cards;

      if (!cards.config || typeof cards.config !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.config)) {
        cards.config = {};
      }

      if (!cards.index || typeof cards.index !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.index)) {
        cards.index = {};
      }
      if (!cards.index.byNamespace || typeof cards.index.byNamespace !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.index.byNamespace)) cards.index.byNamespace = {};
      if (!cards.index.byCardId || typeof cards.index.byCardId !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.index.byCardId)) cards.index.byCardId = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(cards.dirtyQueue)) cards.dirtyQueue = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(cards.pendingWrites)) cards.pendingWrites = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(cards.recentOperations)) cards.recentOperations = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(cards.dryRunLog)) cards.dryRunLog = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(cards.duplicateRefs)) cards.duplicateRefs = [];
      if (!cards.lastWriteHashes || typeof cards.lastWriteHashes !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.lastWriteHashes)) cards.lastWriteHashes = {};

      const cfg = cards.config;
      const firstFinalConfig = cfg.bootstrapCardVersion !== "1.0.7-final";
      let changes = 0;

      if (cfg.namespacePrefix !== "SYS:AIDRPG") {
        cfg.namespacePrefix = "SYS:AIDRPG";
        changes += 1;
      }

      // Final build default: automatically create/update managed AIDRPG cards
      // when AI Dungeon exposes a compatible Story Card API. The safe wrappers
      // still fall back to pending/dry-run records if live calls fail.
      // Only force this once on first final-version setup, so /cardsync live off
      // and /cardsync safe on remain useful manual controls afterward.
      if (firstFinalConfig && cfg.liveWrites !== true) {
        cfg.liveWrites = true;
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (firstFinalConfig && cfg.cardSafeMode !== false) {
        cfg.cardSafeMode = false;
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (cfg.strictCardSync !== true) {
        cfg.strictCardSync = true;
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (typeof cfg.maxWritesPerTurn !== "number" || cfg.maxWritesPerTurn < 14) {
        cfg.maxWritesPerTurn = 14;
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (typeof cfg.maxCardTextChars !== "number" || cfg.maxCardTextChars < 1400) {
        cfg.maxCardTextChars = 1400;
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (cfg.autoBootstrapCards !== true) {
        cfg.autoBootstrapCards = true;
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (cfg.autoUpdateSummaryCards !== true) {
        cfg.autoUpdateSummaryCards = true;
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (cfg.bootstrapCardVersion !== "1.0.7-final") {
        cfg.bootstrapCardVersion = "1.0.7-final";
        changes += 1;
      }

      if (!cards.finalDefaults || typeof cards.finalDefaults !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.finalDefaults)) {
        cards.finalDefaults = {
          bootstrapped: false,
          lastBootstrapTurn: -1,
          lastSummarySyncTurn: -1,
          cursor: 0
        };
        changes += 1;
      }

      return changes;
    },

    makeSpec(family, id, title, lines, keys, root) {
      const C = A.CardSyncSystem;
      if (!C || !C.makeCardSpec) return null;

      const safeLines = [];
      if (Array.isArray(lines)) {
        for (let i = 0; i < lines.length && i < 14; i += 1) {
          const line = this.norm(lines[i]);
          if (line) safeLines.push(line.slice(0, 220));
        }
      }

      return C.makeCardSpec(
        family,
        id,
        title,
        safeLines.join("\n"),
        Array.isArray(keys) ? keys : [title],
        root
      );
    },

    playerLines(root) {
      const player = root.player || {};
      const level = player.levelState || {};
      const stats = player.stats || {};
      const body = player.body || {};
      const cond = player.conditions || {};
      const appearance = player.appearance || {};
      const titles = Array.isArray(player.titles) && player.titles.length ? player.titles.join(", ") : "none";
      return [
        "AIDRPG managed player sheet. This card is a mirror; state.aidrpg is canonical.",
        "Name: " + (player.name || "(unset)") + " | Species: " + (player.species || "human") + " | Age: " + (player.age > 0 ? player.age : "unknown") + " | Titles: " + titles,
        "Level: " + (level.level || 1) + " | XP: " + (level.xp || 0) + "/" + (level.xpToNext || 100) + " | Total XP: " + (level.totalXp || 0),
        "HP: " + (stats.hp ?? 100) + "/" + (stats.maxHp ?? 100) + " | MP: " + (stats.mp ?? 20) + "/" + (stats.maxMp ?? 20) + " | EP: " + (stats.ep ?? 50) + "/" + (stats.maxEp ?? 50),
        "Stats: ATK " + (stats.atk ?? 5) + " | DEF " + (stats.def ?? 5) + " | SPD " + (stats.spd ?? 5) + " | INT " + (stats.intl ?? 5) + " | LCK " + (stats.lck ?? 5),
        "Active Conditions: " + (Array.isArray(cond.active) && cond.active.length ? cond.active.join(", ") : "none"),
        "Permanent Conditions: " + (Array.isArray(cond.permanent) && cond.permanent.length ? cond.permanent.join(", ") : "none"),
        "Injuries: " + (Array.isArray(body.injuries) && body.injuries.length ? body.injuries.join(", ") : "none"),
        "Visible Traits: " + (Array.isArray(appearance.visibleTraits) && appearance.visibleTraits.length ? appearance.visibleTraits.join(", ") : "none")
      ];
    },

    inventoryLines(root) {
      const items = root.items || {};
      const byId = items.byId || {};
      const owned = items.ownership && Array.isArray(items.ownership.player) ? items.ownership.player : [];
      const names = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < owned.length && names.length < 14; i += 1) {
        const item = byId[owned[i]];
        if (!item || typeof item !== "object") continue;
        let label = item.displayName || item.baseType || owned[i];
        const notes = item.notes || {};
        if (notes.specialFunction) label += " [" + notes.specialFunction + "]";
        names.push(label);
      }
      const currency = items.currency && items.currency.byType ? items.currency.byType : {};
      return [
        "AIDRPG managed inventory mirror. Tracks owned items, equipment, source identity, named items, and special functions.",
        "Owned Items: " + (names.length ? names.join(" | ") : "none"),
        "Currency: copper " + (currency.copper || 0) + ", silver " + (currency.silver || 0) + ", gold " + (currency.gold || 0),
        "Important rule: item ownership in state.aidrpg is canonical; this card is the long-term Story Card mirror."
      ];
    },

    abilityBucketLines(root, bucketName) {
      const abilities = root.abilities || {};
      const byId = abilities.byId || {};
      const ids =
        bucketName === "skill" && Array.isArray(abilities.skillIds) ? abilities.skillIds :
