        bucketName === "talent" && Array.isArray(abilities.talentIds) ? abilities.talentIds :
        bucketName === "passive" && Array.isArray(abilities.passiveIds) ? abilities.passiveIds :
        Array.isArray(abilities.allIds) ? abilities.allIds : Object.keys(byId);

      const rows = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < ids.length && rows.length < 12; i += 1) {
        const rec = byId[ids[i]];
        if (!rec || typeof rec !== "object") continue;
        const identity = rec.identity || {};
        const type = String(identity.type || identity.kind || "ability").toLowerCase();
        if (bucketName !== "ability" && type !== bucketName) continue;
        const meaning = rec.meaning || {};
        const progress = rec.progression || {};
        const name = identity.name || rec.name || ids[i];
        const fn = meaning.intendedFunction ? " | Function: " + meaning.intendedFunction : "";
        const source = meaning.sourceAbilityName ? " | Source: " + meaning.sourceAbilityName : "";
        rows.push(name + " [" + type + "] | Mastery: " + (progress.masteryState || "novice") + fn + source);
      }

      const label = bucketName === "skill" ? "Skills" : bucketName === "talent" ? "Talents" : bucketName === "passive" ? "Passives" : "Abilities";
      return [
        "AIDRPG managed " + label.toLowerCase() + " mirror. Stores names, type, mastery, function, source, and observed use.",
        label + ": " + (rows.length ? rows.join(" || ") : "none yet")
      ];
    },

    passiveTraitLines(root) {
      const lines = this.abilityBucketLines(root, "passive");
      const player = root.player || {};
      const cond = player.conditions || {};
      const body = player.body || {};
      const traits = player.traitState || {};
      const extras = [];
      if (Array.isArray(cond.permanent) && cond.permanent.length) extras.push("Permanent: " + cond.permanent.join(", "));
      if (Array.isArray(cond.immunities) && cond.immunities.length) extras.push("Immunities: " + cond.immunities.join(", "));
      if (Array.isArray(cond.vulnerabilities) && cond.vulnerabilities.length) extras.push("Vulnerabilities: " + cond.vulnerabilities.join(", "));
      if (Array.isArray(body.speciesTraits) && body.speciesTraits.length) extras.push("Species Traits: " + body.speciesTraits.join(", "));
      if (Array.isArray(body.bodyTags) && body.bodyTags.length) extras.push("Body Tags: " + body.bodyTags.join(", "));
      if (traits && Array.isArray(traits.allIds) && traits.allIds.length) extras.push("Trait Records: " + traits.allIds.slice(0, 8).join(", "));
      if (extras.length) lines.push(extras.join(" | "));
      return lines;
    },

    questLines(root) {
      const quests = root.quests || {};
      const active = Array.isArray(quests.active) ? quests.active : [];
      const archived = Array.isArray(quests.archived) ? quests.archived : [];
      const rows = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < active.length && rows.length < 8; i += 1) {
        const q = active[i] || {};
        rows.push((q.title || "Untitled Quest") + " | Status: " + (q.status || "active") + (q.objective ? " | Goal: " + q.objective : ""));
      }
      return [
        "AIDRPG managed quest log mirror. Active, completed, abandoned, and failed objectives update here.",
        "Active Quests: " + (rows.length ? rows.join(" || ") : "none"),
        "Archived Count: " + archived.length
      ];
    },

    timeLines(root) {
      const time = root.time || {};
      const current = time.current || {};
      const elapsed = time.elapsed || {};
      const routeLines = [];
      const known = time.routes && time.routes.knownTravelTimes ? time.routes.knownTravelTimes : {};
      const keys = Object.keys(known);
      for (let i = 0; i < keys.length && routeLines.length < 8; i += 1) {
        const row = known[keys[i]];
        if (!row || typeof row !== "object") continue;
        const from = String(row.fromLabel || row.fromRef || "unknown").replace(/^location_/, "").replace(/_/g, " ");
        const to = String(row.toLabel || row.toRef || "unknown").replace(/^location_/, "").replace(/_/g, " ");
        const delay = row.lastDelayReason ? " | Last delay: " + row.lastDelayReason + " (" + (row.lastActualMinutes || "?") + "m actual)" : "";
        routeLines.push(from + " <-> " + to + " = " + (row.minutes || row.baseMinutes || "?") + "m base" + delay);
      }
      return [
        "AIDRPG managed time/route mirror. Base routes remain stable; delays are stored as actual-trip changes.",
        "Day " + (current.day ?? 1) + " | Time " + (current.hour ?? 8) + ":" + String(current.minute ?? 0).padStart(2, "0") + " | Season: " + (current.season || "unset") + " | Weather: " + (current.weather || "unset"),
        "Elapsed Hours: " + (elapsed.totalHours || 0),
        "Known Routes: " + (routeLines.length ? routeLines.join(" || ") : "none")
      ];
    },

    reputationLines(root) {
      const rep = root.reputation || {};
      const bySubject = rep.bySubject || {};
      const subjects = Object.keys(bySubject);
      const rows = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < subjects.length && rows.length < 8; i += 1) {
        const row = bySubject[subjects[i]] || {};
        const identity = row.identity || {};
        const mutable = row.mutable || {};
        rows.push((identity.displayName || subjects[i]) + " | " + (mutable.standing || "neutral") + " | score " + (mutable.score || 0));
      }
      const rumors = Array.isArray(rep.rumors) && rep.rumors.length ? rep.rumors.slice(-3).map(r => r && typeof r === "object" ? (r.label || r.text || "") : String(r || "")).filter(Boolean).join(" | ") : "none";
      return [
        "AIDRPG managed reputation mirror. Tracks local/faction/public reputation, rumors, reports, and delayed spread.",
        "Tracked Subjects: " + (rows.length ? rows.join(" || ") : "none"),
        "Recent Rumors: " + rumors
      ];
    },

    actorLines(root) {
      const actors = root.actors || {};
      const memory = actors.majorMemory || {};
      const byActorId = memory.byActorId || {};
      const ids = Object.keys(byActorId);
      const rows = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < ids.length && rows.length < 8; i += 1) {
        const rec = byActorId[ids[i]] || {};
        rows.push((rec.name || ids[i]) + " | stance: " + (rec.relationshipStance || "neutral") + " | seen: " + (rec.lastSeenTurn || 0));
      }
      return [
        "AIDRPG managed actor memory mirror. Named NPCs, companions, enemies, and recurring creatures are summarized here.",
        "Major Actors: " + (rows.length ? rows.join(" || ") : "none")
      ];
    },

    sceneLines(root) {
      const world = root.world || {};
      const scene = world.currentScene || {};
      const locations = world.locationsById || {};
      const entities = world.entitiesById || {};
      let parentName = scene.parentLocationRef;
      if (scene.parentLocationRef && locations[scene.parentLocationRef] && locations[scene.parentLocationRef].identity) {
        parentName = locations[scene.parentLocationRef].identity.displayName || scene.parentLocationRef;
      }
      const entity = scene.entityRef && entities[scene.entityRef] ? entities[scene.entityRef] : null;
      const mutable = entity && entity.mutable ? entity.mutable : {};
      return [
        "AIDRPG managed current scene/location mirror. Immediate scene truth is still injected through Context; this card mirrors durable location facts.",
        "Scene: " + (scene.displayName || "unknown") + " | Type: " + (scene.sceneType || "unknown") + " | Parent: " + (parentName || "none"),
        "Entity Ref: " + (scene.entityRef || "none") + " | Scene Ref: " + (scene.sceneRef || "none"),
        "Facts: " + (Array.isArray(mutable.factTags) && mutable.factTags.length ? mutable.factTags.slice(-8).join(", ") : "none"),
        "State: " + (Array.isArray(mutable.conditionTags) && mutable.conditionTags.length ? mutable.conditionTags.slice(-8).join(", ") : "normal")
      ];
    },

    worldLines(root) {
      const world = root.world || {};
      const locCount = world.locationsById && typeof world.locationsById === "object" ? Object.keys(world.locationsById).length : 0;
      const entCount = world.entitiesById && typeof world.entitiesById === "object" ? Object.keys(world.entitiesById).length : 0;
      const routeCount = world.routesById && typeof world.routesById === "object" ? Object.keys(world.routesById).length : 0;
      return [
        "AIDRPG managed world canon index. Specific locations/entities receive their own cards when they become dirty or important.",
        "Known Locations: " + locCount + " | Known Entities: " + entCount + " | Known World Routes: " + routeCount,
        "Rule: same-name buildings are parent-scoped, so Stoneford inn and Greybridge inn remain separate records."
      ];
    },

    cardIndexLines(root) {
      const cards = root.cards || {};
      const config = cards.config || {};
      const index = cards.index || {};
      const nsCount = index.byNamespace && typeof index.byNamespace === "object" ? Object.keys(index.byNamespace).length : 0;
      return [
        "AIDRPG managed CardSync index/config mirror.",
        "Live Writes: " + (config.liveWrites === true ? "on" : "off") + " | Safe Mode: " + (config.cardSafeMode === false ? "off" : "on") + " | Strict: " + (config.strictCardSync === false ? "off" : "on"),
        "Namespace Prefix: " + (config.namespacePrefix || "SYS:AIDRPG") + " | Indexed Managed Cards: " + nsCount,
        "Default final behavior: managed player, inventory, quest, skill, talent, passive, time, reputation, actor, scene, and world cards are created/updated automatically."
      ];
    },

    getDefaultCardSpecs(root) {
      const C = A.CardSyncSystem;
      if (!C) return [];

      const specs = [];

      const add = (spec) => {
        if (spec && spec.namespace) specs.push(spec);
      };

      add(this.makeSpec("player", "summary", "Player Sheet / Stats", this.playerLines(root), ["player", "sheet", "stats", "level", "xp", "hp", "mp", "ep"], root));
      add(this.makeSpec("item", "summary", "Inventory Summary", this.inventoryLines(root), ["inventory", "items", "equipment", "currency"], root));
      add(this.makeSpec("quest", "summary", "Quest Log", this.questLines(root), ["quests", "quest log", "objectives", "goals"], root));
      add(this.makeSpec("ability", "summary", "Ability Summary", this.abilityBucketLines(root, "ability"), ["abilities", "powers", "techniques"], root));
      add(this.makeSpec("skill", "summary", "Skills", this.abilityBucketLines(root, "skill"), ["skills", "active abilities", "techniques"], root));
      add(this.makeSpec("talent", "summary", "Talents", this.abilityBucketLines(root, "talent"), ["talents", "styles", "stances", "forms"], root));
      add(this.makeSpec("passive", "summary", "Passives / Traits", this.passiveTraitLines(root), ["passives", "traits", "conditions", "resistances", "adaptations"], root));
      add(this.makeSpec("time", "summary", "Time / Routes", this.timeLines(root), ["time", "routes", "travel", "calendar"], root));
      add(this.makeSpec("reputation", "summary", "Reputation / Rumors", this.reputationLines(root), ["reputation", "fame", "rumors", "standing"], root));
      add(this.makeSpec("actor", "summary", "Major Actor Memory", this.actorLines(root), ["actors", "npcs", "companions", "enemies", "memory"], root));
      add(this.makeSpec("scene", "current", "Current Scene / Location", this.sceneLines(root), ["scene", "location", "current place", "room"], root));
      add(this.makeSpec("world", "summary", "World Canon Summary", this.worldLines(root), ["world", "locations", "entities", "map"], root));
      add(this.makeSpec("cards", "index", "AIDRPG Managed Card Index", this.cardIndexLines(root), ["cardsync", "managed cards", "aidrpg cards"], root));

      return specs;
    },

    hasDirtySummaryReason(root) {
      if (!root || !root.meta || !root.meta.dirty) return false;
      const dirty = root.meta.dirty;
      if (dirty.player || dirty.reputation || dirty.quests || dirty.time) return true;
      const arrays = ["abilities", "items", "actors", "entities", "routes", "cards"];
      for (let i = 0; i < arrays.length; i += 1) {
        if (Array.isArray(dirty[arrays[i]]) && dirty[arrays[i]].length) return true;
      }
      return false;
    },

    ensureDefaultStoryCards(root, force) {
      root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
      if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || !A.CardSyncSystem) return 0;

      this.ensureFinalCardConfig(root);
      const C = A.CardSyncSystem;
      const cards = C.init(root);
      if (!cards || !cards.config || cards.config.autoBootstrapCards !== true) return 0;

      const tracker = cards.finalDefaults || (cards.finalDefaults = {});
      const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
      const shouldBootstrap = force === true || tracker.bootstrapped !== true;
      const shouldUpdate = cards.config.autoUpdateSummaryCards === true && (this.hasDirtySummaryReason(root) || turn - Number(tracker.lastSummarySyncTurn || -10) >= 3);

      if (!shouldBootstrap && !shouldUpdate) return 0;

      const specs = this.getDefaultCardSpecs(root);
      const max = shouldBootstrap ? Math.max(14, Number(cards.config.maxWritesPerTurn || 14)) : Math.max(4, Math.floor(Number(cards.config.maxWritesPerTurn || 14) / 2));
      let count = 0;

      for (let i = 0; i < specs.length && count < max; i += 1) {
        if (!specs[i] || !specs[i].namespace) continue;
        C.safeEnsureSingletonCard(specs[i], root);
        count += 1;
      }

      if (shouldBootstrap) {
        tracker.bootstrapped = true;
        tracker.lastBootstrapTurn = turn;
      }
      tracker.lastSummarySyncTurn = turn;

      if (A.Utils && A.Utils.logEvent) {
        A.Utils.logEvent("AIDRPG managed Story Cards " + (shouldBootstrap ? "bootstrapped" : "updated") + ": " + count);
      }

      return count;
    },

    applyPhysicalResourceStrain(rawText, root) {
      root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
      if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
      if (A.PlayerSystem && A.PlayerSystem.init) A.PlayerSystem.init(root);

      const raw = String(rawText || "");
      const lower = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
      if (!lower) return 0;

      const physicalContext =
        /\b(?:boxing|punch|kick|strike|slash|swing|blade|sword|guard|stance|stoneguard|crescent slash|martial|sprint|run|climb|grapple|wrestle|training|drill|spar|marathon|endurance|physical|muscle|breath|lungs|legs|arms)\b/.test(lower);

      const explicitEnergy =
        /\b(?:stamina|energy|endurance|breath|wind)\b[^.!?]{0,50}\b(?:low|gone|empty|spent|drained|exhausted|depleted|failing|fails|out)\b/.test(lower) ||
        /\b(?:no|without|out of)\s+(?:stamina|energy|breath|wind)\b/.test(lower) ||
        /\b(?:you are|you're|you feel|become|became)\s+(?:exhausted|spent|winded|drained|fatigued|worn out|too tired)\b/.test(lower) ||
        /\byour\s+(?:legs|arms|muscles|body|lungs)\s+(?:shake|shaking|burn|ache|give out|nearly give out)\b/.test(lower);

      const implicitHugeEffort =
        /\b(?:for hours|all day|marathon|long after|refuse to stop|keep going|continue until|massive endurance|endurance drill|repeatedly|again and again)\b/.test(lower) &&
        /\b(?:boxing|training|drill|spar|stance|guard|slash|swing|sprint|run|physical|endurance|breath)\b/.test(lower);

      if (!physicalContext && !explicitEnergy && !implicitHugeEffort) return 0;
      if (!explicitEnergy && !implicitHugeEffort) return 0;

      const player = root.player || {};
      const stats = player.stats || {};
      const conditions = player.conditions || (player.conditions = {});
      if (!Array.isArray(conditions.active)) conditions.active = [];

      const maxEp = Number(stats.maxEp || 50);
      const currentEp = Number(stats.ep);
      if (!Number.isFinite(currentEp)) stats.ep = maxEp;

      let nextEp = stats.ep;
      let condition = "strained";

      if (/\b(?:no|without|out of)\s+(?:stamina|energy|breath|wind)\b|\b(?:empty|spent|depleted|exhausted|give out|too tired|can't continue|cannot continue)\b/.test(lower)) {
        nextEp = Math.min(stats.ep, Math.max(0, Math.floor(maxEp * 0.08)));
        condition = "exhausted";
      } else if (/\b(?:low|failing|drained|winded|fatigued|shaking|burn|ache)\b/.test(lower)) {
        nextEp = Math.min(stats.ep, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(maxEp * 0.25)));
        condition = /winded|breath|wind|lungs/.test(lower) ? "winded" : "strained";
      } else if (implicitHugeEffort) {
        nextEp = Math.min(stats.ep, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(maxEp * 0.30)));
        condition = "strained";
      }

      let changes = 0;
      if (nextEp !== stats.ep) {
        stats.ep = nextEp;
        changes += 1;
      }

      if (A.PersistentTraitSystem && typeof A.PersistentTraitSystem.applyConditionCandidate === "function") {
        changes += A.PersistentTraitSystem.applyConditionCandidate({
          name: condition,
          mode: "active",
          severity: condition === "exhausted" ? "severe" : "moderate"
        }, raw, root) || 0;
      } else {
        changes += this.pushUnique(conditions.active, condition, 12);
      }

      if (changes > 0) {
        if (A.PlayerSystem && A.PlayerSystem.recomputeDerived) A.PlayerSystem.recomputeDerived(player);
        if (A.Utils && A.Utils.markDirty) A.Utils.markDirty("player", true, root);
        if (A.Utils && A.Utils.invalidatePackets) A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
        if (A.Utils && A.Utils.logEvent) A.Utils.logEvent("Physical exertion strained EP: " + condition + ".");
      }

      return changes;
    }
  };

  if (A.CardSyncSystem) {
    const C = A.CardSyncSystem;

    const oldInit = C.init;
    C.init = function(root) {
      const cards = oldInit.call(this, root);
      try { F.ensureFinalCardConfig(root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null)); } catch (_error) {}
      return cards;
    };

    C.tryLiveAddCard = function(spec, root) {
      const payload = this.buildStoryCardPayload(spec, null);
      const keys = Array.isArray(payload.keys) ? payload.keys.join(",") : String(payload.keys || payload.namespace || "");
      const type = spec && spec.family ? String(spec.family) : "system";
      return this.callStoryCardMethod(
        ["addStoryCard", "addCard", "createStoryCard", "createCard", "create", "add"],
        [
          [keys, payload.entry, type, payload.title, payload.description || "", { returnCard: true }],
          [keys, payload.entry, type, payload.title, payload.description || ""],
          [keys, payload.entry, type],
          [payload.title, payload.entry, type],
          [payload]
        ],
        root
      );
    };

    C.tryLiveUpdateCard = function(card, spec, root) {
      const payload = this.buildStoryCardPayload(spec, card);
      const id = String(payload.id || payload.cardId || "").trim();
      const keys = Array.isArray(payload.keys) ? payload.keys.join(",") : String(payload.keys || payload.namespace || "");
      const type = spec && spec.family ? String(spec.family) : "system";
      const argSets = id
        ? [[id, keys, payload.entry, type, payload.title, payload.description || ""], [id, payload], [payload]]
        : [[keys, payload.entry, type, payload.title, payload.description || ""], [payload]];

      const viaApi = this.callStoryCardMethod(
        ["updateStoryCard", "updateCard", "editStoryCard", "editCard", "update", "set"],
        argSets,
        root
      );

      if (viaApi && viaApi.ok) return viaApi;

      try {
        const target = card && typeof card === "object" ? (card.raw && typeof card.raw === "object" ? card.raw : card) : null;
        if (target) {
          target.keys = keys;
          target.keywords = Array.isArray(payload.keys) ? payload.keys.slice() : [keys];
          target.entry = payload.entry;
          target.text = payload.entry;
          target.value = payload.entry;
          target.description = payload.description || payload.entry;
          target.title = payload.title;
          target.name = payload.title;
          target.type = type;
          target.namespace = payload.namespace;
          target.aidrpgManaged = true;
          return { ok: true, result: target, method: "directObjectMutation", source: "storyCards" };
        }
      } catch (error) {
        return { ok: false, error: error && error.message ? error.message : String(error) };
      }

      return viaApi || { ok: false, error: "no compatible Story Card update method found" };
    };

    const oldProcessOutput = C.processOutput;
    C.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
      root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
      let changes = oldProcessOutput.call(this, rawText, root) || 0;
      try {
        changes += F.ensureDefaultStoryCards(root, false) || 0;
      } catch (error) {
        if (A.Utils && A.Utils.logError) A.Utils.logError("Final default Story Card sync failed: " + (error && error.message ? error.message : String(error)));
      }
      return changes;
    };
  }

  if (A.PlayerSystem && A.PlayerSystem.processOutput) {
    const oldPlayerOutput = A.PlayerSystem.processOutput;
    A.PlayerSystem.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
      root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
      let changes = oldPlayerOutput.call(this, rawText, root) || 0;
      changes += F.applyPhysicalResourceStrain(rawText, root) || 0;
      return changes;
    };
  }

  if (A.onInput) {
    const oldInput = A.onInput;
    A.onInput = function(text) {
      const result = oldInput.call(this, text);
      try {
        if (typeof state === "object" && state && state.aidrpg) {
          F.ensureDefaultStoryCards(state.aidrpg, false);
        }
      } catch (_error) {}
      return result;
    };
  }

  if (A.onContext) {
    const oldContext = A.onContext;
    A.onContext = function(text) {
      try {
        if (typeof state === "object" && state && state.aidrpg) {
          F.ensureFinalCardConfig(state.aidrpg);
        }
      } catch (_error) {}
      return oldContext.call(this, text);
    };
  }

  if (A.AdminDebugSystem && A.AdminDebugSystem.handleCommand) {
    const oldHandle = A.AdminDebugSystem.handleCommand;
    A.AdminDebugSystem.handleCommand = function(rawText) {
      const cmd = String(rawText || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      if (cmd === "/cardsync bootstrap" || cmd === "/cardsync init" || cmd === "/cardsync generate") {
        if (A.CoreState && A.CoreState.init) A.CoreState.init();
        if (A.CardSyncSystem && A.CardSyncSystem.init) A.CardSyncSystem.init(state.aidrpg);
        const count = F.ensureDefaultStoryCards(state.aidrpg, true);
        return { text: "[AIDRPG /cardsync bootstrap]\nManaged default Story Cards generated/updated: " + count + "\nLive Writes: " + (state.aidrpg.cards.config.liveWrites ? "on" : "off") + "\nSafe Mode: " + (state.aidrpg.cards.config.cardSafeMode ? "on" : "off") + "\n" };
      }
      return oldHandle.call(this, rawText);
    };
  }
})(globalThis.AIDRPG);



// -----------------------------------------------------------------
// AIDRPG v1.0.8 PRODUCTION HOTFIX: source-clause acquisition cleanup.
// Fixes common AI Dungeon phrasing such as "You take X from Y" so
// the item identity stays clean while the source/location is preserved
// as item notes for Context and Story Card mirrors.
// -----------------------------------------------------------------
(function installAIDRPGProductionFromSourceHotfix(A) {
  "use strict";
  if (!A || A.__v108FromSourceHotfixInstalled === true) return;
  A.__v108FromSourceHotfixInstalled = true;
  A.VERSION = "1.0.8-production";
  A.BUILD_STAGE = "AIDRPG-v1.0.8-from-source-hotfix";

  const H = A.V108FromSourceHotfix = {
    norm(value) {
      return String(value == null ? "" : value).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    },

    stripLeadingAcquireVerb(value) {
      return this.norm(value)
        .replace(/[“”"'`]+/g, "")
        .replace(/^\s*(?:you\s+)?(?:pick(?:ed)?\s+up|grab(?:bed)?|take|takes|took|collect(?:ed)?|gather(?:ed)?|loot(?:ed)?|snatch(?:ed)?|snag(?:ged)?|nab(?:bed)?|yoink(?:ed)?|scoop(?:ed)?\s+up|pocket(?:ed)?|stash(?:ed)?|retrieve(?:d)?|recover(?:ed)?|find|found|buy|buys|bought|purchase(?:d)?|receive(?:d)?|accept(?:ed)?|craft(?:ed)?|brew(?:ed)?|make|made)\s+/i, "")
        .trim();
    },

    cleanSourceLabel(value) {
      return this.norm(value)
        .replace(/[“”"'`]+/g, "")
        .replace(/[.!?;].*$/g, "")
        .replace(/\s*,.*$/g, "")
        .replace(/\s+(?:and\s+keep(?:s|ing)?\s+moving|and\s+keep(?:s|ing)?\s+going|and\s+move(?:s)?\s+on|then\b|but\b|while\b|as\b).*/i, "")
        .replace(/^(?:the|a|an|some)\s+/i, "")
        .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
        .trim()
        .slice(0, 80);
    },

    extractSourceFromPhrase(value) {
      const raw = this.stripLeadingAcquireVerb(value);
      const match = raw.match(/\s+from\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+|some\s+)?([^.!?;\n]{2,120})/i);
      return match && match[1] ? this.cleanSourceLabel(match[1]) : "";
    },

    stripTrailingSourceFromItemPhrase(value, inv) {
      const raw = this.stripLeadingAcquireVerb(value);
      if (!/\s+from\s+/i.test(raw)) return "";

      const before = raw.replace(/\s+from\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+|some\s+)?[^.!?;\n]+[.!?;\n]*.*$/i, "").trim();
      if (!before) return "";

      const candidate = before
        .replace(/^(?:the|a|an|some|my|your)\s+/i, "")
        .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
        .trim();

      if (!candidate) return "";

      // Only strip a source clause if the pre-source phrase is already a
      // concrete item. This prevents unrelated "from" clauses from being
      // incorrectly interpreted as inventory.
      try {
        const cleanedByOld = inv && typeof inv.__v108OldCleanItemPhrase === "function"
          ? inv.__v108OldCleanItemPhrase.call(inv, candidate)
          : candidate;
        if (cleanedByOld && (!inv || typeof inv.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase !== "function" || inv.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(cleanedByOld))) {
          return cleanedByOld;
        }
      } catch (_error) {}

      try {
        const tokens = candidate.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
        if (inv && typeof inv.findConcreteItemHeadIndex === "function" && inv.findConcreteItemHeadIndex(tokens) !== -1) {
          return candidate;
        }
      } catch (_error2) {}

      return "";
    },

    attachSourceToItem(item, sourceLabel, rawPhrase, root) {
      if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return 0;
      const source = this.cleanSourceLabel(sourceLabel);
      if (!source) return 0;

      if (!item.notes || typeof item.notes !== "object" || Array.isArray(item.notes)) item.notes = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(item.notes.observedChanges)) item.notes.observedChanges = [];

      let changes = 0;
      if (item.notes.sourceLocation !== source) {
        item.notes.sourceLocation = source;
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (item.notes.observedSource !== source) {
        item.notes.observedSource = source;
        changes += 1;
      }

      const observed = "Source: " + source;
      let hasObserved = false;
      for (let i = 0; i < item.notes.observedChanges.length; i += 1) {
        if (String(item.notes.observedChanges[i] || "").toLowerCase() === observed.toLowerCase()) {
          hasObserved = true;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (!hasObserved) {
        item.notes.observedChanges.push(observed);
        while (item.notes.observedChanges.length > 12) item.notes.observedChanges.shift();
        changes += 1;
      }

      if (rawPhrase && !item.notes.lastSourcePhrase) {
        item.notes.lastSourcePhrase = this.norm(rawPhrase).slice(0, 140);
        changes += 1;
      }

      if (changes > 0) {
        if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.markDirty === "function") A.Utils.markDirty("items", item.id, root);
        if (A.CardSyncSystem && typeof A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty === "function") A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty("item", item.id, "source_acquisition", null, root);
      }
      return changes;
    },

    extractFromSourceAcquisitions(rawText) {
      const raw = String(rawText || "");
      const out = [];
      const patterns = [
        /\byou\s+(?:pick(?:ed)?\s+up|grab(?:bed)?|take|takes|took|collect(?:ed)?|gather(?:ed)?|loot(?:ed)?|snatch(?:ed)?|snag(?:ged)?|nab(?:bed)?|yoink(?:ed)?|scoop(?:ed)?\s+up|pocket(?:ed)?|stash(?:ed)?|retrieve(?:d)?|recover(?:ed)?|find|found|receive(?:d)?|accept(?:ed)?)\s+([^.!?;\n]{1,180}?\bfrom\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+|some\s+)?[^.!?;\n]{2,120})/gi,
        /\b(?:someone|somebody|a\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{1,32}|an\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{1,32}|the\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{1,32}|[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,40})\s+(?:gives|gave|hands|handed|offers|offered|passes|passed)\s+you\s+([^.!?;\n]{1,180}?\bfrom\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+|some\s+)?[^.!?;\n]{2,120})/g
      ];

      for (let p = 0; p < patterns.length; p += 1) {
        let match;
        while ((match = patterns[p].exec(raw))) {
          if (!match[1]) continue;
          const phrase = this.norm(match[1]);
          const source = this.extractSourceFromPhrase(phrase);
          if (!source) continue;
          out.push({ phrase, source });
        }
      }
      return out;
    },

    processFromSourceAcquisitions(rawText, root) {
      root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
      if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || !A.InventorySystem) return 0;
      const I = A.InventorySystem;
      if (typeof I.init === "function") I.init(root);
      if (typeof I.hasAcquisitionNegation === "function" && I.hasAcquisitionNegation(rawText, root)) return 0;

      const rows = this.extractFromSourceAcquisitions(rawText);
      let changes = 0;

      for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += 1) {
        const row = rows[i];
        const clean = I.cleanItemPhrase(row.phrase);
        if (!clean || (typeof I.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase === "function" && !I.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(clean))) continue;

        let item = null;
        if (typeof I.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase === "function") {
          item = I.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase(row.phrase, "packed", "story_gain", root, {
            rawPhrase: row.phrase,
            sourceLocation: row.source,
            forceDistinct: false
          });
        }
        if (!item && typeof I.findMatchingPlayerItem === "function") {
          item = I.findMatchingPlayerItem(clean, root);
        }
        if (!item && typeof I.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase === "function") {
          item = I.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase(clean, "packed", "story_gain", root);
        }
        if (!item) continue;
        changes += this.attachSourceToItem(item, row.source, row.phrase, root);
      }

      if (changes > 0) {
        if (I.refreshVisibleCarrySummary) I.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);
        if (A.Utils && A.Utils.invalidatePackets) A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
        if (A.Utils && A.Utils.logEvent) A.Utils.logEvent("Inventory source acquisition normalized: " + changes);
      }

      return changes;
    }
  };

  if (A.InventorySystem) {
    const I = A.InventorySystem;

    if (!I.__v108OldCleanItemPhrase && typeof I.cleanItemPhrase === "function") {
      I.__v108OldCleanItemPhrase = I.cleanItemPhrase;
    }
    const oldClean = I.__v108OldCleanItemPhrase || I.cleanItemPhrase;
    I.cleanItemPhrase = function(value) {
      const stripped = H.stripTrailingSourceFromItemPhrase(value, this);
      if (stripped) {
        const cleaned = oldClean ? oldClean.call(this, stripped) : stripped;
        return cleaned || stripped;
      }
      return oldClean ? oldClean.call(this, value) : H.norm(value);
    };

    if (!I.__v108OldMakeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase && typeof I.makeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase === "function") {
      I.__v108OldMakeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase = I.makeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase;
    }
    const oldSeed = I.__v108OldMakeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase || I.makeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase;
    I.makeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase = function(phrase, sourceContext) {
      const seed = oldSeed ? oldSeed.call(this, phrase, sourceContext) : null;
      if (!seed || typeof seed !== "object") return seed;
      const source = H.extractSourceFromPhrase(phrase);
      if (source) {
        if (!seed.notes || typeof seed.notes !== "object" || Array.isArray(seed.notes)) seed.notes = {};
        if (!Array.isArray(seed.notes.observedChanges)) seed.notes.observedChanges = [];
        seed.notes.sourceLocation = source;
        seed.notes.observedSource = source;
        seed.notes.lastSourcePhrase = H.norm(phrase).slice(0, 140);
        const observed = "Source: " + source;
        if (seed.notes.observedChanges.indexOf(observed) === -1) seed.notes.observedChanges.push(observed);
      }
      return seed;
    };

    if (!I.__v108OldProcessOutput && typeof I.processOutput === "function") {
      I.__v108OldProcessOutput = I.processOutput;
    }
    const oldProcess = I.__v108OldProcessOutput || I.processOutput;
    I.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
      root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
      let changes = oldProcess ? (oldProcess.call(this, rawText, root) || 0) : 0;
      changes += H.processFromSourceAcquisitions(rawText, root) || 0;
      return changes;
    };
  }

  if (A.CardSyncSystem && typeof A.CardSyncSystem.serializeItemCard === "function") {
    const C = A.CardSyncSystem;
    if (!C.__v108OldSerializeItemCard) C.__v108OldSerializeItemCard = C.serializeItemCard;
    const oldSerialize = C.__v108OldSerializeItemCard;
    C.serializeItemCard = function(id, root) {
      const spec = oldSerialize.call(this, id, root);
      const item = root && root.items && root.items.byId ? root.items.byId[id] : null;
      if (!spec || !item || !item.notes) return spec;
      const extra = [];
      if (item.notes.sourceLocation) extra.push("Source Location: " + item.notes.sourceLocation);
      if (item.notes.observedSource && item.notes.observedSource !== item.notes.sourceLocation) extra.push("Observed Source: " + item.notes.observedSource);
      if (!extra.length) return spec;
      const entry = String(spec.entry || "");
      for (let i = 0; i < extra.length; i += 1) {
        if (entry.toLowerCase().indexOf(extra[i].toLowerCase()) === -1) {
          spec.entry = (spec.entry || "") + "\n" + extra[i];
        }
      }
      if (this.stableHash) spec.hash = this.stableHash(spec.entry || "");
      return spec;
    };
  }
})(globalThis.AIDRPG);

// -----------------------------------------------------------------
// RPGSTART FINAL HOTFIX: production-playtest parser breadth cleanup.
// Fixes: record/document item heads, "is broken" item mutation forms,
// passive tolerance/resistance traits, and baseline local actor creation.
// -----------------------------------------------------------------
(function installRPGStartFinalHotfix(A) {
  "use strict";
  if (!A || A.__rpgStartFinalHotfixInstalled === true) return;
  A.__rpgStartFinalHotfixInstalled = true;
  A.VERSION = "RPGStart-1.0.9";
  A.BUILD_STAGE = "RPGStart-final-playtest-ready";

  const R = A.RPGStartFinalHotfix = A.RPGStartFinalHotfix || {};

  R.norm = function(value) {
    return String(value == null ? "" : value).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  };

  R.key = function(value) {
    return R.norm(value)
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[’'`´"]/g, "")
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_")
      .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "")
      .replace(/_+/g, "_");
  };

  R.pushUnique = function(list, value, cap) {
    const text = R.norm(value);
    if (!Array.isArray(list) || !text) return 0;
    const low = text.toLowerCase();
    for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) {
      if (String(list[i] || "").toLowerCase() === low) return 0;
    }
    list.push(text);
    while (cap && list.length > cap) list.shift();
    return 1;
  };

  R.markPlayerDirty = function(root) {
    if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.markDirty === "function") A.Utils.markDirty("player", true, root);
    if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.invalidatePackets === "function") A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
  };

  R.markItemDirty = function(item, root, reason) {
    if (!item || !item.id) return;
    if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.markDirty === "function") A.Utils.markDirty("items", item.id, root);
    if (A.CardSyncSystem && typeof A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty === "function") {
      A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty("item", item.id, reason || "item_update", null, root);
    }
    if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.invalidatePackets === "function") A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
  };

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------
  // 1) Record/document-like item vocabulary.
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------
  if (A.InventorySystem) {
    const I = A.InventorySystem;
    const extraHeads = new Set([
      "tablet", "plaque", "codex", "tome", "blueprint", "deed", "charter", "license"
    ]);
    const compoundHeads = [
      "stone tablet", "clay tablet", "rune tablet", "slate tablet", "ancient tablet",
      "bronze plaque", "stone plaque", "folded blueprint", "property deed",
      "guild charter", "merchant license"
    ];

    if (!I.__rpgStartOldIsItemHeadToken && typeof I.isItemHeadToken === "function") {
      I.__rpgStartOldIsItemHeadToken = I.isItemHeadToken;
    }
    const oldHead = I.__rpgStartOldIsItemHeadToken || I.isItemHeadToken;
    I.isItemHeadToken = function(value) {
      const raw = String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/g, "");
      const singular = raw.length > 3 && raw.endsWith("s") ? raw.slice(0, -1) : raw;
      if (extraHeads.has(raw) || extraHeads.has(singular)) return true;
      return oldHead ? oldHead.call(this, value) : false;
    };

    if (!I.__rpgStartOldDetectBaseType && typeof I.detectBaseType === "function") {
      I.__rpgStartOldDetectBaseType = I.detectBaseType;
    }
    const oldDetect = I.__rpgStartOldDetectBaseType || I.detectBaseType;
    I.detectBaseType = function(lowerPhrase) {
      const lower = String(lowerPhrase || "").toLowerCase();
      for (let i = 0; i < compoundHeads.length; i += 1) {
        if (lower.indexOf(compoundHeads[i]) !== -1) {
          const words = compoundHeads[i].split(/\s+/);
          return words[words.length - 1];
        }
      }
      for (const head of extraHeads) {
        if (new RegExp("\\b" + head + "s?\\b", "i").test(lower)) return head;
      }
      return oldDetect ? oldDetect.call(this, lowerPhrase) : "item";
    };

    R.applyExtraItemMutation = function(rawText, root) {
      root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
      if (!root || !A.InventorySystem) return 0;
      const Inv = A.InventorySystem;
      if (typeof Inv.init === "function") Inv.init(root);
      const raw = String(rawText || "");
      if (!raw.trim()) return 0;

      const patterns = [
        { regex: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)\s+(?:is\s+broken|are\s+broken|gets\s+broken|get\s+broken|becomes\s+broken|become\s+broken|is\s+cracked\s+apart|are\s+cracked\s+apart|is\s+smashed\s+apart|are\s+smashed\s+apart)\b/gi, tag: "broken", delta: 45 },
        { regex: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)\s+(?:is\s+smashed|are\s+smashed|gets\s+smashed|get\s+smashed|becomes\s+smashed|become\s+smashed|is\s+splintered|are\s+splintered|gets\s+splintered|get\s+splintered)\b/gi, tag: "broken", delta: 35 },
        { regex: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)\s+(?:is\s+bent|are\s+bent|gets\s+bent|get\s+bent|is\s+dulled|are\s+dulled|gets\s+dulled|get\s+dulled|is\s+chipped|are\s+chipped|gets\s+chipped|get\s+chipped)\b/gi, tag: "damaged", delta: 15 }
      ];

      let changes = 0;
      for (let p = 0; p < patterns.length; p += 1) {
        let match;
        while ((match = patterns[p].regex.exec(raw))) {
          const phrase = Inv.cleanPhrase ? Inv.cleanPhrase(match[1]) : R.norm(match[1]);
          if (!phrase) continue;
          const item = Inv.findMatchingPlayerItem ? Inv.findMatchingPlayerItem(phrase, root) : null;
          if (!item) continue;
          if (Inv.applyStateTag && Inv.applyStateTag(item.id, patterns[p].tag, patterns[p].delta, root)) {
            changes += 1;
            R.markItemDirty(item, root, "item_condition");
          }
        }
      }

      if (changes > 0 && A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.logEvent === "function") {
        A.Utils.logEvent("Item condition updated by expanded mutation wording.");
      }
      return changes;
    };

    if (!I.__rpgStartOldProcessItemStateChanges && typeof I.processItemStateChanges === "function") {
      I.__rpgStartOldProcessItemStateChanges = I.processItemStateChanges;
    }
    const oldStateChanges = I.__rpgStartOldProcessItemStateChanges || I.processItemStateChanges;
    I.processItemStateChanges = function(text, root) {
      let changes = oldStateChanges ? (oldStateChanges.call(this, text, root) || 0) : 0;
      changes += R.applyExtraItemMutation(text, root) || 0;
      return changes;
    };
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------
  // 2) Passive traits and tolerance/resistance safeguards.
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------
  R.applyPassiveTraitPhrases = function(rawText, root) {
    root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
    if (!root || !A.PersistentTraitSystem || !A.PlayerSystem) return 0;
    const T = A.PersistentTraitSystem;
    if (typeof T.init === "function") T.init(root);
    if (typeof A.PlayerSystem.init === "function") A.PlayerSystem.init(root);

    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const lower = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
    if (!lower) return 0;

    const safePassiveContext = /\b(?:adapts?|develops?|developed|becomes?\s+natural|becomes?\s+passive|passive\s+trait|remains?\s+as\s+a\s+trait|settles?\s+into\s+your\s+body|settles?\s+into\s+the\s+body|now\s+part\s+of\s+you)\b/.test(lower);
    if (!safePassiveContext) return 0;

    const passiveMap = [
      { phrase: "fire resistance", category: "resistance", domain: "fire", removeConditions: ["burned", "burning"] },
      { phrase: "cold resistance", category: "resistance", domain: "cold", removeConditions: ["chilled", "frozen"] },
      { phrase: "poison tolerance", category: "adaptation", domain: "poison tolerance", removeConditions: ["poisoned", "poison"] },
      { phrase: "iron stomach", category: "adaptation", domain: "iron stomach", removeConditions: [] },
      { phrase: "pain tolerance", category: "adaptation", domain: "pain tolerance", removeConditions: [] },
      { phrase: "night vision", category: "adaptation", domain: "night vision", removeConditions: ["blinded"] },
      { phrase: "quick reflexes", category: "adaptation", domain: "quick reflexes", removeConditions: [] },
      { phrase: "mana sensitivity", category: "sensitivity", domain: "mana", removeConditions: [] }
    ];

    let changes = 0;
    const player = root.player || {};
    if (!player.conditions || typeof player.conditions !== "object") player.conditions = {};
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.active)) player.conditions.active = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.permanent)) player.conditions.permanent = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < passiveMap.length; i += 1) {
      const row = passiveMap[i];
      if (lower.indexOf(row.phrase) === -1) continue;

      if (typeof T.applyTraitCandidate === "function") {
        changes += T.applyTraitCandidate({ category: row.category, domain: row.domain }, raw, root) || 0;
      }
      changes += R.pushUnique(player.conditions.permanent, row.phrase, 20);

      // Important safety: "poison tolerance" and similar passive phrasing
      // must not leave the player actively poisoned/burning/frozen unless the
      // output separately says the harmful condition is currently active.
      for (let r = 0; r < row.removeConditions.length; r += 1) {
        const cond = row.removeConditions[r];
        if (typeof T.removeConditionByName === "function") changes += T.removeConditionByName(cond, root) || 0;
        if (Array.isArray(player.conditions.active)) {
          for (let a = player.conditions.active.length - 1; a >= 0; a -= 1) {
            const active = String(player.conditions.active[a] || "").toLowerCase();
            if (active === cond || active === cond.replace(/ed$/, "")) {
              player.conditions.active.splice(a, 1);
              changes += 1;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if (changes > 0) {
      if (typeof T.syncLegacyPlayerArrays === "function") T.syncLegacyPlayerArrays(root);
      // Re-add exact plain-language passive names after legacy sync, because
      // trait records use technical labels such as "poison tolerance acclimation".
      for (let i = 0; i < passiveMap.length; i += 1) {
        if (lower.indexOf(passiveMap[i].phrase) !== -1) R.pushUnique(player.conditions.permanent, passiveMap[i].phrase, 20);
      }
      R.markPlayerDirty(root);
      if (A.CardSyncSystem && typeof A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty === "function") A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty("passive", "summary", "passive_trait", null, root);
      if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.logEvent === "function") A.Utils.logEvent("Passive trait captured: tolerance/resistance/adaptation.");
    }
    return changes;
  };

  if (A.PersistentTraitSystem && typeof A.PersistentTraitSystem.processOutput === "function") {
    const T = A.PersistentTraitSystem;
    if (!T.__rpgStartOldProcessOutput) T.__rpgStartOldProcessOutput = T.processOutput;
    const oldTraitOutput = T.__rpgStartOldProcessOutput;
    T.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
      root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
      let changes = oldTraitOutput.call(this, rawText, root) || 0;
      changes += R.applyPassiveTraitPhrases(rawText, root) || 0;
      return changes;
    };
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------
  // 3) Common baseline local actor profiles.
  // These are intentionally local/baseline actors, not forced major memory.
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------
  R.extractBaselineActorPhrases = function(rawText) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const rows = [];
    const heads = [
      "guard", "knight", "mage", "archmage", "bear", "dragon", "orc", "warrior", "golem", "acolyte",
      "spider", "wolf", "troll", "wyvern", "captain", "soldier", "beast", "scout", "archer", "bandit", "goblin"
    ];
    const headRe = heads.slice().sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length).join("|");
    const patterns = [
      new RegExp("\\b(?:an|a|the)\\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{0,32}?(?:" + headRe + "))\\s+(?:stands?|waits?|watches?|looms?|lurks?|blocks?|guards?|paces?|crouches?)\\b[^.!?]{0,80}\\b(?:nearby|here|from\\s+the\\s+door|at\\s+the\\s+door|by\\s+the\\s+door|on\\s+the\\s+road|at\\s+the\\s+gate|the\\s+bridge|the\\s+path)?", "gi"),
      new RegExp("\\b(?:nearby|ahead|before\\s+you|at\\s+the\\s+gate|on\\s+the\\s+road)\\s+(?:stands?|waits?|watches?|looms?|lurks?)\\s+(?:an|a|the)\\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{0,32}?(?:" + headRe + "))\\b", "gi")
    ];

    for (let p = 0; p < patterns.length; p += 1) {
      let m;
      while ((m = patterns[p].exec(raw))) {
        const label = R.norm(m[1]).replace(/^(?:the|an|a)\s+/i, "");
        if (!label || label.split(/\s+/).length > 4) continue;
        rows.push(label);
      }
    }
    return rows;
  };

  R.applyBaselineActorProfiles = function(rawText, root) {
    root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
    if (!root || !A.ActorProfileSystem) return 0;
    const S = A.ActorProfileSystem;
    if (typeof S.init === "function") S.init(root);
    const labels = R.extractBaselineActorPhrases(rawText);
    if (!labels.length) return 0;

    let changes = 0;
    const actors = root.actors || {};
    if (!Array.isArray(actors.localIds)) actors.localIds = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < labels.length; i += 1) {
      const label = labels[i];
      const display = label.replace(/\b\w/g, (c) => c.toUpperCase());
      const roleTags = S.inferRoleTags ? S.inferRoleTags(label) : [];
      const rankTags = S.inferRankTags ? S.inferRankTags(label) : [];
      const category = S.inferCategory ? S.inferCategory(label) : "humanoid";
      const sceneKey = (A.Utils && A.Utils.toKey ? A.Utils.toKey(label) : R.key(label));
      if (!sceneKey) continue;
      const candidate = {
        sceneKey: sceneKey,
        displayName: display,
        roleTags: roleTags,
        rankTags: rankTags,
        category: category,
        importanceScore: 1,
        persistent: false,
        major: false
      };
      const rec = S.findOrCreateRecord ? S.findOrCreateRecord(candidate, root) : null;
      if (!rec) continue;
      changes += S.applyCandidateToRecord ? (S.applyCandidateToRecord(candidate, rec, root) || 0) : 0;
      changes += R.pushUnique(actors.localIds, rec.identity.id, 8);
      if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.markDirty === "function") A.Utils.markDirty("actors", rec.identity.id, root);
    }

    if (changes > 0) {
      if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.invalidatePackets === "function") A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
      if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.logEvent === "function") A.Utils.logEvent("Baseline local actor profile created.");
    }
    return changes;
  };

  if (A.ActorProfileSystem) {
    const S = A.ActorProfileSystem;
    if (!S.__rpgStartOldInferRoleTags && typeof S.inferRoleTags === "function") S.__rpgStartOldInferRoleTags = S.inferRoleTags;
    const oldRoles = S.__rpgStartOldInferRoleTags || S.inferRoleTags;
    S.inferRoleTags = function(label) {
      const out = oldRoles ? (oldRoles.call(this, label) || []) : [];
      const lower = String(label || "").toLowerCase();
      const extra = ["bear", "orc", "warrior", "golem", "acolyte", "spider", "troll", "wyvern", "goblin"];
      for (let i = 0; i < extra.length; i += 1) {
        if (new RegExp("\\b" + extra[i] + "\\b", "i").test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, extra[i]);
      }
      return out;
    };

    if (!S.__rpgStartOldInferCategory && typeof S.inferCategory === "function") S.__rpgStartOldInferCategory = S.inferCategory;
    const oldCat = S.__rpgStartOldInferCategory || S.inferCategory;
    S.inferCategory = function(label) {
      const lower = String(label || "").toLowerCase();
      if (/\bgolem\b/.test(lower)) return "construct";
      if (/\bdragon\b|\bdrake\b|\bwyvern\b/.test(lower)) return "draconic";
      if (/\bbear\b|\bwolf\b|\bspider\b|\bbeast\b/.test(lower)) return "beast";
      if (/\btroll\b|\bgoblin\b|\bogre\b/.test(lower)) return "monster";
      return oldCat ? oldCat.call(this, label) : "humanoid";
    };

    if (!S.__rpgStartOldProcessOutput && typeof S.processOutput === "function") S.__rpgStartOldProcessOutput = S.processOutput;
    const oldActorOutput = S.__rpgStartOldProcessOutput || S.processOutput;
    S.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
      root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
      let changes = oldActorOutput ? (oldActorOutput.call(this, rawText, root) || 0) : 0;
      changes += R.applyBaselineActorProfiles(rawText, root) || 0;
      return changes;
    };
  }

  // Make the final version visible through /cardsync and debug cards too.
  if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.logEvent === "function" && typeof state === "object" && state && state.aidrpg) {
    A.Utils.logEvent("RPGStart final hotfix installed.");
  }
})(globalThis.AIDRPG);

// -----------------------------------------------------------------
// RPGSTART1 REFINEMENT PATCH: playable-beta parser breadth cleanup.
// Targets the remaining tester warnings without broad rewrites:
// - record/document item heads
// - source retention for "take X from Y"
// - expanded item mutation wording
// - passive tolerance/resistance safeguards
// - common local actor baseline generation
// -----------------------------------------------------------------
(function installRPGStart1RefinementPatch(A) {
  "use strict";
  if (!A || A.__rpgStart1RefinementPatchInstalled === true) return;
  A.__rpgStart1RefinementPatchInstalled = true;
  A.VERSION = "RPGStart1-1.1.0-beta";
  A.BUILD_STAGE = "RPGStart1-playable-beta-refinement";

  const P = A.RPGStart1RefinementPatch = A.RPGStart1RefinementPatch || {};

  P.norm = function(value) {
    return String(value == null ? "" : value).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  };

  P.toKey = function(value) {
    return P.norm(value).toLowerCase().replace(/[’'`´"]/g, "").replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_").replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "").replace(/_+/g, "_");
  };

  P.pushUnique = function(list, value, cap) {
    const text = P.norm(value);
    if (!Array.isArray(list) || !text) return 0;
    const low = text.toLowerCase();
    for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) {
      if (String(list[i] || "").toLowerCase() === low) return 0;
    }
    list.push(text);
    while (cap && list.length > cap) list.shift();
    return 1;
  };

  P.cleanSourceLabel = function(value) {
    return P.norm(value)
      .replace(/^[,;:\s]+/g, "")
      .replace(/[.!?;,]+$/g, "")
      .replace(/^(?:the|a|an|some|old|dusty)\s+/i, function(m) {
        // Keep useful descriptors like "old chest" / "dusty shelf" except leading articles.
        return /^(?:the|a|an|some)\s+/i.test(m) ? "" : m;
      })
      .trim();
  };

  P.documentHeads = ["tablet", "plaque", "codex", "tome", "blueprint", "deed", "charter", "license"];
  P.documentAliases = [
    "stone tablet", "clay tablet", "rune tablet", "slate tablet", "ancient tablet",
    "bronze plaque", "stone plaque", "folded blueprint", "property deed",
    "guild charter", "merchant license"
  ];

  P.isDocumentHead = function(value) {
    const raw = P.norm(value).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/g, "");
    if (!raw) return false;
    const singular = raw.length > 3 && raw.endsWith("s") ? raw.slice(0, -1) : raw;
    return P.documentHeads.indexOf(raw) !== -1 || P.documentHeads.indexOf(singular) !== -1;
  };

  P.hasDocumentHeadPhrase = function(value) {
    const lower = P.norm(value).toLowerCase();
    if (!lower) return false;
    for (let i = 0; i < P.documentAliases.length; i += 1) {
      if (lower.indexOf(P.documentAliases[i]) !== -1) return true;
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < P.documentHeads.length; i += 1) {
      if (new RegExp("\\b" + P.documentHeads[i] + "s?\\b", "i").test(lower)) return true;
    }
    return false;
  };

  P.extractSourceRows = function(rawText) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const rows = [];
    const verbs = "pick(?:s|ed)?\\s+up|grab(?:s|bed)?|take|takes|took|collect(?:s|ed)?|gather(?:s|ed)?|loot(?:s|ed)?|snatch(?:es|ed)?|snag(?:s|ged)?|nab(?:s|bed)?|yoink(?:s|ed)?|scoop(?:s|ed)?\\s+up|pocket(?:s|ed)?|stash(?:es|ed)?|retrieve(?:s|d)?|recover(?:s|ed)?|find(?:s|found)?|buy|buys|bought|purchase(?:s|d)?|receive(?:s|d)?|accept(?:s|ed)?";
    const patterns = [
      new RegExp("\\byou\\s+(?:" + verbs + ")\\s+([^.!?;\\n]{1,180}?)\\s+from\\s+(?:the\\s+|a\\s+|an\\s+|some\\s+)?([^.!?;\\n]{2,100})", "gi"),
      new RegExp("\\b(?:someone|somebody|a\\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{1,32}|an\\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{1,32}|the\\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{1,32}|[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,40})\\s+(?:gives|gave|hands|handed|offers|offered|passes|passed)\\s+you\\s+([^.!?;\\n]{1,180}?)\\s+from\\s+(?:the\\s+|a\\s+|an\\s+|some\\s+)?([^.!?;\\n]{2,100})", "g")
    ];

    for (let p = 0; p < patterns.length; p += 1) {
      let match;
      while ((match = patterns[p].exec(raw))) {
        const itemPart = P.norm(match[1]).replace(/^(?:the|a|an|some|my|your)\s+/i, "");
        const source = P.cleanSourceLabel(match[2]);
        if (itemPart && source) rows.push({ itemPart: itemPart, source: source, rawPhrase: P.norm(match[0]) });
      }
    }
    return rows;
  };

  P.attachSourceToItem = function(item, source, rawPhrase, root) {
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return 0;
    const cleanSource = P.cleanSourceLabel(source);
    if (!cleanSource) return 0;
    if (!item.notes || typeof item.notes !== "object" || Array.isArray(item.notes)) item.notes = {};
    if (!Array.isArray(item.notes.observedChanges)) item.notes.observedChanges = [];
    let changes = 0;
    if (item.notes.sourceLocation !== cleanSource) { item.notes.sourceLocation = cleanSource; changes += 1; }
    if (item.notes.observedSource !== cleanSource) { item.notes.observedSource = cleanSource; changes += 1; }
    if (rawPhrase && !item.notes.lastSourcePhrase) { item.notes.lastSourcePhrase = P.norm(rawPhrase).slice(0, 160); changes += 1; }
    changes += P.pushUnique(item.notes.observedChanges, "Source: " + cleanSource, 12);
    if (changes > 0) {
      if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.markDirty === "function" && item.id) A.Utils.markDirty("items", item.id, root);
      if (A.CardSyncSystem && typeof A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty === "function" && item.id) A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty("item", item.id, "source_note", null, root);
      if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.invalidatePackets === "function") A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
    }
    return changes;
  };

  P.processSourceRetention = function(rawText, root) {
    root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
    if (!root || !A.InventorySystem) return 0;
    const I = A.InventorySystem;
    if (typeof I.init === "function") I.init(root);
    if (typeof I.hasAcquisitionNegation === "function" && I.hasAcquisitionNegation(rawText, root)) return 0;

    const rows = P.extractSourceRows(rawText);
    let changes = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += 1) {
      const row = rows[i];
      let cleanItem = row.itemPart;
      try {
        cleanItem = I.cleanItemPhrase ? (I.cleanItemPhrase(row.itemPart) || row.itemPart) : row.itemPart;
      } catch (_error) {}

      let isConcrete = false;
      try { isConcrete = !I.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase || I.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(cleanItem); } catch (_error2) { isConcrete = false; }
      if (!isConcrete && P.hasDocumentHeadPhrase(cleanItem)) isConcrete = true;
      if (!isConcrete) continue;

      let item = null;
      if (typeof I.findMatchingPlayerItem === "function") item = I.findMatchingPlayerItem(cleanItem, root);
      if (!item && typeof I.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase === "function") item = I.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase(cleanItem, "packed", "story_gain", root);
      if (!item && typeof I.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase === "function") item = I.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase(row.itemPart, "packed", "story_gain", root);
      if (!item) continue;
      changes += P.attachSourceToItem(item, row.source, row.rawPhrase, root);
    }
    if (changes > 0 && A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.logEvent === "function") A.Utils.logEvent("RPGStart1 source notes retained.");
    return changes;
  };

  if (A.InventorySystem) {
    const I = A.InventorySystem;

    if (!I.__rpgStart1OldIsItemHeadToken && typeof I.isItemHeadToken === "function") I.__rpgStart1OldIsItemHeadToken = I.isItemHeadToken;
    const oldHead = I.__rpgStart1OldIsItemHeadToken || I.isItemHeadToken;
    I.isItemHeadToken = function(value) {
      if (P.isDocumentHead(value)) return true;
      return oldHead ? oldHead.call(this, value) : false;
    };

    if (!I.__rpgStart1OldDetectBaseType && typeof I.detectBaseType === "function") I.__rpgStart1OldDetectBaseType = I.detectBaseType;
    const oldDetect = I.__rpgStart1OldDetectBaseType || I.detectBaseType;
    I.detectBaseType = function(lowerPhrase) {
      const lower = String(lowerPhrase || "").toLowerCase();
      for (let i = 0; i < P.documentAliases.length; i += 1) {
        if (lower.indexOf(P.documentAliases[i]) !== -1) {
          const parts = P.documentAliases[i].split(/\s+/);
          return parts[parts.length - 1];
        }
      }
      for (let i = 0; i < P.documentHeads.length; i += 1) {
        if (new RegExp("\\b" + P.documentHeads[i] + "s?\\b", "i").test(lower)) return P.documentHeads[i];
      }
      return oldDetect ? oldDetect.call(this, lowerPhrase) : "item";
    };

    if (!I.__rpgStart1OldIsLikelyConcreteItemPhrase && typeof I.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase === "function") I.__rpgStart1OldIsLikelyConcreteItemPhrase = I.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase;
    const oldLikely = I.__rpgStart1OldIsLikelyConcreteItemPhrase || I.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase;
    I.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase = function(phrase) {
      if (oldLikely && oldLikely.call(this, phrase)) return true;
      const text = P.norm(phrase).toLowerCase();
      if (!text || text.split(/\s+/).length > 8) return false;
      if (typeof this.shouldRejectAcquisitionCapture === "function" && this.shouldRejectAcquisitionCapture(text)) return false;
      return P.hasDocumentHeadPhrase(text);
    };

    if (!I.__rpgStart1OldMakeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase && typeof I.makeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase === "function") I.__rpgStart1OldMakeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase = I.makeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase;
    const oldSeed = I.__rpgStart1OldMakeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase || I.makeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase;
    I.makeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase = function(phrase, sourceContext) {
      const seed = oldSeed ? oldSeed.call(this, phrase, sourceContext) : null;
      if (!seed || typeof seed !== "object") return seed;
      const rows = P.extractSourceRows("You take " + String(phrase || ""));
      const source = rows.length ? rows[0].source : "";
      if (source) {
        if (!seed.notes || typeof seed.notes !== "object" || Array.isArray(seed.notes)) seed.notes = {};
        if (!Array.isArray(seed.notes.observedChanges)) seed.notes.observedChanges = [];
        seed.notes.sourceLocation = source;
        seed.notes.observedSource = source;
        P.pushUnique(seed.notes.observedChanges, "Source: " + source, 12);
      }
      return seed;
    };

    P.applyMutationVariants = function(rawText, root) {
      root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
      if (!root || !A.InventorySystem) return 0;
      const Inv = A.InventorySystem;
      if (typeof Inv.init === "function") Inv.init(root);
      const raw = String(rawText || "");
      if (!raw.trim()) return 0;
      const patterns = [
        { re: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,90}?)\s+(?:is|are|gets?|becomes?)\s+(?:broken|cracked\s+apart|smashed\s+apart)\b/gi, tag: "broken", loss: 45 },
        { re: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,90}?)\s+(?:is|are|gets?|becomes?)\s+(?:smashed|splintered)\b/gi, tag: "broken", loss: 35 },
        { re: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,90}?)\s+(?:is|are|gets?|becomes?)\s+(?:bent|dulled|chipped)\b/gi, tag: "damaged", loss: 15 }
      ];
      let changes = 0;
      for (let p = 0; p < patterns.length; p += 1) {
        let m;
        while ((m = patterns[p].re.exec(raw))) {
          let label = m[1] ? P.norm(m[1]) : "";
          if (!label) continue;
          try { label = Inv.cleanPhrase ? (Inv.cleanPhrase(label) || label) : label; } catch (_error) {}
          const item = Inv.findMatchingPlayerItem ? Inv.findMatchingPlayerItem(label, root) : null;
          if (!item || !item.id) continue;
          if (Inv.applyStateTag && Inv.applyStateTag(item.id, patterns[p].tag, patterns[p].loss, root)) {
            changes += 1;
            P.attachSourceToItem(item, "", "", root); // no-op except shape safety
            if (A.CardSyncSystem && typeof A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty === "function") A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty("item", item.id, "item_condition", null, root);
          }
        }
      }
      if (changes > 0 && A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.logEvent === "function") A.Utils.logEvent("RPGStart1 item mutation captured.");
      return changes;
    };

    if (!I.__rpgStart1OldProcessOutput && typeof I.processOutput === "function") I.__rpgStart1OldProcessOutput = I.processOutput;
    const oldInvOutput = I.__rpgStart1OldProcessOutput || I.processOutput;
    I.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
      root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
      let changes = oldInvOutput ? (oldInvOutput.call(this, rawText, root) || 0) : 0;
      changes += P.processSourceRetention(rawText, root) || 0;
      changes += P.applyMutationVariants(rawText, root) || 0;
      return changes;
    };
  }

  P.passiveRows = [
    { phrase: "fire resistance", category: "resistance", domain: "fire", remove: ["burned", "burning"] },
    { phrase: "cold resistance", category: "resistance", domain: "cold", remove: ["chilled", "frozen"] },
    { phrase: "poison tolerance", category: "adaptation", domain: "poison tolerance", remove: ["poisoned", "poison"] },
    { phrase: "iron stomach", category: "adaptation", domain: "iron stomach", remove: [] },
    { phrase: "pain tolerance", category: "adaptation", domain: "pain tolerance", remove: [] },
    { phrase: "night vision", category: "adaptation", domain: "night vision", remove: ["blinded"] },
    { phrase: "quick reflexes", category: "adaptation", domain: "quick reflexes", remove: [] },
    { phrase: "mana sensitivity", category: "sensitivity", domain: "mana", remove: [] }
  ];

  P.applyPassiveTraits = function(rawText, root) {
    root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
    if (!root || !A.PlayerSystem) return 0;
    if (typeof A.PlayerSystem.init === "function") A.PlayerSystem.init(root);
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const lower = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
    if (!lower) return 0;

    const safeContext = /\b(?:adapts?|develops?|developed|becomes?\s+natural|becomes?\s+passive|passive\s+trait|permanent\s+trait|remains?\s+as\s+a\s+trait|settles?\s+into\s+(?:your|the)\s+body|takes?\s+root\s+in\s+(?:your|the)\s+body|now\s+part\s+of\s+you|becomes?\s+part\s+of\s+(?:you|your\s+body))\b/.test(lower);
    if (!safeContext) return 0;

    const player = root.player || {};
    if (!player.conditions || typeof player.conditions !== "object" || Array.isArray(player.conditions)) player.conditions = {};
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.active)) player.conditions.active = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.permanent)) player.conditions.permanent = [];

    let changes = 0;
    const T = A.PersistentTraitSystem;
    if (T && typeof T.init === "function") T.init(root);

    for (let i = 0; i < P.passiveRows.length; i += 1) {
      const row = P.passiveRows[i];
      if (lower.indexOf(row.phrase) === -1) continue;
      if (T && typeof T.applyTraitCandidate === "function") changes += T.applyTraitCandidate({ category: row.category, domain: row.domain }, raw, root) || 0;
      changes += P.pushUnique(player.conditions.permanent, row.phrase, 24);
      for (let r = 0; r < row.remove.length; r += 1) {
        const cond = row.remove[r];
        if (T && typeof T.removeConditionByName === "function") changes += T.removeConditionByName(cond, root) || 0;
        for (let a = player.conditions.active.length - 1; a >= 0; a -= 1) {
          const active = String(player.conditions.active[a] || "").toLowerCase();
          if (active === cond || active === cond.replace(/ed$/, "")) {
            player.conditions.active.splice(a, 1);
            changes += 1;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    if (changes > 0) {
      if (T && typeof T.syncLegacyPlayerArrays === "function") T.syncLegacyPlayerArrays(root);
      for (let i = 0; i < P.passiveRows.length; i += 1) {
        if (lower.indexOf(P.passiveRows[i].phrase) !== -1) P.pushUnique(player.conditions.permanent, P.passiveRows[i].phrase, 24);
      }
      if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.markDirty === "function") A.Utils.markDirty("player", true, root);
      if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.invalidatePackets === "function") A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
      if (A.CardSyncSystem && typeof A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty === "function") A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty("passive", "summary", "passive_trait", null, root);
      if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.logEvent === "function") A.Utils.logEvent("RPGStart1 passive trait captured.");
    }
    return changes;
  };

  if (A.PersistentTraitSystem && typeof A.PersistentTraitSystem.processOutput === "function") {
    const T = A.PersistentTraitSystem;
    if (!T.__rpgStart1OldProcessOutput) T.__rpgStart1OldProcessOutput = T.processOutput;
    const oldTraitOutput = T.__rpgStart1OldProcessOutput;
    T.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
      root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
      let changes = oldTraitOutput ? (oldTraitOutput.call(this, rawText, root) || 0) : 0;
      changes += P.applyPassiveTraits(rawText, root) || 0;
      return changes;
    };
  }

  P.extractLocalActorLabels = function(rawText) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const heads = ["guard", "knight", "mage", "archmage", "bear", "dragon", "orc", "warrior", "golem", "acolyte", "spider", "wolf", "troll", "wyvern", "captain", "soldier", "beast", "scout", "archer", "bandit", "goblin", "cultist"];
    const headRe = heads.slice().sort(function(a, b) { return b.length - a.length; }).join("|");
    const patterns = [
      new RegExp("\\b(?:a|an|the)\\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{0,36}?(?:" + headRe + "))\\s+(?:stands?|waits?|watches?|looms?|lurks?|blocks?|guards?|paces?|crouches?)\\b(?:[^.!?]{0,90})", "gi"),
      new RegExp("\\b(?:nearby|ahead|before\\s+you|at\\s+the\\s+gate|on\\s+the\\s+road|from\\s+the\\s+door)\\s+(?:stands?|waits?|watches?|looms?|lurks?)\\s+(?:a|an|the)\\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{0,36}?(?:" + headRe + "))\\b", "gi")
    ];
    const labels = [];
    for (let p = 0; p < patterns.length; p += 1) {
      let m;
      while ((m = patterns[p].exec(raw))) {
        const label = P.norm(m[1]).replace(/^(?:a|an|the)\s+/i, "");
        if (!label || label.split(/\s+/).length > 5) continue;
        const low = label.toLowerCase();
        let duplicate = false;
        for (let i = 0; i < labels.length; i += 1) if (labels[i].toLowerCase() === low) duplicate = true;
        if (!duplicate) labels.push(label);
      }
    }
    return labels;
  };

  P.applyLocalActorBaselines = function(rawText, root) {
    root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
    if (!root || !A.ActorProfileSystem) return 0;
    const S = A.ActorProfileSystem;
    if (typeof S.init === "function") S.init(root);
    const labels = P.extractLocalActorLabels(rawText);
    if (!labels.length) return 0;
    if (!root.actors || typeof root.actors !== "object") root.actors = {};
    if (!Array.isArray(root.actors.localIds)) root.actors.localIds = [];

    let changes = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < labels.length; i += 1) {
      const label = labels[i];
      const sceneKey = A.Utils && A.Utils.toKey ? A.Utils.toKey(label) : P.toKey(label);
      if (!sceneKey) continue;
      const displayName = label.replace(/\b\w/g, function(c) { return c.toUpperCase(); });
      const roleTags = S.inferRoleTags ? S.inferRoleTags(label) : [];
      const rankTags = S.inferRankTags ? S.inferRankTags(label) : [];
      const category = S.inferCategory ? S.inferCategory(label) : "humanoid";
      const candidate = { sceneKey: sceneKey, displayName: displayName, roleTags: roleTags, rankTags: rankTags, category: category, importanceScore: 1, persistent: false, major: false };
      const rec = S.findOrCreateRecord ? S.findOrCreateRecord(candidate, root) : null;
      if (!rec || !rec.identity) continue;
      changes += S.applyCandidateToRecord ? (S.applyCandidateToRecord(candidate, rec, root) || 0) : 0;
      changes += P.pushUnique(root.actors.localIds, rec.identity.id, 10);
      if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.markDirty === "function") A.Utils.markDirty("actors", rec.identity.id, root);
    }
    if (changes > 0) {
      if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.invalidatePackets === "function") A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
      if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.logEvent === "function") A.Utils.logEvent("RPGStart1 local actor baseline captured.");
    }
    return changes;
  };

  if (A.ActorProfileSystem) {
    const S = A.ActorProfileSystem;
    if (!S.__rpgStart1OldInferRoleTags && typeof S.inferRoleTags === "function") S.__rpgStart1OldInferRoleTags = S.inferRoleTags;
    const oldRoles = S.__rpgStart1OldInferRoleTags || S.inferRoleTags;
    S.inferRoleTags = function(label) {
      const out = oldRoles ? (oldRoles.call(this, label) || []) : [];
      const lower = String(label || "").toLowerCase();
      const extra = ["guard", "knight", "mage", "archmage", "bear", "dragon", "orc", "warrior", "golem", "acolyte", "spider", "wolf", "troll", "wyvern", "captain", "soldier", "beast", "cultist"];
      for (let i = 0; i < extra.length; i += 1) {
        if (new RegExp("\\b" + extra[i] + "\\b", "i").test(lower) && this.pushUniqueLocal) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, extra[i]);
      }
      return out;
    };

    if (!S.__rpgStart1OldInferCategory && typeof S.inferCategory === "function") S.__rpgStart1OldInferCategory = S.inferCategory;
    const oldCat = S.__rpgStart1OldInferCategory || S.inferCategory;
    S.inferCategory = function(label) {
      const lower = String(label || "").toLowerCase();
      if (/\bgolem\b/.test(lower)) return "construct";
      if (/\bdragon\b|\bdrake\b|\bwyvern\b/.test(lower)) return "draconic";
      if (/\bbear\b|\bwolf\b|\bspider\b|\bbeast\b/.test(lower)) return "beast";
      if (/\btroll\b|\bgoblin\b|\bogre\b/.test(lower)) return "monster";
      return oldCat ? oldCat.call(this, label) : "humanoid";
    };

    if (!S.__rpgStart1OldProcessOutput && typeof S.processOutput === "function") S.__rpgStart1OldProcessOutput = S.processOutput;
    const oldActorOutput = S.__rpgStart1OldProcessOutput || S.processOutput;
    S.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
      root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
      let changes = oldActorOutput ? (oldActorOutput.call(this, rawText, root) || 0) : 0;
      changes += P.applyLocalActorBaselines(rawText, root) || 0;
      return changes;
    };
  }

  if (A.CardSyncSystem && typeof A.CardSyncSystem.serializeItemCard === "function") {
    const C = A.CardSyncSystem;
    if (!C.__rpgStart1OldSerializeItemCard) C.__rpgStart1OldSerializeItemCard = C.serializeItemCard;
    const oldSerialize = C.__rpgStart1OldSerializeItemCard;
    C.serializeItemCard = function(id, root) {
      const spec = oldSerialize ? oldSerialize.call(this, id, root) : null;
      const item = root && root.items && root.items.byId ? root.items.byId[id] : null;
      if (!spec || !item || !item.notes) return spec;
      const extra = [];
      if (item.notes.sourceLocation) extra.push("Source Location: " + item.notes.sourceLocation);
      if (item.notes.observedSource && item.notes.observedSource !== item.notes.sourceLocation) extra.push("Observed Source: " + item.notes.observedSource);
      if (!extra.length) return spec;
      const existing = String(spec.entry || "").toLowerCase();
      for (let i = 0; i < extra.length; i += 1) if (existing.indexOf(extra[i].toLowerCase()) === -1) spec.entry = (spec.entry || "") + "\n" + extra[i];
      if (this.stableHash) spec.hash = this.stableHash(spec.entry || "");
      return spec;
    };
  }

  if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.logEvent === "function" && typeof state === "object" && state && state.aidrpg) {
    A.Utils.logEvent("RPGStart1 refinement patch installed.");
  }
})(globalThis.AIDRPG);

// -----------------------------------------------------------------
// RPGSTART1 PASSIVE MEMORY MICROFIX:
// Preserve previously recorded passive trait names when legacy trait sync
// rebuilds the visible permanent-condition array.
// -----------------------------------------------------------------
(function installRPGStart1PassiveMemoryMicrofix(A) {
  "use strict";
  if (!A || A.__rpgStart1PassiveMemoryMicrofixInstalled === true) return;
  A.__rpgStart1PassiveMemoryMicrofixInstalled = true;
  const P = A.RPGStart1RefinementPatch;
  if (!P || typeof P.applyPassiveTraits !== "function" || !Array.isArray(P.passiveRows)) return;
  const oldApply = P.applyPassiveTraits;
  P.applyPassiveTraits = function(rawText, root) {
    root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
    const remembered = [];
    if (root && root.player && root.player.conditions && Array.isArray(root.player.conditions.permanent)) {
      const current = root.player.conditions.permanent;
      for (let i = 0; i < current.length; i += 1) {
        const low = String(current[i] || "").toLowerCase();
        for (let r = 0; r < P.passiveRows.length; r += 1) {
          if (low === P.passiveRows[r].phrase) remembered.push(P.passiveRows[r].phrase);
        }
      }
    }
    let changes = oldApply.call(this, rawText, root) || 0;
    if (root && root.player && root.player.conditions && Array.isArray(root.player.conditions.permanent)) {
      for (let i = 0; i < remembered.length; i += 1) {
        changes += P.pushUnique(root.player.conditions.permanent, remembered[i], 24) || 0;
      }
      if (changes > 0) {
        if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.markDirty === "function") A.Utils.markDirty("player", true, root);
        if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.invalidatePackets === "function") A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
      }
    }
    return changes;
  };
})(globalThis.AIDRPG);

// -----------------------------------------------------------------
// RPGSTART1 PASSIVE WRAPPER ORDER FIX:
// Capture passive names before older wrappers rebuild condition arrays.
// -----------------------------------------------------------------
(function installRPGStart1PassiveWrapperOrderFix(A) {
  "use strict";
  if (!A || A.__rpgStart1PassiveWrapperOrderFixInstalled === true) return;
  A.__rpgStart1PassiveWrapperOrderFixInstalled = true;
  const P = A.RPGStart1RefinementPatch;
  const T = A.PersistentTraitSystem;
  if (!P || !Array.isArray(P.passiveRows) || !T || typeof T.processOutput !== "function") return;
  const wrapped = T.processOutput;
  T.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
    root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
    const remembered = [];
    if (root && root.player && root.player.conditions && Array.isArray(root.player.conditions.permanent)) {
      for (let i = 0; i < root.player.conditions.permanent.length; i += 1) {
        const low = String(root.player.conditions.permanent[i] || "").toLowerCase();
        for (let r = 0; r < P.passiveRows.length; r += 1) {
          if (low === P.passiveRows[r].phrase && remembered.indexOf(P.passiveRows[r].phrase) === -1) remembered.push(P.passiveRows[r].phrase);
        }
      }
    }
    let changes = wrapped.call(this, rawText, root) || 0;
    if (root && root.player && root.player.conditions && Array.isArray(root.player.conditions.permanent)) {
      for (let i = 0; i < remembered.length; i += 1) {
        changes += P.pushUnique(root.player.conditions.permanent, remembered[i], 24) || 0;
      }
      if (remembered.length && A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.markDirty === "function") A.Utils.markDirty("player", true, root);
      if (remembered.length && A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.invalidatePackets === "function") A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
    }
    return changes;
  };
})(globalThis.AIDRPG);

// -----------------------------------------------------------------
// RPGSTART FINAL PASSIVE / SKILL FREEDOM PATCH:
// - Safe story-prose passive discovery from implication phrases.
// - Passive tolerance/resistance wording cannot become harmful active conditions.
// - Trailing classifier words are stripped from ability names.
// - Confirmed story passives are mirrored into AbilitySystem + passive trait memory.
// -----------------------------------------------------------------
(function installRPGStartFinalPassiveFreedomPatch(A) {
  "use strict";
  if (!A || A.__rpgStartFinalPassiveFreedomPatchInstalled === true) return;
  A.__rpgStartFinalPassiveFreedomPatchInstalled = true;

  A.VERSION = "RPGStartFinal-1.1.1-beta";
