  A.BUILD_STAGE = "RPGStartFinal-passive-skill-freedom";

  const P = A.RPGStart1RefinementPatch = A.RPGStart1RefinementPatch || {};

  if (typeof P.norm !== "function") {
    P.norm = function(value) {
      return String(value == null ? "" : value).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    };
  }

  if (typeof P.pushUnique !== "function") {
    P.pushUnique = function(list, value, cap) {
      const text = P.norm(value);
      if (!Array.isArray(list) || !text) return 0;
      const low = text.toLowerCase();
      for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) {
        if (String(list[i] || "").toLowerCase() === low) return 0;
      }
      list.push(text);
      while (cap && list.length > cap) list.shift();
      return 1;
    };
  }

  P.stripAbilityClassifierSuffix = function(value) {
    let text = P.norm(value)
      .replace(/["“”'`]+/g, "")
      .replace(/\s+(?:as\s+)?(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?passive(?:\s+(?:trait|fighting\s+rhythm|rhythm|style|instinct))?\s*$/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+(?:as\s+)?(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?(?:skill|talent|ability|technique)\s*$/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+style\s+style\s*$/i, " Style")
      .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();
    return text;
  };

  if (A.AbilitySystem && typeof A.AbilitySystem.cleanAbilityName === "function") {
    const S = A.AbilitySystem;
    if (!S.__rpgStartFinalOldCleanAbilityName) S.__rpgStartFinalOldCleanAbilityName = S.cleanAbilityName;
    const oldClean = S.__rpgStartFinalOldCleanAbilityName;
    S.cleanAbilityName = function(value) {
      let cleaned = oldClean ? oldClean.call(this, value) : P.norm(value);
      cleaned = P.stripAbilityClassifierSuffix(cleaned);
      if (!cleaned) return "";
      const recleaned = oldClean ? oldClean.call(this, cleaned) : cleaned;
      cleaned = P.stripAbilityClassifierSuffix(recleaned || cleaned);
      if (!cleaned || cleaned.length < 2 || cleaned.length > 48 || cleaned.split(/\s+/).length > 6) return "";
      const banned = /^(?:magic|ability|skill|talent|passive|power|attack|move|technique|training|it|that|this)$/i;
      return banned.test(cleaned) ? "" : cleaned;
    };
  }

  P.isPassiveLikeName = function(name, rawText) {
    const clean = P.stripAbilityClassifierSuffix(name);
    if (!clean) return false;
    const lower = clean.toLowerCase();
    const raw = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();

    if (/\b(?:passive|trait|resistance|tolerance|instinct|reflexes|sensitivity|night\s+vision|vision|iron\s+stomach|patience|rhythm|flow|breath|attunement|affinity|adaptation|sense|stance|style)\b/.test(lower)) {
      return true;
    }

    if (/\b(?:as\s+a\s+passive|passive\s+trait|passive\s+fighting\s+rhythm|becomes?\s+natural|becomes?\s+part\s+of\s+you|settles?\s+into\s+your\s+body|leaves?\s+you\s+with)\b/.test(raw)) {
      if (/^[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]*(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]*){1,4}$/.test(clean)) return true;
    }

    if (/\b(?:poisoned|burning|burned|frozen|freezing|chilled|bleeding|broken|wounded|injured|sick|diseased)\b/.test(lower) && !/\b(?:resistance|tolerance|immunity)\b/.test(lower)) {
      return false;
    }

    return false;
  };

  P.cleanStoryPassiveName = function(value, rawText) {
    let text = P.norm(value)
      .replace(/^(?:a|an|the|your|my)\s+/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+(?:as|into|onto|during|while|because|from|through|with)\b.*$/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+(?:now|finally|permanently)$/i, "")
      .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
      .trim();
    text = P.stripAbilityClassifierSuffix(text);
    if (A.AbilitySystem && typeof A.AbilitySystem.cleanAbilityName === "function") {
      text = A.AbilitySystem.cleanAbilityName(text) || text;
    }
    if (!P.isPassiveLikeName(text, rawText)) return "";
    return text;
  };

  P.extractStoryPassiveCandidates = function(rawText) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const candidates = [];
    const seen = {};

    const add = function(name, reason, matchText) {
      const clean = P.cleanStoryPassiveName(name, raw);
      if (!clean) return;
      const key = clean.toLowerCase();
      if (seen[key]) return;
      seen[key] = true;
      candidates.push({
        name: clean,
        reason: reason,
        evidence: P.norm(matchText || raw).slice(0, 180)
      });
    };

    const patterns = [
      { re: /\b(?:leaves?|left)\s+you\s+with\s+([^.!?;\n]{2,80})/gi, reason: "left as a lasting passive" },
      { re: /\b(?:teach|teaches|taught)\s+you\s+([^.!?;\n]{2,80}?)\s+as\s+a\s+passive(?:\s+(?:trait|fighting\s+rhythm|rhythm|style|instinct))?/gi, reason: "taught as a passive" },
      { re: /\b([^.!?;\n]{2,80}?)\s+(?:becomes?|became)\s+(?:natural|part\s+of\s+you|a\s+passive(?:\s+(?:trait|fighting\s+rhythm|rhythm|style|instinct))?)/gi, reason: "became natural" },
      { re: /\b([^.!?;\n]{2,80}?)\s+settles?\s+into\s+(?:your|the)\s+body/gi, reason: "settled into the body" },
      { re: /\b(?:you\s+)?(?:retain|retains|keeps?|kept)\s+([^.!?;\n]{2,80}?\b(?:resistance|tolerance|instinct|reflexes|sensitivity|vision))\b/gi, reason: "retained as a passive" }
    ];

    for (let p = 0; p < patterns.length; p += 1) {
      let m;
      while ((m = patterns[p].re.exec(raw))) {
        add(m[1], patterns[p].reason, m[0]);
      }
    }

    let resistant;
    const resistantRe = /\b(?:leaves?|left)\s+you\s+resistant\s+to\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{2,40})/gi;
    while ((resistant = resistantRe.exec(raw))) {
      add(P.norm(resistant[1]) + " resistance", "left resistant", resistant[0]);
    }

    let retainResistance;
    const retainRe = /\bafter\s+the\s+ordeal,?\s+you\s+retain\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{2,40})\s+resistance\b/gi;
    while ((retainResistance = retainRe.exec(raw))) {
      add(P.norm(retainResistance[1]) + " resistance", "retained after ordeal", retainResistance[0]);
    }

    return candidates;
  };

  P.passiveSemanticRows = function() {
    const base = Array.isArray(P.passiveRows) ? P.passiveRows.slice() : [];
    const extras = [
      { phrase: "fire resistance", category: "resistance", domain: "fire", remove: ["burned", "burning"] },
      { phrase: "cold resistance", category: "resistance", domain: "cold", remove: ["chilled", "frozen", "freezing"] },
      { phrase: "poison tolerance", category: "adaptation", domain: "poison tolerance", remove: ["poisoned", "poison"] },
      { phrase: "poison resistance", category: "resistance", domain: "poison", remove: ["poisoned", "poison"] },
      { phrase: "iron stomach", category: "adaptation", domain: "iron stomach", remove: [] },
      { phrase: "pain tolerance", category: "adaptation", domain: "pain tolerance", remove: [] },
      { phrase: "quick reflexes", category: "adaptation", domain: "quick reflexes", remove: [] },
      { phrase: "mana sensitivity", category: "sensitivity", domain: "mana", remove: [] },
      { phrase: "night vision", category: "adaptation", domain: "night vision", remove: ["blinded"] }
    ];
    for (let i = 0; i < extras.length; i += 1) {
      let exists = false;
      for (let j = 0; j < base.length; j += 1) if (String(base[j].phrase || "").toLowerCase() === extras[i].phrase) exists = true;
      if (!exists) base.push(extras[i]);
    }
    return base;
  };

  P.removeHarmfulConditionForPassive = function(name, root) {
    if (!root || !root.player) return 0;
    const lower = String(name || "").toLowerCase();
    const remove = [];
    if (/\bpoison\b/.test(lower) && /\b(?:tolerance|resistance|immunity)\b/.test(lower)) remove.push("poisoned", "poison");
    if (/\bfire\b/.test(lower) && /\b(?:resistance|tolerance|immunity)\b/.test(lower)) remove.push("burned", "burning");
    if (/\bcold|frost|ice\b/.test(lower) && /\b(?:resistance|tolerance|immunity)\b/.test(lower)) remove.push("chilled", "frozen", "freezing");
    if (/\bnight\s+vision\b/.test(lower)) remove.push("blinded");

    let changes = 0;
    const T = A.PersistentTraitSystem;
    if (T && typeof T.removeConditionByName === "function") {
      for (let i = 0; i < remove.length; i += 1) changes += T.removeConditionByName(remove[i], root) || 0;
    }

    if (root.player.conditions && Array.isArray(root.player.conditions.active)) {
      for (let i = root.player.conditions.active.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
        const active = String(root.player.conditions.active[i] || "").toLowerCase();
        for (let r = 0; r < remove.length; r += 1) {
          const target = String(remove[r] || "").toLowerCase();
          if (active === target || active === target.replace(/ed$/, "")) {
            root.player.conditions.active.splice(i, 1);
            changes += 1;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    return changes;
  };

  P.commitStoryPassiveCandidate = function(candidate, rawText, root) {
    root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
    if (!root || !candidate || !candidate.name || !A.AbilitySystem) return 0;
    const S = A.AbilitySystem;
    if (typeof S.init === "function") S.init(root);

    const lower = String(candidate.name || "").toLowerCase();
    const semanticRows = P.passiveSemanticRows();
    let semanticRow = null;
    for (let i = 0; i < semanticRows.length; i += 1) {
      if (lower === semanticRows[i].phrase || lower.indexOf(semanticRows[i].phrase) !== -1) {
        semanticRow = semanticRows[i];
        break;
      }
    }

    const tags = ["story_prose", "passive_like", "lasting_adaptation"];
    if (semanticRow && semanticRow.domain) tags.push(String(semanticRow.domain));

    const abilityCandidate = {
      name: candidate.name,
      type: "passive",
      mode: "learn",
      confidence: 0.76,
      originContext: rawText,
      intendedFunction: semanticRow ? ("passive " + semanticRow.category + ": " + semanticRow.domain) : (candidate.reason || "passive adaptation"),
      stableMeaningFlags: ["story_prose_passive", "safe_passive_context", "origin_preserved"],
      keyPhrases: [candidate.name, String(candidate.name).toLowerCase()],
      sourceIntentType: "story_prose_passive",
      sourceTags: tags
    };

    let changes = 0;
    if (typeof S.confirmCandidate === "function") {
      changes += S.confirmCandidate(abilityCandidate, rawText, root) || 0;
    }

    if (!root.player || typeof root.player !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.player)) root.player = {};
    if (!root.player.conditions || typeof root.player.conditions !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.player.conditions)) root.player.conditions = {};
    if (!Array.isArray(root.player.conditions.permanent)) root.player.conditions.permanent = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(root.player.conditions.active)) root.player.conditions.active = [];

    changes += P.pushUnique(root.player.conditions.permanent, candidate.name, 24);

    if (semanticRow && A.PersistentTraitSystem && typeof A.PersistentTraitSystem.applyTraitCandidate === "function") {
      changes += A.PersistentTraitSystem.applyTraitCandidate({ category: semanticRow.category, domain: semanticRow.domain }, rawText, root) || 0;
    }

    changes += P.removeHarmfulConditionForPassive(candidate.name, root);

    if (changes > 0) {
      if (A.PersistentTraitSystem && typeof A.PersistentTraitSystem.syncLegacyPlayerArrays === "function") A.PersistentTraitSystem.syncLegacyPlayerArrays(root);
      // syncLegacyPlayerArrays may rebuild permanent arrays; re-add the visible passive name afterward.
      if (root.player && root.player.conditions && Array.isArray(root.player.conditions.permanent)) {
        P.pushUnique(root.player.conditions.permanent, candidate.name, 24);
      }
      if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.markDirty === "function") {
        A.Utils.markDirty("player", true, root);
        A.Utils.markDirty("abilities", "summary", root);
      }
      if (A.CardSyncSystem && typeof A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty === "function") {
        A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty("ability", "summary", "story_passive", null, root);
        A.CardSyncSystem.queueDirty("passive", "summary", "story_passive", null, root);
      }
      if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.invalidatePackets === "function") A.Utils.invalidatePackets(root);
    }
    return changes;
  };

  P.captureStoryProsePassives = function(rawText, root) {
    root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
    if (!root) return 0;
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    if (!raw.trim()) return 0;
    const candidates = P.extractStoryPassiveCandidates(raw);
    if (!candidates.length) return 0;
    let changes = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i += 1) {
      changes += P.commitStoryPassiveCandidate(candidates[i], raw, root) || 0;
    }
    if (changes > 0 && A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.logEvent === "function") {
      A.Utils.logEvent("Story-prose passive captured as ability/trait.");
    }
    return changes;
  };

  if (A.PersistentTraitSystem && typeof A.PersistentTraitSystem.processOutput === "function") {
    const T = A.PersistentTraitSystem;
    if (!T.__rpgStartFinalOldProcessOutput) T.__rpgStartFinalOldProcessOutput = T.processOutput;
    const oldTraitProcess = T.__rpgStartFinalOldProcessOutput;
    T.processOutput = function(rawText, root) {
      root = root || (typeof state !== "undefined" ? state.aidrpg : null);
      let changes = oldTraitProcess ? (oldTraitProcess.call(this, rawText, root) || 0) : 0;
      changes += P.captureStoryProsePassives(rawText, root) || 0;
      return changes;
    };
  }

  if (A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.logEvent === "function" && typeof state === "object" && state && state.aidrpg) {
    A.Utils.logEvent("RPGStart final passive freedom patch installed.");
  }
})(globalThis.AIDRPG);
